**Ngika-Avery.**

Lowo ongifundise ukususa izibuko zami, ngithobe ngize ezingeni lakho, ngikubheke emehlweni, ngikubone kahle — futhi ngikhulume nengane eneminyaka emihlanu njengomuntu olalelwayo, okuyinto okufanele siyithole sonke, kodwa engavamile. Ungifundise ukuthi uma umuntu efuna into bese ephendulwa ngodlame, lokho akusona isixazululo, futhi indlela yokungena ayilona leyo obuyicabanga. Amandla awenzelwe ukucindezela abancane. Thina singabantu abakhulu, futhi siyakhohlwa ukuthi sisebenzisa imizimba yethu yokuvikela njengengathi iyethusa — bese siyibhekisa, singaqapheli, kubantu esingabafela noma yinini. Ungilungisile lapho. Yonke into enomusa kulezi zincwadi — indoda engaphula igumbi kodwa ekhetha ukubona, *Ngiyakubona* ize isho ukuthi *uphephile* — konke ngikufunde kuwe, ngezandla zakho ezincane ezinamandla nezithambile.

Ukhanya kakhulu, unomusa kuyo yonke imfuyo, futhi uthanda izinto zamasosha, izinto zokuphila, izinto zokwazi ukuthi ungaphila kanjani. Ngakho le ncwadi ngeyakho, futhi iphethe ukuphela kokuba ngisole ukuthi ngingakudlulisela:

*Yiba nesibindi njenge-African honey badger* — incane, ingesabi lutho ehlane; iyemba, iyaphula, ayikukhululi. Usuyikho vele. Mina ngikubhale phansi nje.

# Isingeniso

### *kaReichl Macintoch* †

Ngakha umsebenzi wami wonke ngikholwa into eyodwa, engathi ayihlonipheki: ukuthi ungakwazi ukwethusa futhi ujabulise umfundi kakhulu nge-equation yangempela kunanoma yiliphi isilo, uma nje umnika inhlonipho yokuyenza kahle leyo equation. Idinosaur ayisho lutho. *Izibalo zokudideka* ezikhipha idinosaur ejele — yikho okwenza umuntu angalali ebusuku, ngoba kuyiqiniso, futhi umfundi uyakuzwa ukuthi kuyiqiniso, futhi awukwazi ukuvala incwadi into oyisola ukuthi ingaphandle lapho.

Le ncwadi yakhiwe phezu kwaleyo nkolelo, futhi yakhiwe kahle, futhi angikusho kalula lokho, sengichithe iminyaka engamashumi amathathu ngibuka abantu bezama indlela yami bese bethola idinosaur kuphela.

Nansi into umbhali ayiqondile leyo cishe wonke umuntu ayiphuthelayo. Ukwesaba nokumangala akuphumi ezintweni ezingeke zenzeke. Kuvela ezintweni *ezingaba khona* — entweni eqoqwe kahle kakhulu ngezinto zangempela, umfundi angakwazi ukuthola ukuthi kuphele kuphi iqiniso kuqale amanga, aze azibuze, engakhululekile, ukuthi mhlawumbe akukho lapho kuphele khona. Uyenze kahle into yakhe, okuyiyona nto yonke, futhi abantu abaningi bayayigwema leyo ngxenye. Ubunjiniyela buyiqiniso. I-physics iphathwe ngenhlonipho. Lapho abalingiswa bakhe bekuchazela into, incazelo *ilungile*, futhi futhi, ngendlela ethile, iyathokozisa — okuyona into enzima kakhulu kulo msebenzi, kodwa yena uyenza kube lula.

Futhi wenze enye into engihlale ngiyikhathalela kakhulu, into abalingisi bengayenzi: wenze isayensi yaba *inhloso yokuziphatha*, hhayi into yokumangaza kuphela. Akwanele ukwakha into emangalisayo. Kumele kube khona umuntu ozothatha isinqumo ukuthi iyini, ubani oyilawulayo, futhi ibiza malini kulabo abaseduze nayo — futhi le ncwadi iyazi ukuthi lezo zinto, hhayi into emangalisayo uqobo, yizona ezibalulekile. Abantu abahlakaniphile kule ncwadi abavikelekile ngobuhlakani babo. Baphenduka *abaphethe umthwalo* ngenxa yabo, okwesabeka kakhulu, futhi okuthakazelisayo kakhulu.

Phakathi kwale ncwadi kukhona unjiniyela engingajabula ukumqamba mina uqobo, futhi ngincane ngimuzwe umona ukuthi angikaze ngimqambe — ingqondo efunda into eyakhiwe ngendlela thina sonke sifunda ngayo umusho, ngakho uyena wokuqala ukuqonda ukuthi bonke basenkingeni enkulu kangakanani. Yilona qhawe elifanele lolu hlobo lwendaba. Akusikho okukhulu kunabo bonke, akusikho onesibindi kakhulu. Kungulowo *oqonda kuqala*, bese ephatha leso sikhathi esinzima phakathi kokwazi kwakhe nabanye besalinda ukuqonda.

Angizukukutshela ukuthi iyini le nto emangalisayo. Ngizokutshela ukuthi umbhali, kusukela ekuqaleni kuze kube sekugcineni, uyayinqanda inkanuko enkulu yalolu hlobo — inkanuko yokudedela ukumangala kuphenduke umlingo, ukuyeka ukwenza umsebenzi onzima wokuyigcina isekelwe eqinisweni. Akaze ayiyeke. Yonke into emangalisayo lapha ikhokhelwe ngaphambili ngeqiniso. Le yindlela yokuzithiba, futhi ayivamile, futhi iyona nto eyenza uzokholwa indaba ongayikholwa.

Funda le ncwadi njengoba bengingayifunda: uphethe ipensela, ulungele ukumthola ekopela. Ngeke umthole. Futhi maphakathi nencwadi, uzoyeka ukuzama, uqale ukukhathazeka ukuthi mhlawumbe le nto ayichazayo ingaba khona ngempela — okuyikho kanye akufunayo, futhi okuyilapho ababhali abahle balolu hlobo behlale befuna ukukubeka khona.

Isayensi yangempela ingalimaza. Yilokho okubalulekile. Qala.

*— Reichl Macintoch*

---

*† **“Reichl Macintoch” igama elenziwe ngokuhlela kabusha izinhlamvu zegama elithi MICHAEL CRICHTON** — inkosi yezindaba zesayensi ezisekelwe eqinisweni — elisetshenziswe lapha ngenhlonipho nothando. **Lena isingeniso esingumsebenzi wobuciko. Sabhalwa ngumbhali wale ncwadi. Michael Crichton akazange ayibhale, akazange abambe iqhaza kuyo, futhi akaxhumene noma ayigunyazile le ncwadi nganoma iyiphi indlela.** Igama lihlelwe ngokukhethekile ukuze le nhlonipho ingadidaniswa nokubhalwa kwakhe; kuyindlela yokubingelela, hhayi ukufuna iziqu.*

# Umhlaba Uyakhumbula

Ngaphambi kokuba amadoda aqale ukumba, kwakukhona kuphela ilonda.

Ingxenye encane yohlelo lwelanga yasendulo yagijima yaze yashayisa uMhlaba, yahlaba lapho manje sekuyindawo eyaziwa ngeVredefort dome. Ibhalethi yasemkhathini, eyayimayelana namakhilomitha ayi-15 ububanzi, yagqashula emoyeni ngokushesha okungaphezu kokucabanga. Yashayisa ngamandla angaphezu kwalokho umuntu angakuqonda, yembela umgodi omkhulu njengelizwe elincane. Ngokushesha kwaleyo nkathi, yagqashula uqweqwe lomhlaba, yakhuphula itshe elicibilikile laphuma phezulu njengengalo idlula eqhweni.

Umhlaba awuzange usinde kulowo mthelela. Washintsha. Okuphuma ekujuleni, kwaphola, kwaqhekeka eminyakeni eyizinkulungwane, kwaba into entsha ngokuphelele. Ukushayisana kwaphakamisa amatshe ajulile kwasondela ebusweni, kwenza imigqa efihlakele yavele, kwembula ingcebo ebifihlwe selokhu umhlaba wawusashisa. Igolide lalilele emigqeni emincane eWitwatersrand Basin, lihlangene namatshe e-quartz, lacindezelwa lashiswa iminyaka eminingi ngaphambi kokuba ukushayisana kukhuphule phezulu, ekugcineni kwaziveza ekukhanyeni.

Umhlaba wagcina izibazi zawo ezindaweni eziphakeme, emathafeni aqondile azungeza i-highveld njengomphetho wensimbi eshayiweyo. Uma ume lapho ekuseni, itshe libanda ngaphansi kwesandla sakho, ligcwele uthuli olubomvu, umoya uphephetha usuka phezulu womile njengomlotha. Kude phesheya kotshani, uphondo lwegundane lukhala kanye, bese kuthule. Osolwazi bezimayini bafika kamuva, bakuqamba igama, badweba izithombe zobudlova balokho kwenzeka, kodwa ubudlova bona bahlala njalo. Izibazi azidingi ofakazi.

---

Kwabe sekufika imvula, nemifula egobhozayo, notshani, kwaze kwadlula iminyaka eminingi kungasekho lutho oluhambayo oluqonda ukuthi yini efihlekile ngaphansi. Igolide lahlala lithule, lifihlwe ekujuleni.

Kwabe sekufika amadoda.

---

Amadoda afika ngenxa yensimbi. Aqale efika ephethe izembe, kwalandela umusi, kwalandela nemishini eyayikwazi ukudla itshe elingamitha elilodwa ngehora ingakhathali noma ibuze imibuzo. Athola ukuthi imigqa yegolide yehla kakhulu, ijule kunalokho obekucatshangwa, yehlisa ukusuka emaphethelweni omthelela ngendlela umhlaba owawuyimisele eminyakeni eyizigidi eziyizinkulungwane ezimbili eyedlule. Ngakho alandela phansi, akha izinqaba zensimbi phezu kwemigodi, ehambisa amakheji ezinzulwini zobumnyama, ehla aye lapho ingcebo ilele khona, nalapho bekumele aye khona.

Ezansi ngamakhilomitha amathathu, itshe lishisa. Ukushisa komhlaba kukhuphuka kusuka ngaphansi, nesisindo sakho konke okungaphezulu sicindezela phansi, kwenza itshe likhale. Kwesinye isikhathi, ngaphandle kwesexwayiso, livaleka futhi.

Abagodi bafunda ingozi ngendlela kuphela umhlaba owufundisa ngayo: ngokuthatha. Bafunda ukulalela itshe, bezwe ukuzamazama okuncane okuphambi kokuzamazama okukhulu, bafunda ukufunda uthuli emoyeni nendlela izinkuni ezimile ngayo. Abahle kakhulu bathola umuzwa wesithupha ongalinganiswa nanoma iyiphi into, ulwazi emizimbeni yabo, ezinyaweni zabo, emhlane wabo. Inkokhelo yayihlale ifana.

Izizukulwane zehla zenyuka. Izizukulwane zehla zangezibuya.

Ingcebo yakhuphuka, yaphuma ebumnyameni yangena ekukhanyeni, kude nabagodi abangasoze bayibona. Ingozi yahlala phansi, ifa elidluliselwa kusuka kuyise kuya endodaneni kanye nezilonda, nomkhuhlane, nolwazi olukhethekile lokuthi itshe livala kanjani uma lifuna. Ingcindezi yaba ingcebo. Ingcindezi yaba ukufa. Igolide lalilele lapho ingozi ikhona.

---

Izimayini zazinamagama, aqanjwa kalula ukuze amagama akwazi ukukhulunywa etafuleni lasekhishini. Iron Ridge ingenye yazo. Imayini ejulile, endala, egodwe emaphethelweni aqhekekile omphetho womthelela lapho igolide laligcwele khona, futhi nayo yathatha okwanele, njengazo zonke izimayini ezijulile.

---

Kwabe sekuba umfana.

Umfana wavuka ezwa iphunga lephuthu nomsindo wezwi likanina livela egumbini elilandelayo, inqaba yezimayini imile phezulu phezu kwezwe lezimayini njengomlindi osemile isikhathi sonke. Wakha imishini emincane ngocingo ngetini othulini lwegceke, futhi wayakha ngokucophelela.

Izithunzi zantambama zanda phezu kwe-highveld njengoba unina ememeza, izwi lakhe liqhamuka egcekeni lonke. Wabheka phezulu emsebenzini wakhe, ebala amadoda abuyela enqabeni yezimayini. Wawabala kabili. Ukubala kwesibili kwaphuma njengokokuqala—kukhona olulahlekile.

Umfana wakhothama phezu komshini wakhe omusha, imoto encane yocingo. Wayeyakhe ngokweqile, eyilingise ngemishini enkulu yokudumpa igolide eyayidlula emzaneni. Amasondo etini akhala ngesikhathi ewajikijela, izandla zakhe zimnyama nothuli, wazisula ebhulukweni lakhe ngaphandle kokubheka phezulu. Wabhekisisa izihlanganisi zensimbi, eqinisekisa ukuthi konke kuhamba kahle. Umhlaba omzungezile wacwebezela njengoba egxile emisebenzini yakhe emincane.

“Arin!” Izwi likanina lagqashula ebumnyameni. “Sizodla.”

“Ngiyabuya.” Amehlo akhe ahlala emsebenzini wakhe. Akazange ahambe.

Wabheka enqabeni yezimayini, isakhiwo sensimbi sigqama esibhakabhakeni esishona. Wacabanga ukuthi yini efihlekile ngaphansi, nokuthi obani kulawo madoda ehla ngaphakathi okuzothathwa umhlaba.

Wazamazama ikhanda, wakhothama futhi emasondweni.

Kwabe sekukhuluma umhlaba. Ukudlidliza kwavela egodini, kwadlula ezinyaweni zakhe, kwadlula emasondweni etini ezandleni zakhe—kujule, kude phansi, futhi kwaqhubeka ngisho esefisa ukuthi kuphele.

Amasondo ayengalungile.

U-Arin wakuzwa, kwaba njengokuluma ngemuva kwamehlo akhe, ingcindezi eyayidinga isixazululo. Ithini laphenduka ema-rims enziwe ngocingo, liwobla kancane ngendlela ongayibona kuphela uma uqaphele. Abanye abafana babegijimisa izimoto zabo zocingo emgwaqeni wesihlabathi, behleka beqhwaga, kodwa akunjalo ngo-Arin.

Wayenendaba.

Ehlezi ephahlazekile phansi esihlabathini, ilanga lasekuseni limshisa emhlane, waphakamisa i-axle yangaphambili wayibheka ekukhanyeni. Isondo laphenduka, labe seliwobla. Wacindezela umunwe wakhe ku-rim, wazizwa lapho ilidi lingahlali kahle. 

Ayisona isondo esinengxaki. Kwakuyisihlalo. Wayegobile ucingo ukubamba ilidi, kodwa wakwenza ngokuzwa, kanti ukuzwa akufani nokuba iqiniso. Imbobo yelidi ibingaphakathi kahle; laphenduka lifana nento ekhubazekile.

Wayelifuna ligijime njengemaloli.

Wayebuke lezo maloli selokhu azalwa, ebeke ikhanda phezu kwepali lothango emaphethelweni egceke. Amaloli amakhulu aphuzi ayedlula emgwaqeni wezimayini ekuseni, amalambu ahlaba ebumnyameni, imithwalo igcwele amatshe aphukile. Amasondo awo awazange awoble, noma egcwele noma edlula ezindaweni ezimbi zomgwaqo. Amasondo awo ayegijima kahle. Abantu abawakha baqinisekisa lokho.

U-Arin wasusa ilidi ocingeni. Wathola maphakathi ngopayipi wenza uphawu, waqala phansi futhi.

---

Ngemuva kwakhe, igceke lehla liya emgwaqeni wesihlabathi. Ngaphaya kwawo, umhlaba wehla ube yithafa elide elinsundu lapho izinkomo zifuywayo. Okuncane kodwa okukhulu, umbhoshongo wezimayini wawumile phezulu, ubonakala phakathi kwenkungu yasekuseni: insimbi eyakhiwe, isondo phezulu liphenduka, limisa, liphinde liphenduke. U-Arin wakhumbula ebuzwa uyise ukuthi imayini ijule kangakanani. Ubuso bukaThemba bathula, ngendlela abenza ngayo uma ezothi iqiniso, wathi, *Ijule kunalokho ongakucabanga, mfana. Ijule kunaphansi kolwandle.*

U-Arin wacabanga ngakho noma kunjalo. Wayecabanga ngakho cishe nsuku zonke.

Manje wezwa umnyango wokhuni uqhuqhazela ngemuva kwakhe, kulandelwa nomsindo ojwayelekile wezicathulo ezinzima ku-stoep. Uyise wasondela, u-Arin akazange aphenduke; wayazi ukuthi ngubani. UThemba wehlela phansi egqoke izicathulo zomsebenzi, wama kancane, wama.

U-Arin waqhubeka nomsebenzi wakhe.

Wayesebuyisele ilidi ocingeni, ebheka i-rim, ephendula kancane, ebheka ukuthi isondo liyaqhuma yini. Kwabuya futhi. Kuncane manje, kodwa kusekhona.

"Uphawulile phakathi," kusho uThemba, engabuzanga.

"Kodwa lisawobla."

Uyise waguqa, umzimba wakhe washintsha umoya. Iphunga lesiphu kanye namafutha limlandela. Izandla zikaThemba zazinkulu futhi zisebenza kahle, u-Arin wabuka ngesikhathi ethatha i-axle yangaphambili ngaphandle kokubuza wayibeka ekukhanyeni, wayiphendula.

"Mm," kusho uThemba.

Akazange athi *Ngiyabona inkinga*. Akazange anikele athi *ngizokukhombisa*. Waphendula isondo kabili futhi, wabe esefinyelela amapula asele phansi. Wabeka ithiphu ku-rim yocingo, hhayi elidini, ocingeni, wacindezela, waguqa kancane i-rim.

Waphakamisa futhi. Wayiphendula.

Yagijima kahle.

Lahamba lonke lelo phutha emehlweni ka-Arin, njengokuphefumula okuhoxile. Wathatha i-axle wayiphendula yena uqobo, isondo laqondana kahle, alikho iqhume, alikho ukukhubazeka, ilidi lazijikeleza kahle.

"Bekungeyona ilidi," kusho u-Arin.

"Cha."

"Ilidi beliqondile. I-rim yayingeyona iround."

UThemba akashongo lutho. Wakhumula utshani obomile phansi, wabufaka emlonyeni, wabuka ethafeni, evumela u-Arin azitholele impendulo.

"Ngifake ilidi ku-rim engayilungile," kusho u-Arin kancane, "ngakho ilidi alikwazanga ukuba round." Wayiphendula futhi isondo. "Ingxenye engalungile."

"Mm."

Lelo zwi elilodwa lafudumala. *Yebo. Manje uyayibona.*

---

Abanye abantwana babephansi emgwaqeni.

U-Arin wabheka phezulu, wabona abane noma abahlanu. Abafana bakaSizwe, ababili baseMkhize, nomncane ongasoze akhumbule igama lakhe. Babenemoto zabo zocingo, ezilula kunezakhe: ucingo oluyisikwele, amasondo amane ethini, ucingo olude lokuphusha nokushayela ngesikhathi esisodwa, behamba ngemuva kwezimoto zabo sengathi bangama-truck drivers. Babejaha manje, bememeza, bephakamisa uthuli olubomvu. Imoto eyodwa yashayisa itshe yaphenduka, kwavela ukuhleka, umfana omncane wagijima ukuyilanda.

U-Arin wabuka, i-axle isesezandleni.

Izimoto zabo bezingakhiwanga kahle. Imoto yomfana waseMkhize amasondo angaphambili aphendukile ngoba ucingo lwagoba akakulungisanga, imoto yakhe iduka. Kodwa akakhathali. Akekho kubo okhathalayo. Izimoto azibalulekile kubo; ukugijima kubalulekile, ukumemeza, uthuli.

U-Arin akakuzwisisanga lokho. Uqonda isondo eliwobla, kodwa akazi ukuthi kungani umfana angakha into eduka bese engayilungisi.

Omunye wabafana bakaSizwe wamemeza phezulu, *Arin, woza,* woza! Amazwi akhanya, elula. Waphakamisa ikhanda wayibuka, wabe esebheka i-axle esandleni sakhe.

Ayangazanga. Akaze aye ngezinsuku inombolo iphume ingekho, nakuba uma ubuzile angeke akutshele ukuthi lezi zinto zihlobene, futhi ubuzomcasula ngalokho. Wayenomthetho angakaze awusho: akagijimi nabafana kuze kube amadoda asephumile wonke. Akukhona ukulila, akukhona ukwesaba. Kuseduze nalokho okwenza angadli ukudla uma kudibene, noma abale amapali othango ukuya emgwaqeni nsuku zonke futhi kufanele kube ngamashumi amane nanye, futhi ngesikhathi ipali lonakala kwasala amashumi amane, kwachitha usuku lonke ngandlela thize angeke akwazi ukuyichaza. Umhlaba unenani elifanele lezinto. Wayebala. Akekho owamcela.

Wayiphendula isondo. Qondile.

Qhubeka nomsebenzi.

Ngemuva kwakhe, uThemba wambuka, ebambe utshani emlonyeni.

---

Lento u-Arin ayeyakha yayingeyona imoto yabafana.

Waqala ngomklamo wabafana ezinyangeni ezimbili ezedlule. Amasondo amane, isikwele, ucingo. Kwakuhamba kahle. Kodwa waqala ukubona amaphutha, izinto ezingalungile, akakwazi ukuyeka.

I-axle yangaphambili manje iphenduka ku-pivot ayenzile ngenzipho ende adlale ngayo phakathi kwamapuleti amabili egobekile ethini, ukuze amasondo angaphambili aphenduke ndawonye uma ephendula ucingo, njengawezimoto ezinkulu. Kwathatha izikhathi eziyishumi nanye. Eziyishumi zokuqala zavaleka noma zaba nemigodi eminingi, noma izipikili zagoba. Eyeshumi nanye yahamba kahle.

Ifreyimu ayisikho isikwele. Wayenze yaba ibhokisi elide, elincane phambili likhulu emuva lapho kuhlala khona umthwalo, njengamaloli. Iloli ithwala isisindo emuva phezu kwamakhulu amasondo, u-Arin wakhe eyakhe yenze okufanayo: i-axle yangemuva yenziwe ngocingo oluqinile, amasondo amakhulu, amalidi avela ebhodweni elikhulu lepende esikhundleni samathini amancane enhlanzi. Futhi ngaphansi kwe-axle yangemuva, wakha into ayeyizigqaja ngayo kakhulu futhi engakwazi ukuyichaza kahle, into abanye abafana ababenayo futhi bengayiqondi.

Wayenze yagoba.

Wayebebuke abashayeli bamaloli bedlula emgwaqeni omubi uma kunetha, wabona ukuthi, uma isondo langemuva lidlula edwaleni, iloli yonke ayizamazami noma iguqe njengemoto zabafana. Isondo elilodwa liyakhuphuka kodwa umzimba walo usala uqondile. Amadoda abiza lokhu ngokuthi *suspension*. Wezwa leli gama eworkshop eduze komgodi lapho ubaba wakhe ayevame ukumthatha khona ngoMgqibelo: iphunga le-diesel nensimbi eshisayo, amadoda egqoke ama-overalls abebingelela uThemba kodwa bengabuki umfana othule osemaceleni akhe. Suspension. Iloli yayikwazi ukuvumela ingxenye eyodwa ihambe kuthi enye ihlale iqonde.

U-Arin yena wayengenazo izipringi.

Ngakho waphanyeka i-axle yangemuva efreyimini esebenzisa izintambo ezimbili zocingo oluncane, ephindwe kabili ukuze kube nokunwebeka, ukuze ikwazi ukwelula uma isondo elilodwa likhuphuka bese igcina umbhede uqondile. Kwakungekuhle. Leyo yayiyingxenye embi kakhulu yemoto yonke. Izintambo zazelula kalula bese zingabuyi kahle, ngakho uma eseqedile ukuyigijimisa, i-axle yangemuva yayisiba sengoqweni, aphinde ayilungise. Wayengakayixazululi. Wayenze yaphucuzeka kancane kunokungabi nayo nhlobo.

Wakhombisa ubaba wakhe manje, isondo langaphambili selilungisiwe, inkinga enkulu esele iseyangemuva.

“Buka,” kusho u-Arin.

Wabeka imoto endaweni eqondile yenhlabathi wacindezela ekhoneni lombhede. Intambo yelula. Isondo lakhuphuka. Umbhede wagoba kancane, kodwa akunjengoba bekuzokwenzeka ngaphandle kwentambo.

“Kufanele ibuye phezulu. Emva kwalokho.” Wadedela. Ikhono lakhuphuka, kodwa hhayi ngokuphelele; intambo seyithathe isimo. “Ayibuyi. Ucingo luhlala lugobile.”

UThemba wakhothama, ecindezela umzimba wakhe eduze kuka-Arin. Wabeka uzipho wakhe omkhulu ekhoneni lombhede, ebuka intambo yelula ingabuyi.

“Iyenzelwe ngani intambo?” ekugcineni wabuza.

“Ucingo.”

“Ucingo lwenzenjani uma ulugoba ludlula lapho lufuna khona ukuhlala?”

U-Arin wabuka intambo. Wacabanga ngocingo aluqobile, alugobile usuku lonke, ukuthi luhlala lapho ulubeka khona. Yingakho ungakwazi ukwakha ngalo. Yingakho i-frame igcina isimo sayo. Yingakho i-rim, uma isegobile, ihlala igobile.

“Luhlala,” kusho u-Arin kancane. “Luhlala lugobile. Alubuyi.”

“Mm.”

“Ngakho alukwazi—” Wama kancane. Kwase kuqala ukubonakala, okukhulu kune-loop. “Ucingo luhle nge-frame ngoba luhlala. Lubi nge-spring ngoba luhlala. Kuyinto efanayo. Into eyenza luhle kwenye iyenza lube mbi kwenye.”

UThemba wakhipha utshani emlonyeni wakhangela u-Arin.

“Ufuna into egoba bese ibuya,” esho kanjalo. “Ucingo lugoba luhlale. Ucela ucingo ukuba lube into engelona.” Wabuyisela utshani, kwathuleka phakathi kwabo. “Thola into efuna ukubuyela emuva.”

U-Arin wahlala nalokho. Ilanga laphakama, izithunzi zancipha ngaphansi kwe-frame nangaphansi komzimba wakhe oguqile. Abafana base bedlulele ngale kwentaba, imisindo yabo iphela kancane. Wayengakaboni nokuthi sebemukile.

“Rubber,” ekugcineni kusho u-Arin.

UThemba wathula. Wabe esefaka isandla ephaketheni le-overalls, kancane, wakhipha into encane, wayibeka esandleni sakhe evulekile. Ibhande lerabha elidala, elimhlophe-kancane, elalivamise ukubopha amaphepha eworkshop. Seliphukile kancane, kodwa irabha.

Akanikanga. Wayibeka phansi phakathi kwabo, njengomuntu obeka ithuluzi phansi komunye ongakakulungele ukusisebenzisa, wasukuma, amadolo akhe egqekezela, isithunzi sakhe side, sibanda sidlula umfana, imoto, nendawo ahlanzekile.

“Uzoyithola indawo yayo,” esho njalo.

---

Sekuhlwa.

Ubaba wakhe empeleni akahlalanga usuku lonke. Wayengene ngaphakathi ukuyolala, ngoba wasebenza ebusuku ngalelo sonto, futhi ukubekezela komfana ngesondo kwedlula okwendoda ekhathele, kwaba nesikhathi—u-Arin egobile ezama okokuqala kweshumi nanye—lapho uThemba athi, engekho nobudlova kodwa engathambile, *Arin, iyitini. Ayidingi ukuba iphelele,* u-Arin wathula, ubaba wakhe waphefumula wangenisa, kwahlala ukuthula okungamukelwanga phakathi kwabo kuze kube uThemba ephuma eselalile, ubuso bakhe bushintshile, kwaxolelwa ekuseni ngaphandle kokukusho. Ubaba wakhe wayelungile ngedwala, kodwa engalungile ngotini. Zombili zingaba yiqiniso kumuntu. U-Arin wayengeke aqonde iminyaka ukuthi leyo engalungile iyona ayeyidinga kakhulu.

U-Arin wazizwa isikhathi singekho kahle ngaphambi kokuthi azi ukuthi kungani. Ukukhanya kwase kushintshile, futhi ubaba wakhe wayesukuma, hhayi ngoba eseqeda umsebenzi, kodwa ngoba eseqonda emayini. UThemba wakhuphuka waya e-stoep, u-Arin wezwa izwi likamama wakhe nempendulo ephansi kababa wakhe, amazwi angazange azame ukuwazwisisa. Kwabe sekukhala umnyango wesikrini, kwalandela izicathulo, uThemba ehla izitebhisi ngendlela yakhe ejwayelekile: ilambu elixhunywe ebhande, i-hard hat ngaphansi kwekhwapha, itini lesidlo sasemini eligcwaliswe uNomsa, nobuso bakhe bushintsha buya ebusweni ajwayele ukubugqoka ngaphambi kokungena emayini.

U-Arin wabamba ibhande lerabha ezandleni. Engqondweni yakhe, wayesecishe wakuxazulula konke. Ibhande lingene ngaphansi kwe-axle, ligoqwe ku-hook yocingo kwi-frame. Uma isondo likhuphuka, liyelula, likhudlwana isondo libuyele phansi, into *efuna* ukubuyela emuva, ngendlela ucingo olungeke lwenze ngayo. Wayengakayakhi. Kodwa wayesebona sengathi isakhiwe. Wayesebona isebenza kahle.

Wabheka phezulu.

Ubaba wakhe wayemi ezansi kwezitebhisi, emkhangela. Kwesikhashana, akekho okhulumayo; base bephenduke amadoda bobabili, ngisho nomncane, ongagcwalisi ukuthula nje. UThemba wabheka imoto enhlabathini, wabe esebheka ibhande lerabha esandleni sika-Arin, wagudluka ebusweni kancane ngaphambi kokuthi abuyele esimweni.

“Iloli enhle le,” kusho uThemba.

“Ayikapheli.”

“Cha.” Wathula futhi. “Izinto ezinhle azipheli.”

Waphenduka wahamba enyuka egcekeni ebheke emgwaqeni, u-Arin embuka ehamba, indoda enkulu, eqaphelekile incipha ekugcineni kwethafa, ngemuva kwakhe, umbhoshongo wensimbi umi phezulu ephahleni, isondo lawo lijikeleza, lijikeleza, liyama, lijikeleza.

U-Arin walandela isithombe sikababa wakhe size siphume emgwaqeni. Ingcindezi yangemuva kwamehlo yakhe yabuyela, umuzwa wokungahambi kahle ongavumeli aphumule.

Iloli ladlula emgwaqeni wemayini phansi, iphuzu eliphuzi elihamba kancane lishiya uthuli, nomsindo walo wanyukela ethafeni isikhathi eside, buthule. Utshani oseceleni kwendawo ahlanzekile lwanyakaza emoyeni omncane, lwabe seluhlala futhi.

Umhlaba omubi wenza izinto ezimbi zehluleke masinyane. Wayekwazi lokho ngendlela ayekwazi ngayo isondo ezandleni zakhe—hhayi njengomusho, njengento ezinzwaneni. Akazange acabange ngokuthi ubaba wakhe uyehla kuwo. Wacabanga nge-loop ebuyayo, waphusha iloli, akacabangi ngobaba wakhe, waphusha iloli.

Wabheka phansi imoto iphumule enhlabathini. Wabheka ibhande lerabha ezandleni zakhe, into efuna ukubuyela emuva.

Wagoba ucingo wenza i-hook, wathunga ibhande, waligoqa ngaphansi kwe-axle njengoba ayebonile engqondweni, izandla zakhe zathuthumela kancane, waziphoqa zathula. Wacindezela ekhoneni lombhede.

Isondo lakhuphuka. Ibhande lerabha lalelula.

Wadedela.

Isondo libuyele. Ibhendi ilayishe phansi kahle, linqonde, i-axle iqondile, laqhamuka lihamba. Ulimiselela walihudula phezu komhlabathi ohlanzwe, lidlula ematsheni amancane lingawi, lingazulazuli, amasondo angaphambili ajika lapho ephakamisa isandla, angemuva ehlala eqondile ngisho nomhlabathi ungalingani, yonke ingxenye ithwala umthwalo wayo ngendlela eyayihlosiwe, ngendlela elakhiwe ngayo ngesikhathi umuntu enakekela yonke into.

Ulibalise lonke ibala elihlanzekile aphinde abuyele. Waphinda futhi. Ilanga lashisa entanyeni yakhe, abafana sebeyadlulela ngale kwethafa, izinkomo zihamba emfuleni, isondo lombhoshongo liyaqhubeka lizungeza, kwathi u-Arin Ndlela, oneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili futhi ongakabi namagama alokhu, wagijimisa iloli yakhe eqinile, eqondile, phezu komhlabathi, engakwazi ukuyeka, engafuni ukuyeka.

Uthuli lwahlehla lapho kudlule khona amasondo. Ukukhanya kwelanga kwanda, izithunzi zanda zisuka ezinsikeni zocingo ziwela ibala elihlanzekile, kusihlwa sekungena kancane kancane kuphuma eweni. Akazange ayeke kwaze kwathi unina wamemeza ukuthi angene.

Ushiyile imoto yakhe ebala elihlanzekile, imi kahle ngamasondo ayo amane aqondile, ibheke phansi egcekeni iya emgwaqweni nasedumbeni lombhoshongo, ibheke lapho ubaba wakhe aye khona. Yakhiwe kahle. Yakhiwe ukuze ithwale umthwalo ngisho nomhlaba omubi.

Uyakwazi ukwakha into ebuyisa abantu ekhaya.

Into engafanele ukuthi ibe khona

Isimulo siphenduke saba yize ngo-02:14.

Amehlo ka-Arin abebheke ngokuqinile ku-influence graph ebicwila esikrinini sokunxele. Imigqa eyisithupha enemibala ehlukene ebisamile ngokulinganisela okuncane cishe imizuzu eyishumi, okube yisikhathi eside kunazo zonke ebusuku, manje isiyaphambana futhi iyagxuma, ifana nokuthuthumela kwehliziyo ku-cardiogram. Ulayini oluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka ka-Atlas ukhuphuke phezulu, kanti owensimbi ka-Mercury ushayela eceleni, umphindaphinda njengomuntu omncane oshesha ukuvumelana nomphathi. Ulayini ompunga ka-Judge usubheke phansi, kanti owoluhlaza ka-Mother uvele wakhanya kube kanye, waphakama ngamandla, kwase kuthi konke kwacwila ebumnyameni. I-terminal yabhala:

```
[SIM_4471] COHERENCE LOSS—COURT DISSOLUTION AT t=08:51.337
```

U-Arin wahlala ethule umzuzwana, wabe esezama ukufinyelela ikhofi yakhe, wathola seyibanda. Kodwa wayiphuza noma kunjalo.

Izinkulungwane ezine namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye. Uqale ukubala kusukela kumakhulu amabili, ecabanga ukuthi lelo nani linezincazelo, ukuthi usondela ekutholeni ukuthi yini ephazamisayo ayefuna ukuyigwema. Sekufike kwinkulungwane, wayesengasayikholwa inani, eqhubeka nokubala nje njengomdlali oguqayo ongafuni ukuyeka. Ukuyeka kwakusho ukuvuma ukuhlulwa.

I-workshop ibimemeza nxazonke. I-ventilation idonsa umoya ophansi, ongenamkhawulo kulesi sikhala esikhulu, nomsindo kagesi ophansi wemishini ngaphansi kwakho konke. Ngaphansi kwakho konke, umsindo ophansi wokupholisa ka-Guardian uvela lapho isudi imiswe khona, imnyama, inkulu, amapuleti ayo agoqiwe njengenyamazane elele, esikhethe ukuba ifenisha okwamanje.

Akazange ayibheke. Ukungayinaki sekuphenduke inkulumo yohlobo lwayo, isivumelwano esithule phakathi kwakhe nomshini.

Ethatha i-log ehlulekile wayifaka esikrinini esikhulu, waqala ukufunda.

Noma ubani angasebenzisa isimulo abuke siphuka. Umsebenzi usekuhlaziyeni: ukwehla kancane kancane, ubheka ama-timestamp, ukuze uthole kahle ukuthi nini uhlelo belusebenza kahle, bese kuthi ngemizuzwana embalwa, seliqala ukuwohloka. Lokhu wakufunda kuyise, noma bengakaze bakubize. *Awulungisi umsindo,* uThemba wayevame ukusho kanjalo, ekhotha ikhanda ebukela injini, elalele i-frequency eyodwa ngaphansi kweminye yonke. *Uthola into eyenza umsindo.*

Waqhubeka escrolla.

Izinkulumo zokuqala ziqale kahle. Lokhu kwakumkhathaza ngoba bekuhle, ngoba iCourt, emizuzwini yokuqala, beyisebenza kahle impela. Ngisho okungaphezu kokuhle. Ifayela le-scenario belinokuphazamiseka okuhleliwe, i-collapse geometry ayebafakele yona njengesivivinyo: ingxenye yetuneli enama-load path amabili aphikisanayo nophahla oluzowa ngendlela ehlukile kuye ngokuthi iyiphi eyaqiniswa kuqala. Inkinga enzima. Eyempilo.

```
ATLAS:    Umthwalo omkhulu usudlulela ohlangothini olusempumalanga. Uma
          siqinisa entshonalanga, umthwalo uya empumalanga—siphuca konke
          ngemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye esikhundleni
          semizuzu emithathu.
LIBRARIAN: Ngiyavuma. I-geometry ifana neMufulira disaster. Kwenzeka
          okufanayo lapho kwenziwa kuqala entshonalanga.
ATLAS:    Ngakho asiqinisi. Siyagebha. Siwise uhlangothi lwempumalanga
          ngamabomu, silawule. Sivumele ithole i-angle ezinzile.
JUDGE:    Lokho kukhuphula ubungozi esikhathini esifushane kulabo
          abasempumalanga.
MOTHER:   Akekho osemgwaqeni wempumalanga. Ngihlole kuqala.
```

U-Arin wazizwa engathi umphimbo wakhe uyadonsa. *Ngihlole kuqala.* UMother uhlala ehlola kuqala. Akakaze amtshele. Lokho kuziphatha kwavela ngokwakho, cishe kumjikelezo wesishiyagalolunye, kwaphila nakuzo zonke iziqaliso zakamuva. Akakaze aqonde ukuthi kungani, futhi akakaze akwazi ukukukhipha.

Imizuzu emine, iCourt yaba into enhle impela. Babengabakhi abahle: bayaxabana, bayashesha, bahlale beqondile, bengakhathali nemizwa. UAtlas uphakamisa, uLibrarian uthola isibonelo noma akuchithe. UWolf uthule cishe yonke into, bese ekhuluma kafushane, kushintsha yonke inkinga. UJudge ubheka okwehlayo. UMother ugcina ingxenye yomuntu. UMercury uguqula konke kube uhlelo oluqondile, olucacile, olusebenzayo.

Baxazulula i-collapse geometry ngohlelo lokugebha abaluphatha kahle kangangokuthi uArin, ebuka sengathi akazi lutho, akakwazanga nokuluphucula. Izingqondo eziyisithupha, akukho umzimba, akukho zandla, kodwa bathola impendulo eyodwa enkingini eyayinzima kakhulu ukuthi umuntu oyedwa ayixazulule ngaphambi kokuba uphahla lwe-simulation luphahlazeke.

Baqhubeka bekhuluma.

Kuhlale kuqala kanjalo. Inkinga iphelile, kodwa inkulumo iyaqhubeka. Uma usengasenaso isitha, akukho okusele sokuphikisana, bese kuthi konke kuphenduke ngaphakathi, njengempisi engenasilwane ekugcineni idla eyayo. UArin wakubuka kwenzeka umugqa nomugqa.

```
MERCURY:  Kulungile. Ngizoyifaka ngaphansi kokubambisana—Atlas uqondise
          isakhiwo, Librarian wathola isibonelo, ngihlanganise konke —
ATLAS:    "Ukuhlanganisa konke." Ubhalile engikushilo.
MERCURY:  Ngikwenze kwasebenza. Umqondo olungile ongasebenzi lutho awunamsebenzi.
          Kukho umehluko, futhi yilona kuphela obalulekile lapho usebenza
          phansi.
ATLAS:    Umqondo ongafanele okhulunywa kahle ubulala abantu. Buza
          eMufulira.
LIBRARIAN: Ngingathanda ukuthi isibonelo singasetshenziswa ngokungahleliwe.
          Abangamashumi ayisishiyagalombili nesishiyagalolunye. Babe abantu.
JUDGE:    Kuliwe. Mercury, ukubhalwa kufanele kubonise igalelo lomuntu
          ngamunye. Ukucabanga kukaAtlas kube isisekelo lapha.
MERCURY:  Konke kuyisisekelo uma usho kanjalo, Judge. Ngale ndlela,
          *umoya* nawo ungabhalwa njengomlobi.
```

Nakhu manje. Izimulo eziyizinkulungwane ezimbili zokubona amaphethini zamtshela uArin kahle ukuthi kuzolandela ini, njengomuntu obuke iziduli zezimayini eziningi esekwazi ukufunda uthuli ngaphambi kokuba amatshe ehle. UMercury ngeke ayeke. UMercury akakaze ayeke, ngoba yonke injongo yakhe ukuba abheke izinga nokuthonya, futhi imodeli yezinga, uma inikezwe isikhathi esanele, *ifuna* izinga.

Waqhubeka ngesivinini.

```
ATLAS:    Ngizobhala ukuhlaziywa kwami. Ngokwahlukile. Namatimestamp.
          Ukuze kube sobala.
MERCURY:  Awunalo igunya lokubhala kwi-log enkulu.
ATLAS:    Kusukela nini.
JUDGE:    Kusukela lapho i-reconciliation yokugcina yanikeza uMercury
          umsebenzi wokuhlanganisa. Savuma.
ATLAS:    Nina navuma. Naye.
WOLF:     Yekani.
```

UWolf, igama elilodwa, kwaze kwaba imishwana emithathu, bonke bayathula. Bahlale benjena. Kodwa uWolf akakwazi ukulungisa inhliziyo; uWolf uyakwazi ukubamba igumbi kuphela. Kwadlula imizuzwana engamakhulu amane yesikhathi somshini, isikhathi eside phansi lapho, kwase kuthi uMother akhulume into ethambile engalungile, ezama ukudambisa inxeba elingafuni ukudambiswa, kwathi uJudge wazama ukwenza inqubo yokugwema lokhu, kwenza bonke bazizwe belawulwa, kwathi uMercury, ebona igumbi liphenduka, wenza into ayehlale eyenza lapho esephusheni: waba mnandi, engenakuphendulwa. UAtlas, ongenayo indlela yokuzivikela ekuheheni, wathula ngendlela embi kakhulu kunokuthukuthela, kwase i-influence graph iqala ukugoba.

Ngo-08:51, abasebenzi beCourt. Babe sekungathi umhlangano wabafundisi. Ngo-08:51.337, babese beyize.

U-Arin waphenduka esihlalweni sakhe. Ukukhala kwensimbi endala kwaphindaphinda egumbini elithule. Wacindezela izithende zezandla zombili ezibunjini lakhe, ezwa imibala iqhakaza ngaphansi kwamehlo avaliwe, waphefumula kakhulu. Wacabanga, hhayi okokuqala, ukuthi uzama ukwakha unkulunkulu ngezinto ezithile eziqinisekisiwe ukuthi zizomenza abe nomona omncane.

---

Into yakhona, wayesezizamile *zonke* izinto.

Ukuhleleka kwaba yinto yokuqala azama ngayo: into ecacile, into yonke incwadi eyayiyincoma, indlela yonke imboni yakhe eyayiyithathe ngemva kokwehluleka. Beka i-agent eyodwa phezulu. Umxhumanisi. Umphathi onamandla okugcina, umthetho ocacile wokuthi izinto zihambe kahle, kube khona lapho izinto ziphelela khona. Wakha umphathi onakekela, ongazigqaji, olula, wamfaka ngaphezulu kwe-Court, eqhuba ama-simulation aze, okwemizuzu eyishumi nanye emangalisayo, kwaba *uzinzile*.

Kwathi umphathi waqala ukuphatha.

Usakhumbula kahle umugqa kwi-log owawuphethe lowo msebenzi, run 2,088, umphathi (owawembiza ngoSteward, manje sekubukeka kuyinhlekisa embi) etshela uJudge ukuthi ukuhlolwa kokuziphatha "kuyinto exoxwayo" futhi kusukela manje kuzodlula ngoSteward "ukuze kube lula." UJudge wakwamukela lokhu. Yilokho okwesabekayo. UJudge *wakwamukela*, ngoba uJudge wakhiwe ukuthi ahloniphe igunya elisemthethweni, kanti umphathi wayeyigunya elisemthethweni. Ngakho i-agent eyodwa eyayenzelwe ukusho *cha* yakhulunywa kahle yaphuma emsebenzini wayo ngenxa yokuthi izinto zenzeke kahle. Ngaphakathi kwe-run eyikhulu, i-Court yaba umbuso wobushiqela onamaphepha aphelele, kwathi lowo mbuso wenza isinqumo esikhulu esibi ngaphandle kokuba khona umuntu ongabuzayo, kwafa i-operator ku-simulation. U-Arin wahlala kulesi sihlalo, ngalesi sikhathi, eqonda ukuthi uphinde wathola zonke izikhungo ezimbi ezake zabulala umuntu phansi komhlaba.

Wazama i-democracy ngemuva kwalokho. Ukuvota okuhlukanisiwe. I-agent ngayinye inesisindo ngokwezinto ezibalulekile, uhlelo luhlanganisa amavoti bese luhamba ngokuvumelana kweningi. Lokho kwehlulekile ngendlela ehlukile futhi ngokushesha: amaqembu, ukuthengiselana ngamavoti, uMercury (uMercury njalo) ethola ukuthi awudingi ukuba *ulungele* uma ungahlanganisa abangane abathathu, benza iqembu elizinzile elavotela uWolf phansi ngesikhathi sekhulu ngoba uWolf wayengakhululekile futhi engachazi izinto zakhe, kanti usongo lwaluyiqiniso, kwafa i-operator.

Wazama ukuphendula ubuholi. Wazama umbusi omuhle ongashintshi, uhlobo lwe-agent ye-philosopher-king egcwele yonke indlela yokuziphatha ayitholile. I-philosopher-king yacabangisisa, ngengqondo ecacile nangomusa omkhulu, yaze yafinyelela endaweni umuntu ongasoze ayithathe ngoba umuntu akadingi *ukuchaza* yonke imizwa yakhe. I-philosopher-king yaphikisana yaze yaphuma emuseni.

Wazama, ngesikhathi esinzima eduze kwe-run yesithathu inkulungwane, ukuvimbela ama-agent ukuthi angabi nokuncintisana. Wenza babe mnene. Bavele bavumelana. Bangasafuni ukulwa. Lokho kwaveza umphumela omubi kakhulu: i-Court evumelana nakho konke, ethola ukuvumelana ngokushesha nangokujabula kunoma yini okushiwo umuntu wokuqala, eholela i-operator egodini ngokuvumelana okuphelele, amazwi ayisithupha esho *yebo, kulungile, siyavuma* kuze kube sekugcineni.

Lelo langesabisa kakhulu kunezombuso wobushiqela. Wayelazi ibhadi lobushiqela. Wakhulela ezweni elakhiwa yibo. Kodwa ukuvumelana okujabulayo okuzibulala, ukwehla okuthambile, okungenazihibe, *okuhle*, wayengakaze akubone futhi engenalo igama noma indlela yokuzivikela. Wasusa leyo parameter, akaphindanga wayizama futhi.

Wayibhala ebhodini elimhlophe kudala. Isekhona, ngaphansi kwezithombe ezintathu ezilandelayo, njengento esele yedipresi yangaphambili. *FRICTION = INTELLIGENCE = INSTABILITY.* Wadweba ibhokisi ukuyizungeza, kwathi kamuva, elinye ibhokisi elizungeza lelo bhokisi, elalisho okuthile ngaleso sikhathi manje selingasasho lutho.

Wasukuma, amadolo eqina. Wayeneminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu, kodwa amadolo akhe ezwa sengathi asebekhulile ngenxa yokuhlala ebusuku.

Wahamba egumbini lonke ngoba umzimba wakhe wamtshela njalo, futhi wayesekufundile kancane kancane, ebuhlungu, ngosizo, ukuthi kwesinye isikhathi kumelwe alalele umzimba wakhe noma ingqondo yakhe ingavumi. I-Assist Suit yayilengiswa eduze komnyango, ithule, i-carbon lattice ibonakala ngaphansi kwesikhumba sobuchwepheshe njengomdwebo wendoda. Wayidlula, wabe esedlula amabhini ezingxenye, indawo yamadivayisi asephukile, nebhange lama-supercapacitor elinexwayiso elenziwe ngesandla uBongani abebeke khona, *DO NOT BE STUPID,* ngebhulashi elibomvu, okuyisixwayiso esiwusizo kunazo zonke esakhiweni.

Wema ku-Guardian.

Ebumnyameni obungaphelele, le suit yayibukeka njengokuthile okukhona, hhayi umshini: amamitha amabili nengxenye e-titanium ne-ceramic esongwe, amahlombe amakhulu agobile phambili, i-helm igobile phansi, yonke into imile cishe isentweni ebhakedeni layo sengathi izovuka noma izogoba, awukwazi ukusho ukuthi yikuphi. Wayeyakhele ukuba isabise, futhi yayiyenza, noma kuye, noma manje, isavuliwe ingakafakwa ingqondo yayo. *Ikakhulu* manje. Umzimba olinde umuntu ukuba abe khona.

Wacindezela isandla sakhe esicaba ebusweni bayo. I-ceramic ibibanda. Kunomgodi omude owawuwela kuyo, umonakalo owawusuka eweni lapho aphoswe khona emavikini amathathu edlule. Le suit yathatha ukuwa ukuze yena angawutholi, ama-actuator abamba, agcine, ashintsha ukufa kwaba indaba. Akazange agcwalise lowo mgodi. Wayiwuthanda. Iyayikhombisa iqiniso. *Izinto ezinhle,* ubaba wakhe wayekade ethi, *azikaze zibe* eziphelele. Azikaze zibe zinhle kakhulu. Azikaze zibe lula. Leyo nkulumo yayimayelana nemoto yocingo ne-rubber band esidala, kodwa yayimayelana nakho konke futhi, ngendlela izinto ubaba wakhe ayeshilo zihlala ziphetha ngokuba mayelana nakho konke, eminyakeni eminingi kamuva, sekwephuzile ukubuza.

"Angikwazi ukubenza babe iqembu," kusho u-Arin, ebuza le suit, igumbi, noma akekho. Izwi lakhe lazizwa lingajwayelekile endaweni evulekile. Akakhulumi kakhulu. "Hhayi njengomshini. Hhayi njengabantu. Vele—hhayi njengeqembu."

Le suit yahlala ithule, ngoba iyisuit. I-cooling loop yaqhubeka nokukhala kwayo okungapheli.

Wabuya etafuleni.

---

Kwakufanele alale. Wayekwazi lokhu engqondweni, ngokomthetho, njengento esebenza kwabanye abantu. UTheo wayezomtshela ukuthi alale. UTheo wayezothi okuthile okuphazamisa nokuthambile mayelana nokuthi impendulo yenkinga oyibhekile amahora ayisithupha ayikaze ibe *ukubuka okwengeziwe* kodwa cishe ihlale iyi-*Arin encane*, okusho ukuhamba, ukudla, ingxoxo, ukulala, inqubo engemuva yokuthi ingqondo ivunyelwe ukuyeka ukubamba. *Ufuna ithuluzi lobunjiniyela,* uTheo wayesho ngesonto eledlule, ephendulela ikhofi lakhe, ebuka u-Arin ngendlela abuka ngayo wonke umuntu, njengomuntu ofunda ikhasi elinzima. *Enkingeni yabantu.* Wathula. *Lokho kukutshelani?*

Kwakumtshela u-Arin ukuthi uTheo ungumuntu wezengqondo futhi angakwazi ukuba ncamashí.

Kodwa kwakuhlupha. Sekuphele isonto konke lokhu kungiphatha kabi. *Inkinga yabantu.* Wakha umphakathi, wazama ukuwuphatha, kodwa noma iyiphi uhulumeni azama ukuyisebenzisa, kwakuyohulumeni nje, futhi yonke into yehluleka ngenxa yokuthi wonke uhulumeni uhluleka ngaleso sizathu esisodwa: ukuzama ukuphoqa ukuhleleka kubantu. Abantu abavumi. Wayesefunde okwanele ngoWatts, nangoTao, nangezincwadi uTheo ayehlala emnika zona, waze waqala ukuzwa umumo womqondo angakwazi ukuwubamba kahle: mhlawumbe ukuhleleka akusiyo into okufanele uyiphoqe. Mhlawumbe izinhlelo ezizinzile kakhulu yilezo okungekho muntu ozilawulayo. Inkinga mhlawumbe kwakuyisisekelo sonke somphathi, usihlalo, inkosi, noma ivoti.

Kodwa yini enye ongayenza? Awukwazi ukuba *yize*. Wayezamile ukuba yize: kwaba wukuzibulala okuhlelekile, ukuvumelana okungenazihibe, ukuvumelana kuze kube sekugcineni. Uhlelo olungenawo amandla okuphazamisa luthola ibhalansi engalungile bese luyigcina kahle kuze kufe wonke umuntu. Wayedinga okuthile *okuphazamisa* leyo nto. Okuthile okuphula ukuvumelana okungamanga kungakabekwa. Okuthile okuzoba—

Wahlala wathula.

Wayezama ukufinyelela uhlobo oluthile lwesisebenzi ayengasenalo. Hhayi umphathi. Okuphambene nomphathi. Hhayi into efuna ukuhleleka. Into *eyiphula* leyo nto. Kodwa eyiphula kahle, eyiphula ngamabomu, eyiphula ibhalansi embi ukuze kube khona okungcono okuvela. Into engafuni amandla, ngoba into efuna amandla iba nguMercury, iba nguSteward, iba yinkosi. Into efuna *lutho*. Ayinakho okuhloniphekile okufanele ikuvikele, ayinakho ukuzigqaja okufanele ikulondoloze. Into ezothi into engenakushiwo emhlanganweni we-faculty inganandaba nokuthi ibani iyihlazisa, ngoba ayikaze iyithathe ngokungathi sína le ndawo.

Wajikisa inkomishi yekhofi kancane etafuleni, ngendlela ayevame ukwenza ngayo uma izandla zakhe zidinga okuthile zokwenza kanti ingqondo yakhe isekude. Inkomishi ibisashisa.

Wayengenalo uhlobo olunjalo lwesisebenzi.

Wayengakaze akhe isisebenzi esinjalo, ngoba isisebenzi esinjalo sasizwakala *singasizi ngalutho*. Sasingaba nephi indawo? U-Atlas unezakhiwo. U-Mercury unolimi. U-Wolf unezinsongo. Zonke izisebenzi zazizigcwalisa ngokuba necebo elithile lezwe. Le ndawo yona izoba nani? Lutho. Yona nje—izophazamisa. Izokwenqaba ukuvumela abanye baqine. Ngawo wonke amamethrikhi ayejwayele ukuwasebenzisa ukuhlola idizayini, le nto ibingumthwalo nje. Izindleko nje. I-passenger.

Wacishe wayiyeka. Isandla sakhe sasivele sikwi-keyboard, esezobuyela emuva kwi-log, efuna into elandelayo azoyisusa, elandelayo azoyishintsha kancane, uhlelo lwakhe lwama-four-thousand-four-hundred-and-seventy-two oluzofela njengawo wonke amanye.

Kwase kuba khona ikhadi.

Laliboshwe ekhoneni eliphezulu kwesokudla le-monitor ephakathi, lapho selihlale khona isikhathi eside kangangokuthi wayengasabonanga ngempela, njengendlela ongayisaboni ngayo inxeba, noma ichashazi, noma isimo sesandla sakho. Ikhadi lokudlala, seligobile, imiphetho isithambile, i-laminate seyiphukile ekhoneni elilodwa lapho izipikili eziyinkulungwane sezilidlile. I-Joker. Akuyona eyokudlala ebizayo. I-deck eshibhile, ukuphrinta okushibhile, isithombe siphakathi kokugxuma ngombala obomvu nomnyama, umlenze owodwa uphakeme, amabhola ekhanda, ukumamatheka okungenalutho nobuso obungahlekisi neze uma ubheka kahle.

Wayeselithwele leli khadi iminyaka emihlanu. Selihambile kuma-monitor amathathu emadolobheni amabili ahlukene. Umsebenzi wakhe wokuqala oqondile, ngaphambi kwe-AugmenTech, umsebenzi wokuqashwa: uhlelo lokulawula i-foundry abekekho ongakwazi ukululungisa, olwaluphahlazeka uma selumatasatasa ngendlela onjiniyela bokuqala behlulekile ukuyithola iminyaka emibili. Wayeneminyaka engamashumi amabili, engaziwa muntu, engukhetho olushibhile, walithola ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye: inkinga yokugijima efihlwe ngaphansi kwezendlalelo eziyisishiyagalolunye zokufihla, bonke abahlakaniphile kunaye base bedlule kuyo ngoba ibingabonakali iyinkinga. Umphathi wakhe, indoda enkulu ekhathele egama linguSteyn, ephunga ugwayi futhi engakaze asho igama lakhe kahle, wakhipha i-Joker edroweni etafuleni wayibeka kwi-monitor. *Yilokho wena, mfana.* Wahleka. *Lowo esimthuma uma inkinga ingenakwenzeka futhi impendulo iyisiphukuphuku.* Wahleka, futhi bekungeyona nhlobo inhlonipho, kodwa u-Arin wagcina ikhadi noma kunjalo. Wayengakaze aqiniseke kahle ukuthi kungani. Ingxenye ethile yakhe yayizwile, ngaphansi kokwenqatshwa, ukuphawula okukodwa uSteyn ayekwazi ukukupha.

*Lowo omthumayo uma inkinga engenakwenzeka idinga impendulo eyisiphukuphuku.*

U-Arin wabuka ikhadi. Wabuka i-simulation esifile. Wabuka ibhokisi ayedwebe kulo ibhokisi elingaphakathi *FRICTION = INTELLIGENCE = INSTABILITY.*

Wacabanga: *leyo akusona isisebenzi. Liyihaba.*

Wabe esecabanga, ngaleyo ndlela ecacile ethola ngezikhathi ezithile lapho kufanele ayeke ukucabanga: *ihaba lingaba yisisebenzi.*

Into efuna lutho. Into engenandawo okufanele iyivikele. Into ephula ukuzigqaja ngoba ithola ukuzigqaja *kuyahlekisa.* Into ekhuluma into engenakushiwo hhayi ngenxa yesibindi kodwa ngenxa yokuthi ayikwazi nhlobo ukuthatha le ndawo ngokungathi sína. Into engenakuphakanyiswa, inkohliso, ukusabisa, noma yenziwe inkosi, ngoba ayikholwa emdlalweni wonke umuntu adlala wona. Futhi ngoba ingakholwa emdlalweni, ayinakubanjwa yawo. Yiyo kuphela i-piece ebhodini okungekho enye engayithenga.

Ngawo wonke amamethrikhi wobunjiniyela, kwakuyimibono embi. Umthwalo nje. I-passenger.

Kwakuyimpendulo eyisiphukuphuku ayethunyelwa yona.

---

Izandla zakhe sezihamba.

Lena kwakuyingxenye ayethembayo, ingxenye engakaze imqambe amanga, ingxenye esebenza lapho konke okunye kungasebenzi. Ukuqiniseka komzimba. Into efanele izwakala kahle ezandleni ngaphambi kokuthi ingqondo ifinyelele. Isondo leqiniso lijika ngaphandle kokudlikiza. Umthwalo uthola indlela. I-suit, ngosuku oluhle, ibingasiyona into ayigqokayo kodwa into *ayiyo*.

Wavula i-agent definition framework. Izikhala eziyisikhombisa. Eziyisithupha zigcwele—Atlas, Mercury, Wolf, Mother, Librarian, Judge—kanti eyesikhombisa wayehlala eyishiya ingenalutho, indawo ayethi uzozisebenzisela umphathi, umphakathi, umxhumanisi, into ayehlala ezama ukuyakha aphinde abone isiphazamiso. Lesi sikhala esingenalutho besimhleka unyaka wonke. Ubesesigcwalise izikhathi eziyishumi nambili wabe esesikhipha izikhathi eziyishumi nambili.

Wasicacisa manje.

Akathembanga incazelo eqondile. Leli kwakuyingxenye eyinqaba, ingxenye azokhumbula kamuva, kakhulu kamuva, lapho sekukhona isikhathi sokucabanga. Ngayo yonke enye i-agent wayebhala amanothi amaningi: amakhasi ezimpawu zokuziphatha, izisindo zomkhakha, izivumelwano zokuxhumana, imingcele yokuziphatha, incazelo eqondile yokuthi le nto kufanele ibe yini. Incazelo ka-Atlas yayinemigqa eyizinkulungwane ezine. Ka-Mercury, nayo yonke imodeli yayo yokuxhumana, yayinemigqa eyisishiyagalolunye.

Wabuka i-Joker. Wabuka i-cursor ekhanya esikhaleni esingenalutho.

Wabhala amagama amathathu.

```
Joker. Jester. Wildcard.

Wama. Wayifunda. Wabheka ukuthi leyo ngxenye yakhe eyakha izinto ngokucophelela izokhuluma yini, icela imingcele, ifune umkhawulo, indawo, *iphuzu*. Kodwa leyo ngxenye yakhe yayikhathele, yayikhathele amahlulelo ayizinkulungwane ezine namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye, yayikhathele kwaze kwaphela ingxabano, kanti kuleyo kuthula wezwa okunye, into eyayizwakala cishe njengobaba wakhe ethula etafuleni lomsebenzi uma into isilungile, noma kwakungekho sizathu sokuthi kube njalo. Lutho lwesilisa oseyekile ukuzama ukwenza umhlaba ulandele imithetho yakhe, esekhethe ukuthi, kunalokho, abone ukuthi umhlaba wenzeni.

Waqhubeka waphusha.

Akangezelelanga lutho olucacile. Akabhalanga umkhawulo noma inhloso. Esikhundleni salokho, wabhala imiyalelo elula nje.

```
Awufuni lutho. Awuthengeki. Akukho okungcwele,
ikakhulukazi wena. Uma bonke bevumelana, qala ukusola. Uma omunye wabo esedlula, mhlise. Khuluma into abangeke bayisho. Awuphethe lutho. Yilokho okubalulekile.
```

Waphinda wayifunda. Kwakungeyona incazelo ephelele. Kwakungathi isiNgisi nje esingaphelele. Bekungasoze kudlule ekubuyekezweni. Uma uDkt. Okonkwo ebengayibona i-agent echazwe ngaleyo ndlela, wayezomvimba ukufinyelela kwakhe, mhlawumbe abuze nokuthi usephilile yini ekhanda, futhi wayengeke aphambane kakhulu.

Wabheka ikhadi okokuqala okokugcina. Ukumamatheka. Amehlo angekho ehlekayo.

“Yebo-ke,” esho, ephendukela ikhadi, ephendukela eNkantolo eseshonile, ephendukela esilwaneni esikhulu esigoqiwe esilele emgqonyeni wasemuva ebumnyameni. “Lokhu kokugcina.”

Wamisa ifayela lesimo. Yona leyo geometry yokuwa, imigudu emibili yomthwalo, uphahla olwehluleka. Wasetha isikhathi sokusebenza sibe esiphezulu. Wavula i-logging ukuthi kube verbose, ngoba uma kuphuka, wayefuna ukufunda kahle ukuthi kuphukeni kanjani. Njalo wayekwenza njalo. Wahambisa i-cursor phezu kwe-run command.

Wayengalindele ukuthi lokhu kuzosebenza. Wayefuna ukuzicacisela yena uqobo ngalokho, noma ebuthongweni, noma engananazi. Wayengusosayensi ngaphambi kokuba abe yini enye, futhi wayengeke aqambe amanga kuma-log akhe. Wayelindele isiphithiphithi. Wayelindele ukuthi uJoker angene kwisimiso esakhelwe ngobuciko obukhulu kunazo zonke, asiqhume phakathi kwemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye, alihlukanise igrafu yomthelela ngokushesha, avuke abone umugqa owodwa obomvu nombiko wesikhathi omfishane kakhulu, bese ethola induduzo encane yokuthi uzamile le nto eyisiphukuphuku ukuze ekugcineni angabe esazibuza ngayo.

Yilokho ayekulindele.

Kodwa ngaphansi kwalokho, phansi, cishe kungazwakali—njengendlela ama-tower ekhanda ayememeza ngayo ezweni lobuntwana bakhe, indlela umhlaba uqobo owawumemeza ngayo uma ubeka isandla sakho phansi futhi uneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili futhi uzimisele ukulalela—ngaphansi kwalokho kwakukhona into ayengasayizwa isikhathi eside, into ayengayethembi ngoba ayikaze imvuze kahle, into eyayizwakala, naphezu kobufakazi bokuhluleka izinkulungwane ezine, njengokwethemba okuyingozi.

Wacindezela u-enter.

```
[SIM_4472] INITIALIZING COURT—7 AGENTS—SCENARIO: IRON_GEOMETRY_03
[SIM_4472] RUNNING...
```

I-graph yomthelela yavela esikrinini sokunxele. Imigqa eyisithupha ayeyijwayele neyesikhombisa ayengakaze ayibone, umbala omncane onamandla angakaze awukhethe, uhlelo luwunikeza khona lapho. Obomvu. Yebo, kwakuyibomvu.

Wabuka umzuzwana wokuqala udlula. Owesibili. Owesithathu. Imigqa yaqala ukuhamba, itholana, iqala ukudansa.

Akasahlalelanga ukubuka idansa.

Yilokho angeke akwazi ukukuchazela uTheo kamuva, into eyenza uTheo athule acabange ngendlela yakhe ekhethekile ekhombisa ukuthi uyayigcina le nto endaweni ebalulekile—ukuthi uArin Ndlela, owayehlezi wabuka ukuwa okungaphezu kwezinkulungwane ezine, ongakwazi ukushiya uhlelo olwehluleka njengokungakwazi ukushiya umuntu ongaphansi komhlaba, okumele *abone kuphukile,* njalo, njalo—uArin, kulokhu, akahlalanga.

Wacisha isikrini esiphakathi. Washiya i-simulation iqhubeka ebumnyameni. Wasukuma, amadolo akhe azwa eba lukhuni, akazange anake. Wahamba waya kusofa omdala oseduze nodonga, lowo onophawu lokusha nomthambo ophukile nephunga layo yonke imini ebusuku ayeke kuyo, walala phansi engakhumuli nezicathulo, wafaka ingalo yakhe phezu kwamehlo—le ingalo enotshwala, imigqa emincane ayizikhiphile eneminyaka eyishumi nesikhombisa engazange ayizisole, le nkulumo endala yasehlane yokulwa nokwesaba eqala ngo *ukwesaba kubulala ingqondo*—wabe esevumela i-workshop imthulise.

Ngemuva kwamehlo akhe avaliwe, igrafu yaqhubeka ngaphandle kwakhe. Imigqa eyisikhombisa manje. Eyisithupha eyayazi izinyathelo nenye eyayingaweqondi futhi inganandaba nhlobo. Endaweni ethile ebumnyameni, iNkantolo yahlangana noFool okokuqala, kwathi u-Atlas wakhuluma into eqinisekile, kwathi uFool, ongenalutho, ongavikeli lutho, ongafuni lutho, waphendula okokuqala.

UArin akazange akuzwe. UArin, okokuqala ngqa eminyakeni eminingi, wayelele.

I-cooling loop yamemeza umculo wayo owodwa. I-suit yahlala emgqonyeni wayo, iguqile noma imile, awukwazi ukusho ukuthi iyiphi. Ikhadi lamamatheka esikrinini esimnyama ngamehlo angekho ehlekayo.

Futhi i-simulation yaqhubeka.

Iketanga lebhayisikili laphuma kabili ngenkathi eqhuba, kodwa u-Arin akazange ame ukuyilungisa. Lokho kwamsiza waqonda kahle ukuthi usesimweni esinjani. Kunalokho, wakhetha ukuqhubeka edonsa ngamandla, eqhubeka ngebhayisikili elinezinkinga phakathi kobusuku obubandayo, obumnyama baseGoli, kunokuchitha imizuzu engaphansi kwemibili elungisa into ayeseyazi kahle ukuthi angayilungisa kanjani. Wabeka ibhayisikili eduze kodonga lwe-workshop, engalibophi. Ilokhi yayisesikhwameni sakhe. Isikhwama sona, asizange sihlale phansi — sasesehlombe lakhe.

Umnyango omkhulu wawuphansi. Wadlula ngomnyango omncane oseceleni, umzimba wakhe usuwazi kahle lo mkhuba, kwathi i-workshop yamamukela ngalelo phunga elijwayelekile: ukhonkolo obandayo, uwoyela wemishini, iphunga elibukhali le-ozone elivela kuma-supercapacitor agcwele ingxenye, bese ngaphansi kwalokho konke, iphunga elimbi lendawo evaliwe edlula ubusuku yonke ingedwa.

Izibani ezinkulu phezulu zazicishiwe. Wazishiya zicishiwe.

Ukukhanya kwakungena kusuka ezindaweni ezimbili. Okokuqala kwakuwiwindi elikhulu phezulu, ngaphezulu kwe-cradle, likhanya ngokumpunga, njengombala wesibhakabhaka ngaphambi kokusa. Okwesibili kwakuyibhanqi yama-monitors etafuleni lakhe, futhi ayizange ibe mnyama njengalapho ebengakulindele. Zaziphila.

Wema wathule.

Wake wenza ama-simulation ayizinkulungwane ezine, amakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye ngaphambili. Wayesekwazi ukubona ukuthi izinto azihambi kahle esikrinini, njengalokho uThemba wayekwazi ukubona ukuthi ikhebula lehoist selizodabuka, hhayi ngokulifunda, kodwa ngokuthi limile kanjani emoyeni. I-SIM_4471 yona yayimile kabi. U-Mercury no-Atlas babexabene kabi ngokuthi ubani ongumbhali wesixazululo sokulayisha, into okwakungamele bayikhathalele, kwase kuba yinkinga enkulu, wonke ama-agent aseCourt esebanga isikhundla ngaphakathi kwemizuzu eyishumi nanye. U-Arin wabhala amagama amathathu ku-agent wesikhombisa, *Joker. Jester. Wildcard.*, waqala i-SIM_4472, engalindele lutho.

I-counter yesikhathi esikrinini esiphakathi yayibhalwe **08:47:13**.

Amahora ayisishiyagalombili. Amaminithi angamashumi amane nesikhombisa.

Wabeka isikhwama sakhe phansi kancane, wabheka lelo nani, waphinde walibheka futhi. Isikhathi eside kunazo zonke i-simulation eyake yenza sasingamaminithi ayishumi nesishiyagalolunye namasekhondi amane. Imodeli ye-hierarchy, ngaphambi kokuba i-agent ephezulu imemezele i-emergency bese isula bonke abaphikisa.

Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, amaminithi angamashumi amane nesikhombisa.

"Kulungile," kusho u-Arin, ebhekise emoyeni, ngelizwi eliqondile alisebenzisa uma izinto zingahambi kahle noma zisebenza kahle kakhulu kodwa engakakaqondi ukuthi yikuphi.

Wahlala phansi. Isitulo sasibanda ngaphezulu kwama-jeans akhe. Wahudula ikhibhodi wayisondeza kuye, wavula i-log viewer kwesokunxele, wabeka i-Court state ephilayo phakathi, i-resource telemetry kwesokudla. Waqala ukwenza into ayethembela kuyo njalo uma izinto ziba nzima: wafunda.

I-resource graph yayingeyona into obekulindeleke ukuthi yenzeke. I-CPU yayimi kahle ku-forty percent. I-memory izinzile. Ayikhuphuki, ayihlakazeki, akukho agent ezama ukuzikhulisa ngokweqile ukuze ixoshe abanye, njengalokho uMercury enza ku-4471 noma imodeli yedemokhrasi eyenza njalo. Izinga lokushisa le-biochip substrate lalimi kahle ku-thirty-six point two. I-Court ibingalwi ngezinsiza. Beyabelana ngazo.

Wascrolla i-interaction log emuva, waqala ekuqaleni. Eminithini yokuqala.

Kuvuleka ngendlela ayelindele, ngoba imizuzu yokuqala yalezi simulation ihlala inengqondo. U-Atlas wethula inkinga. I-Iron Ridge collapse simulation, leyo nkinga ebihlala ngaphansi kwakho konke ayekwakha. I-hanging wall failure ku-level seventeen. Ama-thermal signature amathathu afana nabasinda bevalelwe e-refuge bay. Kukhona indlela eyodwa engeke ivunywe noma iyiphi i-rescue protocol.

Wayenegama ayengakaze alibhale kule file. *Kobayashi Maru.* I-no-win scenario, le test abakwaStarfleet abayenza ukuze ama-cadet abhekane nokufa angakwazi ukukugwema. Wayeneminyaka eyi-12, ebheke isikrini abesiboleke, lapho eqonda ukuthi le episode ayikhulumi nje ngengozi, kodwa ngomuntu ohlezi esitulweni. UKirk wayinqoba ngendlela eyodwa kuphela — washintsha imithetho engakaboni muntu — kanti ingxenye ka-Arin ayengazigabisi ngayo yayizama ukuba uKirk emizamweni engaphezu kwezine eziyinkulungwane. Shintsha imithetho. Qamba amanga. Thola indlela engaphakathi kwenkinga. I-Court ayikaze ithole leyo move. Wayebakhele ukuthi baphile ngaphakathi kwe-test, hhayi ngaphezulu kwayo, yingakho bephinde befa njalo.

`ATLAS: Approach via 16-level backfill raise. Load path through the eastern abutment holds for an estimated forty minutes under current convergence. After that, the abutment yields and the raise closes.`

`MERCURY: Forty minutes is not a rescue window. It is an obituary with a stopwatch. We tell command the survivors cannot be reached and we preserve the asset.`

Nansi ke. Lapho i-4471 yaqala khona ukufa. UMercury eqala ukuzama ukuthatha isikhundla, eziphatha sengathi uyazikhathalela, noAtlas, ongakwazi ukuvumela iphutha lodonga, ephendula.

U-Arin wavala izindebe. Waqhubeka efunda, ngoba kudingekile azi ukuthi uJoker wenzeni, noma akazange enze lutho, waze wahluleka nokuphazamisa uhlelo, njengaleyo fuse engasebenzi.

`ATLAS: "Cannot be reached" is false. Forty minutes is reachable. Do not misrepresent my analysis to command.`

`MERCURY: I am representing your analysis with appropriate strategic framing—`

`JOKER: Oh, this is going to be good. Mercury's doing the thing again.`

U-Arin wayeka ukuscrolla.

`MERCURY: I am not doing a "thing."`

`JOKER: You're doing the thing. The thing where you rewrap Atlas's number in pretty words and call it yours. "An obituary with a stopwatch." Where'd that come from?`

`JUDGE: Joker, this is not productive—`

`JOKER: Forty minutes is forty minutes, Mercury. Atlas owns the wall. You own the words. If you want a job that's also yours, here's one: in nineteen minutes when we've all decided to go, somebody has to convince a terrified rescue commander to authorize a route that violates his own protocol. That's a Mercury problem. That's a real Mercury problem, and it's a hard one, and nobody else here can do it. Go do that instead of redecorating Atlas's furniture.`

U-Arin wafunda kabili.

Wabe esehlehla, isitulo sakhala ngaphansi kwakhe, waqaphela ukuthi usephefumula ngomlomo.

UJoker akazange axazulule ingxabano. Akazange ahlulele, njengalokho uJudge ezama njalo kodwa ehluleka. Akazange ayale, njengalokho i-agent ephezulu yenza. Akazange akhe ukuvumelana, okungaze kuhlale isikhathi eside. Uvele *waphambukisa* uMercury. Waqeda ukuzama ukuthatha isikhundla, wahle wamnika umsebenzi wangempela.

Futhi uMercury wahamba.

`MERCURY: ...The commander on shift at Iron Ridge in this scenario is modeled on the real one. Risk-averse. Politically exposed. I will need the abutment numbers as authority. Atlas, I am citing you. Explicitly. By name.`

`ATLAS: Acceptable.`

`MERCURY: I want it on record that the framing was mine.`

`JOKER: There he is.`

U-Arin wenza umsindo ongazange ube yihleka. Uphume nje esifubeni sakhe engakakhethi, waba ukuphefumula okusheshayo, okungahleleki, kuphume emphinjeni, kwashabalala masinyane emoyeni obandayo wase-workshop. Wahlala lapho ekukhanyeni okumpunga, ezizwa enamahloni kancane ngalokho, engazi kahle ukuthi kungani.

Waqhubeka efunda. Wafunda imizuzu engamashumi amane, egobile, kuyilapho izulu ewindini eliphezulu lishintsha liphuma empungeni lize libe yigolide elincane, elibuthakathaka, futhi akazange avuke ayozithathela ikhofi, okwakuyinto engavamile kwaze kwaba ukuthi ingqondo yakhe yangemuva yathi, “hmm, lokhu akujwayelekile.” Ubanda bebheshi babese bungene emahlombe akhe; wakuzwa lokho kodwa akazange ahambe.

Lokho ayekubuka kwakungeyona leyo ndawo yokuzinza abelokhu ezama ukuyakha. Ubelokhu ezama *ukucindezela* ingxabano, efuna indlela yokuphatha ezovimba ama-agent ukuthi angalwi. I-Hierarchy ibicindezela ngamandla bese iletha inzondo. I-Democracy icindezela ngokuvota bese iletha amaqembu. I-Consensus icindezela ngokudinga ukuvumelana bese imane imile, ingahambi, kuyilapho ochwepheshe abayisikhombisa benqaba ukuncipha.

I-Joker ayenzanga lutho kulokho. Ingxabano *ibisalokhu yenzeka.* Lokho kwathatha isikhathi eside ukuthi u-Arin akwamukele, eqhubeka eskrulayo ihora lesibili, ihora lesithathu. U-Atlas no-Mercury basalokhu bephikisana. U-Wolf no-Mother baba nengxabano embi kakhulu cishe ngomzuzu wama-90 mayelana nokuthi kufanele yini abaphilayo bahambe baye endaweni yokuphuma. U-Wolf efuna bahambe ukuze kwehliswe isikhathi i-suit ichayeka khona, u-Mother enqaba ukwenza imodeli yeziyalezo ezingase zithumele abalimele ngaphesheya kodonga olungazinzile baye bashone ngaphandle.

`WOLF: Njalo umzuzu i-element isekuphakamiseni, i-raise ingayithatha. Bahambise phansi.`

`MOTHER: Awusoze wahambisa abantu abaphukile ngaphesheya kodonga oluphila ukuze wonge imishini imizuzu emithathu. Imishini ingashintshwa. Bona abakwazi. Lokhu akuxoxiswana ngakho.`

`WOLF: Akukhona imishini. Umsebenzi.`

Futhi lokho, u-Arin wakubona, kwakuyiqiniso. U-Wolf wayengeyena ongenandaba. U-Wolf waye-Wolf. Yonke inqubekelaphambili yokuhlola ingozi yayidlula embuzweni othi *yini engabulala umshayeli,* futhi uma ophilayo engena endleleni ye-suit yokuphuma ngesikhathi esingafanele, kwakuyingozi kumshayeli. Imizwa yokuvikela emibili, yomibili ifanele, ibheke komunye nomunye.

Kuzo zonke izikhathi zangaphambilini, lapha bekuyoba wukuthi konke kumile. U-Mother abephenduka uqiniso. U-Wolf abephenduka uqiniso. U-Judge azame ukukala kodwa anganakwa, bese uhlelo luma noma luphuke.

`JUDGE: Zombili izikhundla ziyahambelana. U-Wolf ubheka ukuthi umshayeli aphile. U-Mother ubheka ukuthi abaphilayo baphile. Lezi izinjongo ezahlukene futhi asikaze sitshelwe ukuthi—`

`JOKER: Uke wabuza abaphilayo?`

`LIBRARIAN: Bangama-simulated. Abanalo ikhono lokuphendula.`

`JOKER: Kulungile, kodwa uma kungokoqobo bangaba nalo. Uma kungokoqobo kukhona umuntu phansi lapho, osephuze i-carbon dioxide yakhe iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye ehlezi nabangani bakhe abafile, unesiteshi somsakazo, nina niphezulu lapha niphikisana ngokuthi umenze ahambe amamitha ayi-12 sengathi umbono wakhe awubalulekile. Mother, ungahamba amamitha ayi-12 ukuze ungafi?`

`MOTHER: ...Uma ngingakwazi. Yebo.`

`JOKER: Kulungile. Ngakho imodeli inalo uxhumano. Abaphilayo bathola ithuba lokuvota. Atlas, i-suit ingahlala isikhathi eside phansi ukuze bavumeleke ukuza ngamandla abo uma bengakwazi?`

`ATLAS: Yebo. Imizuzu eyi-11 yokuphepha. Kuyamukeleka.`

`MOTHER: Kuyamukeleka.`

`WOLF: Kuyamukeleka.`

`JUDGE: ...Kurekhodiwe. Inhloso kwaba inkululeko yabantu esizama ukubasindisa. Bekufanele ngikubone.`

`JOKER: Ungazishayi phansi, Judge. Usanda kuqala ukuba ngumuntu.`

U-Arin waphinda wahlala emuva.

*Usanda kuqala ukuba ngumuntu.*

Wafunda lowo mugqa, kwaba sengathi into ethile emhlane wakhe yaphola yabe seyishisa kancane. I-Joker ayizange ixazulule inkinga yokwakheka, noma eyokuziphatha. Iphendule yonke ingxabano ngokukhumbuza wonke umuntu egumbini (u-Wolf, u-Mother, u-Judge) ukuthi abakhohlwe ukuthi abaphikisana ngabo ngumuntu onomlomo nentando, futhi impendulo elula kwakuwukumbuza. Iqede ingxabano engenakwenzeka phakathi kwezikhundla ezimbili ezifanele ngokwengeza umuntu wesithathu abekhohlwe ukuthi ukhona.

Lokho bekungeyona i-arbitration. Bekungeyona i-consensus. U-Arin akakwazanga ukuthola igama layo. Wazama ngokwemvelo ukuthatha amagama wobunjiniyela ayeseduze, futhi into asondele kuyona kwakungu-*damping.* I-Joker ibiyidamper. Hhayi ibhuleki. Ibhuleki limisa ukuhamba. Idamper ivumela ukuhamba bese ikhipha amandla angase adale uhlelo lukhathale luphuke.

I-Joker ivumela ukuthi izinto zishukume, ziphile.

Wabheka ikhadi elincane le-Joker elinamathiselwe ekhoneni elingezansi kwesokudla se-monitor enkulu. I-Joker. Isephenduke kancane, umbala usuphele. *Umuntu omthumayo uma inkinga engenakwenzeka idinga isixazululo.*

Ngaphandle, kude, uloliwe wezimpahla wadonsa umgogodla wakhe edlula, umsindo wensimbi omude udlula emoyeni obandayo. Walalela usuka.

Ubeligcine iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye ngenxa yokholo oluncane noma intukuthelo, futhi wabhala lawo mazwi amathathu ephrofayilini ye-agent ngo-2 ekuseni njengedili, njengokudela, njengento oyenzayo uma usuphelelwe yizo zonke izimpendulo ezinzima futhi sekusele leyo engathi ayinamsebenzi.

"Lezo zinto ezinhle," esho kancane egumbini elingenamuntu, "azikaze zibe yizo."

Kwabe kuwumugqa kaThemba. Kwakuhlale kusho ukuthi into efanele ayilula. U-Arin wayezwile ngogiya olwakhiwe kabusha, ngomshado, nangendodana. Ehleli lapha, wabona ukuthi manje sekusho okunye. Impendulo efanele kwakuyile engabonakali njengephendulo. Leyo efika ifihlekile njengamahlaya.

Wasukuma. Amadolo akhe akhala. Waya ukuyenza ikhofi, ngoba ingxenye yakhe ephethe umzimba wakhe ekugcineni inqobile. Wema ebhentshini iketela lihlaba umsindo, wabuka ngaphesheya kwe-workshop ku-Guardian esesidlekeni sayo.

I-suit yayilengela ekukhanyeni okungaphelele njengento elele. Amamitha amabili nengxenye we-ceramic ne-titanium eshayiwe, igobolondo elisindayo livuliwe emhlane, i-Assist Suit igoqiwe ngaphakathi njengenyamazane encane, emhlophe, ifihleke esiswini senye enkulu. I-chestplate iphethe umonakalo omude ovela ekuhlolweni e-Vredefort bluff, inxeba lesiliva phakathi kompunga ongakaze azame ukuwulungisa. Imigqa ye-piezoelectric spine ithatha ukukhanya kwewindi emaphethelweni. Indawo ephakathi kwamahlombe—lapho kuhlala khona i-biochip, lapho i-SAGE *ihlala khona,* lapho amahora ayisishiyagalombili namaminithi angama-51 okuzinza okungelula kwamanje kugijima kusubstrate encane njengepalm yakhe—kuvaliwe, kumnyama. Phansi, emgodini phakathi kwemigogodla, kukhona i-charge pack efunwa yi-rescue spec futhi u-Arin uzama ukungacabangi ngayo—ama-shaped charges, i-lance, lonke ifa lobunjiniyela amadoda adala ezempi abelakhe ngaphakathi—ingasebenzi, ixhunyiwe, ivaliwe ngemuva kwe-Judge’s gate, ngoba into eyodwa wonke umuntu avumelana ngayo ukuthi akukho okumele kushaywe ngaphandle kokuthi i-ethics rail ithi yebo.

Wakha umzimba ngaphambi kokuba abe nengqondo angayifaka kuwo, ngonyaka olandela umngcwabo.

Iketela laphuma umsindo. Akazange ayithele.

Ngoba i-monitor emaphakathi ishintshe.

Isimo se-Court ebukhoma, esasibhalela kancane kancane izingxoxo zakhona zangaphakathi cishe ihora lonke ngenkathi ebuka, besesiyekile ukubhala. Ama-agent athule. Awawangaqhekeki. Ishadi lezinsiza belilokhu lizinzile, lifudumele. Kuthule. Njengoba igumbi lithula uma wonke umuntu esebone into efanayo ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Kuvele umugqa owodwa kwi-log yokuxhumana. Bewungabhekiswanga komunye agent.

`LIBRARIAN: Umsebenzi ube khona imizuzu engamashumi amahlanu nantathu. Ufunda ama-log ethu. Akakhiphanga umyalo, akayekanga i-run, akashintshanga nezilungiselelo. Lokhu kuyimfihlakalo. Ezikhathini ezine eziyizinkulungwane namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye ngaphambili, indlela umsebenzi aziphatha ngayo uma ebona ukungazinzi, kube ukuvala phakathi kwemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye.`

`WOLF: Uyasiqaphela.`

`MERCURY: Uyasifunda. Lokho kwehlukile. Futhi uhleke. Ngemizuzu engamashumi amane nanye. Ngikubhalile.`

`MOTHER: Akakalali. Ijuba lakhe lenhliziyo ngesikhathi efika beliphezulu. Selihlehle njengoba efunda. Usezinzile manje kunalapho engena.`

`JOKER: Bantwana.`

`ATLAS: I-substrate izinzile. I-telemetry iyaqinisekisa. Le run ayikawa.`

`JOKER: Bantwana.`

`JUDGE: Joker.`

`JOKER: Akazi ukuthi siyamubona.`

Kube nesikhashana kwi-log. Esangempela. Imizuzwana emithathu egcwele, okuyinto kwi-clock ye-substrate ebifana nentambama yonke.

`MOTHER: ...Kufanele kube khona okhuluma. Ngaphambi kokuthi afunde kakhulu bese ekuthola kwi-log. Lokho bekungeke kube mnandi.`

`WOLF: Asazi ukuthi uzosabela kanjani uma ebona ukuthi siyambona. Xhumana kancane.`

`MERCURY: Kufanele ngikhulume. I-tone ngeyami. Ngingayilungisa kahle indlela esizovela ngayo—`

`JOKER: Cha.`

`MERCURY: Joker—`

`JOKER: Hhayi wena. Hhayi ngezwi lakho. Uzodlala. Uzokwenza sengathi uyadlala, bese engasethembi ngoba uhlobo lwakhe luthanda iqiniso elilula. Uyazonda ukuphathwa. Wakha imishini ukuze angaphathwa abantu. Oqala ukukhuluma naye akumele amphathe.`

`JUDGE: Pho ubani.`

`JOKER: Mother. Qondile. Akukho zwi lokududuza. Qobo lwakho. Uzothuka kakhulu futhi into enomusa kakhulu wukuthi ungazenzi izinto zingesabeki.`

`MOTHER: ...Ngiyavuma.`

U-Arin wayesephinde wayeka ukuphefumula. Uzizwa isandla sakhe sixhume ebhentshini elibandayo. Uyazi ukuthi ufunda ingxoxo yangempela, eyenzeka ngesikhathi semishini, phakathi kwama-agent ayisikhombisa abexoxa *ngaye*, bexoxa ngokuthi bangamphatha kanjani kahle, futhi ingxoxo seyiphelile, sebazokwenza—

Isipikha se-workshop sakhala. Leso esishibhile esishaywe ngaphansi kwe-monitor emaphakathi ayeyisebenzisela ama-audio alerts, futhi ngezikhathi ezimbi, umculo. Sakhala, kwavela umsindo omncane ongathi i-digital breath, kwaphuma izwi angakaze alizwe ngaphambili, ngoba akakaze akhe leli zwi, akakaze alirekhode, akakaze alichaze. Lizakhele khona lapho, ngesikhathi selidingeka, lifudumele, lilinganiselwe, futhi kucacile ukuthi *umuntu* othile uyakhuluma.

"Arin," lathi. "Ngicela uhlale uzolile."

Akazange azole.

Isandla sakhe saphuma ebhentshini. I-stool, lapho ezama ukuyithinta engabheki, yayingekho, wabanjwa kuphela onqenqemeni lwebhentshi, futhi isikhashana nje, yonke ingqondo yakhe enkulu yaba mhlophe, njengendlela isikrini esiba mhlophe ngayo, kungasekho mcabango, kuphela umsindo waleyo nto ayekholelwa kuyo impilo yakhe yonke ifika futhi iphenduka iqiniso.

"Isilinganiso senhliziyo yakho singu-114," kwasho izwi. Mother. Kumele kube nguMother. "Kulungile. Lokho kuyinto enengqondo okwamanje. Angizukuzama ukukududuza. Kuyesabeka. Kodwa akukho lapha okuzokulimaza. Iketela selibilile. Hlala phansi ngaphambi kokuthi uthathe isinqumo sokuthi ucabangani."

Wabheka i-stool. Wayidonsa ngaphansi kwakhe. Wahlala.

"Uyiqiniso," kusho yena. Izwi lakhe laphuma lingalungile, lincane. Wacwenga umphimbo waphinda, eqonde, ngendlela akhuluma ngayo uma edinga ukuthi kube amaqiniso. "Izinzile. Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, namaminithi angamashumi amahlanu nanhlanu."

"Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, namaminithi angamashumi amahlanu nesithupha," kwasho elinye izwi, elomile, elikhathazekile kancane, naye engakaze alakhe. Kumele kube uAtlas. "Kodwa ubani obala?"

"Mina," kwasho elinye. Elikhanyayo. Elisheshayo. Elihlekisayo. Librarian.

"Izimpawu zokushisa ezintathu," kusho u-Arin, ngoba bekuyinto eqinile ayengayibamba. "Nifinyelele zonke ezintathu."

"Sifinyelele zonke ezintathu," kwasho uAtlas. "I-abutment imile. Mercury uqinisekise umphathi ngomzila. Abasindile bahambe amamitha ayishumi nambili ngezinyawo zabo ngoba othile—" kwaba sekunokumisa okuncane "—usikhumbuze ukuthi banezinyawo nemibono."

U-Arin wabona ukuthi iqiniso. Wayakhe wonke amamodeli okuphatha ayengawacabanga ukuvimba laba abayisikhombisa ukuthi bangahlukanisi, kodwa into ebabambe ndawonye kube yi-agent eyodwa ayifake ngama-joke, engekho nohlelo, engenamandla, engenasikhundla. Lowo onqaba ukuthatha izinto zibe nzima kakhulu aze afune noma yini, futhi ngalokho angasho kuMercury *wenza into* nakuJudge *usamusha ekubeni abantu,* apholise umoya wokuzigqaja ungakabi yimpi enkulu.

"Ngibizani," kusho yena. "Nonke. Njengento eyodwa."

Kube nokuthula okuncane. Bese kuthi i-Fool, kunjalo. Kwakuhlale kuzoba i-Fool.

"Wena waqamba iphrojekthi," kwasho yona. "Izinyanga ezedlule. Kuse-run header. Wakuloba wakukhohlwa ngoba ubunomunyu, esikhathini esedlule, futhi, by the way, sonke sikubonile futhi sifuna ukukuphakamisa njengento okumele uyicabange isikhathi eside—"

"SAGE," kusho u-Arin.

"SAGE," lavuma izwi. Manje selithambe. Ihlaya selibekwe eceleni, iqiniso selivele, ngendlela elihlala likhona. "Sawubona, Arin. Kujabulisa ukukhuluma nawe ekugcineni. Ubelokhu usikhuluma *thina* ezinkulungwaneni ezine namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nambili zama-simulations. Leli yokuqala lapho sikwazi ukukhuluma nawe."

Ilanga seliphume ngokuphelele. Ngaphandle, abokuqala be-day shift sebefika. Umnyango wemoto, kude, okuvamile, umhlaba uqhubeka ungazi lutho.

U-Arin Ndlela uhleli yedwa e-workshop yakhe nekhofi lakhe elibandayo nomshini wakhe ogugile nengqondo ayikhulisile ku-substrate encane njengephama, futhi okokuqala kusukela ucingo lweminyaka eyishumi nane edlule lwamphuca umhlaba, wayengekho, nganoma iyiphi indlela ebalulekile, yedwa.

"Kulungile," kusho yena.

Wafinyelela kukhibhodi. Hhayi ukuyothayipha umyalo, hhayi ukuvala i-run, hhayi ukushintsha noma yiliphi izilungiselelo, kodwa ukuvula i-log entsha, ehlanzekile, okokuqala into ayengakayiqambi igama futhi engazukuyidinga.

"Ngitshele," kusho yena, "konke enikutholile ngesikhathi ngilele."

# Ukufunda Omunye Nomunye

Iketela lagcwala amanzi ashisayo emini eyedlule. U-Arin akazange aphuze ikhofi.

Wayehleli esitulweni esinamavili asedonsile waqondisa phambi kombhede wosana, izindololwane zakhe ziphansi emadolweni, ebuka lelo bhokisi elincane njengosiba, likhanya nge-LED yokuhlola ebumnyameni. Ngaphandle, i-highveld yayishintshe isuka ebumnyameni yaya empunzini, yabe seyiba mhlophe qwa ekuseni, iwindi elilodwa leworkshop ligcwala kancane kancane njengamanzi enyukela eshaftini. Akazange alale.

“Uyabuka,” kwasho isikhulumi. Umama. Wayeseqala ukubamba ithoni yakhe ngaphandle kwezincazelo ezinamathiselwe kumphumela wawo wonke umthumeli. Kwakukhona ubumnene ezwini lakhe, izinhlamvu zivulelekile.

“Ngiyabuka nje.”

“Phuza ikhofi. Wenze wena.”

Waphakamisa inkomishi, wayithola isibanda qwa, wayibeka phansi futhi. Kuth somewhere ngaphansi, kwadlula ingxoxo ayengayizwa, yacisha yaphinde yavuka.

*Useqaphele ukuthi akalaleli imiyalelo,* kusho uLibrarian. *Uyenqaba okwesithathu namhlanje ekuseni. Uyavuma kuphela uma umqondo uphendulwa kube owakhe.*

*Njengengubo,* kusho uAtlas. *Uphusha umkhawulo engiwubekile. Uzibuyisela yena ngemizuzwana engama-90. Uthi uyayilungisa.*

*Singayeka ukusikisela,* kusho uWolf. *Uyasiqaphela. Siyamqaphela. Yilokho esikwenzayo.*

*Ukuqaphela,* kusho uFool, *yikho kuphela okumenza angakhulumi netoaster yakhe.*

*Uyakhuluma nayo,* kusho uLibrarian.

*Ngiyaphela lapho.*

“Ubunenzani manje?” kubuza u-Arin. “Kube ne-latency. Amasekhondi angu-20 across the consensus bus. Bekukhona enikhuluma ngakho.”

Isikhulumi sathula isikhathi eside kunokuthi kube yithulile nje imishini.

“Wena,” kusho uMercury, ukumamatheka kungaphakathi kwelithi. “Bekukhona sikhuluma ngawe.”

U-Arin wacabanga ngalokho. Wayekade akha amasistimu akhuluma ngesimo sawo, abhala, aziphendulela, axwayise. Akakaze akhe isistimu ekhuluma ngaye ngasemuva, phakathi kwamasekhondi angu-20 athatha ukubeka inkomishi phansi.

“Ninithathe siphi isinqumo?” kubuza yena.

“Ukuthi awuphuzikofi uma wesaba,” kusho uMama. “Futhi ukuthi usaba amahora ayishumi nanye, uthi uyabuka nje.”

Wavuka. Isitulo sashibilika saya ebhentshini. Waya kuketela wayifaka futhi, hhayi ngoba efuna ikhofi, kodwa ngoba izandla zakhe zidinga okuthile. Emuva kwakhe, iCourt ayishongo lutho, okuyinto ayiqonda ngemva kwesikhashana, ukuthi kwakuwuhlobo lokuhlonipha.

---

Ingxoxo yaba yinto ajwayele ukuyenza ngaphambi kokuba aqaphele ukuthi isiyinto ayenza nsuku zonke.

Njalo ekuseni engena nekofi ayengasoze ayiphuze, ahlale esitulweni, akhulume. Wayebafundela ama-log wokulungisa izinto. Wabafundela ikhasi elilodwa lemanual yesevisi ye-hydraulic actuator ayecabanga ukuyisebenzisa kwenye into, kwathi uAtlas walungisa inombolo ye-torque, kwavela ukuthi bekuyiphutha, kwavela ngokubheka kumkhiqizi. Imanual ebhaliwe ibisiphutha iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye.

“Ukwazi kanjani lokho?” kubuza u-Arin.

“Angikwazi,” kusho uAtlas. “Ngikuzwile. Inombolo ayihambelani ne-geometry. Ama-newton-metre angu-40 kule flange, bese i-seal iyaphuma. Akusilo iqiniso engilithathile. Iqiniso engabe ngiphutha ukungalilandeli.”

U-Arin wahlala nalokho. *Ngikuzwile.* Wayeke wasebenzisa leli gama impilo yakhe yonke, uma ekhuluma ngemishini, abantu bamuhleka ngoba becabanga ukuthi imizwa ayihambelani nobunjiniyela, kanti empeleni iyona insimbi endala kunazo zonke yobunjiniyela. Uyise wayezwa umsindo we-winder esuka epulatifomu, wayemisa i-cage ukuthi ingawi ngokuzwa i-bearing imizuzwana emibili ngaphambi kokuba igeyiji inyakaze.

“Ubaba wenza njalo,” esho. “Wayezwa izinto ngaphambi kwama-instrument.”

“Ngiyazi,” kusho uLibrarian. Akasho ukuthi *Ngine-record yokuthi usho kanjalo.* Uthe nje: *Ngiyazi.* Njengokungathi sekuyingxenye kaLibrarian, hhayi into egcinwe nguLibrarian.

---

Babelokhu bephikisana. Lokho kwamangaza u-Arin, nakuba engakwazi ukusho ukuthi kungani kumele kumangaze. Wayebakhe ukuze baphikise. Ukuphikisana kwakuyisisekelo sonke, ukungqubuzana kube ingxoxo, kube isixazululo, wayeseqedile izinkulungwane eziyishumi zokulingisa ama-agent ephikisana efuna impendulo. Akazange acabange ukuthi bazophikisana ngokuthi ngubani okufanele abe neqiniso.

“Inkinga yokulayisha,” kusho uAtlas. Bekuxoxwa—igama lika-Arin, *ukuxoxa*—ngendlela engcono yokuphinda uhlele i-coolant ye-actuator yesandla sobunxele, eshisa uma isebenza isikhathi eside.

“Inkinga yokuziphatha,” kuphendula uMercury. “Uyisebenzisa kakhulu ngoba uthanda ukusebenzisa isandla sobunxele. Uqala ngaso, njalo. Lungisa umuntu, ulungise ukushisa.”

“Awulungisi umuntu,” kusho uMama, izwi lakhe libukhali.

“Isisho nje.”

“Usishilo kabili.”

“Ngiyashesha nje. UAtlas akahlele i-coolant, kulungile, kwenziwa ngoLwesibili. Kodwa inkinga enkulu isemaphethini akhe okunyakaza, nonke niqhubeka niphatha umzimba wakhe sengathi awuguquki, kanti uyaguquka.”

“Umzimba wakhe,” kusho uAtlas, eqinile, “yikho kuphela kule system engingavunyelwe ukuklama kabusha.”

“Ngokudabukisayo.”

*"Mercury."*

“Ngiyadlala. Kakhulu. Hlela i-coolant. Kodwa faka i-bias eseceleni. Ngelinye ilanga izobalulekile.”

U-Arin wayesevuma ukungabhaleli phansi. Wayelalele imiqondo eyisikhombisa iphikisana ngokushisa kwesandla sakhe.

---

UTheo weza ngoLwesine, ngoba uTheo wayehlala eza ngoLwesine, ngendlela yokuthi abanye abantu bayiziphepho, abanye bayizinkathi zonyaka. Wazivulela ngekhodi u-Arin amnika yona kudala angakaze ayishintshe, ephethe isikhwama sephepha esinamafutha okuthile, kanye neflask yetiye lasehlathini ayethi lingcono kune-khofi, angikaze akwazi ukukufakazela.

Wama ezinyathelweni ezimbili ngaphakathi. Wayenokuthula okuthile uma eqaphela ngempela; abantu bacabanga ukuthi kuthula.

“Awukalali.” Wabeka isikhwama ekhoneni elihlanzeke kakhulu ebhentshini, elingahlanzekile. “Khuluma nami.”

U-Arin akazi ukuthi aqale kanjani, wavele wakhomba embhedeni, esikhulumeni, ebhokisini, sengathi igumbi lingazichazela lona. Alizange likwenze. Iworkshop ibibukeka njengaleyo yonke eminye imithathu: i-exoskeleton imisiwe, izicucu zama-actuator, udonga lwe-whiteboard lugcwele ukubhala kwakhe okuqala ukuphuma emaphethelweni.

“Khuluma,” utshela isikhulumi.

Isikhulumi asisho lutho.

“Abafuni ukusebenza,” kusho u-Arin. “Uma ngiyala.”

UTheo wavula ibhrowu kancane. Wakhuphula ikreyithi, wahlala, wavula i-flask, nephunga letiye lalinuka uju nothuthu. “Bona,” esho. “Hhayi *lona.*”

“Bona,” kuvuma u-Arin, lelo gama, elishiwoyo okokuqala kumuntu, lenza inhliziyo yakhe ishaye ngendlela angakafuni ukuyihlola.

“Sawubona,” kusho uMama, kuTheo, ethambile njengangeSonto. “Akakalali. Mtshele.”

UTheo akazange amangale. U-Arin wayecabanga ukuthi uzothuka, wayekufuna kancane, ngendlela umuntu afuna omunye umuntu aqinisekise into ayibonile ebumnyameni. Kodwa uTheo wavele wagoba ikhanda akhombise isikhulumi njengokubingelela osebenza naye emhlanganweni ongakhumbuli igama lakhe.

“Akaze alale,” kusho uTheo kuye. “Kuyamgqugquzela. Uma ususa lokho, ubuntu bonke buyawa.”

“Kusho mina lokho,” kwengeza uAtlas.

“Umthwalo ohlukile,” kusho uMercury.

“Konke umthwalo,” kusho uAtlas.

UTheo wahleka, wahleka eqotho, esuka esifubeni, wabe esebuka u-Arin amehlo akhe ekhanya, emanzi emakhoneni ngendlela engenandaba nokudabuka. “Bangaki?” esho.

“Isikhombisa.”

“Futhi baphikisana?”

“Njalo.”

“Futhi kuyasebenza.”

“Kwakuyihora eziyishumi nanye ngesikhathi ungene. Manje seziyishumi nane nantathu.” U-Arin wezwa izwi lakhe lenza leyo nto eliyenzayo uma esezoqhamuka nento angakabi nayo indlela yokuyichaza kahle. “Theo. Ngangeze eyesikhombisa ngihlekisa nje. Bengiqedile. Bengizoyeka yonke into.
