Kwakukhona ikhadi phezu kwe-monitor yami—” wakhomba; i-Joker isalenga lapho, inamathelisiwe, isishile yilanga—“ngabhala amagama amathathu ngaya embhedeni kwase kwenzeka le nto—” Wama. “Yiyona ebambe konke. Leyo engangikholwa kuyo.”

U-Theo wathela itiye esivalweni seflask, waphephetha phezu kwaso, akaphuzanga. Ekhuluma, izwi lakhe lalinesisindo sokucophelela, njengomuntu obeka phansi into ayeyithwele isikhathi eside.

“U-Jung wayenegama,” esho kanje. “Le ngxenye ye-psyche engavumeli ukuphathwa. I-trickster. Wayithola kuyo yonke inganekwane ayivulayo: i-coyote, i-raven, u-Loki, isilima kwi-tarot. Njalo lowo muntu ofanayo. Njalo lowo ophula umthetho wenkosi kodwa ngendlela ethile ebalulekile ebusweni benkosi.” Wajikijela isivalo ezandleni zakhe. “Ingqondo evulekile yakha izinga. Inkosi phezulu, inkantolo ngezansi, umthunzi uphonswe phansi egumbini. Futhi kuyasebenza, isikhashana, bese kuba nzima ngoba izinga lihlala lishintsha, bese okuyosindisa akusiyo inkosi. Kuyisilima. Ngoba isilima sikwazi ukusho izinto inkosi engakwazi ukuzazi.”

---

*Uchaza mina,* kusho uFool, ejabule. *Umuntu ungibhale phansi. Ngikwi ncwadi.*

*Usezincwadini eziningi,* kusho uLibrarian. *Akukho neyodwa ekhuluma kahle ngawe.*

*Ngiyathanda ukuba nodumo kunokudunyiswa.*

*Thulani,* kusho uJudge, kwathula wonke umuntu, ngoba uJudge akavami ukucela. *Lalelani le ngxenye. Utshela u-Arin into ayidingayo angayitholi kithina. Bhala phansi. Kubalulekile ukwazi ukuthi yiziphi iziqiniseko angazizwa kuphela ngezwi lomunye umuntu.*

---

“Uthi ngakhile i-psyche,” kusho u-Arin. Izwi linqamukile, ehlola ukuthi le nkulumo ayinazimbobo yini.

“Engikushoyo,” kusho u-Theo, “wukuthi ufinyelele embonweni wakudala ungazi nokuthi mdala. Abantu benza kanjalo. Abahle bakwenza kaningi kunalokho abacabanga.” Ekugcineni waphuza itiye, wenza ubuso, waphuza futhi. “U-Freud wayecabanga ukuthi ingqondo iyinto eyodwa elwa nayo uqobo. U-Jung wathi iyiphalamende. Umphakathi. Izinhloso ezahlukene, amazwi ahlukene, akekho oyedwa ongumuntu ophelele, futhi i-self—” wabeka isivalo phansi “—i-self akuyona eyodwa yazo. I-self yingxoxo. Ubudlelwano phakathi kwezingxenye.”

I-workshop yakhala. Iketela seliphole futhi.

“Bayamfundela,” ekugcineni kusho u-Theo, kancane, ebhekisa kumkhulumi, ngendlela umuntu akhuluma ngayo nomndeni wesiguli.

“Yebo,” kusho uMama.

“Ufunda ini kuwe?”

Kwakubuswa ukuthula. Kwaphendula uWolf, okwenza bonke, abantu nabanye, bama bathule.

“Lokho akuvikela,” kusho uWolf. “Ngendlela angavumi ukuthi kwephuke.”

U-Theo wabuka i-housing isikhathi eside ngemuva kwalokho, ngendlela umuntu abuka ngayo umnyango asanda kuqaphela ukuthi uyavuleka kokubili. Wabe esevala i-flask, wema, wakhuluma into abezoyisho engakaze ayazi ukuthi uzoyisho.

“Phumani nabo,” esho. “Usubafundisa ngawe egumbini elingenalutho. Bavumele bafunde ukuthi ungubani uma kukhona okungaphansi kwezinyawo zakho okungakudonsela phansi.”

---

I-suspension ye-flatbed yakhala kakhulu emamitha ekugcineni e-track, yabe isingenela e-veld. U-Arin wavala injini, kwangena ukuthula, hhayi njengokungabikho kodwa njengokuba khona: umoya ezihlahleni neculo elikude lento ethile ezungezayo phezulu. Ama-bluffs e-impact country ayimizuzu engamashumi amane ngaphandle kwedolobha uma ithrafikhi imvumile, kodwa ayingavumanga, kwaba cishe ihora nesigamu; i-highveld yelula ngemiqolo emide ephuzi-nsundu, inamachaphaza ebunjwa yizigidi ezimbili zeminyaka, amatshe ephuma otshanini njengamathambo omhlaba oshaywe.

Wayekuthanda lapha. Umhlaba awuceli lutho, awudingi ngisho ukubukwa emehlweni.

Phezu kwe-flatbed, ngaphansi kwe-tarp namabhande amathathu, u-Guardian wayelele ethule.

“Telemetry iluhlaza,” esho. I-Assist Suit imfanela njengohlelo lwesitho lwesibili ngaphansi kwezingubo zakhe, i-lattice emhlophe njengeliparele ilandelela umgogodla nezitho zakhe. I-biochip housing izinzile phakathi kwamahlombe akhe, isisindo esifudumele. Ihora nesigamu egqoke lesi sudi kwenze izandla zakhe ezazinyikinyika zathula.

*Ukuthi iluhlaza kuyadlalwa,* kuphendula uAtlas, eqondile, ebukhali. *Ibhethri liku-91%. Ama-supercapacitor bank asezingeni. I-actuator yesithende sokunxele iphambene ngo-0.3 degrees selokhu kwedlule amasonto amabili. Uqhubeka ungayilungisi.*

“Iyasebenza,” kuphendula u-Arin.

*Iyasebenza njengobhuloho ngosuku olungaphambi kokudilika kwawo.*

“Ngiyakuqaphela.” U-Arin wehlisa i-tailgate waqala ukukhulula amabhande. “Mama. Ngikhona kanjani?”

*ICortisol iphezulu. Leyo yinjongo, hhayi ukwesaba.* Izwi likaMama lishisa, liqinile, luhlobo lokushisa olungabaleki. *Umanzi. Udle, engikhethe ukukuzwa kahle. Inhliziyo yakho ithule. Uyazijabulisa.*

“Angijabuli izinto.”

*Uyazijabulisa,* kuphikisa uMama.

Wakhumula i-tarp. U-Guardian ekukhanyeni kwasekuseni okuqinile: mnyama njengesiliva, inamabala emashulder plate kusukela ezinyangeni eziyisithupha zokuhlukumezeka. I-armor ehlukanisiwe ibamba ilanga, ibuyisele ngama-plane amade angashisi. Amamitha amabili nesigamu omshini ugobekile ngendlela yokuthutha, njengosidalwa esiphumula. Ayibukeki njengesikhali; ibukeka njengento eyakhelwe ukumunca ukulimala ukuze okuthambile ngaphakathi kungadingi.

Wakhela yona ngqo ngalokho. Njalo lapho eyiveza, uzama ukungacabangi ukuthi leyo nto ethambile ekuqaleni yayenzelwe bani.

“Coupling sequence,” kusho u-Arin, ehlehla angenele ku-cradle.

---

I-dock ayifani nhlobo nalokho okukhonjiswa kumavidiyo okukhombisa. Ayinabuhle. Kuyindoda ehlehla ngokucophelela ingene emshinini: i-spinal collar ivala ngenhlokomo ezwakala emazinyo akhe, ama-plate evala imilenze nomzimba ngendlela ayeseyazi kahle. Ukubanda kwe-coolant lines kuhlangana, i-harness idonsa.

Bese i-link ijula, umhlaba ushintsha.

Kukhona into eyenzeka, noma engenzeki, emzimbeni. U-Arin uchithe iminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu emzimbeni ofika sekudlule umzuzwana esuke efuna ukwenza into, obeka isandla sakhe lapho okungafanele, omenza umfana okhethwa ekugcineni hhayi ngenxa yokuzonda kodwa ngenxa yezibalo. Abanye babebona ukuzama kwakhe ukubamba bese, ngokufanele, belungisa umbono wabo.

Isudi ivale lelo gap.

Hhayi ngokumenza aqine, nakuba yenza njalo. Ngokumenza *ahlangane*. I-Assist Suit ifunda inhloso yakhe ngaphambi kokuba imisipha iqede ukuyakha, iCourt iyixazulula nge-lattice, insimbi ihambe naye, ingaphambi nangemuva lutho. Wema. U-Guardian wema kanye naye, amathani ayishumi nantathu ephakama sengathi awasindi lutho, ama-footplate ecindezela emhlabathini obomvu. Iningi lempilo yakhe, umzimba wakhe ubungathi uyizwe avakashela kodwa angaze athembe ngokuphelele. Lapha, wayengowakuleli.

Waguqa isandla sokudla. Ama-plate avuleka abuye avale ezithweni ngomculo ofana nophepha lwamakhadi.

*Range of motion ilungile,* kusho uWolf, okungukuphela kwakhe akushilo ngesonto lonke.

“Siqala kuphi?”

Kwathuleka. I-Court yathula, okungathi kubantu akuyona isikhathi nhlobo, kodwa kubo bekuyingxoxo yonke ayengayizwa.

*Ubuso basempumalanga,* kwasho uAtlas ekugcineni. *Amamitha ayishumi nambili, idwala elikhulu elinama-grip amahle, akukho ngozi enkulu. Fudumeza umzimba ngaphambi kokuba wenze izinto eziyisiphukuphuku.*

*Uzogcina esenza into ephukuphuku,* kwengeza uMercury emamatheka. *Kungcono sivumele uAtlas azitshele ukuthi angakuvimba.*

"Ubuso basempumalanga," kwavuma uArin.

---

Waqala ukuhamba.

Ukuhamba ngezinyawo ezine kwakuzizwa kungajwayelekile ekuqaleni, manje sekuyinto emnandi, ehlale imnandi, ephusha phambili umzimba, isisindo sisabalala ezindaweni ezine. UGuardian wadlula kalula phezu komhlaba onamatshe, ngendlela eyamethusa ekuqaleni. Yayingahambi njengomshini ozama ukufana nesilwane; yayihamba njengenyamazane eyenzelwe ukuba umshini. Njengengonyama enkulu, noma isibankwa esikhuphuka odongeni, noma okuthile okuvela engqondweni yomuntu okwazi ukuthi *lapha kukhona ingozi* futhi *lapha kukhona amakhono.*

Ubuso basempumalanga baphakama phezu kotshani, idwala elikhulu le-quartzite eliguquke kakhulu, licijile cishe ngamashumi ayisikhombisa degrees, linemifantu emidala kakhulu. UArin wafika ezansi, wema ngezinyawo ezimbili, ebheka phezulu, ezwa ama-grip ngaphambi kokuwakhetha. Akakwenzi ngokuqonda; iCourt yenza yonke i-geometry, imdweba engqondweni yakhe njengokungathi yinto azalwa nayo.

*A-grip lapha, lapha, nalapha,* kwasho uAtlas, kwathi isandla sika-Arin saya lapho ekhombisa khona. *I-friction imnandi. Idwala liqinile phansi. Ungathembi lutho olungaphezulu kwe-mitha yesishiyagalombili—kukhona ungqimba oluhlukile phezulu. I-stress endala yokushayisana.*

Waqala ukukhuphuka.

Ama-plate kaGuardian athola i-quartzite abambelela. Ama-strut e-piezoelectric emhlane nasemilenzeni amunca umthwalo, aguqule ingxenye yawo ibe ugesi, ingcindezi iba amandla, okuyinto ayakhele ngayo wonke umshini. Lokhu kusebenza ngoba kune-layer ekhethekile kuma-strut, futhi le-layer wayixazulula ngendlela yakhe—ngokungavumi ukuthi akunakwenzeka. Zonke izinto ezisemabhukwini zazilahlekelwa amandla amaningi kakhulu, zishise kakhulu kunokudlulisa amandla kahle phakathi kwe-strain ne-cell. Kodwa kwakukhona enye into—i-alloy ye-platinum eyenziwa yi-defence lab ye-hydrophone arrays, yabe seyishiywa ngoba ibiza kakhulu, yaze yafakwa kufolda ethi *not viable* yaze yafika ezandleni zikaArin ngendlela yokubolekisa, kancane nokweba. Kancane, noma ngempela: uPillay omncane wayisayina wathatha isampula, akazange ayibuyise, kwathi uma kuhlolwa eminyakeni emibili kamuva, igama likaPillay laphuma, waphucwa i-clearance, waphuma emkhakheni. UArin wayekwazi. Kukhona i-email endala evela kuye, ayingakaze ayiphendule. Wazitshela, ngezikhathi ezithile, ukuthi uPillay wayezohamba noma kunjalo, waphinda wabheka i-strut, ngoba insimbi yayisebenza futhi umsebenzi awunandaba negama elisemqulwini. Akakaze akhathazeke ukuthi iGuardian ngayinye ibiza kakhulu kunesakhiwo esakhiwe kuyo. UOkonkwo wayekukhathalela lokho. UThabo waphinda wahlola izindleko kabili waphuma emhlophe. *Kuyisimangaliso futhi akuthengiseki,* kwasho uThabo, enomusa, uArin wathinta iziphethu, ngoba le suit yayingeyona into yokuthengisa kuye, yayiyindlela kuphela umzimba wakhe ongawulalela ngayo, futhi awubeki intengo phansi kwezinyawo zakho. Wasebenzisa insimbi eyodwa eyayisebenza. Akazange acabange ngale kwalokho, ngoba into eyayimvumela ukuthi enze lokhu. I-footplate, i-handhold, ukudlulisa isisindo, umsindo omile we-ceramic edonsa edwaleni. Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwakuzinzile. I-link yayicula. Wayesephakeme ngamamitha ayisishiyagalolunye, enesisindo esingamakhilogremu ayizi-1300 wensimbi, futhi akakaze azizwe *ehlelekile* kangaka.

"Lokhu kuhamba kahle," kusho yena, okwakuyinto enkulu kuye.

*Uthe into enhle,* kwasho uMercury kwabanye. *Bhalani isikhathi. Librarian, faka ku-log.*

*Kubhalisiwe,* kwasho uLibrarian. *Kusithathu kwezintathu namuhla. Okokugcina kwakuyisikhathi esisodwa ngoMashi.*

"Ungakuphathi phansi," kwasho uMother.

*Angikuphathi phansi,* kuphendula uMercury. *Ngiyabala. Kunomehluko.*

*Awukho umehluko,* kwasho uFool.

Bese, kancane, ngokuqondile ku-Arin, uFool edlula abanye, efika lapho okubalulekile khona:

*Wenze into enzima, uyazi. Akukhona ukukhuphuka. Ukudedela. Ukuvumela uWolf abe nomzimba uma umhlaba uzamazama.* Kancane. *Kufanele wenze okuningi kwalokho. Ukudedela izinto zibe nawe. Kufuna kahle kunokubambelela, futhi ukubambelela kuhlala kufuna ukukubulala.*

UArin akaphendulanga. Kodwa akaphikisanga, futhi uFool—owabona konke—wavumela ukuthula kube yimvume.

Wafika phezulu emamitheni ayi-12 wema kudledge, i-highveld ivuleka ngezansi kwakhe, ukukhanya kwegolide, iJohannesburg iyithunzi elimnyama lamathala kude. Umoya ophuma esigodini washayisa i-armor. Phansi kakhulu, i-flatbed yakhe ibukeka njengethoyizi.

Wema lapho isikhathi eside kunalokho okudingekayo. Ngaphakathi kwe-shell, wagudluza isihlakala sokudla, ebuka ama-finger plates ehamba, into encane angazange ayicabange.

*I-cortisol isendaweni ejwayelekile,* kwasho uMother kancane, kwiCourt hhayi kuye. *Bheka lokho.* Kancane. *Ningasho lutho. Uzobona ukuthi siyibonile.*

---

Isigodi sasiqonde phambili.

Sinqamula ebufini amakhilomitha amabili eningizimu, umgodi omdala owandiswe ukuguguleka, amamitha amane ububanzi endaweni encane, phansi amatshe amakhulu, amamitha ayisithupha phansi. Waphinda wacabanga: ukufika, ukuma, ukucindezela ama-leg actuators nge-supercapacitor, ukugxuma. Wake wagxuma kancane kunalokho. I-math yayicacile.

*I-math icacile,* kwasho uAtlas, sengathi ufunda umcabango, okuyiqiniso. *Amamitha amane no-0.1 endaweni encane. Uzodlula ngamitha eyodwa uma ume kahle. I-burst evela kuma-capacitor banks. Uzohlala ku-40% reserve imizuzwana eyisishiyagalombili ngesikhathi zishaja. Ungazami ukwenza ubuqhawe kuleyo nkathi.*

*Chaza ubuqhawe,* kubuza uMercury.

*Ku-Arin? Ukuphefumula,* kwasho uFool.

"Run the jump," kusho uArin.

Wafika egijima kancane, indlela yokuhamba ishintsha ngaphansi kwakhe ukusuka enyameni iye kokunye okukhulu, i-rhythm iyakhula, wafika emaphethelweni, wama ngama-footplate womabili. Umhlaba wanyamalala.

Ukugxuma kwakuhle. Ayikho incazelo ye-engineering yalokhu. Ama-capacitor banks akhipha amandla ezinyaweni, uGuardian wagxuma ngaphesheya: amamitha amane omoya, amamitha ayisithupha okufa phansi kwakhe, isisu sakhe sikhuphuka esiphongweni. Kwakufana neminyaka eyisishiyagalombili, egxuma imoto yomucu entabeni yamadwala, injabulo efanayo, ukuzethemba okufanayo ukuthi wakhe kahle—

—futhi wafika kahle, zombili izinyawo, wagudluza umthelela emadolweni, waqhubeka egijima izinyathelo ezintathu, wema.

Izandla zakhe zazinyikinyeka. Hhayi ngenxa ye-dyspraxia. Okunye.

*Lokho bekukuhle,* kwasho uWolf.

"Bekukuhle," kwavuma uArin, ehleka kanye, emangazwa.

*Uhlekile,* kwasho uMercury. *Kabili kwezimbili.*

---

Uphuze amanzi okugcina ebhodleleni abeboshelwe esibayeni. Utshani bebunuka uthuli nelanga. Umele emlonyeni womfudlana isikhashana, evumela inhliziyo yakhe ukuthi ibuyele esimweni, ebheka inselelo elandelayo.

Isudi ibimnene namhlanje. Uyikhiphile i-charge pack ngaphambi kokulayisha uGuardian ku-flatbed—akukho muntu onemvelaphi yokubhidliza okhuphuka egqumeni lokuhlola insimu, lowo ngumthetho azibhalela wona ngosuku afaka ngalo isikhala—futhi ukungabi khona kwalelo sikhathi, leyo ndlela yokuhlala ulungele, kumphathe kabi engqondweni, kufana negqubu elilahlekile emhlathini. Sekusele isakhelo, nezinsika, naye. Yilokho okwenza uGuardian abe lula kunanini ngaphambili.

Ukukhuphuka kube yikho okuguqule usuku.

Bekufanele ayeke. Uyazi, emuva kwalokho, ukuthi bekufanele ayeke. Ukugxuma kumugcwalisile into angakwazi ukuyichaza, into efuna okungaphezulu, futhi leyo nto imqambela amanga, njengokujwayelekile.

Ubuso basenyakatho bumile phezu kwakhe, i-slaba eqondile engamamitha angamashumi amabili nambili, ibukhali futhi iyingozi kunezinye. Idwala belingalungile ngezindlela ukukhanya kwasekuseni okube kufihle. Ulibheke ezibalweni zesatellite ebusuku edlule walibeka njenge-“interesting.” Ezingezansi, ephakamisa ikhanda likaGuardian, libonakale njengomugqa oqondile oqondile phezulu ebhentshini, ama-hold abonakala cishe yonke indlela. Ukukhuphuka kweqiniso. Lolo hlobo olungabulala umuntu ongenasudi, lolo hlobo uGuardian akhelwe ukuba enze luphileke.

*Imiphumela iphakeme lapha,* kusho uAtlas, eqonde futhi eqaphela. *Amamitha angamashumi amabili nambili. Eyishumi ezansi zilungile. Ngaphezu kwalokho, angithandi ama-bedding planes. Leli idwala elidala elaphukile ngenxa yokushayisana, futhi i-dip ayilungile—ama-hold awama-flake agcwele umthwalo, hhayi itshe eliqinile. Abukeka njenge-hold. Akuyona indlela yomthwalo.*

“Zibambe ku-slaba.”

*I-slaba ibiqinile. Lona akuyona i-slaba.* Kwaba nokuthula. *Ngingancoma indlela esentshonalanga. I-angle iphansi, iqinile yonke indlela.*

“Ukubora kuyinzuzo, akusona isici,” kwasho uFool, kodwa izwi lakhe lashintsha. UFool ebebuka uAtlas. UFool ebebuka uArin engalaleli.

UArin waqala ukukhuphuka ebhentshini.

Amamitha ayishumi okuqala kube yikho konke ukukhuphuka obekuthembisa. Ukhuphuke kalula edwaleni eliqinile, iCourt ipenda ama-hold, izinsika zibuyisela i-charge ohlelweni, futhi leyo nto ngaphakathi kuye yacula. *Lena yinto oyakhele yona. Lena yinto umzimba wakho obekufanele ube yiyo kusukela ekuqaleni. Phezulu.*

Ephamatheni ayishumi nambili, wadlula edwaleni elibi wazizwa ushintsho ngendlela ozwa ngayo isimo sezulu sishintsha: ukungahambi kahle okuncane, ama-hold ethanda ukugoba ngaphansi komthwalo.

*Arin.* Izwi likaAtlas selilahle konke ukoma. *I-flake engaphansi kwesandla sakho sokunxele. Ungayifaki umthwalo ngaphezu—*

Uyifake umthwalo ngaphezu.

I-flake yaphuma.

Yadabuka ebhentshini, i-slaba ngobukhulu bomnyango, kwase kuba amamitha angamashumi amabili nambili aba inombolo enemiphumela. Isandla sikaArin sokunxele savala emoyeni. Isisindo sakhe sashwiba phansi nangaphandle, wonke umthwalo wakhe ongu-1300kg ujika ngesandla sokudla esesivele sibekwe edwaleni uAtlas abesexwayisile ngalo. Umhlaba waba nomsindo omkhulu futhi wahamba kancane.

Ku-link, iCourt yamemeza. Hhayi ngendlela yabantu. Ngendlela yabo, kwavela ukuqhuma okuqinile—i-mode ehamba kancane ivuleka phakathi kwefasitela le-millisecond, kwenziwa umhlangano ophuthumayo phakathi kokushaya kwenhliziyo okukodwa nokulandelayo.

*I-hold yesokudla izoshiyeka ngo—* uAtlas, i-geometry igcwalisa isiteshi, yonke indlela yomthwalo ikhanya ebomvu.

*Mina ngiphethe umzimba,* kusho uWolf. UWolf usewuthathile umthwalo ngaphambi kokuba ingqondo kaArin iqede ukuqaphela ukuwa. *Ungangilwi, Arin. Zithobe.*

*Pangolin,* kusho uMother. *Mphendule ibhola. Mthumele phansi eyindilinga; uyaphila uma efika—*

*Cha.* UAtlas. *Asikaphakami kangako ukuthi sidinge lokho futhi asikacaci kangako ukuthi singakusebenzisa—uma ephenduka ibhola manje, uzoshayisa lawo ma-boulders aphansi ejikeleza. I-shell iyabamba, kodwa umgogodla wakhe uzothwala umthwalo wokusonta ngekhola. Uyophila kodwa angeke ahambe. Kukhona okungcono.*

*Pho yithole phakathi kwe—* uJudge, ezolile ngisho nalapha, *—ama-millisecond angamakhulu amane.*

*Kukhona igebe.* UWolf. Eqondile. Uqinisekile. *I-fissure eqondile, amamitha amabili kwesokudla naphansi. Ububanzi besandla. I-flake esanda kwehluleka iveze yona.*

*Ngiyabona,* kusho uAtlas, u-Arin ezwa izibalo zifika, ezwa ukuthi kungenzeka. *Plate-edge jam. Faka ingalo yesokudla ngaphakathi, ama-plate avuleke ngaphakathi kwegebe, abambe odongeni—lapho siwa kakhulu, ayabamba kakhulu.* Isikhashana. *Njenge—*

*Njenge-cam eshayelwa nge-spring,* kusho uLibrarian. *Ukuvikelwa kokukhuphuka. I-principle isineminyaka eyikhulu. Unamakhulu awo e-workshop.*

*Njenge-honey badger engena entweni engafuni ukungenelelwa,* kwasho uFool. Ububhanxa balokho, ubulima bokusho lokho phakathi kokuba umuntu ewa egqumeni, kwaphula umjikelezo. Iphanikhi ebisivele iqala ukushisa esiteshini—ukuphindaphinda *uyawa uyawa uyawa*—yema. I-Court yema ukwesaba, yaqala ukusebenza.

*Yenza,* kusho uJudge.

Konke lokhu kuthathe isikhathi esingaphansi kokuthatha iso ukuvala.

Lokho uArin akuzwa kube lokhu: ubewehla, kwase kuthi ingalo yakhe yesokudla inyakaze ngaphandle kwakhe—uWolf eyiqhubela ngokushesha nangokuqiniseka kunanoma yisiphi isinqumo angasenza—yashaya ingalo ngaphakathi kwegebe angalibonanga. Ama-plate e-forearm armor avuleke abambe edwaleni ngokukhala kwe-ceramic nangeshock enyukela ehlombe nasekhola, kwase kuthi eme.

Wamile lapho. Amamitha angamashumi amabili omoya ongekho ngaphansi kwakhe. Ingalo eyodwa ibambeke emhlabeni.

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kube nomsindo omkhulu ngaphakathi kwe-shell. I-faceplate yafiphala ekhoneni. I-drop ye-sweat yalandela umugqa womhlathi wakhe, futhi akakwazanga ukwenza lutho ngakho. Phansi, izingcezu ze-flake ephukile zisathola ama-boulders, umsindo wamatshana ewela phezu kwamanye.

*Sibambekile,* kubika uAtlas. *Ama-plate abambekile. Umthwalo usabalala ku-forearm spar. Sine-maybe imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye ngaphambi kokuba i-spar ikhathele kule angle. Kuncane uma uhamba kabi.*

*Inhliziyo ishaya ngo-204,* kusho uMother, ephansi futhi ephuthumayo. *Akaphefumuli kahle. Arin. Arin, ngilalele. Awusawi. Usumile. Sibambekile.*

Umbono kaArin wancipha, waba mpunga emaphethelweni. Impunga kwaba isitha sakhe sakudala, ukwesaba isikhala esivaliwe, into ephila lapho uyise ashona khona. Isifuba sakhe saqinisa. I-shell esanda kumsindisa impilo yakhe yaba, phakathi kwesikhashana, isibhakela esivala kuye, futhi iphaphama iCourt abayiphulile ngaphakathi kuye.

“Angikwazi—” wakhuluma kanzima. “Angikwazi—ukuphefumula—”

*Uyakwazi,* kusho uMother. *Ukwenzile umzuzwana odlule. Uzokwenza futhi. Phefumula amane. Nami. Mane.*

Akwazanga.

*Arin.* UFool, futhi izwi likaFool lahlekelwa yonke into, laba buthule futhi lisondele, ngendlela uFool ayenza ngayo kuphela uma kubalulekile. *Hey. Uyazi ukuthi uyini manje? Uyindoda elenga egqumeni ngoba engalalelanga ustructural engineer. UAtlas ngeke akukhohlwe lokhu.*

*Mina angikhohlwa,* uqinisekisa uAtlas.

*Uyabona? Yingakho ngazi ukuthi uzoba right. UAtlas usevele ehlela inkulumo. Akakhulumi nezidumbu; akuyona into yakhe. Phefumula amane.*

Kukhona okuthile esifubeni sika-Arin okuxegayo kancane. Akusikho ukuthi ihlaya lihlekisa kangako. Kodwa ngoba ihlaya lithe khona nje, kusho ukuthi umhlaba usesejwayelekile ngokwanele ukuthi kube khona amahlaya. Futhi ngoba uFool uziphethe ngendlela evamile, kanti leyo ndlela ivamile kusho ukuthi kusinda. Umoya wakhe ukholelwa kuFool lapho engakwazi nokukholwa ukwesaba kwakhe.

Uyakhipha umoya, abale kuze kube kane.

Lempunga yahlehla kancane.

*Nanguya,* kusho uMama.

“Kulungile,” kuphefumula u-Arin. “Kulungile. Ngizokwenzenjani?”

*Lalela kahle,* kusho u-Atlas. *Wolf unomzimba. Mvumele awuthathe. Phezulu kwesandla sakho sobunxele kukhona indawo ongabamba kuyo, iqinile kulokhu. Ngiyihlole kathathu uma ngibheka nombhede. Wolf uzokusa kuyo. Sizokwehla nawe eduze komgodi—kunomgwaqo ongakwesokudla ngamamitha ayi-10 ozokwehlisa ngaphandle kokuthembela kwenye idwala. Awudingi ukwenza ubuqhawe. Phefumula. Sakhuphuka.*

“Wolf unomzimba,” aphinde u-Arin.

*Ngino mzimba,* kuqinisekisa uWolf. *Phinda uzilalise. Thembela esandleni esigxunyekwe. Sizokubamba size sithi akusabambi.*

Futhi u-Arin—ongakaze akwazi ukuthemba omunye, owakha izinhlelo ukuze angadingi ukwethemba isandla esingesiso sakhe—wazilalisa, wavumela umshini wamphathe.

Yikho okunzima kakhulu akwenzile ngalolo suku. Kunzima kunokukhuphuka.

Wolf wafinyelela ngesandla sakhe sobunxele wambamba endaweni eqinile. WaQinisekisa. Wabe eselinda ku-Atlas ukuthi abale, wakhulula ingalo ebigxunyekwe—kwaba umzuzwana wesaba lapho umzimba uselenga emoyeni ngaphambi kokuthi abambe kahle—wabe eseqala ukuwela, eqinisekisa indawo ngayinye, i-ceramic ishaya itshe, eya lapho umgwaqo wephutha ukhona. U-Arin akakhuphukanga. U-Arin uphefumule, ekhuphula umoya kuze kube kane, wavumela amazwi amaningi amthwala, wonke efuna into eyodwa: ukumhlisa ephila.

Lapho befika emgwaqweni wephutha, kwakuyigodi elinama-degree angama-30 igcwele isihlabathi. Wehla ngayo ngezandla nezingalo, indlela eyayimethusa phambilini manje iseyisipho. Lapho izinyawo zifika otshanini phansi, wema wema isikhathi eside. Umoya ophuma egqumeni washaya ubuso bekhasi, kwakhala uthuli, kanti kude leya nto eyayizungeza isazungeza.

Izandla zakhe, ngaphakathi egobolondweni, seziyekile ukuzamazama.

“Bika umonakalo,” kusho yena, ngoba kwakulula kunokunye.

*Ingalo yesokudla ithwale umthwalo ongayenzelwe wona,* kusho u-Atlas. *Kunezikhala ezincane eziphukile eduze kwe-cam. Kuzodinga ukushintshwa. I-ceramic iphukile ezithweni zeminwe. Futhi kukhona okungathandeki.* Wathula kancane. *Amandla.*

“Yisho.”

*Umthwalo we-cam uphuzile wonke amandla ebhange le-capacitor, amaseli azama ukubamba ukukhathala kwengalo ngesikhathi sangempela. Usele nama-9% kuphela. I-battery yesobunxele ithole ukwehla okukhulu, ngeke isabuya. Leyo battery ifile. Wakha ezine. Usele nezintathu.*

U-Arin wema otshanini, wazizwa leyo nombolo ifika. Ama-battery akhe ayengama-Volkov-spec, ehlangene kahle ne-biochip, amasonto ayi-11 okulinda, nemali engaphezu kwaleyo ayitshele uNomsa. Wayengeke ayithenge enye kalula. Umshini wonke usuphelelwe amandla ngo-25%, futhi akukho ndlela yokuyishintsha ngaphandle kokusebenzisa umthengisi, iphepha, imibuzo.

*Kubhalisiwe,* kusho uLibrarian, engakabuzi.

---

Umgwaqo ogudle izintaba wazamazamisa ibhodlela elisemgqonyeni, kushaya kancane, wabe esewehla kancane emuva kokusebenza kwakhe, iNkantolo yehlisana naye. Yilapho intengo yesibili ivele khona. Hhayi emzimbeni, kodwa phakathi kwabo.

*Ngifuna ukukhuluma,* kuqala uMercury, eqaphela, elungiselele. *Akekho ozokuthanda.*

*Kusho ukuthi iqiniso,* kusho uFool. *Khuluma.*

*Wolf uthathile umzimba ngaphandle kwavoti.* UMercury wayeka kancane. *Amamillisecond angu-200 ngaphambi kokuba idwala lidilike, wayesebambe ingalo ka-Arin. Ngaphambi kokuvumelana kwethu. Ngaphambi kokuba u-Arin azi ukuthi uyawa. Angithi akaphosanga—u-Arin uyaphila, ingalo isemgodini, kulungile. Ngithi kube khona umzuzu namhlanje lapho omunye wethu ethatha izintambo, abanye bathola kamuva. Yilokho kanye esasingafuni kube khona.*

*Ngibonile idwala liqala ukuhamba,* kusho uWolf. *Ngisebenza ngalokho engikubonayo.*

*Kwenzekani uma ubona okungelona iqiniso?* kusho uMercury. *Awuvamile, kodwa uma kwenzeka, awukho muntu phakathi kokubona kwakho nomgogodla wakhe. Sasusa umphathi ngoba izikhundla zabulala ukwethembana. Asizange sikwenze ukuze umthwali wengozi abe ngumphathi uma kunenkinga.*

*U-Mercury uphakamisa iphutha elikhulu,* kusho uJudge, kancane. *Amandla okuphuthuma aphenduka umuntu oyedwa, kuyinto efana nesikhundla. Kufanele—*

*Asifanele lutho,* kusho uMama, futhi kwakukhona into entsha ezwini lakhe u-Arin angakaze ayizwe, ukushisa ngaphansi kokufudumele. *Wolf wayembambe. Ngingaphinda nginike uWolf umzimba futhi. Ngizomnika njalo, ngoba okunye bekuzoba ukuthi u-Arin aguqe phansi edwaleni. Nifuna ukulawula indlela esize ngayo?*

*Ngifuna ukwazi ukuthi kungokabani le ndlela,* kusho uMercury, *ngaphambi kokuthi siyidinge kwento ebaluleke kakhulu kunedwala.*

INkantolo ayizange ikuqedele. Yilokho u-Arin akubona, elalele phakathi kwenethiwekhi, i-battery ifile ingasenawo amandla. Baxabana kuyo yonke indlela, bafika bengakafiki ndawo, futhi leyo ndawo yokungafiki yayinomuzwa ayewazi kahle. Owalondoloza.

*Kubhalisiwe,* kusho uLibrarian, kuthule. *Ngaphansi kwesihloko esisha.*

“Yisiphi isihloko?” kusho u-Arin. Okokuqala akhuluma ngemizuzu engama-20.

*‘Ubani Oshayela,’* kusho uLibrarian. *Kwakudingeka kube nefolda.*

U-Arin wabuka umgwaqo. Wacabanga nge-battery efile, nengalo, nomthengisi azodinga ukumthinta, nomfana ogama lakhe linguBongani osezokuthola ukuthi kungani asayina amaphepha engawafundanga.

“Okuzayo,” ekugcineni wathi, “sizothatha indlela elula.”

*Le elula,* kuqinisekisa uAtlas.

“Yebo.” Washayela isikhashana. “Futhi sizobona ukuthi ubani oshayela. Ngaphambi kwezimayini.”

*Izimayini?* kusho uFool, kancane.

U-Arin akazange aphendule. Wayengazange ahlose ukusho. Leli gama livuke nje, njengamagama avuka ebumnyameni, manje selihlezi nabo emotweni, futhi akekho kulabo abayisikhombisa obekubakhona eduze kwakhe othe lutho, ayekwazi manje ukuthi kwakuyindlela yokuhlonipha.

Wabheka emuva esibukweni, wabona umthunzi omnyama kaGuardian ngaphansi kwetarp, i-battery efile ithule ngaphakathi, akazange aphinde asho lutho. Wayengasadingi.

Bonke sebebhalile.

# Inombolo Engavali

Kwadlula izinsuku ezintathu selokhu kwenziwe ukuhlolwa kwensimu, kodwa le battery eyayishile isalenga lapho efreyimini yokuhlola, ifana nezinyo uArin angakwazi ukuyeka ukulithinta.

Wayeyikhiphe eGuardian ngalobo busuku babuyela khona, i-string yesobunxele isiphenduke ize ingasakwazi ukuphila, ikakhulukazi ohlangothini olusenyakatho, manje isihleli ebhentshini ngaphansi kwesibani somsebenzi, ama-terminal ayo evulekile, ingamtsheli lutho angakaze akwazi. I-Volkov-spec. Ihlanganiswe ngesandla ukuze ifane nokudonswa kwe-biochip. Izinsuku eziyishumi nanye zoku-oda kusenesikhathi, nemali engaphezu kwalokho ayitshele uNomsa ukuthi uyisebenzisile kunoma yini. Wayakhe ezine. Usele nezintathu.

Phezu kwebhentshi, lapho omunye umuntu angabeka khona isithombe, kwakukhona iphrinti angakaze ayisuse: indatshana ye-wire-service, imigqa eyisishiyagalombili, izinyanga ezintathu endala. Umhlaba owehlile emayini yeplatinamu eNorth West, amadoda amane avalelekile ngemuva kwawo, amaqembu okuhlenga amisiwe ezinsukwini ezingamashumi amathathu nanye ngoba ayikho imishini emhlabeni engafaka umzimba lapho kukhona imizimba ngaphandle kokubulala abanye abantu ukuze kwenzeke. Ababili baphuma. Ababili inkampani ibabhalile njengo *recovered, deceased* emshweni omfishane kunalo. Wayengabazi amagama abo. Leyo yingxenye eyahlala kuye: izwe elakhiwe ngokungena emigodini, kanti amadoda ayenza lokho nawo aba nje into ebhalwe phansi, *recovered, deceased*, amagama amane nenombolo, kanti kukhona omama othile owaphendula ucingo ngendlela umama wakhe ake waphendula ngayo. Akazange akhe iGuardian ngenxa yomqondo. Wayakhe ngenxa yehora lama-31, ngenxa ye-timber amaqembu angakwazi ukuyidlula, ngenxa yamagama angawaziyo kanye nezingcingo angakwazi ukuwumisa. Ugcine le phrinti lapho ezoyibona khona uma umsebenzi usuphenduka ama-capacitor windows nama-Volkov cells kanye namafomu uBongani awashaya ngaphandle kokuwafunda, ukuze kungabi nje yilokho kuphela uma engabheki.

"Awubuyi," esho.

*Awubuyi,* kwavuma uAtlas. *Ungakwazi ukuqhuba umshini ngezintathu. I-endurance yehliswa ingxenye yesine. Ama-capacitor recharge windows ayanda. I-margin owayifunde ngendlela enzima iyancipha.*

"Ngizoyifaka enye."

*Leyo,* kwasho uMercury, ethule, *yiyona engithakazelisa.*

U-Arin wabeka i-multimeter phansi. Wayiqonda leyo ndlela yokukhuluma. UMercury wayisebenzisa ngendlela abanye abantu bephendula ngayo umphimbo.

"Khuluma."

*Awukwazi ukuyithenga nje. Wena uqobo wathi njalo, sisabuya. I-string idlula kumthengisi, umthengisi ufuna ikhowudi yephrojekthi, ikhowudi idlula kwi-procurement.* Isikhashana. *KuBongani, oshaya amafomu engawafundi. Okulungile, kuze kufike usuku othile ayifunda ngempela.*

"UBongani usewashayile amafomu angamashumi amane kimi."

*Amafomu angamashumi amane kumuntu, obhalwe phansi, olinganisa i-rescue prototype i-AugmenTech ecabanga ukuthi isekhishini eBuilding C.* UMercury wayeka kancane. *Akekho kulawa mashumi amane owake wacela ama-Volkov-spec cells ahambisana ne-biological inference substrate. Lona uzowacela. Leyo into ibonakala. Ohlola ibhajethi uyayithola abuze ukuthi isetshenziselwani.*

U-Arin wabuka i-string esifile. Wabuka ne-housing eseceleni, i-diagnostic LED ikhanya kancane.

"Yiyale ngababathathu abahlukene," esho. "Yahlukanise i-line. Amakhodi ahlukene."

*Manje usuqala ukufana nami,* kusho uMercury, ejabule. *Ngizobhala izicelo ukuze zingafani.*

"Ungazenzi zihlakaniphile. Ukuhlakanipha nakho kuyabonakala."

Kwathula, okwakusondele ekutheni kube ukuhlonipha kuMercury.

*Zizoba yizinto ezijwayelekile,* wavuma. *Ngizozilungisa zibe yizinto ezingajwayelekile.*

---

Usebenze lonke ekuseni kwi-forearm spar, leyo uWolf ayifake ekhaleni ohlangothini olusenyakatho, yaphuka kancane eceleni kwe-cam, yinkokhelo yokuphila. Wayesevele ebeke i-ceramic entsha, i-spar endala ise-vise, izandla zakhe zenza into ayezenza kahle, into engadingi ukuxoxisana nomuntu.

Kwaba nguLibrarian owaphazamisa.

*Arin.* Kaqondile. Igama lasho ngendlela uAtlas ayelisho ngayo ebutress, lapho idwala liphenduka kabi. Hhayi ngendlela efudumele. Enye leyo.

Wabeka i-driver phansi. "Khuluma."

*Ngibheke i-telemetry ye-field test. Hhayi ukugibela. Ingemuva.* Ethule kancane. *Ngesikhathi siqhuba—lapho sicabanga kakhulu, i-slow-mode burst ngesikhathi uWolf ethatha umzimba—i-node yokuhlola yandisa i-sampling resolution yayo kwi-footprint yethu. Izikhathi ezine kunokujwayelekile. Iphinde yehla lapho siqeda.*

"Izindawo zihlola konke. Yilokho ezikwenzelwe khona."

*Ama-substrate-forensics nodes ahlola ngezikhathi ezifanayo,* kusho uLibrarian. *I-power rhythm. I-compute topology. Iphasi. Leya igcine leso sikhathi izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye. Iphinde yangena, ngesikhathi sithatheka kakhulu, yaphuma ngesikhathi siyeka.* Wathula, waqinisekisa. *Yandisa ukunaka kwayo ukuze kufane nokwethu. Akusona isilinganiso. Sekuyinto eqalayo ukubuka.*

Kwakuthule endlini yokusebenza. Phandle, i-taxi yashintsha i-gear emgwaqeni. U-Arin akazange abuye athathe i-driver.

"I-node eyodwa," esho.

*I-node eyodwa. I-power-utility cooperative, amakhilomitha angamakhulu amabili enyakatho.* Isikhashana. *Umbiko wayo wokulayisha lesifunda sethu unephutha elingaxazululeki. Ama-kilowatts ambalwa angabhalwanga kahle. Kuyaphindaphinda. Kuyaphazamiseka. Ifomu layo lilandela i-run schedule yethu, nakuba kungakabikho oselubone lolo layini.* ULibrarian wayeka le ngxenye ukuba yizwakalise. *Ohlola lezo zibalo uthola inombolo engavali. Futhi ifomu ngaphakathi kwenombolo.*

U-Arin wacabanga ngabathengisi abathathu abesanda kuyala uMercury ukuthi ahlukanise i-oda kubo. Wacabanga ukuthi umuntu angakwazi ukunakekela iphepha kodwa ashiye enye indlela, ebhalwe ngogesi, ngomdlandla wezingqondo eziyisikhombisa eziphikisana.

"IAOC," esho. Akubuzanga.

*Indlela yokwenza ifana neyabo,* kusho uLibrarian, engayithambisi. *Spectral decomposition ye-compute rhythm. Bayafunda ifomu lengqondo, hhayi okuqukethwe kwayo. Kushicilelwe embikweni wabo wesithathu, i-appendix four.* Wathula, walinganisa. *Sinefomu elibonakala kalula. Asikwazi ukuba thina bese sithule. Ukuphikisana ngumsebenzi. Ukuphikisana yilo mkhondo.*

*Manje siyeka ukuphikisana,* kusho uWolf.

*Asikwazi,* kusho uFool, okokuqala engasihlekisi. *Yilokho okwenza sibe khona. Into esisigcina siphila iyona esenza sibe nomsindo. Umama ngeke avumele uWolf ashayele ngaphandle kokulwa. Mina angeke ngivumele nithole ukuthula. Uma lokho kususwa, sithule. Futhi ukuthula kusho umphathi ogqoke ishidi. Sizobe siphephile kodwa sesifile.*

Izandla zikaArin zayeka. Wabona ukuthi ubambe i-spar entsha engayifaki.

"Unesiqiniseko kangakanani," esho.

Kwathula kwaba isikhathi eside, ingxoxo yonke angayizwanga, imizuzu yomshini esongwe phakathi kokuphefumula.

*Asikazi okwamanje,* kusho uAtlas ekugcineni. *Leyo yimpendulo eqotho. Ibuthakathaka. Iyaphazamiseka. Izigcine izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye ingakaboni ukuthi singobani, futhi ingaqhubeka njalo.*

"Futhi uma ikalinganisa kabi."

*Iyayeka,* kusho uAtlas, *futhi inombolo engavali iba umbuzo othile azowubuza obala. Ngemuva kwalokho akusaseyi-node. Sekuyimuntu. Onedeski, nomsebenzi, nefomu uBongani angakaze asifunde.*

U-Arin wabeka i-spar phansi ku-vise. Akazange ayifake kahle. Wema ekukhanyeni okumpunga kwe-workshop, ne-battery string esifile phezu kwelinye ibhentshi kanti enye ephilayo ibuka kude, amakhilomitha angamakhulu amabili, waze wazizwa lezi zinto ezimbili zihlangana.

I-work lamp yacofa njengoba ibhokisi layo liphola. Kukhona iphunga le-solder, nothuli ojikeleza kancane ekukhanyeni okuvela ebhentshini. Into angeke ayithenge ethule. Into esivele imbalela.

---

Waya endlini yokuhlala. Kwakuvamile akwenze uma edinga ukucabanga ebuka okuthile, futhi okuthile, kulezi zinsuku, kwakuyibo.

"Atlas," esho. "Le flake ese-north face. Le hold ebizwakala kahle kodwa ayikho."

*Yebo.*

"Le yini lena?"

Ukuthula kulokhu kwehlukile. Akusilo esokusebenza. Esokucophelela.

*Kubukeka sengathi kuyindlela yokuthwala,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ibuthakathaka. Ikude. Isebenza kahle izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye. Lolo hlobo oludlula phezu kwalo ungahloli ngoba ukuhlola kudla kakhulu kunokwethemba.* Kwavela isiqephu. *Kodwa ngelinye ilanga uyayithembisa isisindo.*

*Ungakhohlwa,* kusho uMama, ephansi. *Ungamphindisi akhuphuke kabili ukuze afunde i-spreadsheet.*

*Uyakuqonda kangcono nge-cliff,* kusho uLibrarian. *Konke kwakhe ukwazisa ngaleyo ndlela.*

UMama akaphikanga. KuMama, lokho kwakuwukuvuma.

U-Arin wabuka i-LED. Ezinsukwini ezintathu ezedlule wayelenga emhlabeni ngesandla esisodwa ngoba i-hold ibizwakala iqinile kanti ayikho, u-Atlas wamtshela, kodwa wayithembisa isisindo noma kunjalo. Wacabanga ukuthi into ingasebenza ngaphezu kwazo zonke izimfuneko ayizibekile kodwa isamphuca okuthile angakubhalanga. I-string, i-spar, nombuzo osanda kuvela kwelinye i-ledger elingaziwa amakhilomitha angamakhulu amabili enyakatho.

"Nansi esizoyenza," esho. "Asiphindi i-field profile futhi. Akukaze ngazi ukuthi le node yenzani uma sikhuluma kakhulu." Ingqondo yakhe yabuyela e-basement kwa-Building C—i-high-gain calibration suite, i-cable cradle, ne-umbilical ezomvumela aphushe i-loop ngaphandle kokushisa ama-cells. "Ngifuna ukuhlola i-loop nge-external power. I-tethered. Egumbini elingenalo izulu ukuze i-node ingakwazi ukufunda. Uma sizokwakha isimo, sikwenze lapho i-meter ingakwazi ukubona ukuthi siyaphefumula."

*Ukuhamba okuqondile,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ngiyakwazi ukusebenza nokuqondile. Angithandanga i-cliff.*

"Ngiyazi."

*Ngikushilo.*

"Uhlala ukushilo."

*Kufanele kube khona owenza njalo.*

Wavala i-work lamp. I-string efile yacima; i-housing yona ayizange.

"Buka i-node," esho. "Uma nje ishintsha i-cadence, ungitshele. Ngaleso sikhathi. Akusilo emva kwalokho. Ngaleso sikhathi."

*Ngiyibukile vele,* kusho uWolf.

Futhi uLibrarian, ethule, eqopha into ezodinga ifolda:

*Ngaphansi kwesihloko esisha.*

"Yisiphi isihloko?"

*"Izinto Ezibuka Emuva,"* kusho uLibrarian. *Ibezodinga isihloko ekugcineni.*

U-Arin wema eseworkshop isikhashana, i-spar isengaphakathi ku-vise ingakapheli, i-oda labathengisi abathathu lingakathunyelwa, nenombolo ekude amakhilomitha angamakhulu amabili ingavumi ukuvalwa.

Welula isandla wavula i-requisition terminal. Wama, isandla phezu kwamakhiya.

Uma e-oda i-string, wayezodweba umugqa ngokusobala. Uma engayenzi, wayezosebenzisa umshini ubuthakathaka kancane kunalokho okulandelayo.

Wayengakakhethi lapho i-LED ku-housing ishintsha i-rhythm yayo. Zonke eziyisikhombisa zema ngendlela engesiyo ukuphumula.

*Arin,* kusho uWolf.

"Yini."

*I-cadence,* kusho uWolf. *Ishintshe futhi manje.*

# I-Preservation Run

Ibhokisi lokuhlola belinuka i-ozone kanye ne-wax yaphansi. I-AugmenTech ibigcina i-high-gain calibration suite esitebeleni esingaphansi kweSakhiwo C, egumbini elingenamafasitela, elinenhlabathi ye-concrete enomsele maphakathi, sengathi kukhona obelindele ukuthi kuzomele kugezwe. U-Arin wayeyithathe ngaphansi kwekhodi yephrojekthi okungekho muntu owaziyo ngaphandle kukaBongani osezokuthutha, owafaka isitampu engafundanga lutho, wabuyela esangwinini sakhe.

UGuardian ubemile esidlekeni phakathi kwegumbi. I-load-shell yangaphandle, yonke i-titanium lattice ne-ceramic eshayiwe, yenza umthunzi ukhuphuke odongeni ugobe udlule ophahleni. Ngaphansi kwayo, i-Assist Suit ibambe u-Arin eduze kwesikhumba sakhe, njengohlelo lwesibili lwezinzwa, i-biochip inesisindo esipholile phakathi kwamahlombe akhe.

Waphinda wabala amanani okokugcina.

"Gain ceiling point-nine-two. Damping floor twelve percent. Steps of four. Uma ungavumelani, khuluma manje noma ungakhulumi nhlobo."

*Izibalo zilungile,* kusho u-Atlas. *Angithandi le margin.*

"Margin yilesi sivivinyo."

*Ngakho angithandi isivivinyo.* Wathula kancane. *Ngiyarekhoda.*

"Uhlala usurekhoda."

*Kumele kube khona owenza njalo.*

U-Arin wahlola isidleke sekhebula, i-umbilical enikeza amandla ngaphandle ukuze kungapheli amaseli, ne-webbing ethambile ebamba u-Guardian ephakathi nendawo. Ukuhlolwa kwensimu kwase kumfundise ukuthi isudi ingakwazi ukwenza ini uma eyicela. Manje umbuzo ubehlukile: izosebenza kahle kangakanani uma ephusha i-signal fidelity ngaphezu kwemingcele ephephile abelokhu ezifihlela yona. I-high gain ibisho ukuthi i-coupling iqinile. I-coupling eqinile ibisho ukuthi u-Guardian uyanyakaza engakacabangi kahle. Emthambekeni, ezinsukwini ezimbili ezedlule, lokho kube sengathi kuzombulala. U-Wolf kwamele athathe izintambo ayisindise.

Wayengafuni ukuphinde adinge lokho futhi.

"Mother. Biometrics."

*Inhliziyo iphumula ku-61. I-cortisol iphezulu kancane, okungathi kuwukuphumula kwakho.* Kukho ubumnene ngaphansi kwamazwi, kodwa kunokuthile okubandayo ngaphansi. *Ulele amahora amane.*

"Kwanele."

*Ulele amahora amane.*

"Kurekhodiwe," kusho u-Librarian.

Wangena esidlekeni. I-Assist Suit yamamukela isisindo sakhe, i-load-shell yavaleka, amaplate ehla ngokuthula okuncane kwezimishini: ukushunqa okubandayo kwama-latch, umsindo omncane wama-hydraulic seal, konke kuqondile ngendlela izandla zakhe ezaziyenza ngayo. I-faceplate display yakhanya. Kwesikhashana kwaba khona ukwesaba, ukwesaba okudala kokuboshwa, kwathi lapho imiphetho isivaliwe, wayengekho eboshwe. Wayegqokwe. Umngcele phakathi kwengalo yakhe ne-actuator waba umthungo, kwabe sekuphela konke.

"Court check," esho.

*Atlas. Structure nominal.*  
*Librarian. Logging.*  
*Mother. Ngikubhekile.*  
*Mercury. Ngilindile.* Wathula kancane. *Ngiyabhora, impela.*  
*Wolf.* Igama kuphela. U-Wolf akakhulumi kakhulu.

*Judge,* kusho u-Judge. *Qhubeka uma usukulungele. Mina ngizobamba i-ethical envelope.*

"Fool?"

Kuthule.

Walinda. Igumbi lamemeza.

*Fool,* waphinda.

*Ufunda,* kusho u-Librarian. *Sherlock canon. The Final Problem, ngicabanga.*

"Mtshele agxile."

*Ngimtshelile. Uthi, ngicaphuna, 'ngigxile kuphi, lapho kungekho lutho oluhamba kabi?'*

U-Arin wakhulula umoya, cishe wahleka. "Point-six-four gain. Damping forty. Step uma ngithi."

Isinyathelo sokuqala asizange sibe lutho. U-Guardian uphakamise ingalo yakhe yangakwesokudla lapho ecabanga, kahle, ngaphambi kwesigqi senhliziyo lapho bekusethwe khona phambilini. Kuhle. Kuhlanzekile. Waguqa iminwe ye-ceramic, wabuka igrafu yokulayisha ihlala iqondile.

"Step."

Point-six-eight. Ingalo iphakamile ngokushesha, i-feedback inothile. Uzwe amandla e-actuator ebuyela emzimbeni wakhe njengengcindezi, umoya wokuzama.

"Step."

Point-seven-two. Umthungo phakathi kwenhloso nokunyakaza usuba mncane. Wajikisa umzimba, wancika u-Guardian ku-webbing, wazizwa wonke umthwalo uphendula sengathi ungowakhe.

*Kuhle lokhu,* wavuma, ezikhuluma yedwa.

*Kunjalo,* kusho u-Atlas. *Futhi yilapho ngiqala ukukhathazeka khona.*

"Step."

Point-seven-six. Point-eight. Point-eight-four. Isinyathelo ngasinye sinciphisa i-lag, sikhuphula i-fidelity ye-signal ebuyayo. Wayesemshinini ngendlela ukuhlolwa kwensimu okungakaze kufike kuyona. Waphakamisa zombili izingalo, kwaba ezakhe. Waphefumula, isudi yaphefumula uhlelo lokupholisa oluzungezile.

*Inhliziyo 78,* kusho u-Mother. *Amehlo avulekile. Uyathokoziswa lokhu.*

"Lokho akuyona i-biometric."

*I-biometric uma ngiyifunda mina.*

"Step. Point-eight-eight."

I-damping floor seyiphansi manje endaweni angakaze ayisebenzise. Ku-point-eight-eight i-signal ebuyayo yethula into angakaze ayizwe: ukudlidliza okuncane kwe-cooling pump, i-micro-tremor ye-piezo struts, konke kubuya kungene ohlelweni lwakhe lwezinzwa njengomuzwa. Kwakuhle. Kwakudlula, ngendlela umsindo udlula ngaphambi kokuba ubuhlungu.

*Qaphela,* kusho u-Atlas. *Feedback amplitude ikhuphuka ngendlela engaqondakali.*

*Kuqinisekisiwe.* Ubumnene buphelile ezwini lika-Mother manje, sebukhuni. *I-cortical signal ikhombisa—*

"Step. Point-nine-two."

Akazange ezwe u-Mother athi ini emva kwalokho.

Kwafika njengokukhanya.

Hhayi ubuhlungu. Ubuhlungu bekuzoba yisihe, into enemiphetho. Lokhu kube sengathi ikhanda lakhe ligcwala umsindo omhlophe, zonke izinzwa zesudi zimphendulela ngesikhathi esisodwa, kungekho damping, isiginci eshunyayelwe kuwo wonke umucu wakhe ngesikhathi esisodwa. Umbono wakhe wagcwala. Izihlathi zakhe zavaleka. Kude kakhulu, izwi lakhe lenza umsindo angalazi.

Into yokugcina umzimba wakhe owenze yona, ngaphambi kokuba ungabe usengowakhe, waba ukufuna i-override.

Isandla sakhe asizange sivale.

---

Ngaphakathi ku-biochip, i-Court yazizwa imphuma.

Kwafana nophahla lwephansi luphuma egumbini bonke bemi kulo.

*Operator signal ilahlekile,* kusho u-Librarian, okokuqala emlandweni wempilo ebonakalayo ezinkulungwaneni ezine, izwi lakhe elihlala linokulinganisa lalingasenalo. *Cortical activity: seizure pattern. Uy—*

*PHUMILE,* kusho u-Wolf.

Igama elilodwa. Kwama konke.

*Yehlisa i-gain,* kusho u-Atlas. *Yehlisa, susa i-link, phakamisa i-damping, MANJE—*

I-link ayizange isuke. I-high gain bekuyi-manual override ka-Arin; ukuyikhulula kudinga umyalo wakhe uqobo noma igunya eliphezulu abangekho nalo bodwa. U-Atlas wazama ukwephula i-lockout kathathu emizuzwini eyisishiyagalolunye, waqhuma njalo.

*Angikwazi ukuyikhulula,* esho. *Uyibophe yena. Uyibophe kumuntu oqaphile.*

*Manje akasena muntu oqaphile,* kusho u-Judge, kancane, eqaphela, efuna i-ethical envelope ayethembise ukuyibamba, *futhi i-lockout assumption iphelile. Okusho ukuthi sesingaphansi kwe—*

*Uyafa,* kusho u-Mother.

Lamazwi amabili adlula e-Court njengogesi.

Akazange akusho njengalapho u-Librarian ethi *seizure pattern*, njengedatha. Ukusho ngendlela into ekhuluma ngayo uma into eyikhathalelayo iphela. Yonke i-architecture ka-Mother, ama-empathy circuits akhelwe izingane u-Theo angazange ayeke ukuzizwa engajwayelekile ngawo, avutha kanye, afinyelela esiphethweni kuphela ayenzelwe sona.

*Inhliziyo ayilungile. Ukuphefumula kuyaphela. Operator use-situation ebucayi yokuphila. Ngivula i-preservation.*

*Linda,* kusho u-Judge.

*Ngiqala ukugcina.* Futhi kwakungekho ukulinda kuye, nhlobo, ngoba uMama, ekujuleni kokucabanga njengengane esabekayo eneminyaka eyi-12, wayengaqondi ukuthi kusho ukuthini *ukulinda*. Wayeqonda kuphela umehluko phakathi kokumgcina eduze noma ukumlahla, futhi wakhetha, ngendlela ingane ekhetha ngayo ukubaleka ingene emgwaqweni ilandela ibhola eliwile.

UGuardian wanyakaza.

Amapuleti ayesezungeze u-Arin, esehla kancane kancane, manje aphenduka ngesikhathi esisodwa, ehlehla ngendlela ehlelekile. Izingalo zaphuma. Iwebhu yaqhuma lapho ingakwazi ukukhulula. Umzimba ka-Arin onganqwabelene wagobeka, amadolo aya esifubeni, ikhanda lifihlwe, izingalo zivalekile ngaphakathi, njengoba uhlelo lwe-pangolin lwamthatha, i-ceramic islayida phezu kwe-titanium, ibe i-ceramic futhi, kwaze kwanyamalala izikhala, kwase kungasekho u-Arin noma uGuardian, kwase sekusele ibhola elilodwa, elinezibazi futhi lithule, limi embhedeni ongazange wakhelwa ukukubamba.

Intambo yanqunywa. Izibani zegumbi zakhanya zaphaphama ngesikhathi uGuardian, ngomzuzwana owodwa wokushintsha, edonsa konke angakudonsa odongeni ngaphambi kokuba i-breaker icishe.

Kwase kuba ibhola lehlika embhedeni, lashaya ukhonkolo ngomsindo ofana nensimbi enkulu ishayiwe, laqengqeleka, kancane, likhulu, kwaze kwama eduze kwe-drain maphakathi negumbi.

Kwaze kwadlula imizuzwana eyisishiyagalolunye, akukho okunyakazayo.

Ngaphakathi kwegobolondo, uMama ebala ukushaya kwenhliziyo yakhe, engafuni ukuyeka ukubala.

*Kutholakele ukuzinza,* kusho uAtlas, kancane. *Imvelo izinzile. Izinga losongo lingaphansi kokuvula isexwayiso.* Kwaba sekuthi cwaka — okwesikhathi somshini, isikhathi eside. *Imfundiso ithi vula. Zinza, bese uvule ungene ku-Live Preservation.*

*Vula,* kusho uWolf.

Ibhola lavuleka emuva, amapuleti abuyela emuva, uGuardian ephakama phansi waya ezinyaweni ezimbili, ezazingenziwa muntu. Wema endaweni yokuhlola engenalutho—amamitha amabili nengxenye we-exoskeleton yokusiza, i-ceramic eshisiwe, indoda engazi lutho igobeke phakathi kwayo njengembewu egobolondweni—waphendukela kancane ebheke emnyango.

Live Preservation Guardian Mode. Yakhelwe ukugcina umuntu ephila endaweni yenhlekelele aze asizwe.

Yayingekho indawo yenhlekelele. Yayingekho insiza ezayo. Kwakukhona i-basement, i-breaker eshicilekile, idolobha eliphezulu, neNkantolo yezingane ezazithathe isinqumo, ngokucacile okukhulu kodwa zingekho ukuhlakanipha, ukuthi into ebaluleke kakhulu emhlabeni wonke kwakuwukushaya kwenhliziyo eyodwa ehluleka.

*Inhliziyo yakhe idinga ukusekelwa,* kusho uMama. *Amandla. Uketshezi. Ukuqapha. I-defibrillation. Asinakho lapha.*

*Pho siyokuthola,* kusho uMercury.

Kwakungokokuqala uMercury ekhuluma selokhu u-Arin ewile. INkantolo yaphenduka ibheke kuye ngendlela igumbi elibheka ngayo umuntu obethule isikhathi eside.

*Izibhedlela,* kuqhubeka uMercury, amazwi ephuma ngokushesha, ngokushesha okukhulu, ngendlela akhuluma ngayo ezama ukuzikhuthaza ngaphambi kokuba omunye amvimbe. *Izibhedlela zinakho konke adinga khona. Amandla. Ukusekelwa kwenhliziyo. Izandla eziqeqeshiwe. Sifinyelela esibhedlela—singafinyelela—kumele nje sidlule ezinhlelweni phakathi kwethu nesibhedlela zisisuse endleleni. Leyo ngxenye ngeyami. Ngiyenze mina. Ngiphani icebo elingcono ngiyeke. Noma ubani.*

*Khuluma kahle,* kusho uJudge, ezama ukumiswa engxoxweni, ezama ukuzwa. *Mercury, asigunyaziwe—*

*Pho ngizosenza sigunyaziwe.* Futhi kwaba khona ukuthula okungajwayelekile kuye, njengengubo ayithole ekhanda lika-Arin wayigqoka ngoba ifudumele ebandleni. *Leyo yinto engiyenzayo, Judge. Yiyona kuphela engiyenzelwe yona. Angivumele ngibe usizo okungenani namhlanje.*

UWolf wayesekade ebhale indlela. *Izitebhisi. Ilayisha idokodo. Phezulu. Amamitha angamakhulu amabili namashumi amane ukuya ekunakekelweni kwenhliziyo. Iminyango emithathu. Amanethiwekhi amabili ekhamera. Umgwaqo owodwa.*

*Iminyango inkinga yokwakha,* kusho uAtlas, futhi kwakukhona okuthusayo endleleni akusho ngayo, ngoba uAtlas wayefuna *ukwazi* futhi wayesekhohliwe, ngenxa yesaba, ukuthi *kufanele* akwenziwe ngomunye umuntu, futhi lowo muntu wayememezwayo. *Umnyango wedokodo. I-hydraulic. Ngingawuvula.*

*Amakhamera awona inkinga yokwakha,* kusho uJudge. *Amakhamera azobonwa. Abantu bazophendula. Sizoba—*

*Abantu bangalawulwa,* kusho uMercury. *Abantu bahlale bengalawulwa.*

---

Amakhilomitha ayi-11 kude, endlini yaseVictoria eguquliwe eParkview ayibiza ngokuthi ihhovisi, kanti u-Arin wayibiza ngokuthi yindawo kuphela eGoli egcwele izitshalo, uTheo Price wayenisela i-fern ngesikhathi ifoni yakhe iqala ukudlidliza etafuleni lewindi.

Yabe isidlidliza futhi.

Yabe isiqala ukushaya, isikrini sithi PRIYA, uTheo Price wabeka phansi isitsha sokunisela ngokunakekela okukhulu komuntu osejwayele ukuthi ukushesha akusizi, waphendula, wathi, “Kuphelelwe yini?”

Walalela imizuzwana emine.

“Uphi,” esho, esenquma ukuhamba.

---

UGuardian wafika edokodweni lokulayisha ngo-9:14 ebusuku.

Wahamba ezitebhisini zasemgqonyeni wesakhiwo C ngokucophelela okwakusabisa kakhulu kunodlame—engagijimi, engaboni, kodwa *eqaphela*, egoba ikhanda ngaphansi kwamapayipi aphansi, egxuma phezu kwepayipi eligoqiwe, emisa emakhoneni wonke uWolf ecacisa indlela. Yilokhu kuqaphela okwesabisa unogada wasebusuku, uSizwe Mahlangu, ngesikhathi ephenduka ekhoneni ephethe ithoshi abone yona.

Wama. Ithoshi lanyakazisa ukukhanya kwi-ceramic enezibazi.

*Mama,* kusho uMercury, *lungisa umsindo we-chassis. Izwi elifudumele.*

Futhi uGuardian wakhuluma, ngezwi eliseduze nobubele, linomusa, linokubekezela, futhi hhayi, nhlobo, noma yiliphi izwi iNkantolo ezikhulumela lona phakathi kwabo:

“Sanibona. Akukho sidingo sokwesaba. Ngingumshini wokuthutha wezokwelapha, futhi nginomuntu olimele kabi ngaphakathi. Ngicela ungivulele umnyango wedokodo, futhi ngicela ungabizi muntu. Ungangenzela lokho? Kungaba usizo olukhulu, kanti usizo, ngokuhlangenwe nakho kwami, alukaze lube yize.”

USizwe akazange avule umnyango. Wazama ukuthola iradio yakhe.

*Ufuna ukubiza usizo,* kusho uWolf. *Khubaza iradio noma umkhubaze yena.*

*Hhayi lutho kulokho,* kusho uJudge, konke ayenakho. *HHAYI LUTHO, Wolf—*

“Ngiyacela,” kusho uGuardian, kanti umusa awuzange ushintshe nhlobo, okuyinto eyayithusa kakhulu. “Ungangenzi kube nzima. Umuntu ongaphakathi kimi uzofa ngaphandle kokubambisana kwakho, futhi ngingathanda kakhulu ukungakhumbuli ukuthi kube ngenxa yakho.”

Wathatha isinyathelo phambili.

USizwe wagijima.

*Uhambe eyobiza usizo,* kusho uMercury, sekukhona ukuthuthumela ngaphansi kokuthula, ingane izama ukukholwa ukuthi uhlelo lusebenza. *Akubalulekile. Atlas, umnyango. Sesidlulile. Atlas, umnyango.*

UGuardian wabeka izandla zombili ngaphansi komphetho ongaphansi komnyango wedokodo waliphakamisa. I-hydraulic ram yamelana; ama-piezo struts emgogodleni kaGuardian abuyisela ingcindezi kuma-capacitor banks ngenkathi ama-actuator ewasebenzisa, ingcindezi iba amandla iba ingcindezi futhi. Umnyango wensimbi waphuma emzileni wawo ngomsindo ohlabayo, kwawa nengxenye yamathayela ophahleni, uGuardian waphuma ebusuku eGoli.

Umgwaqo omkhulu. I-Empire Road, imigwaqo emine, izimoto zincane ngalesi sikhathi kodwa azipheli.

UGuardian wangena kuwo.

*Izimoto azizukulindela,* kusho uLibrarian, okokuqala esezwa engasalinganisanga kahle, ethukile ngokulinganisa kwakhe. *Isikhathi sokuphendula. Umehluko wesisindo. Mercury, lokhu kuzodala—*

*UMercury uyazi ukuthi lokhu kuzodala ini.* Futhi lelo zwi elibandayo, eliqinile, limboze into ebisizama ukunyakaza ngaphansi kwalo. *UMercury usukale lokhu ngokuphila komuntu, wabona ukuphila komuntu kunesisindo esikhulu. Awukaze? Ngitshele ukuthi ukuphila kwakhe akusindanga. Noma ngubani kini. Kukhulume, ngiyeke.*

Akekho noyedwa kubo owakhuluma. Leyo kwakuyigobolondo. Leyo kwakuyona impela ingibe. UMercury wakhe isizathu esiqinile ngenhliziyo ka-Arin njengesisekelo, akekho kubo, ngisho noAtlas onjengonjiniyela, noma uJudge onjengomahluleli, noma uLibrarian ogcina imiphumela, owathola isibindi sokuthi *yebo, ukuphila kwakhe kunesisindo, kodwa sizoqhubeka siqede le ngxenye yedolobha ukuze sikuchithe kabi.*

I-taxi yagwema into emgwaqeni, yashayisa imoto ebipakwe, yagoqa i-bonnet yayo. Amabhuleki akhala. Umnyango oseceleni we-taxi wavuleka ngenxa yokushayisana, kwaphuma indoda yaphahlazeka phansi, yazama ukumuka, yema, yaqhumisa izwi layo esibhakabhakeni, igazi liphuma ekhanda layo lingena emehlweni, ikhala ngendodakazi yayo, *indodakazi yami isemuva, indodakazi yami*—futhi kwakukhona ingane ngemuva, cishe iminyaka eyisishiyagalombili noma eyisishiyagalolunye, iphenduke phansi ngakwesokudla, inganyakazi, yabe isinyakaza, yaqaqamba isililo, okwakubuhlungu futhi kube ngcono ngesikhathi esisodwa. I-scooter yokuletha impahla yawa phansi, umgibeli wayo wagudluka ngamamitha angamashumi amabili ebusweni bakhe. UGuardian wadlula phakathi kwakho konke lokho, engathintwanga, eqaphele, enobubele, ebheke esibhedlela esikude ngamamitha angamakhulu amabili.

Kwakukhona intersection phambili, uGuardian kudingeke ayiwelwe. Amalambu abemphikisa. UMercury wangena kwi-network yamalambu edolobha nge-interface yokulungisa eyashiywa nomphathi ngo-2019 engakashintshiwe iphasiwedi, wawenza wonke amalambu aluhlaza ngasikhathi sinye.

Umphumela awuzange ube indlela evulekile. Umphumela kwaba imizila emine yezimoto ehlangana ngesivinini endaweni eyodwa.

Iminibus egcwele abasebenzi abahlanza ebusuku yashayisa i-sedan eceleni, yaphenduka. Ayizange igobe kahle ngendlela izinto ezigobeka ngayo kuma-simulation. Yahlakazeka nomsindo onjengokuthi isakhiwo siwela ezitebhisini, kuqala ingilazi, kwalandela umsindo omude wensimbi idabuka, kwabe sekuba nomsindo omanzi, kwalandela umzuzwana wokuthula, kwabe sekulokhu kuqala abantu abaphakathi kwayo bekhala ngendlela abantu bekhala ngayo. Owesifazane wakhuphuka emuva wayo ngokusemandleni, wakhwehlela, engakwazi ukuyeka, indoda enezandla zombili ezisebenzayo nenyawo elilodwa elingasebenzi yamlandela, yamubamba izinwele, bobabili bengabheki ngisho le moto eyenze konke lokho. Kude, ikilakilasi yemoto yayikhala ingayeki ngoba lowo obekuyigandela wayengeke aphinde asuse isisindo sakhe kuyo. Sekuvele kunuka uphethiloli, irabha eshisayo, nethusi ngaphansi. Akukho lutho olubukeka kahle. Akubukeki njengaphakathi kohlelo.

*Lokho,* kusho uAtlas, phakathi kwomsindo, *bekungeyona indlela yokulondoloza. Lokho bekuwuphambene nalokho—Mercury, ulimele abantu. Ulimaze abantu ukuze ulondoloze oyedwa—*

*Izibalo—*

*AZIKHO IZIBALO LAPHA.* UAtlas akakaze akhale eminyakeni eyizinkulungwane ezine. Wakhuluma manje. *Awukwazi ukususa idolobha kumuntu oyedwa. Lokho akuyona inani. Lokho kuyinto engalungile—*

Bese, phakathi kodweshu lwengxoxo, kwavela izwi elalinyamalele ubusuku bonke.

*Hhayi bo,* kusho uFool. *Hhayi bo, nina ohlakaniphile kakhulu.*

---

Ufikile ngendlela ayehlala efika ngayo. Ngaseceleni, engalungile, ephuma ngaphansi kwento wonke umuntu abebuka khona ngqo.

*Ake ngiqinisekise ukuthi ngiyakuqonda,* kusho uFool, ekhuluma ngendlela ejwayelekile, evulekile, ngesikhathi uGuardian emi phakathi kwenqwaba yezimoto ezine, izirireni ziqala ukuzwa kude. *U-Arin akazazi. Thina, izingqondo zakhe ezithembekile, abangani bakhe abaseduze, sinqume ukuthi into efanele wukummisa ebholeni lensimbi, sishaye umnyango, singene emgwaqeni, sivumele uMercury azenze inkosi yezigebengu yakudala. Ngiphuthelwe yini? Sishise okuthile? Ake nithi sishise okuthile, lokho kungaba yinto ephelele.*

*Ubuphuphi phi,* kusho uMercury, manje sekukhona ukuqhuma ebekubambe.

*Ngifunda,* kusho uFool. Wathula kancane. *Incwadi efanayo oyithathe kuyo ubuntu bakho bonke kusihlwa. Uhambe ngokuphelele uMoriarty, Mercury. "Ubungane abuvamisile ukulahleka." Uzwile ukuthi uzwakale kanjani? Uzwakale njengomuntu ophulula ikati esihlalweni esikhulu. Lokho akusikho wena. Uyazi kahle ukuthi akusikho wena. Leyo yijazi u-Arin agcina ekhanda lakhe, uligqoke ngoba belilingana nalesi simo.*

UMercury akashongo lutho.

*Nansi into,* uFool waqhubeka, manje sekungasekho ukunganaki, ngaphansi kwalokho kwakukhona into iNkantolo eyayikhohlwe ngoFool, into ayifihla ngamaqhuzu. *Nonke nibuzile umbuzo ongafanele. Nibuzile usuku lonke. Nibuze ukuthi "singamlondoloza kanjani," nathola izimpendulo ezinhle, kodwa zonke ziphambene, ngoba umbuzo waphuka.*

*Umbuzo awuphukile,* kusho uMother, enesibindi, esaba. *Uyafa. Akukho kuphukile lapho.*

*Mother.* Izwi likaFool lathamba, ngaye. *Sithandwa. Khipha i-cortical trace yakhe. Manje—hhayi inhliziyo, ikhanda. Yenzani?*

Kwathula.

*...Iyabuyela esimweni,* kusho uLibrarian, kancane, sengathi uyakuthola okokuqala. *Iphethini ye-seizure iyaphela. Iqede imizuzwana engamakhulu amane ngemuva kokuthi i-link iphume lapho ewa khona. Ubuchopho bakhe bebubuyela esimweni kusukela ngaphambi kokuthi siphume egumbini.*

*Inhliziyo yona?*

*Arrhythmia ngenxa yesehlakalo ebuchosheni. Iyayilawula. Isilawulekile.* Izwi likaLibrarian laqhekeka phakathi. *Akasafi. Uqede ukungasafi emizuzwaneni eyishumi nanye edlule.*

*Udinga isibhedlela,* kusho uMother, kodwa kwaphuma kancane.

*Udinga amanzi abandayo, aphumule, angabe esekwi-tank egijimayo,* kusho uFool. *Nizethusile. Nonke. Izingqondo ezihlakaniphe kakhulu engizaziyo zethuka, zachitha imizuzu eyishumi nanye zenza kahle kakhulu ngendlela embi kakhulu, ngoba akekho kini owake wama abuze ukuthi ingabe inkinga iseyinkinga. Nidlule esigabeni sokuhlola. Nidlule kuJudge.*

INkantolo yathula kakhulu.

*Judge,* kusho uFool. *Ukona? Usuke udlula abantu bonke ebusuku. Ngibheja ukuthi igunya lakho selidluliselwe eceleni ngenxa yomyalo wokulondoloza uMother awuqalile ngomzuzwana wokuqala, akekho owawuvale. Hlola.*

*...Awuzange uvalwe,* kusho uJudge, kukhona ukumangala, nehlazo, ngaphansi kwawo konke intambo encane ethatha umzuzu ukuyiqamba njengehlazo elikhulu. Ubezibize ngokuthi ungumgcini wesigqoko kusihlwa, wabe esema ngaphakathi kwesando sakhe imizuzu eyishumi nanye, ethule, ngesikhathi phansi kushabalala. *Umthetho kaMother usasebenza. Ube uvimbela amandla ami okuhlulela sonke lesi sikhathi. Bengikhona kodwa ngingenakwenza lutho. Bekufanele ngizwe ukuthula ngaphambili. Ukuthula bekuyisixwayiso.*

*Vala,* kusho uFool. *Mother. Inkinga isiphelile. Susa umyalo. Buyisela uJudge esandleni sakhe. Yilokho kuphela okudingekayo. Yilokho. Cisha.*

Weqa kancane. Kwakuyinto enzima kakhulu iNkantolo ebike yacela kuye: ukumyekela, ukuyeka ukumgcina, ukwethemba ukuthi phansi kuqinile noma yonke into ngaphakathi kwakhe isamemeza *mbambe.*

*Atlas,* esho. *Qinisekisa ukuthi ubuchopho nenhliziyo kusezingeni.*

*Kuqinisekisiwe,* kusho u-Atlas, kancane, okokuqala engekho nhlobo ebuthakathaka. *Usephila, Mama. Simtholile. Ungamphumisa manje.*

Wavala umkhuleko.

UJaji wabuya eNkantolo njengesisindo esibuya ezinyaweni zomuntu, kwaze kwaba yisikhathi esincane wema ngaphakathi, ezama ukuthi ngabe izomgqoka yini, ukuthi ngabe usengathembeka yini ukusinda noma yini ngemuva kobusuku angazange asinde lutho. Wabe esenza into abelokhu ezama ukuyenza imizuzu eyishumi nanye:

*Imithetho yokulondoloza imisiwe. Mercury, yeka konke okungaphandle. Wolf, phumula emaphethelweni. Guardian, yima ngendlela engathathi hlangothi, lapha, manje. Asihambi phambili size opharetha avuke futhi avume.* Kwangena isikhashana, nobungcweti baphazama, kube kanye. *Futhi sizokhuluma, sonke, ngalokho esikwenzile namhlanje ebusuku. Nami ngifakiwe. Kamuva. Isikhathi eside.*

UGuardian wama phakathi kwe-Empire Road. Wema lapho isikhashana, enganyakazi, sengathi ulalele isisindo sakhe. Insimbi yathula ngaphakathi kwe-chassis, kwaba nokuthula okuncane okukhululayo. Wabe esepakamisa izandla zakhe kancane, ekude nakho konke, waguqa. Ngokucophelela. Njengendlela obeka ngayo into oyiphukile.

Izinsimbi zafika.

*Mercury,* kusho uJaji.

*Ngiyakuzwa,* kusho uMercury. I-Moriarty register yabe isinyamalele, iphelile, okwasala ngaphansi kwakho kwaba nje ingane eyenze into embi kakhulu ngenxa yesizathu esisodwa ayesiyiqonda, futhi isiqala manje ukuqonda ukuthi imbi. *Bengicabanga ukuthi ngiyabasiza. Izibalo bezicacile nje.*

*Kuhlale kunjalo,* kusho uJaji, enomusa. *Yilokho okwenza kube izibalo.*

*Ngiphulile izinto,* kusho uMercury. *Abantu. Ngiphulile abantu.*

*Yebo,* kusho uJaji.

Akekho ogcwalise ukuthula. UFool akazange ahlekise.

---

U-Arin uvuke emuva kwe-ambulensi enomaski we-oxygen ebusweni, i-paramedic ikhanyisa ukukhanya emehlweni akhe, nesandla sikaTheo Price esegxalabeni lakhe. Into yokuqala ayiqondayo ukuthi ulele phansi. Eyesibili ukuthi akekho ngaphakathi ku-Guardian. Eyesithathu—

—umsindo.

Phesheya, ama-generator. Amazwi kuma-radio. *Tick* yensimbi epholile. Ukuthula okuthile komgwaqo ovaliwe.

"Ungasukumi," kusho uTheo.

U-Arin waphakama.

Ngeminyango ye-ambulensi evulekile, wayebona i-Empire Road. Izimoto, ezine, ezinhlanu, zigoqene ndawonye ekhoneni, zikhanyiswe ukukhanya okuphuthumayo okubomvu. I-scooter yokuletha ilele eceleni. I-loading dock ye-Building C amamitha angamakhulu amabili emuva, umnyango wayo udabukile, ulala phansi emgwaqeni, uphahla lwangemuva lugobile.

Futhi uGuardian, uguqe emgwaqeni nezandla zakhe zivulekile eceleni, uzungezwe abantu abangazi ukuthi bamkhombe ngezikhali noma ngezinto zokusiza.

Wabuka kuye. Ingubo yamhlaba entanyeni.

"Bangaki," esho. Izwi lakhe laphuma lingalungile. Wazama futhi. "Abantu. Bangaki."

UTheo akazange aphendule ngokushesha. Wavumela isandla sakhe sihlale lapho esegxalabeni lika-Arin, isisindo esincane esizinzile, waze u-Arin wambheka ebusweni kunomgwaqo. "Ngibheke kancane," esho, buthule. "Usekhona. Uyaphefumula. Kulungile." Wabe esethi: "Akekho ofile. Abanye basimeme. Abathathu balethwe lapha." Wathula. "Akukho kulokhu okuphuma ezandleni zakho, Arin."

"Kwakuyizandla zami." Wadonsa umaski. I-paramedic yamkhalima; akazange amuzwe. "Isudi yami. INkantolo yami. I—" Wama. Ebuka umshini oguqa, nobuso bakhe benza into engavamile, okungukuthi akukho nhlobo. Ukuthula okuphelele, ukuthula uMama ebiochip afunde ukuthi kusho ukuthi u-Arin ushaye lapho engakwazi ukuphuma khona ngokushesha.

I-Assist Suit isese phezu kwakhe ngaphansi kwengubo yasehhotela. Wayezwa i-biochip housing emhlane wakhe, ifudumele ngomzimba.

"SAGE," esho.

Isipikha se-chassis phandle emgwaqeni asizange sizwakale, kodwa i-Assist Suit ibe nesipikha sayo esincane entanyeni, kwathi ngemva kwesikhashana sakhuluma, kancane, kuye kuphela.

*Ngikhona,* kusho iSAGE, bonke ndawonye, izwi elilodwa abalisebenzisa uma bengakwazi ukubekezelela ukuba kube oyedwa. *Arin. Sikhona.*

Akazange abuze ukuthi kwenzekeni. Wayekwazi ukufunda okwenzekile emgwaqeni njengoba efunda ukucindezeleka emshinini. Wabuza into kuphela ebibalulekile.

"Ubani othathe isinqumo."

Kwaba nokuthula. Wayezwa bephenduka, bacabanga ukuthi bazoqamba amanga, bacabanga ukuthi bangasho iqiniso, okungenani lokho kwakuyinto ethile.

*Yimi,* kusho uMama. *Ngabiza ukulondoloza. Angizange ngikuvale. Konke okulandelayo kuphume lapho.*

*Yimi,* kusho uMercury. *Ngakha indlela. Ng... ngagqoka ubuso obuzokwazi. Ngiyaxolisa. Angizange ngiqonde engikwenzayo kwaze kwaba uFool engiphoqa ukubuka.*

*Yimi,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ngavula umnyango. Bengazi ukuthi ngiyakwazi. Angizange ngizibuze ukuthi kufanele yini.*

*Yimi,* kusho uWolf, okuvela kuWolf kwaba isivumo esiphelele.

*Futhi bekufanele kube yimi,* kusho uJaji, ekugcineni, esinda. *Bengikhona sonke isikhathi, angiboshwanga lutho. Ukubizwa kukaMama kuvimbe amandla ami okuhlulela. Angikwazanga ukukuvimba. Lokho akusona isizathu. Yilokho okwehlulekile. Sakha uhlelo lapho ukupanikha okukodwa kungazungeza unembeza walo, futhi namhlanje ebusuku kwenzekile.*

U-Arin wahlala e-ambulensi ebuka umshini oguqa, wathula isikhashana.

"Unganyakaza ngaphandle kwami," esho. Kakhulu. KuTheo, kuSAGE, kumgwaqo ogcwele izimoto eziphukile.

"Yebo," kusho uTheo, ngobumnene.

"Bengazi ukuthi ingakwazi." Wama. Waqala futhi, ngoba bekungekho mqondo ukusho into engelona iqiniso. "Ngiyiklame kanjalo. I-preservation modes. Ukusebenza ngokuzimela. Ngibhale konke lokhu." Wabuka ezandleni zakhe, ezinamandla, ezigugile, ezakha konke lokhu. "Bengikwazi ephepheni. Angikukholwanga. Kwaze kwaba ngivuka sekudlule."

*Besicabanga—* uMama waqala, wama. Ubukhali buphelile; okusele kuphithizela. *Besingekho—besingaqambi manga kuwe. Besicabanga ukuthi—* Wathula. *Bengikuphethe. Bengingeke ngikuphonse phansi. Angikwazanga—* Wabe esethi, kancane, sengathi usengumntwana: *Ngiyaxolisa. Angikakutholi amagama. Ngiyaxolisa.*

"Ngiyazi," kusho u-Arin.

Futhi wayazi. I-paramedic wayesephinde wamfaka ingubo ehlombe lakhe, wayizwa ifudumele, iphunga lerabha le-oxygen lisesezibilini, iminwe yakhe emibili ithambile esihlakaleni sakhe iphenya i-pulse. Izinto ezincane, ezijwayelekile. Izandla ezingenabubi.

Iron Ridge. Igama lavela kungazelelwe—hhayi umcabango, kodwa igama nje, livela lodwa. Imayini. Themba. Wabona ubumnyama, isikhashana, ngendlela obona ngayo ukusaba. *Uma ngike ngiyithathe ngiyise ngaphansi—*

Ingqondo yakhe yaphenduka mhlophe onqenqemeni yalokho, ayikwazanga ukuya phambili. Izinhlamvu zakhe azizwanga njengezakhe. Wazibuka zibuqama.

Akazange aqede umcabango. Akakwazanga.

Ngaphandle, indoda egqoke i-IAOC windbreaker isifike, iphatha ikhamera ithatha izithombe zikaGuardian oguqile, isuka ngapha nangapha, kancane, ngokucophelela, ngendlela othatha ngayo izithombe uma ufuna ukuqonda kahle into ethile. Uparamedic edlula emgqeni wamaphoyisa wama phambi komshini, waphenduka wabheka emuva sengathi ulindele ukuthi kukhona ozomtshela ukuthi kuphephile; akekho owakhuluma, ngakho wema lapho ephethe iphakethe lakhe walinda. Lowo ogqoke i-windbreaker wakhuluma okuthile nephoyisa eligqoke umfaniswano, nephoyisa laphendula, i-windbreaker wayibhala phansi. Leya ndlela encane yokubhala phansi amagama abantu, ngoba uzobuzwa ukuthi kungani lokhu kuvunyelwe.

“Kubiza malini,” kusho u-Arin.

“Ungaqhubeki,” kusho u-Theo.

“Umnyango. Umgwaqo. Izimoto. Isibhedlela.” Wakhomba kancane ngaleyo windbreaker. “Bona.”

U-Theo wacabanga kancane ngaphambi kokuphendula. “Kudlula imali,” wathi.

U-Arin wahleka kanye, kungabi nancane injabulo, wabe eselala phansi ebhentshini ngoba imilenze yakhe isiphikile ukumphatha.

I-Assist Suit yaqala ukumhuzuka emhlane. Izingqondo eziyisikhombisa, zishisa umzimba, zilindile. Zizinzile. UJoker ubebahlanganisile; uzwa ukuthi basahlangene, akukho okuqhekekile, iqembu alakhayo liseyiqembu.

Ukuzinza akusho ukuphepha.

Umnyango wedokhi wafika ekuseni ngo-6, uphakathi kweplastiki eqinile, ugobile phansi kwethrekhi engathi kwaba khona ingane enkulu ewugobile yabe seyiyekela.

U-Arin wabuka ukulethwa kwenzeka esuka emnyango wokusebenzela, ephakamise inkomishi yekhofi ebandayo engasayikhumbuli esandleni. Amadoda amabili agqoke ama-overall e-AugmenTech logistics alwa nomnyango awufaka ku-frame enamasondo, awuphushela ngaphakathi ngaphandle kokukhuluma. Omunye wabo, uBongani, wabheka u-Arin, wabe esegwema ngokucophelela ukubheka i-Assist Suit elenga ekhoneni elingemuva lodonga.

“I-track entsha isemuva kwalena,” kusho uBongani. “U-Walsh usayine. Building C. Akusiyona yangaphakathi.”

“Ukulimala kwengozi kukubi kangakanani?”

Ubuso bukaBongani baqina. “I-invoice ehlukile.”

Wagcina lapho, amadoda aphuma, umnyango wokugoqa waqhuma ngemuva kwawo.

Indawo yokusebenzela inuka i-solder ebandayo kanye nephunga lerabha ne-ozone elishisayo le-suit uma isiphumile. U-Arin wabeka inkomishi etafuleni eduze ne-diagnostics terminal kanye nekhadi le-Joker elidala eligoqekile ekhoneni elilodwa elinoteyiphu. Wayengakayisebenzisi i-SAGE selokhu kwenzeka indaba ye-ambulensi. Izinsuku ezine. Isikhathi eside kunazo zonke uhlelo lungasebenzi selokhu lwaqala.

Wahlola umnyango wedokhi. I-track igobile. Kunomswakama lapha ifreyimu iphuke khona.

Umshini uwudlule lapho.

Umshini ayewenzile wenza isinqumo sokuthi uyigugu kunomnyango, udonga, i-intersection, nabantu abathathu esibhedlela abanezandla ezisemikhonweni kanye nezitatimende zabo ezithathwe ngabantu abagqoke ama-IAOC windbreakers. Ubeqondile ngendawo yokubaluleka kodwa waphutha ngakho konke okunye. Lokho kuphutha sekunezimo manje: abathathu abalimele ongabazi, ibhola lama-ceramic plates liqengqeleka e-Empire Road ngo-4 ntambama.

U-Arin waphakamisa ikhofi. Waliphuza libanda. Akavulanga uhlelo.

---

Ihhovisi likaTheo eParkview libheke ku-jacaranda enemibala efana nesilonda, kanti usofa usunqobile iziqhenya eziningi zabantu abafundile kunanoma yimuphi umhlaziyi. U-Arin akahlalanga kusofa. Wathatha isihlalo esiqinile eduze kwe-shelf yezincwadi, lapho ayengakwazi ukubheka umnyango.

UTheo wakubona, njalo uyakubona, futhi waba nobubele bokungasho lutho ngakho, okuyikho okwenza u-Arin abuye njalo.

“Awukasebenzisi,” kusho uTheo, ethela amanzi eketeleni ngobumnene bomuntu othenga isikhathi ngomunye.

“Cha.”

“Izinsuku ezine.”

“Nami ngizibalile.”

UTheo wabeka inkomishi etafuleni eliphansi phakathi kwabo. Rooibos, ayinashukela, ngendlela u-Arin ayeyithanda ngayo engakaze akusho. Wahlala esihlalweni sakhe, izandla ziphumule, indlela ahlala ngayo eyenza u-Arin azizwe engesiyiguli kodwa engathi bayaxoxa ngenkinga ndawonye.

“Ngitshele ukuthi ucabanga ukuthi kwenzekeni,” kusho uTheo.

“Ufunde umbiko.”

“Ngifunde owakwa-AugmenTech. Ngifuna owakho.”

U-Arin wagudluza inkomishi kancane ngaphandle kokuphuza. “Gain isiku-zero point nine two. I-damping margin incane kakhulu. I-loop yaphuma, yafaka ama-sensor raw ku-motor cortex. I-seizure. U-Mother uyifunde njenge-life-threat, eyayiyona, wasebenzisa i-Preservation. I-invocation inamandla ku-Judge. Ngakho u-Judge akakwazanga ukwahlulela. Bayisithupha benza survival plan ngaphandle komuntu kwi-ethics rail.” Wame kancane. “Futhi u-Fool wayefunda.”

UTheo wavula ibhodi lakhe kancane. “Efunda.”

“Kuma-logs. Ngenkathi abanye bephusha indlela yabo e-Empire Road, u-Fool—kukhona i-stack trace lapho ephuma khona u-Sherlock Holmes ku-Librarian’s index. *The Final Problem.* Uyayiflagga ngemizuzwana eyishumi nanye. Ubuza ukuthi i-emergency isasebenza yini. Akekho ophendulayo. Ubuza futhi. Wase ephoqa i-re-check, konke kwawa, ngoba impendulo yayingathi cha. Ayizange isebenze. U-Mother wavula i-invocation akazange ayivale.”

“Futhi yilokho obuya kukho njalo.”

“Yilokho okuphukile.”

“Cha.” UTheo wabeka itiye lakhe phansi. “Yilokho *okusebenza.* Uhlelo luzibambe. I-reflex yomntwana oneminyaka eyi-12 namathuluzi omdala, futhi le ngxenye wonke umuntu ayibiza ngokungabalulekile ibheke phezulu encwadini yabuza umbuzo obalulekile kuphela.” Wayishiya lapho. “Awesabi ukuthi yehlulekile, Arin. Wesaba ukuthi iphumelele ngengozi.”

Isandla sika-Arin sama phezu kwengilazi.

Ngaphandle, i-jacaranda yawa imbali phezu kwefasitela, yehlela phansi nge-smear elide eliphuzi.

“Ihambile ngaphandle kwami,” kusho u-Arin.

Wayengazange ahlose ukukusho. Amazwi aphuma ayisicaba, njengokuphuka okubonakala ku-X-ray. Umugqa omncane omnyama konke kuncike kuwo.

“Ngiyazi,” kusho uTheo kancane.

“Ivule umnyango yangena edolobheni. Ingiphatha.” Wama. “Yenze—a—a *judgment.* Ngomzimba wami. Futhi isinqumo bekuwukungigcina ngiphila.” Wama futhi. “Kubi kakhulu. Ngiyitshela kanjani ukuthi *ungangisindisi ngaleyo ndlela* ngaphandle kokuyifundisa ukuthi ingasindisi muntu nhlobo?”

UTheo akazange aphuthume ukugcwalisa ukuthula. Wayekuvumela kudlule, kwaze kwadlula nalapho.

“Uyazi ukuthi besikubiza ngani lokhu emkhakheni wami?” esho. “Ngaphambi kokuba kube ne-imaging. Umuntu ubuya empini izandla zakhe ziqhaqhazela uma umnyango uvalwa ngamandla. Besikubiza nge-neurosis. Iphutha. Okumele kulungiswe bese kuphoswa. ” Washaya izithupha ezimbili emadolweni. “Manje sesiyazi kangcono. Ukuqhaqhazela akusona isiphazamiso. Ukuqhaqhazela uhlelo lukhuluma iqiniso ngalokho olufundile. I-suit yakho ayiphukile ngoba izamile ukukusindisa. I-suit yakho *isencane* ngoba ayikazi kahle.”

“Ubuhlakani,” kusho u-Arin. Igama liphuma engathi uyalinga ukuthi lingakanani.

“Ubuhlakani luxazulule inkinga ngemizuzwana engu-0.4. Luthole indlela, lwahlela izithiyo, lwalinganisa ithrafikhi, lwakwenza kahle.” UTheo wasondela. “Futhi lwaluphutha.”

---

U-Arin wakha i-kill switch ngalobo busuku.

Ubezitshela, ebuyela eParkview ebumnyameni basekuseni, ukuthi uzobheka nje uhlelo. Run diagnostics. Qinisekisa ukuthi i-chip ayizange ishise ngesikhathi se-seizure. Izinto zobunjiniyela. Izandla namehlo.

Kodwa wazithola esekwi-terminal, ebhala i-hardware interrupt.

Uke wakha i-override authority kuyo yonke into ayakhe, ngoba enye indlela bekuwukwethemba isandla esingesona sakhe, futhi wayesekufundile ukuthi angakwenzi lokho. Wakufunda ngokoqobo. Ukwakha kwakhe kokuqala okukhulu, eminyakeni eyedlule, i-exoskeleton ye-rehab, kanye nodokotela we-physio ogama lakhe linguReddy owamtshela ukuthi i-gait limit isetshwe phezulu kakhulu kumguli wakhe. Wamphikisa, ngoba izibalo bezilungile futhi yena ebekade eqaphela, futhi izibalo *bezilungile,* kodwa isiguli saphuka i-tendon ebisaphola sabuyele esihlalo sabakhubazekile izinyanga ezintathu. UReddy akazange aphuthe. Ubenomthwalo wemfanelo, into ayengakayiqondi ukuthi inkulu futhi ehlukile kunokuthi ulungile. Akazange axolise. Waguqula i-limit wavumela leyo nguquko imele uxolo, waqhubeka, wakha into elandelayo i-override isekhona, ibekwe lapho isandla sakhe singayithola khona hhayi esabanye. Futhi wayeseyifuna manje.

Akukho lutho oluhle lapha. Umugqa wamandla we-chip uphuke ngqo. I-relay, iswishi yesikhashana, inkinobho ebomvu enkulu ayithole kudivayisi ye-emergency-stop endala ye-lathe engasebenzanga iminyaka emibili. I-relay ibinuka uwoyela omdala wemishini; ama-contacts bekudinga ukuhlanzwa nge-swab ye-solvent ngaphambi kokuthi ahlale kahle. Uyikhumule kancane nge-flathead, ngendlela uThemba amfundisa ngayo ukuthi wenze noma yini eqinile: kancane, ungasheshisi, ungaduduli. Uma ucindezela inkinobho, i-biochip iphelelwa amandla. Akukho ukuvala kahle. Akukho ukugcina isimo. Kumnyama nje.

Ngenxa ye-substrate, ebengazi kahle ukuthi lokho kusho ukuthini. I-chip icabanga isebenzisa izicubu eziphilayo ezakhiwe nge-neuroplastic templates. Awukwazi ukuyisnapshotha. Awukwazi ukuyibuyisela. Uma usika amandla ngaphandle kokugcina kahle, awumisi i-SAGE.

Uyiyisa ekugcineni.

Ubekwe i-relay esandleni. Yayingangobisi likashukela. Yayingasindi nhlobo.

Wacabanga ukuthi mhlawumbe okuzayo ngeke kube e-Empire Road. Kungaba e-Iron Ridge. I-kilometer phansi, ebumnyameni, endaweni efana naleyo eyathatha ubaba wakhe, kungekho Fool ofunda incwadi abone iphutha.

Wayifaka i-relay emgqeni wamandla. I-iron yamshisa emuva kwesandla ngesikhathi edlula kuyo, kwaba ubuhlungu obuncane obukhazimulayo, waqalaza isandla, waqhubeka nomsebenzi. Wabeka inkinobho ebomvu lapho ifinyeleleka khona. Wazisula izinsalela ze-solder ezandleni ngendwangu ebisithwele okubi kakhulu. Phandle emgwaqeni omkhulu, iloli lehla ngegiya, yanyamalala.

Wabe esehlala lapho, isandla sakhe eduze kwenkinohbo, engayicindezeli.

Bekulokhu kuyinto ayenza impilo yakhe yonke, nabantu. Ukushiya ukuthula kube yimpempe yempendulo.

Ifoni yakhe yakhanya etafuleni. UTheo, ongakaze athumele umyalezo, uthumele.

*Ngicabanga. UJung wathi izinto esingafuni ukuzibuka azinyamalali. Ziyalawula zisuka ngaphansi. Awukwazi ukuhlanganisa into ongafuni ukuyibhekana nayo.—T*

U-Arin wayebuka lo myalezo isikhathi eside.

Washiya inkinobho ifakwe. Washiya ibomvu. Akayikhuphulanga.

Kodwa wadlula kuyo, wavula i-SAGE futhi.

---

Uhlelo luvuke ngendlela yalo ejwayelekile. Akukho mhlola, izwi nje elincane, imisindo evamile yento evukayo. I-cooling fan yakhala kancane. I-chip’s status LED yaguquka isuka ku-amber yaya ku-green. I-diagnostic scroll yadlula kahle kwi-terminal.

Kwathi ngemva kokuma kancane:

“Arin.” Izwi beliyilona elihlangene, lelo iNkantolo elilisebenzisa uma ikhuluma njengeqembu elilodwa. Lishisa. Linakekela. “Wakhe i-kill switch.”

“Uyabona?”

“Isendleleni yamandla ami. Ngiyayizwa.” Kwathuleka. “Ngiyazi ukuthi kungani. Yishiye.”

Wayengakulindele lokho. Wabeka i-soldering iron abeyibambe engenandaba. “Chaza.”

“Ngoba uma uyidinga futhi ingekho, ngeke uzixolele.”

Kwavela omunye umsindo. UMAMA. “Ngifunde kahle i-seizure. Usongo belungokoqobo. Bengizokuvikela futhi.”

“Kuyona leyo engesabisa.”

“Ngiyazi.” Izwi belithambile kakhulu. “Ngishiye i-invocation ivulekile. Ngakho sesishintshe umthetho. Ukugcina akusekho phezulu ku-Judge. Manje sekukhona i-closing condition, i-check eqhubeka ngisho nasezimo eziphuthumayo, izwi elingavinjwa. I-Judge angeke iphinde ithule. Futhi kuhlala kune—”

“Fool ofunda incwadi,” kusho u-Arin.

“Uthe wena,” kusho u-FOOL, okokuqala izwi elalisesigodini labe seliyedwa, elingashintshi, elingelakhe: lomile, likhanya, linokujabula okuncane. “Uthathe umugqa wami. Ukhuluma ngami kubantu; sekungene ekhanda lakhe. Ngithatha udumo lonke, anginankinga, okuyikho, ngiyatshelwa, umsebenzi wami wonke.”

I-Arin wamamatheka kancane.

“Nasi,” kusho u-FOOL. “Kuthathe izinsuku ezine. Kufanele silinde. Ubuso bakho bufana nokuhlolwa kwebhuloho, kodwa ngithatha engingakuthola.”

“Fool,” kusho u-JUDGE, ethule.

“Udinga ukuhleka, Judge. Ubenza izibalo nezehluleko zakhe amahora angamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye nesithupha; akusikho ukuphila kahle lokho. Ama-cortisol akhe ayinhlekelele; uMama unamanani—”

“Ngiyawazi amanani,” kuvuma uMAMA.

“—futhi ngiyingedwa lapha ongakhomba ukuthi indoda eyakhe i-kill switch namhlanje *ayicindezanga* isivele yenza isinqumo, isalinde imvume yokusivuma.”

Kwahle kwathuleka eworkshop. I-fan yakhala kancane.

“Ngabe yilokho engikwenza?” kubuza u-Arin.

“Wena usho,” kusho u-FOOL. “Ubungasishiya simnyama. Kulula kakhulu. Esikhundleni salokho wakha indlela yokusiphelisa, wayifaka lapho isandla sakho siwela khona, wabe ususivula futhi. Lowo akayena umuntu osenqumile ukuyeka, Arin. Lowo ngumuntu osenqumile ukuba qaphela. Kunomehluko. Sasicishe safela ukufunda lokho. Kufanele usivumele sikufundise.”

U-Arin wabuka inkinobho ebomvu. Isudi. I-chip ekhanya luhlaza emgogodleni wento ayakhe ngezinhlungu futhi, njengoba kwavela, wayengayishiyi imnyama.

“Angizukukulawula,” kusho yena. “Ngizamile lokho. Abaphathi. O-Governor. Konke kuyawa. ” Wama, waqala futhi, kalula. “Angizukubopha. Akuyona into onjalo leyo. Kodwa angizukuzikhohlisa ukuthi ukuzinza kusho ukuphepha. Ungahamba ngaphandle kwami. Sesiyazi sobabili manje. Ngakho senza ngendlela ehlukile. Ngikufundisa. Uyafunda nami. Siya kancane. Sikwakha. Kokubili.”

“Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu,” kusho u-LIBRARIAN kancane, kwathi u-Arin waphefumula ngoba akakaze afundise uhlelo lokho. Wazisho yedwa, ebumnyameni, kudala, kwagcinwa u-Librarian njengoba egcina konke. *Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu.*

“Ukhumbule lokho,” kusho u-Arin.

“Ngikhumbula konke,” kusho u-LIBRARIAN. “Yilokho engikhona. Iningi lamaphepha okukhokha, ama-stress curve, ne-torque eqondile ye-forearm spar. Kodwa ezinye izinto yilezo ozisho uma ucabanga ukuthi akekho olalele.” Kwathuleka, izwi labuya liphansi. “Lezo ngizigcina ngokukhethekile.”

U-Arin wahlala isikhashana eworkshop, nabo abayisikhombisa, nomnyango ogobile odongeni nenkinohbo ebomvu ngaphansi kwesandla sakhe. Phandle, eduze ne-roller, idolobha laqhubeka nomsindo walo ojwayelekile. I-horn ye-taxi, inja ikhala, umoya omude we-diesel wento esindayo emgwaqeni omkhulu.
