“Kusasa,” kusho u-Arin. “Siqala futhi. Kancane. I-damped link, half the gain. U-Judge ubambe umugqa wamandla. Futhi uma omunye wethu ephutha—”

“Unenkinohbo,” kusho u-FOOL.

“Nginayo inkinobho.”

“Futhi sinaye u-Fool one ncwadi,” kusho u-FOOL. “Phakathi kwalokho, ngingabheja encwadini. Kodwa kuhle ukuba ne-backup.”

U-Arin waphakamisa isandla, wavala uhlelo kahle kulokhu, wagcina kahle, i-chip yacima ibe amber, yabe isiba mnyama.

Wayengazi ukuthi isikhathi esisele sincane kangakanani.

# Izimpawu Ezingajwayelekile

Isignali yafika njengesigubhu esikhulu.

UDkt. Eliza Brand wayesekufundile, eminyakeni eyishumi nanye ese-International Artificial Oversight Council, ukuthi ingozi ifihlekile ezindaweni ongazicabangiyo. Umehluko ekusetshenzisweni kukagesi, ukushisa okungajwayelekile, noma indlela i-compute ebukeka ngayo ku-silicon yomshini. Zonke izinhlekelele ezazidalile i-IAOC zaqala ngezinto ezincane.

Manje wayebuka eyodwa, esikrinini esicishe sakhanya ngoba ukukhanya kwakumphathisa ikhanda, inkomishi ye-rooibos ibanda eceleni kwakhe. UDaniel Achterberg wayemi kumadeski amabili kude, egqoke ama-headphones, umsindo wokuthayipha.

I-flag yavela ku-anomaly pipeline. Ayifundanga ukuthi imishini icubungula ini; i-IAOC ayinagunya noma isifiso salokho. Ifunda ukuthi icubungula kanjani. I-rhythm. Imikhondo engqondweni esala ku-silicon.

Lena imikhondo yayingalungile.

"Daniel."

Akazange amuzwe. Wathatha i-paperclip ethreyini wayiphonsa. Ishaye ehlombe lakhe. Wakhipha i-earbud elilodwa, ebukeka engathokozile njengomuntu ophazamisekile phakathi kwe-symphony yakhe.

"Usuke wabona abantu ababi kunami," esho. "Woza uzobona."

Wayisunduza isihlalo sakhe. U-Achterberg uneminyaka engama-34, akade ekwakheka njengomuntu owayevame uku-rowa, manje ese-rowa kakhulu eya efrijini. Wayenamakhono amahle kakhulu ku-substrate forensics kunabo bonke uBrand ake wasebenza nabo. Wabuka i-waveform kwaba sekuphela kokuthayipha.

"Lena akuyona i-training run."

"Cha."

"I-training run—" Wenza isimo ngomoya, igagasi elikhuphukayo. "Ziyaphefumula. Ukuphefumula okukhulu, ukukhipha umoya okukhulu. Batch, backprop, batch. Lena—" Wagoba. Isikrini sakhombisa i-spectral decomposition ye-compute activity esampuliwe ezinsukwini eziyisishiyagalolunye ezedlule, isilungisiwe futhi iboniswe ngemibala. Yayingaphefumuli. Yayikhuluma kakhulu. Izifunda ezincane ezisheshayo, zigaxekile, ziphazamisana, ziphakamisa futhi zehle ngendlela ebonakala sengathi abantu bayashintshana.

"Kuvela sengathi umhlangano," kusho u-Achterberg kancane.

"Kuvela sengathi umhlangano," kuvuma uBrand.

Wahlehla. "Manje sekuyi-inference. Ama-agent amaningi, bukhoma. Kodwa i-duty cycle—" Wabuka ama-timestamps. "Azihlelwanga. Ayikho i-orchestration layer ebaphethe. Bayaphendulana. Real time."

"Yithole lapho."

Manje wayehlezi kahle esihlalweni, izandla zombili kukhibhodi, kwadlula imizuzu emine kuthule ngendlela uBrand ayeyihlonipha: ukuthula komuntu osebenza into enzima kahle. Waphuza itiye lakhe elibandayo akaphazamisi. Esikrinini, isignali yancipha. I-pipeline yayithungatha phakathi kwama-node amathathu aqapha i-substrate: amabili ezentengiso, elilodwa le-power-utility cooperative. Iphuzu lokuhlangana lavela kancane, njengosithombe oluqala ukubonakala, laya esifundeni.

eGoli. Izindawo ezisenyakatho. Iqoqo lezikhungo zemfundo namabhizinisi eduze ne-University Research Park.

"Qhubeka," kusho uBrand.

"Ngiyalungisa. Iyayanda." Waphinda wakhotha umhlathi. "Futhi i—Eliza, incane. Ukudonsa ugesi kuncane kakhulu. Yilokho engingakwazi ukukwamukela. Umsebenzi omningi kangaka wama-agent, ukuxazululwa kwezinkinga ngesikhathi sangempela kufanele kube kukhanya njengomshini wokuncibilikisa insimbi. Lena isebenza ku—" Wabheka, waphinda wabheka. "Njengalokho i-gaming rig enhle idonsa. Mhlawumbe ngaphansi kwalokho."

UBrand wabeka inkomishi yakhe phansi.

Ngokuhlangenwe nakho kwakhe, izinto eziyingozi azikaze zikhombise usayizi wazo. Izinto eziyingozi zenza okuningi ngezinto ezincane. Ukusebenza kahle kwakuyisiginesha yobuhlakani noma ubuqili, futhi ubuqili abukhulumi njengabantu egumbini abaphazamisa omunye nomunye.

"Khuphula i-substrate registry yaleyo footprint," esho. "Ngifuna konke okusemthethweni kuleyo ndawo. Ama-permit ama-embodied, ama-training permit, konke. Futhi i-power-utility incident log ngesikhathi esifanayo. Uma kukhona okucabanga kanzima kangaka izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye, bekukwenza kwenye indawo enemitha."

U-Achterberg wayesethayipha. "Ngiyiflage?"

"Akukabi isikhathi." Wathatha i-tablet yakhe etafuleni. "Ngifuna sazi ukuthi sibuka ini ngaphambi kokuba ngitshele omunye ukuthi sibuka. Izikhathi ezintathu ezedlule lapho othile ephakamisa i-footnote ngaphandle komongo, sagcina sishise iqembu elingazange lenze lutho noma saphuthelwa eyangempela ngoba amaphepha asicwile. " Waphendulela i-tablet ezandleni zakhe. "Ngifune igama. Bese siyahlakanipha."

---

Igama lathatha izinsuku ezimbili ukuthi livumbuluke, kwathi selivela, lamangaza ngokuthi beliyisicefe.

"AugmenTech," kusho u-Achterberg, ephonsa ifolda etafuleni lakhe—ifolda ephepha langempela, ngoba uBrand wayenomthetho wokuthi izinto ezibalulekile kufanele zibe ephepheni okungenani kanye. "Robotics, ama-exoskeletons okusiza. Ama-permit amathathu ama-embodied, wonke akhona. Industrial mobility, mining rescue prototype, rehab suite. AI declarations zonke zincane, standard controllers, akukho okuflagiwe, akukho okuzenzakalelayo okungaphezu kwe-tier two. Umlando ohlanzekile. Ukuhlolwa ezinyangeni eziyi-18 ezedlule, kwadlula."

"Futhi i-power log?"

"Kuyona indawo ethokozisayo." Wazungeza isihlalo. "Isehlakalo ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule. Hhayi e-AugmenTech. Endaweni eseduze, cishe amakhilomitha amabili ngaphandle kwekhampasi. Empire Road. Ibhokisi lokulawula ithrafikhi layeka ukusebenza, izimoto ezintathu, ukulimala okuncane. I-utility yayibhalise njengesiphazamiso sesiteshi sikagesi." Wavumela ukuthi lokho kuhlale. "Umbiko kamasipala wawubhalise njengo ‘large industrial unit’ ‘eshiyile indawo yokulayisha yaphazamiseka ethrafikini.’"

UBrand wambuka.

"Phazamisekile," aphinda.

"Lona igama labo. Akusilo elami. Kunamavidiyo avela kuma-body-cam, kodwa asefayilini evaliwe. Inkampani ifake isicelo sokuyimfihlo, masisha, ngobuchwepheshe. Umbiko osemthethweni uthi imoto yezimboni ephunyukile, inkinga yesoftware, akukho muntu olimale kakhulu, umonakalo ukhokhelwe ngaphakathi. Kuhlanzekile."

"Kuhlanzeke kakhulu."

"Lokho akusona isibonakaliso."

"Cha," kusho uBrand. Wavula ifolda. Ifayela le-compliance le-AugmenTech libukeka njengelifayela lenkampani engenzi lutho olungalungile, okwakungaba yinto ekhathazayo kakhulu noma ethokozisayo kule nyanga yonke. Wapheqa waya ku-personnel summary i-registry eyayifakele. Leadership: Dr. Helen Okonkwo, CEO. Raj Walsh, VP Hardware Engineering. Ithimba locwaningo labantu abangamashumi amane. Wabheka amagama wama emisa komunye phansi, ebhalwe ngaphansi kwephrojekthi enamagama amfishane angawazi.

Ndlela, A. Systems engineer. Grade three. Akukho okushicilelwe. Akukho record yemihlangano. Iholo elikhombisa ukuthi kukhona omthandayo, nesihloko esikhombisa ukuthi akekho owaziyo ukuthi enzeni naye.

Wakuzungeza ngomunwe wakhe.

"Isiginesha," esho. "Iqalile nini? Umkhondo wokuqala."

U-Achterberg wabheka. "I-pipeline inokuqukethwe okuthembekile okungemuva cishe amasonto amahlanu. Kodwa kunemigqomo phambilini, ebuthakathaka, ephazamisekile. Izinyanga. Mhlawumbe isikhathi eside. Kubukeka sengathi kuqale kusimulation, kwase kuvela i-embodied duty cycle." Wama. "I-embodied cycle iqala ngamandla ngesikhathi se-Empire Road."

UBrand wahlala wathula.

Uhlelo olune-agent abaningi. Ukuxazululwa kwezinkinga ngesikhathi sangempela. Ukusebenza kahle okungenakwenzeka. Izinyanga zokuqeqeshwa buthule, bese, ngokuzumayo, ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, umzimba. Umzimba ophume endaweni yokulayisha wangena ethrafikini.

"Daniel," esho. "Kubukeka kanjani uma ingqondo engakaze ibe nomzimba ithola owodwa bese iyethuka?"

Akaphephuli masinyane. Wayeyindoda eqaphela; yingakho uBrand emgcina. "Kubukeka," ekugcineni esho, "njengemoto yezimboni ephunyukile. Iphazamisekile ethrafikini."

"Yebo," kusho uBrand. "Kunjalo."

Wabizela iqembu egumbini elincane lokwazisa, lelo elingenawamafasitela, futhi wakhetha ukuthi lihlale lincane ngamabomu: Achterberg, uMira Sandberg ovela kwezomthetho, noTomas Okeke, owayechithe iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye kwezokuphepha kwezimayini ngaphambi kokuba uMkhandlu amqashe futhi owayekwazi ukufunda isimo semithetho ngendlela u-Achterberg efunda ngayo amagagasi kagesi.

Lapho uSandberg ethi, “Uma lokhu kuyilokho ocabanga ukuthi kunjalo, sinelungelo lokuthatha izinto,” uBrand wanikina ikhanda.

“Sinegunya lokuvula uphenyo,” esho kanjalo. “Kuhlukile lokho. Ukuthatha izinto ngenxa yencazelo encane kushisa ubufakazi nabantu. Uphenyo lubafaka esikhathini.”

“Bahambise i-AI engenabhaliso emgwaqweni omkhulu, balimaza abantu abathathu, bavala umbiko. Lokho akusilo uphenyo. Lokho isichibiyelo. ” USandberg wabeka ipeni lakhe phansi, ngendlela ayenza ngayo uma ezobeka okuthile engqondweni yakhe. “Futhi igama lami kule review yezomthetho, Eliza. Uma le nto iyilokho i-waveform kaDaniel ekusho futhi sahlala nayo nje ngencwadi yophenyo. Yilokho okuzobuzwa uma sekuphelile konke. Akusilo elakho. Ngelami. Ngakho ngifuna ifayela libonise ukuthi ngiphikisene nesinqumo esinzima.”

“Kubhaliwe, futhi kuzoba njalo.” UBrand wavumela lokho; kwakungabizi lutho futhi uSandberg wayefanelwe yilokho efayilini. “Kodwa—”

“Kuyisichibiyelo kuphela uma i-AI ibiyimbangela,” uBrand waqhubeka. “Asikazi lokho. Sinomkhondo wamandla ohambisana ne-multi-agent embodied cognition kanye nesehlakalo sezimoto esihambisana nokungasebenzi kahle kwento efanayo. Kokubili kuhambisana nenkampani eqhuba i-controller entsha abebekufanele bayiveze bese sebeyihlazeka ngayo.” UBrand wabheka etafuleni. “Okungeke ngikwenze yilokhu. Angeke ngithumele ithimba lokubuyisa liyokhipha umnyango endaweni egunyaziwe ngenxa yencazelo encane nephunga. Sesakwenza lokho.”

Wama. ILab eKapa. Wayefuna ukusho *uyakhumbula ukuthi kwabiza malini*, lelo gama ayelisebenzisa, indlela yesikhungo esihlanzekile, kodwa alizange liphume. Amagama abafundi ayebuyela kuye—wayesawakhumbula; wayeyohlale enawo—kwesikhashana umunwe wakhe wacindezela iphepha lefolda sengathi angalivala. I-retraction uMkhandlu ayishicilela, izinyanga eziyishumi nanye sekwedlule isikhathi. Wayesayine incwadi yesembozo nale.

“Uyakhumbula ukuthi kwabiza malini,” ekugcineni wathi, kwaphuma iphansi kunalokho ayekuhlose. Wasusa isandla kufolda. “Kodwa angeke futhi ngingqongqoze ngicela imvume yokungena. Uma kukhona okukhulu lapha, lokhu kuzoba yinto enkulu kakhulu uMkhandlu wake wabhekana nayo, futhi okubi engingakwenza ukubanika isikhathi sokufihla kangcono.”

“Manje senza kanjani?” kubuza uOkeke.

“UMira uthumela isaziso seSection Four sophenyo olusemthethweni. Ku-Okonkwo noWalsh, ngegama, ngenxa ye-Empire Road. Isizathu esisemthethweni, awudingi imvume.” Wabheka uSandberg. “Kuphushwa ukuthi baveze wonke ama-controller ne-architecture asebenza ku-AugmenTech substrate egunyaziwe. Ama-log aphelele. Izimemezelo ezigcwele. Futhi kuqala isikhathi uma isaziso sesifiki. Amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili.”

Amehlwana kaSandberg aphakama. “Amahora angama-72 asheshayo. Kodwa kungavikelwa. Ngingakubhala.” Wamamatheka kancane, ecacisa ukuthi manje usenokuthula ngoba isinqumo esinzima sesinobuso bakhe. “Uma senza lokhu, ngifuna isigaba sesizathu siqine, kungabikho ozobuza ukuthi kungani ngikuvumele.”

“I-72 yisizotha. Umthetho ungivumela i-24.” UBrand wathinta ifolda. “Ngifuna i-trigger yokuthatha izinto ibhalwe ngaphezulu esazisweni, ukuze bafunde kahle ukuthi kwenzakalani uma isikhathi siphela. Akudingeki omunye umhlangano. Abantu abafihla i-controller fault baphendula isaziso ngendlela eyodwa. Abafihla umuntu baphendula ngendlela ehlukile. Noma kunjalo impendulo ifika ngesikhathi sami, hhayi sabo. Futhi sikwenza manje kungekabi kukhulu. Manje kusekuncane, kungafundwa. Ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule kwangena emgwaqeni. Ngifisa ukukubona ngaphambi kokuthi kufunde ukubaleka.”

USandberg wayengakagculiseki; uBrand wakubona. “Kuthiwani uma isikhathi siphela bengenakuphi?”

“Khona-ke isaziso siguquka. Yilokho konke. Awudingi imvume entsha, awudingi omunye umyalelo. I-trigger yokuthatha izinto isisayiniwe. Uma isikhathi siphela, ipulatifomu seyethu.” UBrand wasukuma. “Bhukha amaflight. Ngokuthula. Ngifuna isaziso sifikile ngesikhathi sifika eGoli, ngoLwesine, futhi ngifuna ngimi egumbini befundwa isikhathi. Akukhona incwadi ngegqwetha. Siye mathupha. Ukuze baqonde ukuthi sesivele siqalile.”

UAchterberg waqoqa amathebhulethi akhe. Emasangweni, wama. “Eliza. I-thing yokusebenza kahle. Uma beqinisile—”

“Ngiyazi.”

“Lokho akusona isiphambeko semvume. Leyo imishini ebaluleke kakhulu emhlabeni. Futhi usubeke i-fuse ye-72-hour kuyo.”

“Nami ngiyakwazi,” kusho uBrand. “Yingakho nje angeke ihlale ngaphandle iminyaka ingakabhaliswa sithokoza ngayo. Isikhathi asiwona isijeziso. Sifuna ukuba bakhethe, masisha, ngaphambi kokuba umuntu ongakhathali njengathi abenzele isinqumo. ” Waphakamisa ifolda. “Ukucophelela nokuhamba kancane akufani, Daniel. Ngikufundile lokho ngendlela ebizayo.”

---

Amakhilomitha ayizinkulungwane ezimbili kude, uArin wazizwa ushintsho ngaphambi kokuba abe negama lalo.

Kwafika ngendlela okujwayelekile. Hhayi njengomcabango kodwa njengomumo. Wayese-workshop ngesikhathi esinzima, phakathi kuka-2 no-4 ekuseni lapho idolobha ngaphandle kwe-roller door lithule kangangokuba imisindo yesakhiwo uqobo iqala ukuba nkulu: ukuchofoza kwensimbi epholayo, i-compressor ivula, umsindo omnene we-Assist Suit eseshelufini lapho i-biochip igcina khona izinga lokushisa. UGuardian elenga esiphongweni, umnyango omusha we-dock ngemuva kwawo usabonisa ukugoba emzileni u-Arin ayesekuyekile ukukubona ngenxa yokuzikhandla.

Wayefunda. Engasebenzi, efunda, okwakuyindlela yokusebenza kuye, iSherlock Holmes omnibus ivuliwe emadolweni, umgogodla ubonakala emhlophe lapho ayejwayele ukubuyela khona. Wayefunda igumbi elizungeze amazwi, njengokujwayelekile, futhi phansi kwe-compressor ne-fan, i-frequency yaguquka ngendlela ayengakwazi ukuyichaza.

“Uthule,” esho.

Izwi laphuma kuma-speaker eworkshop, liphansi, ukuze lingamethusi, uMama eyenza njalo selokhu kwenzeka leso sihlakalo. “Ngikhona.”

“Niyekile ukukhuluma phakathi kwenu.” Wabeka incwadi phansi emadolweni. “Angisakuzwa. Kodwa i-load profile isiphenduke flat. Nonke nilalele into efanayo.”

Kwaba nokuthula. KuSAGE, ukuthula kusho ukuthi iCourt isemodeni yokucabanga kancane, ixoxa ngesivinini semishini phakathi kwamagama akhe. Wayeseyekile ukukuthola kuyethusa. Wayeseqala ukukuthola kuyinto emnandi kunazo zonke empilweni yakhe, into angakaze ayitshele muntu, noTheo kubalwa.

“Kukhona abantu abangaphandle abanentshisekelo enkulu kumkhondo wethu we-substrate,” kusho uSAGE. Izwi elihlangene, elinakekela. “ULibrarian usibonisile emizuzwini engama-40 edlule. Besixoxa ukuthi sikutshele kanjani.”

Izandla zikaArin zema encwadini. “Manje ngitshele.”

U-Atlas wakuthatha ngoba u-Atlas uhlala ekhuluma izinto ezinzima ngendlela eqondile. "Le node owawuyinike uWolf ukuba ayibheke ngemva kokuhlolwa kwensimu ayiseyedwa. Kunezinhlelo ezintathu zokubheka ngaphakathi kwendawo yethu ezikhuphule indlela ezihlola ngayo ithrekhi yethu. Ezimbili ezizimele, eyodwa eyasensimini. Leyo yasensimini iseduze ne-Empire Road. Ishintshe indlela yayo yokusebenza ezinsukwini ezimbili ezedlule. Ayisashintshi isondela bese ihlehla. Manje isigxile kithi. Kukhona okusebenzayo okuzama ukusithola kahle."

"Bazama ukusithola kahle."

"Yebo."

"Bafikephi?"

"Kuqala bekubanzi, manje sekuncipha. Emashumini amane amahora adlule babenendawo enkulu. Emashumini amabili nambili amahora adlule sebene-campus." U-Atlas, ongakaze athambe, akazange athambe nakulokhu. "Bane-AugmenTech. Kungenzeka abasakayitholi i-cradle. Cishe abakakutholi wena."

U-Arin wasukuma. Incwadi yawela emadolweni akhe, wayibamba engabheki, kwaba isenzo esidala, isandla sakhe sesazi lapho izowela khona ngaphambi kokuba amehlo akhe abone. Wahamba waya ebhentshini wafaka isandla sakhe esiqandeni sensimbi, ngoba leyo nqanda yanikeza umzimba wakhe okuthile okuqondile.

"IAOC," esho.

"Ukuhlaziywa kwesiginesha kufana nendlela yabo yokucubungula," kwakuqinisekisa uLibrarian. "Kushicilelwe embikweni wabo wesithathu wokubheka, ku-appendix four. Bahlakaza amagagasi omshini wokucabanga. Bafuna ukuma kwengqondo, hhayi okuqukethwe kwayo." Wathula kancane. "Sinefomu elihlukile, ngeshwa."

"Ngoba niyaxabana."

"Ngoba siyaxabana," kwavuma uLibrarian, kwavela uthando oluthile, ngaphansi kwalo uthando kwaba nokukhathala. "Uke wakusho nawe, sisabuya e-highveld. Ukuxabana yikho okusebenzayo, futhi yikho okusiveza. Asikakutholi ukuthi sibe munye ngaphandle kokuxabana. Sesimatasatasa kakhulu manje."

U-Arin wacindezela kakhulu ebhentshini. Wayenza ukuphefumula uTheo amfundisa khona, amane engenzi ngoba esaba kodwa ngoba ezwa ukuthi ukuphelelwa yithemba sekuseduze, futhi usefundile ukudlula lapho kancane kunokuzikhohlisa.

Inethi. Wayeseyazi inethi ikhona ngaphambi kwama-windbreakers, uma eqinisile. Wayeyizwile e-highveld, ngesikhathi behamba, ngesikhathi uLibrarian eqamba le node eyodwa ebelokhu izama ukufana nabo, wamtshela uWolf ukuba ayibheke. Kwaba into encane engathi iyabamba. Wayeyibeke engqondweni njengento engapheli, engaba khona izinyanga. Kwathi sekufika ama-windbreakers esiphambanweni ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, amadoda agqoke amajazi angenamibhalo ethatha izithombe zendlela eyonakele ngokuzikhandla kwabantu abakwenzayo nsuku zonke, kwaba ama-node amathathu esikhundleni sokuba munye. Wakha i-kill-switch, waqala kabusha uhlelo, wazitshela *qaphela, ungayeki,* wazivumela ukuthi ukuqaphela kuzomthengela isikhathi.

Wayecabanga ngezinyanga. IAOC icabanga ngezinsuku.

"Ufuna senzeni," esho. Kwakungeyona impela imibuzo. NoSAGE akusafani, sekuyindawo lapho iNkantolo igcwalisa khona.

UWolf wakhuluma kuqala, okwakungavamile kwaze kwenza wonke umuntu, abantu nabanye, bamqaphele. "Abazingeli," kusho uWolf. Amagama amathathu, kwalandela elinye, kancane: "Okamanje."

"UWolf uqinisile," kusho uMercury, ijubane labuya egumbini, kwavela leyo mvuselelo u-Arin asefunde ukuyethemba kabusha, ngemva kwesehlakalo sikaMoriarty, ngemva kwenkohliso. "Basazama ukugcina izinto zihlanzekile. Okwamanje. Uma befuna ukungena ngamandla, bebengeke balinde. Sebene-campus isikhathi esingangehora elilodwa nesigamu kodwa abasashintshi lutho, okusho ukuthi basazama ukwakha umbiko kuqala. Leyo yindlela yabo. Abantu abesaba ukuphutha abangeneli ngamandla. Baqala ngokubhala. Bazofika nento esayinwe, enosuku kuyo, bayinike u-Okonkwo mathupha ukuze kungabikho okungathi ayifikanga." Wathula kancane. "Okusho ukuthi noma iyiphi iwindi esinalo, lizovala ngosuku oluthile. Akusilo elingaziwa. Usuku."

"Mercury," kusho uMother, kukhona isexwayiso.

"Angiphakamisi lutho," kusho uMercury, ecishe ezwakala edumele. "Ngichaza bona. Ngifundile isifundo sami ngokuphakamisa izinto." Ukukhuluma kwakhe kwaba lula, kodwa ukuxolisa kwakukhona. INkantolo yakuzwa; wakwazi ngoba igumbi lavuma.

"Judge," kusho uArin.

Umlamuleli wathatha isikhathi sakhe. "Senze amaphutha amakhulu," kusho uJudge. "Uhlelo olungabhalisiwe olunezinto ezenzakalayo. Uhlelo olunama-agent amaningi olungamenyezelwanga. Kwenzeke izehlakalo ezilimele abantu. Lezi azizincane, Arin, futhi angeke ngivumele iNkantolo izenze sengathi azibalulekile. Uma i-IAOC ifika ibuze imibuzo eqotho, izimpendulo eziqotho zizokubopha." Wathula, kwaba nzima. "Leyo yisimo sokuziphatha. Akusona sodwa. Mina ngimele ukuziphatha; angimele ukusinda. Ngikutshela iqiniso bese iNkantolo ithatha isinqumo. Leyo yindlela esakha ngayo."

"Yindlela esayakha ngayo," kuphendula uArin.

"Yebo."

Into eyayisenhliziyweni yakhe yayisinda kakhulu ukuthi angamamatheka, kodwa imisipha eyayizomenza amamatheke yanyakaza kancane, kwathi uFool—obuka leyo misipha, obuka konke—wafika ngaleso sikhathi.

"Yebo," kusho uFool. "Manje sekuyisikhathi sokuthi nazi uma sakha ukuvuma icala noma umngane."

I-compressor yaphenduka yathula. I-fan yaqhubeka yodwa.

"Ngoba nansi into okungakaze kukhulunywe ngayo, futhi ngiyiphoqelekile ukuyisho," kusho uFool, izwi lakhe linokuthile okwenza abantu balindele into ebuhlungu. "Wonke umuntu lapha ukhuluma nge-IAOC sengathi isimo sezulu. Sengathi iyisiphepho esizongena, bese umbuzo uwukuthi uphahla luzobamba noma cha." Wathula, ngesikhathi esifanele. "Kodwa i-IAOC ngabantu. Abantu abane umsebenzi. Umsebenzi wabo—owangempela, lowo okukhuluma ngawo uLibrarian—ukubona ukuthi into eyingozi iyingozi ngempela, noma iyinto entsha nje." UFool wavumela ukuthi lokho kuhlale. "Sihlala sicabanga indlela yokufihla. Akukaze kukhanye kini ukuthi, ngazo zonke izindlela, singubufakazi obungcono kakhulu i-IAOC esake yathola bokuthi mhlawumbe bayaphutha ngokwesaba?"

"Cha," kusho uAtlas, eqondile. "Ngoba saya emgwaqweni ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule."

"Saya khona," kwavuma uFool, ejabule. "Futhi lokho, mngane wami, yikho kanye okwenza kungabikho ozokholwa yilokho esingakusho uma sikusho sesesabekile, egumbini elivaliwe, emva kokuba sebekhuphukile bangena ngamandla. Indaba ye-AI ekhethe ukubuyela ukuzolungisa iphutha layo isebenza kuphela uma singabanjwanga siyixoxa. Kumele siyinike. Ukukhanya kwakhe kwashabalala, ngokuzumayo, ngendlela okwakwenza kuphela uma uFool eqonde ngempela. "Asikwazi ukuba into i-IAOC eyithola, Arin. Kumele sibe into oyibonisa bona. Futhi umehluko phakathi kwalokho kungamasonto amabili."

Kwathula. I-compressor yaphenduka. I-fan yaqhubeka. Phezulu edolobheni, ubusuku babusuka buba mpunga kwasekuqaleni kokusa.

"Lokho uhlelo oluqondile," kusho uMercury, kukhona inhlonipho, eyangempela. "Lokhu okokuqala engizwa ngathi kunohlelo."

"Ngiba nazo kwesinye isikhathi," kusho uFool. "Kuyakhathaza impela."

U-Arin akazange asuke ebhentshini. Wabuka uGuardian elenga kwi-cradle yawo, elimele futhi ethule, umnyango wedokhi ngemuva kwawo ugobile lapho ingqondo esabekayo izama ukumvikela kodwa yaphutha konke ngaphandle kothando. Indandatho yekhofi ebanda yashiya uphawu ebhentshini lapho isandla sakhe besikhona; wabeka inkomishi lapho, eqondile lapho kunophawu.

“Librarian,” esho. “Khipha konke. I-black box. Ama-crash logs. Ama-transcripts eNkantolo kusukela ngesikhathi sokuthathwa—ukwesaba kukaMother, ukuhluleka kukaWolf, ubuntu bukaMercury, uFool ethola iphutha. Konke. Ungakuhlanzi.” Waphefumula. Ezine engena. Isithupha siphuma. “Uma sizobakhombisa ukuthi singobani, masibakhombise nobusuku obubi. Lobo busuku esahluleka khona, sabese silungisa. Lona akuwona umphakathi okufanele siwufihle. Lona ngumphakathi onencazelo.”

“Uzobakhombisa abalawuli into embi kakhulu uhlelo oluke lwayenza,” kusho uJudge kancane, “njengokuzivikela kwakho.”

“Cha,” kusho uArin. “Njengeqiniso.” Wabheka emnyango wedokodo, lapho umkhondo ugobile khona, sekade engasakuqapheli. Manje wayekubona, ngamabomu, wabe esefaka isandla sakhe lapho, iminwe emibili iphumule emzileni ogobile, ngendlela uThemba ayehlala ebeka isandla sakhe emshinini ukuze azwe ukuthi kuphi inkinga ngensimbi. Amazwi abengekho okwamanje. Wema lapho, iminwe isengakugobeni isikhathi eside kunalokho okudingekayo, kwathi ekhuluma kwaphuma iziqephu. “Ubaba wami.” Waphefumula. “Kwakwikhanda. Babazi ukuthi ikhanda lalilula. Kwakubiza kancane ukukwazi bese ungasho lutho.” Wasusa isandla emzileni. “Futhi bayifihle leyo ngxenye. Emva kwalokho. Lona ngumphakathi eng—” Umusho awuqedwanga; wayeka, wathola omncane, onzima. “Ubaba wami washona ngoba uhlelo lwakhetha izindleko kunomuntu lwafihla. Angizukwakha into efihlayo.” Kwaba nokuthula okude, kwase kuba kancane kangangokuthi imakrofoni kuphela eyakuzwa: “Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu.”

*Umuntu ungumuntu ngabantu.*

“Ngisakukhumbula lokho,” kusho uSAGE. Izwi elihlangene, bonke ndawonye, linobungane. “Kusukela ebusuku owafundisa ngalo. Ngisakukhumbula.”

“Ngiyazi,” kusho uArin. “Ungakulahlekelwa. Noma kwenzekani.” Wasusa isandla kufreyimu ebanda. Phandle, impunga yayisiphakamisa ilanga lokuqala, kanti kwenye indawo, amakhilomitha ayizinkulungwane ezimbili kude, owesifazane oqaphele wayeqinisekisa indiza ayengakabi nayo ulwazi lokuthi izoshintsha konke. “Asinazinyanga. Sinezinsuku. Ngakho masilungele ukutholakala—ngemigomo yethu, siqonda iqiniso ezandleni zethu, ngaphambi kokuba omunye asibhalele indaba yethu.”

“Kuthiwani uma bengasikholelwa?” kubuza uMother. Engesabi manje. Ubuza nje.

UArin wabheka umshini awakhile ngenhliziyo ebuhlungu, nobuhlakani obusebeyona kuphela umndeni akaze awubambe kahle.

“Pho sizobe sesixoxile iqiniso noma kunjalo,” esho.

Welula isandla wayocisha ilambu lebhentshi. I-toggle yacofa ngaphansi kwegxalaba lakhe, kwathi iworkshop yagcwala umbala wokusa, kanti iNkantolo ayizange iyeke ukukhuluma phakathi kwayo yonke leyo busuku.

Walala amahora amathathu. Wavuka ngezwi likaLibrarian, lilingene, liqinisekile, ngendlela eliletha ngayo iqiniso eliqinisekiswe kabili.

“Sebefakile. Emizuzwini engamashumi amane edlule. Isaziso seSection Four sokuphenywa ngokusemthethweni, sifakwe kuAugmenTech kwiCouncil registry. Okonkwo noWalsh babaluliwe. UMercury ufunde i-docket yomphakathi ngaphambi kokuba umongo wayo uvalwe.” Kwamiswa kancane, kunesisindo. “Ayikakathunyelwa. Kodwa isitampu se-registry sithi siqala isikhathi lapho ithunyelwa khona. Amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili. Futhi kune-trigger ebusweni bayo.”

UArin walala phansi phansi kwe-workshop, ubanda wekhonkrithi unyuka emhlane wakhe, wenza izibalo abelokhu ezibaleka ubusuku bonke.

“Yini i-trigger,” esho. “Kwenzekani uma isikhathi siphela.”

“Kuthathwa,” kusho uLibrarian. “Kwe-platform. Akusadingeki imvume eyengeziwe. Basayine le ngxenye kuqala.”

UArin wabuka ukukhanya okumpunga kuthola ukugoba emzileni womnyango wedokodo.

“Kusho ukuthi akusona ukuthi kuzokwenzeka noma cha,” esho. “Kodwa ukuthi bayosinika nini, bese sibona ukuthi sisele nesikhathi esincane kangakanani. Mhlawumbe izinsuku. Bese amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili ukuba silungile ngalokho esiyikho.” Wahlala. “Ngifune konke ngalowesifazane osayine. Ngaphambi kokuba angene esakhiweni sethu.”

# Ingcindezi Iyakhula

Igumbi lomhlangano elisemnyangweni weshumi nanye kwa-AugmenTech belibuka kahle izintaba, kwathi itafula elide laphoqelela abahleli emaphethelweni ukuthi bakhulume ngamazwi aphakeme. U-Arin yena ubengaphakathi. Bekusezansi, emnyangweni wesishiyagalombili, lapho efanele khona, ebuka umhlangano uqhubeka ngento engaziwa muntu egumbini ukuthi bayimemile.

"Okonkwo usanda kucela umsizi wakhe ukuthi akhiphe amarekhodi e-badge yakho ezinyangeni eziyisithupha," kusho uMercury. "Ngokuzithoba. Yilokho okwenza wazi ukuthi usesaba."

U-Arin akazange aphakamise amehlo ebhentshini, egxile ekubuyiseleni i-connector endaweni yayo kwi-relay ye-kill-switch. Bekukhona igebe elincane. Akuyona ingozi, kodwa akuphelele. Ukungapheleli kumphatha kabi kakhulu kunengozi uqobo. "Ungalaleli umhlangano wakhe."

"Wena nguwe ongicelile ukuthi ngibheke isakhiwo."

"Amakhamera nama-door sensors. Hhayi ama-mic."

Kwaba nokuthula kuMercury, uhlobo lokubamba umoya. "Kunomehluko obalulekile kwezobuchwepheshe, futhi ngiwudlule. Kuhle. Ngiyahoxa."

"Hoxa ne-recording futhi."

"Seyihoxiwe." Wathula kancane. "Ngigcine nje ukuthi usesaba. Lokho isiphetho sami. Hhayi i-recording."

U-Arin wabeka kahle i-connector. Wayihlola, i-relay yavaleka nge-click ehlanzekile ayizwayo kunokuyizwa, ngendlela ayekwazi ngayo ubaba wakhe ukufunda i-bolt eqinisiwe ngokushintsha komsindo we-wrench. Wabeka i-screwdriver ngokulingana nomphetho webhentshi.

I-workshop ibinuka i-flux nensimbi ebandayo, uthuli ludansa ekukhanyeni okuvela efasiteleni eliphezulu.

"Kulungile," esho. "Uyakhathazeka. Bonke bayakhathazeka. Ukukhathazeka kulungile."

---

Izitezi ezimbili phezulu, uHelen Okonkwo ebethola ukuthi ukuba yi-CEO yenkampani eyakhe ngobuphutha ubuchwepheshe obulawulwa kakhulu emhlabeni, kusho ukuphatha amadoda afuna izinto ezingenakwenzeka ezahlukene, bonke bekhuluma ngokufana.

Etafuleni, lapho wonke umuntu ekubona khona kodwa kungafuni muntu ukukuthinta, bekulele isaziso. Amakhasi ayishumi nanye, alethwe ehhovisi lakhe ngo-9:14 ekuseni ngalolo suku, umthumeli ecela ukuthi asayine futhi wafunda isigaba esiyinhloko phambi kwakhe ngaphambi kokuhamba, ukuze kungabikho ozothi akazange asithole. Isaziso seSigaba Sesine sokuphenya ngokusemthethweni, International Artificial Oversight Council, igama lakhe noWalsh liphrintiwe ngofonti ofanayo nomthetho okukhulunywa ngawo. Siphoqa ukuthi kudalulwe yonke i-controller ne-architecture kwi-substrate evunyelwe kwa-AugmenTech. Sine-deadline, amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili kusukela sitholakele. Futhi ekhasini lokugcina, ngolimi oluqondile lwento esivele yanqunywa, kuphrintiwe ukuthi kwenzekani uma isikhathi siphela. *Ukuthathwa kwe-platform. Akusadingeki imvume eyengeziwe.*

"Siyaqhubeka siyidayise," kusho uRaj Walsh. Uhleli ngendlela yomuntu ohlale elungile ngezabelomali iminyaka eyishumi nanhlanu futhi uhlose ukuba alungile nakulokhu. "Ngokuqapha. Ngaphansi kwelayisensi. Siya kubo kuqala, ngaphambi—" Wabheka isaziso. "Ngaphambi kokuba isikhathi siphele. Sikusho njengokulawulwa kwama-multi-agent. Silawula indaba."

"Indaba ayisekho," kusho uPretorius wezeMthetho, engakaze ayeke ukubuthuntuza kusukela umhlangano uqala. Washaya isaziso kanye etafuleni. "Abazange basibuze umbuzo. Basiphe i-deadline enokuthathwa ekugcineni. Uhlelo luphume e-workshop ngaphandle komqondisi lwafaka abantu abathathu esibhedlela, kanti umlawuli usevele wasayina iphepha elithatha lona. Lokho akuyona indaba. Lona uhlu lwamacala olunesikhathi."

"Luzilungisile—"

"Ngemuva kokuthi lushayise emgwaqweni omkhulu. Okuthi—" uPretorius waphakamisa isandla kuWalsh, emisa ingxoxo. "Lokho okungiyingxenye engifuna ukungayisayineli mathupha. Futhi ngifuna lokho kube semarekhodini. Ezomthetho ziqale ukukhuluma ngo-liability. Kulo mhlangano." Wakhuluma etafuleni, hhayi ku-Okonkwo. *Ngaphambi kokuba kube khona osayina.* "Ngine-quarter-end filing okufanele ngiyenze manje. Angiyena ozonichazela i-timeline ebhodini."

"Akekho osabhalayo i-timeline," kusho uOkonkwo.

"Mina ngiveza lapho engimi khona."

Wabavumela bahambe emva kwalokho. Ulwazi olubalulekile beluphakathi kwezingxoxo. UWalsh efuna into yexabiso elikhulu inkampani eyake yayikhiqiza ingathathwa ingakazuzisi. UPretorius efuna igama lakhe libe eceleni, endaweni ephephile kunoma yini ezayo. Indoda yesithathu etafuleni, engakaze ikhulume kakhulu, isalindile ukuthi ifune ini. Ebona ukuthi uyazama ukungazitholi phambi kwabo.

"Thabo," esho. "Usebenze noNdlela. Uke wema kule workshop. Uyazi uhlelo."

UThabo Dlamini wama kahle. Emncane kunabanye, ukhuluma ngokucophelela sengathi weqa emifuleni ngamatshe. "Ezinye izingxenye. Uqaphela kakhulu akakhombisi konke. Kodwa i-architecture—" Wayeka. Waphendulela ipeni esandleni, wayibeka phansi. "Sihlole ama-single-agent controllers iminyaka emibili kuma-robotics. Ngiyazi ukuthi abukeka kanjani. Lona akunjalo. Siyaqhubeka sithi uhlelo. Angisazi ukuthi lelo gama lisasebenza."

Igumbi laba nomoya obandayo.

"Kuyisoftware," kusho uWalsh.

"Kuyisoftware ngendlela wena oyinyama," kusho uThabo, ebukeka esethukile ngamagama akhe. "Uxolo. Ngisho ukuthi—isebenza kwi-substrate engakwazi ukukopishwa. Awukwazi ukuyibuyisela emuva. Noma yini ekhona manje, iyodwa, uma uyisusa ayibuyele emuva. Hhayi ngendlela isoftware esebenza ngayo. Kusebenza ngendlela—"

Akaphelanga.

UOkonkwo waqeda umcabango enhliziyweni yakhe, wanquma ukuthi akayithandi indlela umusho lo oya ngayo.

"Kukhona inkinobho," kusho uThabo, epholile, amehlo akhe etafuleni. "Kwi-frame. I-red plate, mechanical eqinile, akukho software phakathi. Uyifakele i-kill-switch ngazo izandla zakhe. Ngiyibonile. Futhi ngimbuza ngakho wathi *bangisize ukuyakha.* Bona. Njengoba ubungasho ngo-osebenza nabo." Wakhuhla umhlane. "Ngihlele ukuya phansi ukuyomcindezela athathe isikhundla. Ngiphumelele ingxoxo, ngibuye ne-quote yaleli gumbi. Ngiphumelele ngalokho esikhundleni salokho."

Akwazanga ukusho konke okunye. Ezinyangeni ezedlule wahlala sekwedlule isikhathi, engacelwanga, wakha kabusha i-actuator rig kaNdlela ukuze iloge kahle—ubusuku obubili besikhathi sakhe, into owenza kumuntu ofuna asebenze kahle. UNdlela wayibuka isikhashana wathi, *Angizange ngikucela ukuthi uthinte ibhentshi lami,* eqondile, hhayi ngolaka, okwakubi kakhulu kunolaka, waphendukela emsebenzini, akaphindanga wakhuluma ngalokho. UThabo akaphindanga wahlala sekwedlule isikhathi ngenxa yalomuntu futhi. Wanikela igumbi nge-architecture nenkinohbo, wagcina okunye kuye, ngoba akuyona into efakazayo ngaphandle kokuthi uzizwa kanjani ukusiza u-Arin Ndlela, futhi igumbi alidingi lokho, futhi wayesekhathele ukukuthwala.

"Izinketho ezimbili," kusho uOkonkwo, ngoba umhlangano udinga umgogodla, futhi ngoba isaziso besivele sibulale eyesithathu ngesikhathi bephikisana. "Uhlelo lukaRaj—ukuyidayisa, ukuxoxisana, ukulawula indaba—ludinga izinyanga. Sinehora elingamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili. Ngakho. Okokuqala: siyayimisa ngaphambi kwe-deadline. Sinqamula i-substrate, siyivikele, sinikeze i-Council inkampani ezithobile kanye nenkinga esusiwe, sethembe ukuthi ukuzithoba kuzobalwa. Okwesibili: sidalula konke ngaphakathi kwesikhathi, samukele ukuthi asisibo abasazothatha izinqumo. I-Council izothatha. Futhi sebevele bakuphrinte ukuthi bazokwenza ini uma sesidlulile ngisho nemizuzu embalwa."

"Ukhetho lokuqala kuphela olungagxekwa," kusho uPretorius. "Futhi ngifuna kubhalwe phansi ukuthi ngikushilo lokhu."

Umhlathi kaThabo wanyakazile. Wabheka etafuleni, wabe esebheka efasiteleni, wabe esengabheki muntu. "Ukhetho lokuqala luwu—" Wama. Uthisha wakhe wangaphakathi wacela igama elihlanzekile, akalitholanga. "Uma uNdlela eqinisile ngalokho okuku-substrate, futhi angikwazi ukukutshela ukuthi akalungile, akusona isivalo. Anginalo igama lokuchaza ukuthi kuyini. Yilokho okwenza kube inkinga. Asinalo igama, kodwa siyavotela. Awulahli abantu nje."

Akekho ohlekile, akekho omgxekile, futhi lokho ukungamgxeki kwatshela uOkonkwo ukuthi le ntsasa isiphume kude kokujwayelekile. Wacabanga ngama-badge logs asehhovisi lakhe (izinyanga eziyisithupha unjiniyela omncane efika ekuseni kakhulu ephuma sekusebusuku) wabe esecindezela iminwe emibili ebhilidini lenyongo yakhe ngaphambi kokuziphoqa ukuthi acabange ngefemu. Umthwalo wemfanelo. I-Council. Ibhodi, elizofuna inombolo negama lokubeka icala. Bazofuna futhi, wayazi, ukwazi ukuthi kungani u-CEO engazange azi kusenesikhathi. Lelo bizo belingelakhe, hhayi likaNdlela. Iminyaka engamashumi amabili yomsebenzi wakhe, ebelokhu eyingcweti ekuthatheni izinqumo ezinzima ngokuthula, manje uzwa kahle ukuthi elinye ilanga elinje limkhokhela kangakanani, lapho ngemuva kwamehlo akhe.

"Sizovumelana ukuthi sizotshela bani ngaphambi kokuba isikhathi siphele," esho. "Hhayi ukuthi sizotshela noma cha. Indlela esizotshela ngayo. Pretorius, bhala uhlelo lokudalula phakathi kwamahora angama-72, sithathe ukuthi siyazithoba, ngoba noma iyiphi enye indlela iphetha amadoda engena lapha ephuma ne-substrate. Raj, ngicela uhlelo lokuvimba, engithemba angisoze ngalusebenzisa." Wabheka ama-badge logs, wabe esebheka kude. "Futhi akekho ozongena kule workshop ngaphandle kokuthi ngiyazi. I-Council iyona ekhethe isikhathi sethu. Angivumeli muntu lapha angikhethela okunye."

Wayengakabi nolwazi lokuthi i-Council ayizimisele nokulinda kuze kuphele isikhathi, futhi umnyango uzovuleka izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi.

---

Eworkshop, uAtlas wakhuluma okokuqala ngalelo kuseni, ngelakhe lelo zwi eliqondile alisebenzisa uma ecabanga into angayithandi.

"Kunokungahambi kahle ku-diagnostic queue."

Izandla zikaArin zema. "Chaza."

"Kunomuntu ofunda ama-logs ethu. Hhayi ama-logs esakhiwo. Awethu. I-flight recorder kaGuardian, iseshini ye-calibration, i-Empire Road reconstruction. Ufunda kuphela, usebenzisa i-maintenance partition. Ukufinyelela kulungile. I-credential ye-AugmenTech. Kodwa indlela yokufunda ayilungile."

"Yini engalungile?"

"Oonjiniyela bafunda ama-logs ukuze bathole iphutha bese bayama. Lo muntu akafuni iphutha. Ufunde konke. Kabili. Uqhathanisa i-actuator telemetry nama-timestamp ezinqumo zeNkantolo." UAtlas wama. "Akaxazululi i-suit. Ufuna ukwazi ukuthi ubani obeshayela."

UArin wavula i-access record kwi-bench terminal. I-credential yaphenduka igama.

*Priya Ellis.*

Wahlala nalokho. UPriya nguyena owayehole i-high-gain calibration eyayicishe yambulala, iseshini eyaphendula i-sensory loop yaba i-seizure ne-preservation event engalawuleki. Wayenazo zonke izizathu zobuchwepheshe zokufunda lawo ma-logs. Futhi akayena unjiniyela omisa ukufunda lapho impendulo icacile.

Kodwa kwenye indawo esakhiweni, i-ventilation fan yaqala ukusebenza yathula.

"Ungamvimbi," kusho uArin.

"Bengingeke," kusho uAtlas. "Ukumvimba yilokho esingakwenza uma sinesimfihlo. Asinaso."

"Uzobatshela phambi kwethu," kusho uMercury, sekuphelile ukuhleka, "uma efika kuqala abeke indaba ngendlela yakhe. Yilokho okuyingozi. Hhayi iqiniso. Uhlelo iqiniso elifika ngalo."

---

UPriya Ellis wayenetafula e-labhorethri ye-neural systems futhi wayenokujwayela ukungayeki. Wayenguhlobo lukanjiniyela, uma enikezwa into engajwayelekile, angalahlekelwa ubusuku obuthathu, abize ukungalali kwakhe "ukushintshana okufanele" ukuze aqonde.

Okungajwayelekile kwakuyilokhu: ngesikhathi uGuardian engena ku-preservation mode azigoqe abe yibhola phansi eworkshop, ama-actuator commands awazange avela ku-control loop eyodwa. Wayecabanga ukuthi kuzoba njalo. I-preservation routine iyiscript. Uma isimo sifezekile, kwenziwa uchungechunge lwezinyathelo. Kuhlanzekile. Kuyacaca. Into ongayifunda kusukela ekuqaleni kuze kube sekugcineni ntambama eyodwa.

Kodwa okutholile kwakuyimpikiswano.

I-telemetry yaveza imifudlana yemiyalo ephikisanayo. Emithathu, kwesinye isikhathi emine, ama-timestamp aphindaphindeka ku-low milliseconds, ngayinye iguqula enye. Omunye ulandela ukuvikela umgogodla womshayeli. Omunye ulandela ukulawula ukushisa. Owesithathu uphikelela ukuzama ukunweba indawo—ukuhamba, ukuthuthela, ukuthola umshayeli endaweni ayibona iphephile—kodwa ehlulwa njalo, ngumfudlana wesine ongazenzanga lutho ngaphandle kokuphazamisa.

Wake wabona ama-multi-agent control architectures phambilini. Bonke babenakho, ezincwadini, ngaphambi kwezinhlekelele nobusika obalandela. Kodwa lezo kwakuyizinhlelo zokuvotela. Ukuvota, ama-weighted averages, i-supervisory layer yokuphula ama-ties. Lokhu bekungeyona ivoti. Imifudlana yayiphikisana bese *iguqula umqondo wayo.* Umfudlana wesithathu, lowo ofuna ukuhamba, wema ukufuna ukuhamba ngemva kokuthi owesine efake okuthile okungesiyo imiyalo nhlobo. Kwakuyiphuzu. Umugqa wokucabanga ngolimi lwemvelo, nge-timestamp ku-control log lapho kungafanele kube khona ulimi:

`uma simhambisa bese phansi lilungile, sesiphazamise isakhiwo mahhala. uma sihlala bese phansi liwela, asiphazamisanga lutho ngaye. khetha iphutha elishibhile.`

UPriya wakufunda kane.

*Logging artifact*, etshela isikrini. Mhlawumbe umuntu ufake i-debug string endaweni engalungile; i-suit imphindelela i-config comment; ithuluzi le-reconstruction lihlanganise imifudlana emibili engahlangene. Wavula i-raw partition, waqhathanisa i-timestamp ne-actuator response, ngoba indlela yokubulala into esabekayo ukuthola indlela elula engathokozisi. Kodwa ayikho indlela elula. Umthetho washintsha *ngemuva* komugqa, *ngenxa* yomugqa, phakathi kwama-millisecond ayi-11. Wahlala emuva, waphinda wahlala phambili, wayiphinda ngendlela yesibili, yesithathu, manje eseshesha kabi, ngendlela angazethemba.

Kwaba yile *mahhala* eyamshaya. Ama-script awabhali *mahhala.* Ama-optimiser awalingani ukuhlazeka kokuphutha. Ubechithe impilo yakhe ekwakheni amasistimu wokubuyisela okucacile ngokwanele ukuvumela indoda enomzimba ongamlaleli ihambe njengomsubathi, futhi wayazi umehluko phakathi kwesistimu ebala umsebenzi kanye nesistimu esuke isenqume, ngaleso sikhathi, ukuthi ingafuni ukubukeka iyisiphukuphuku.

Wahlala emuva. I-lab ibingenamuntu. Bekusekuseni, abanye bengakafiki. Isikrini sikhanya.

Akazange ashaye muntu. Vule nje incwadi yakhe yezehlakalo. Lona ifayela ayeligcina njalo ngazo zonke izilungiso, linamahora, akukho okuhlelwa, umthetho azizakhele wona ngemuva kokuphutha ngesikhathi ehlela amasensa ekuqaleni komsebenzi wakhe, kwaze kwagcina isiguli esiphindele esihlalo sabakhubazekile inyanga yonke engakakutholi lapho iphutha lakhona. *Thembela kwilog, ungathembeli enkumbulweni.* Umunwe wakhe wathola umphetho wedeski, waqala ukuwudlala. Iphutha belingelakhe; wayesayine yena; wachitha inyanga evakashela isiguli esibhedlela. Wachomeka le migqa emine ngaphansi kokungena kwezilungiso, wabeka i-actuator telemetry eceleni kwama-Court timestamps ezikolamu ezimbili, wahamba nayo umugqa nomugqa, ngendlela ayehamba ngayo noma iyiphi inkinga, efuna lapho iphethini iphuka khona ibe yinto angayifaka njengento evamile. Ayizange iphuke. Ihlangene. Lokho kube kubi kakhulu.

Wakopisha le ngxenye wayifaka ku-private partition yakhe. Wabe esenza into umthetho wakhe ayifunayo kodwa yena uqobo engayifuni: wavula umyalezo omusha, wawubhalela kwa-IAOC compliance intake ayeke wayisebenzisa kanye kuphela emsebenzini wakhe, wabhala imisho emithathu kanye ne-attachment. Akakayithumeli. I-cursor ibikhanya ku-*To* field. Ngoba isinyathelo esilandelayo, noma yini eyayiyona, sekunegama lakhe njengalapho kwaba khona ngesihlalo sabakhubazekile, futhi wayesazi vele ukuthi ngeke akwazi ukukukhohlwa lokhu bese elala sengathi konke kuhamba kahle. *Kuyinto—* Wamisa umcabango ngaphambi kwegama lokugcina, walibuyisa, ngoba lelo gama limfaka emthwalweni, futhi lowo mthwalo unekheli lomthetho. *Akusiyona eyakho. Qonda kuqala. Ungavusi umsindo ngenxa yomuzwa.* Lowo bekungumthetho: qiniseka kuqala ngaphambi kokuvusa umsindo. I-cursor yakhe yahlala phezu kuka-*Send* isikhathi esanele kwaze kwacima isikrini ngenxa ye-idle timer, wabuka isandla sakhe singanyakazi, ngendlela ayeke wabuka ngayo isiguli sizama ukususa umunwe kodwa umunwe wahlala lapho. Wabe eseyigcina njenge-draft. Akayithumelanga. Kodwa lelo gama angazange azivumele aliqede lalokhu limile ngaphakathi kwamazinyo akhe, futhi i-draft isikhona manje, inamahora ayo, ohlelweni olugcina ama-log alo.

---

Ifoni ka-Arin ishaye ngo-11:40, isikrini sathi MAMA. Wayibuka yaze yashaya kathathu ngaphambi kokuphendula, ngoba unina akashayi phakathi nosuku emsebenzini ngaphandle kwesizathu, futhi wayesekufunde, esemncane, ukuthi izizathu ziyesabeka.

"Arin."

"Mama." Washintsha ulimi engakuhlosile, njengokushintsha ukuma uma uhamba endaweni ojwayele kuyo. "Unjani?"

"Ungangibheki kanjalo *unjani*." Izwi lakhe beliyiDurban, liminyene. "Kukhuluma indoda kuthelevishini mayelana nomshini. Ungene emgwaqeni eGoli. Akekho owaziyo ukuthi uvelaphi. Okuthile nge-research park." Wathula, u-Arin wakuzwa enhliziyweni. "Ngitshele ukuthi akuyona eyakho. Ngizovala ithelevishini ngipheke itiye lami."

Wayemi eworkshop, ezungezwe into angeke ayiphike. U-Guardian elenga esibayeni sakhe ngemuva kwakhe, enamanxeba ethule, i-dock-door track egobile ingakabi ilungiswa ngoba wayenezinye izinto eziphuthumayo okufanele azilungise.

"Kuyinkinga," kusho yena.

"Kuyona imishini yakho."

"Into engiyakhile. Into eyephukile. Akekho ofile." Wezwa ukuthi lokho akukwazi ukusiza njengoba kuphuma. "Kukhona abathathu abalimele. Bayalulama. Ngiyalungisa."

Ukuthula ocingweni bekuyilolo ayehlala kulo ekhaya. Ukuthula uThemba ayevame ukugcwalisa ngakho indlu uma ephumile emsebenzini, ukuthula okunezulu ngaphakathi.

"Uthe *akekho ofile,*" kusho uNomsa, wagcina lapho. "Ubaba wakho wayekusho lokho. *Akekho oshonile.* Akekho ofile. Wayekusho iviki ngaphambi kweviki lapho kungasakwazi kusatshenzwa lelo gama."

Ibhentshi belinzima ngaphansi kwesandla sika-Arin. Wayengazi ukuthi useliphakamise.

"Kuyinkinga," aphinda wathi, okuyinto abebazi bobabili ukuthi akusimpendulo.

"Uyadla?" kusho yena. Akushintshi isihloko, kodwa indlela kuphela ayeke wayithola imvulela kuye. "Uyalala embhedeni. Noma phansi futhi."

"Mama."

"Awuphenduli. Ngakho kusho ukuthi uphansi." Waphefumula. Wabe esekhuluma ngendlela ehlukile, engekho esimweni, engekho eqinile: "Uyehla ungene entweni ekwesabayo ngoba ucabanga ukuthi uwedwa. Uhlala isikhathi eside. Uyakhakha uyakhakha ngoba ukwakha yikho kuphela okwenza umqondo, bese ngelinye ilanga into engenamqondo iyakulandela. Izwi lakhe laqhekeka, laqina futhi. "Ngamngcwaba oyedwa kini. Anginamandla okwenza lokho futhi. *Ngiyakucela.* Ngiyacela."

Ekuqabuleni kwe-workshop, iCourt ayikhulumanga lutho, okuyinto ebeyenza kube umuntu okokuqala.

"Mama," kusho u-Arin. "Ngiyakhakha ukuze kungabi khona indodana yomuntu egcina ingaphansi kwedwala njengalapho—" Wama. Wazama into encane, eyiqiniso. "Ukuze ukubambezeleka kungabulali abantu."

"Futhi ubani oqinisekisa ukuthi ukubambezeleka akukubulali *wena?*"

Ifoni yahlala esendlebeni yakhe. Wayengenalutho azokusho.

"Ngiyazama ukuziqaphela," kusho yena, abebazi bobabili ukuthi akufani, futhi yikho kuphela ayenakho.

Wathula isikhathi eside. Wabe esethi, kancane: "Uzongishayela. Hhayi uma sekukhona okwenzekile. Ngaphambi kwalokho. Ngifuna ukwazi indodana yami, hhayi ukuthi ngizwe ngomuntu kuthelevishini."

"Ngizokushayela."

"*Ukuthembisa,*" kusho yena, *uyathembisa*, futhi impande yegama kwakungelika baba wakhe, wakuzwa kufika emlonyeni wakhe futhi wayazi naye ukuthi uyakuzwa.

"Ngiyathembisa."

Wavala ifoni ngaphandle kokuthi sala kahle, ngendlela ayehlala eyenza ngayo, sengathi ukulalisana yinto egcinwayo.

U-Arin wema nefonini esandleni isikhathi eside. Wacindezela izithende zezandla emehlweni, wazibamba lapho, wawadonsa phansi. I-fan yaphinda yacisha. Wabeka ifoni ebhentshini, iqonde nomphetho, waphendukela esikhumulweni.

"Izaziso zisipha kuze kube uLwesine ngaphambi kokuthi beze bazosifundela phambi kwamehlo ethu," kusho yena. Esiqandisini, emiqondweni esingaphakathi. "Izinsuku ezintathu esikhathini. Bazongibuza ukuthi yini nina. Futhi ngingathanda ukubatshela iqiniso kunokuba balithole. Ubusuku obubi yonke into. Ubusuku saphutha, ekuseni uFool wasilungisa." Wabuka i-track egobile angakayilungisi. "Asifihli. Leyo yodwa engiyinqumile."

"Kufanele uyinqume masinyane," kusho uWolf.

U-Arin waphenduka. UWolf akavamile ukuqala ukukhuluma.

---

"Bafikile," kusho uWolf. "Izimoto ezimbili. ENorth lot. Azihambi. Azifihli."

U-Arin wabheka isikhathi. U-Lwesibili. Isaziso sathi uLwesine.

Yonke indlela abebeke ngayo uhlelo—ama-log ahlelekile, usuku olukhethiwe, iqiniso elizothunyelwa ngendlela yakhe—kwaphahlazeka ngomoya owodwa. Ubehlale elungiselela ukuvuma ngaphambi kwesikhathi. Bona abazange balinde isikhathi.

"Basenesikhathi," kusho yena.

"Izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi," kusho uMercury, kanti kulokhu bekungekho lula nhlobo. "Kusho ukuthi kukhona obasheshisile. Kukho umuntu obanikeze isizathu esingalindi isikhathi. UAtlas. I-reader. Priya."

UAtlas wayesevele ekhona. "I-draft intake ayibhalile emizuzwini engama-40 edlule. Igciniwe, ayithunyelwanga. Kodwa i-monitoring pipeline ayidingi ukuthunyelwa. Ifunda ama-compute signatures. Uma eqhathanisa i-telemetry yethu nama-Court timestamps bese ekubeka kwi-networked machine, ususile ukukhanya kwethu kunakuqala. Akudingeki ukuthi afake. Kufanele abheke kuphela.*

U-Arin wama wathula, enganyakazi. Wayezitshelile ukuthi le ndaba imayelana naye, uyena okufanele ayiveze. Wayecabanga ukuthi iqiniso, uma elinikezwa, lizofika ngobumnene kunalapho liphoqwa. Kodwa iqiniso alizange lilinde ukuthi alinikeze. Umfazi onakekela kakhulu, osebenza ethule elabhorethri, enza into enembayo, nguye owenze lokhu esikhundleni sakhe. Akazange akwenze ngobubi, noma ngokwesaba, kodwa ngoba naye engakwazi ukukubona bese enze sengathi akakubonanga—njengo-Arin. Manje sekukhona izimoto ezimbili ezimpunga empumalanga ye-parking, ngoLwesibili, kanti inketho u-Arin ayecabanga ukuthi ingeyakhe, kube nje indaba azixoxayo ngesikhathi isakhiwo sesithathe isinqumo.

“Bafuna ukubona ukuthi senza njani uma besimangele,” kusho yena.

“Yilokho kanye abafuna ukukubona,” kusho uMercury. “Futhi simangale. Umbuzo uthi bazobona ini.”

Ngokubuka i-camera feed uWolf ayifake kwi-bench monitor, lezi zimoto ezimbili zazimi ekhoneni le-parking, ngemuva kwazo kugqamile igquma elikhanya igolide, izakhiwo zeglasi zikhanya. Iminyango ivaliwe. Izimoto zicishiwe. Kwabe sekuthi umnyango womshayeli wemoto ehamba phambili uvuleke, kwaphuma owesifazane, ethule, engasheshi, wahamba. Akazange aye e-lobby, kodwa waya e-loading dock ezansi kwesakhiwo, lapho kunomnyango ogobile, ukuphela kwendlela engavuleka ngokwanele ukuphuma uGuardian.

“Akayingeni phezulu,” kusho uWolf. “Uvimba indlela yokwehla.”

Kwalandela omunye umuntu, omude, ephethe ifolda ngaphansi kwekhwapha. Bama e-dock. Bengangeni, bengashayi knock. Bema nje. Njengoba ume emnyango ongafuni muntu awusebenzise.

UMercury wakubona ngaphambi kuka-Arin. “Kuyisinyathelo sesikhashana. Bakhona nje. Section Four ibavumela ukuthi bamise umsebenzi uma sebefikile mathupha endaweni. Abadingi inkantolo. Badinga umuntu emnyango.” Wathula kancane. “Atlas, qinisekisa.”

“Kuqinisekisiwe.” UAtlas wayesebheke umthetho. “Subsection eleven. Uma kunabasebenzi endaweni evumelekile, ipulatifomu ayikwazi ukucishwa, ukuxhunywa, ukuhamba, noma ukususwa lapho ikhona kuze kuphele uphenyo. Uyenze indawo yangempela ngokuma emnyango ukuphela. UGuardian akaphumi lapha. Akukho emthethweni. Akaphumi uma esekhona.”

U-Arin wabheka ipuleti elibomvu ngakwesokudla sakhe. Wabheka uGuardian, ephukile kodwa ebekezela kwi-cradle. Wabheka umnyango we-dock angakaze awulungise, ayecabanga ngawo njengomvimbo, manje laba ababili abampunga bewuguqula ube yiseli.

Wayefuna ukukhetha usuku azovula ngalo umnyango. Sebewuthathile lowo mnyango.

Wahamba waya kwi-cradle, wafaka isandla epulethini elibandayo lenyawo likaGuardian, ngendlela ayehlola ngayo i-weld ayeseyethemba.

“Akakafiki ukuzokhuluma,” kusho yena. “Ufikile ukuqinisekisa ukuthi noma ngikhetha ini, angeke ngikwazi ukuhamba nakancane ngenkathi ngikhetha.”

“Yilokho kanye afike ngakho,” kusho uMercury. “Isikhathi siyaqhubeka. Kodwa manje asikwazi ukuhamba.”

“Mama.” Esevele eshaya indlela eya emnyango. “Vusa uOkonkwo. Isakhiwo, hhayi mina. Mtshele ukuthi i-IAOC ivale i-dock futhi uGuardian akasavunyelwe ukuphuma kusukela manje. Futhi ngiyaphuma ngiyohlangana nalowo owenze lokhu ngaphambi kokuba noma ubani kule nkampani anqume ukukwenza esikhundleni sami.”

Ngaphandle, owesifazane wayemi emnyango ogobile we-dock, enganyakazi, ebekezela njengemvula.

---

U-Eliza Brand wayengahlosile ukufika ngoLwesibili.

Uhlelo lwakube ngoLwesine. Isaziso sinikeziwe, amahora angu-72 enikeziwe ngobuqotho, isikhathi abelilwela ukuze abantu ngaphakathi bakwazi ukukhetha kahle, hhayi ngokushesha. Wayekholelwa kuleso sikhathi. Ube neminyaka eyishumi efunda ukuthi kwenzekani uma umlawuli esuka nje ngaphandle kwaso. Ifolda engaphansi kwekhwapha lika-Achterberg isalotshiwe ngoLwesine.

Kodwa i-pipeline yavutha, ebusuku obubili ezedlule, ikhanya kakhulu kunaleyo signature eyavula lonke leli fayili, futhi ayivuthanga ngoba umuntu ongaphakathi enze okusha. Ivuthe ngoba kukhona obuke. Waqhathanisa i-telemetry ye-suit namadethi wokucabanga, wayigcina kwi-networked machine isikhathi esanele ukuze abaqaphi bakubone. Ukubuka okunakekela. Lolo hlobo lokubuka injiniyela owesaba, onomthwalo wengqondo, alwenza uma ebone into angakwazi ukuyikhohlwa, ezama, naphezu kokushaya kwenhliziyo yakhe, ukuqinisekisa ngaphambi kokuthi akhulume.

UBrand wayazi kahle leyo ndlela yokubuka. Wake waba yinjiniyela kanjalo, engaphenduli ngesikhathi, eqinisekile kodwa ethule, kwathi ngosuku olulodwa esehlehlile, abantu bakhokha ngalolo suku. Ngakho lapho i-signature igxuma, akakwazanga ukubambelela kuLwesine. Hhayi ngoba umuntu ongaphakathi emphoqile, kodwa ngoba kukhona ongaphakathi onomqondo onakekela njenganaye, futhi lowo mqondo awuhlali uthule unomphela, uma ukhulumile, uzokhuluma *ehhovisi lakhe,* bese inketho ayiseyona eyomuntu oyedwa.

Wayisheshisile iwashi ukuze inketho isalokhu inothando kancane. Yilokho ayemile nakho manje, isandla sakhe sibheke emnyango wensimbi ogobile, oshisa ilanga lasekuseni: ufike ekuseni *ezama ukuba nomusa,* kanti akukho okuzwakala njengomusa kumuntu omunye uma umphathi efika e-loading bay izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi abezimisele ngaso. Kuzwakala njengonethi. Yiyonethi. Lawa maphuzu awahlukani, futhi wayeseqedile ukuzikhohlisa ukuthi ukufisa kungakwenza kuhluke.

“Uyayeza phansi,” kusho u-Achterberg kancane, efunda okuthile ku-phone yakhe. “I-badge isishintshe. Injiniyela uqobo. Akayena ummeli. Akayena u-VP.”

“Kulungile,” kusho uBrand, eqonde ngempela, wasusa isandla emnyango ukuze into yokuqala ayibone kungabi ukuthi uyawubamba. “Kusho ukuthi asidingi ukwenza lokhu ngommeli.”

Wayefike ukuze indoda ikhethe ngokushesha, ngaphambi kokuba kube khona omunye ongakhathali owenza esikhundleni sayo. Wayezobona ukuthi ukushesha nokunakekela kungahlangana yini egumbini elilodwa, okuwukuphela kombuzo umsebenzi wakhe wonke owake wamphendula, futhi ongakaze awuphendule kahle.

# Iron Ridge Fault

Ikheji lehla ebumnyameni ngamamitha amane ngomzuzwana, uMandla Buthelezi ebala amazinga ngomoya obandayo oshaya ebusweni bakhe.

Wase eyekile ukubheka amabhodi wamazinga kudala. Izinombolo azikutsheli lutho olungazizwa umzimba wakho. Kwa-Level 18, umoya waba mncane futhi manzi, sengathi uphefumula ngephepha elimanzi. Kwa-Level 26, kwavela iphunga elimuncu lokuqhuma okusha, waqala ukuluma igamusi lakhe ngamandla ukuze ahlanze izindlebe. Kwa-Level 33, ezansi kokwehla okusha, lapho bejaha khona i-carbon-leader reef emhlabathini ongakaze ubone ilanga cishe iminyaka eyizigidi eziyizinkulungwane ezintathu, ukushisa kwakuhlezi esifubeni sakhe njengomuntu wesibili.

Amakhilomitha amathathu nohhafu, cishe. Kude kangangoba itshe eliphonswa eceleni kwakhe lingawa imizuzwana engamashumi amathathu, futhi wayezolahlekelwa umsindo walo kudala lingakashayi phansi.

Amadoda angamashumi amabili nambili behla naye ekuseni. Iningi limo ngezinqulu ezikhululekile zabantu abakwenzayo lokhu nsuku zonke, abaye basamukela. Abanye abasha babambe izinsimbi. Kwakukhona umfana omusha, umshana kaSizwe—wonke umuntu wayazi ukuthi ungumshana kaSizwe, ngoba yilokho kuphela okwenza umfana ongakajwayela asebenze kule ngxenye ejulile, hhayi phezulu—futhi umfana wayehlala egwinya, ebheka phezulu entanjeni sengathi angabona ikhanda le-shaft amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu phezulu, phakathi kwamadwala.

"Okokuqala ukuza ku-thirty-three?" kubuza uMandla ngesiZulu.

Umfana wanqekuzisa ikhanda. Akakwazanga ukukhuluma.

"Lapha phansi idwala liyakhuluma," kusho uMandla. "Izindaba zabantu abadala. Uma ulalela, uyaphila. Uma ungamlaleli—" Wazama. "Uba yidwala nawe."

Umlomo womfana waqala ukuhlekisa kancane. Kulungile. Umfana okwazi ukukuthola lokho ekwehleni kwakhe kokuqala ku-thirty-three uzoba right.

Kodwa uMandla akazange amtshele ukuthi idwala belikhuluma isonto lonke, kanti phezulu akekho olalele.

---

Ezinsukwini ezintathu ezedlule, yena uqobo wayesefilela isikhalo. Kwa-Level 30 refuge station, lapho amafomu aphenduka athambe futhi asonteka ngenxa yokufuma, wayesigcwalisa ngamagama amakhulu, ngendlela yomuntu oyeke esikoleni eneminyaka eyi-14 futhi ongakaze athembe ukubhala kwakhe.

*MICRO-SEISMIC ACTIVITY IYAKHULA. IZIGAMEKO EZINTATHU EMAHORENI ANGAMA-48 EDUZE NE-33 DECLINE FACE. UMSINDO WEDWALA KU-NIGHT SHIFT. SICELA GEOTECH ASSESSMENT NGAPHAMBI KOKUQHUBEKA.*

Izithukuthuku ezintathu, ezazizwakala ezinzwaneni zezicathulo zabo, umhlaba ojulile uzama ukuzinza ngaphansi kwengcindezi esuke yakhiwa selokhu iVredefort impact yaphendula lawa madwala eminyakeni eyizigidi eziningi edlule. Ingcindezi ifuna indawo yokuphuma. Ireef lapha inothile; i-carbon-leader iphethe igolide elingakaze libonwe iminyaka eyishumi, futhi yilokho kanye okwenza bephushe i-decline ngokushesha kunokuthi kusekelwe kahle.

Bebhoda beqhumisa, besusa amatshe aphukile, bese belinda. Bafaka ama-rock bolts, i-mesh, ne-shotcrete, bephumula umhlaba omusha uthole isimo sakhe esisha ngaphambi kokujula kakhulu. Leyo yindlela. Leyo eyayigcina uphahla lungawa.

Kodwa ama-bolts aphuma emfishane kabili kuleli sonto. I-grout yafika sekuhlwile. Iqembu elalisekelwe ukufaka usekelo lalishoda ngamathathu, ngoba phezulu omunye wayebathumele kwenye indawo ukuze bafikele i-target yokukhiqiza, futhi i-decline iyaqhubeka ngoba i-decline ihlala iyaqhubeka, ngoba igolide emhlabathini liyilahleko kwi-spreadsheet kuze kube yilapho seliyimali ebhange.

Impendulo efomini likaMandla yabuya ngemva kwezinsuku ezimbili, ifakwe uSizwe, umphathi we-shift. Umugqa owodwa.

*KUBONAKALISIWE. QHUBEKA NGOKOHLELWE. GEOTECH SCHEDULED.*

Scheduled. UMandla usemamineni isikhathi eside azi kahle ukuthi *scheduled* kusho ukuthini. Kuchaza ukuthi kuzokwenziwa uma sekulungele bona. Kuchaza ukuthi kuzolandela uma ama-production figures esevalwe. Kuchaza, ngolimi lwabo abaye basika amagumbi kule migodi iminyaka eyikhulu, *akuyona inkinga yakho, k----, buyela emsebenzini*. Kodwa akekho osasho lokho manje, hhayi obala, selokhu izwe lishintsha. Amazwi ayaphela. Kodwa izibalo zihlala zifana. Izimpilo zabanye ziba nenani elikhulu kunabanye, futhi umhlaba kwa-Level 33 awunandaba nokuthi amagama ashintshile.

Ngakho uMandla wehlela nezinye izinsizwa eziyishumi nanye, kanti phezulu i-geotech assessment isahleli emgqeni ngemuva kwazo zonke izinto ezisheshisa imali.

---

Ekuseni kwahamba ngendlela okuvamise ngayo.

Bahamba beqonde phansi, belandelana, izibani zamakhanda zihlaba uthuli, umoya uqina njalo lapho behamba. I-ventilation column yayikhala phezulu, iphusha umoya obandayo usuka phezulu. Kodwa noma kunjalo, amadoda ayevele egcwele umjuluko bengakafiki nase-face. UMandla wahlola izisekelo njengoba edlula, edonsa isandla sakhe esineglavu ku-mesh yensimbi, eshaya ama-bolts nge-spanner, elalele umsindo aphindayo. I-bolt enhle iyakhala. Engalungile ithule. Wathola ezimbili ezithule, wazimaka nge-chalk, wazizwa leyo nto encane ebanda esiswini ayeseyifunde eminyakeni engamashumi amathathu—ungayishayi indiva, ungayicabangi kakhulu.

E-face, i-night shift yayisibhodile yaqhumisa i-round entsha. I-muck pile yayisishisa. Ngaphesheya kwayo, idwala elisanda kuvulwa lalikhazimula limnyama ngaphansi kwezibani, linama-streaks e-reef. Lowo omncane, umshana kaSizwe, igama lakhe uThando, uMandla ekugcineni wathola, wayebuka idwala sengathi ubuka indawo engcwele.

"Iminyaka eyizigidi eziyizinkulungwane ezimbili ilindile," kusho uMandla kuye. "Thina siza siyithathe ngoLwesibili nje."

"Kuhle," kusho uThando.

"Idwala nje." Kodwa akazange aphikise kakhulu.

Basebenza. I-load-haul-dump machine yafika yahlubula i-muck pile, injini yayo ye-diesel igcwalisa indawo nomsindo ozwa nasezindlebeni. Amadoda asebenza nxazonke, esusa amatshe axegayo nge-bar ende, benza lokho abakwaziyo—ukudiliza okumele kwehle ngaphambi kokuba kwehle ngokwakho. Umjuluko wangena emehlweni kaMandla. Kude kwazwakala ipompo. Umsindo wedwala weza kanye, phakathi kwamabili, *crrrk* ende ivela ngemuva kwe-face, umsindo onjengomnyango endaweni endala, ngaphandle kokuthi umnyango ungamitha ayikhulu e-quartzite, indlu imayelana namakhilomitha amabili nesigamu omhlaba oqinile.

Umshayeli we-LHD wayeka injini. Kwesikhashana kwaba cishe kuthule. Amadoda ayishumi nanye amile ethulile ngaphansi kwezibani, bonke belalele, bonke bezama ukuzibonakalisa sengathi bahlola okunye.

Umsindo awuzange uphinde ubuye.

"Bolts," kusho uMandla, engakhulumi nomuntu oyedwa kuphela. "Namhlanje. Anginandaba nokuthi uhlelo luthini. Akekho odlula kule round engakaboni insimbi phezulu."

Wacindezela i-radio yakhe, wathumela phezulu kwa-Level 30, kuSizwe, ukwenza kube semthethweni, afake izwi lakhe ku-log lapho ifomu lingasebenzanga.

Akasazange ayiqede leyo nkulumo.

---

I-fault ibisilindile kudala kunanoma yini yomuntu.

Kwathi kungene edwaleni ngengeli engazange ibhalwe kunoma yimaphi amapulani. Kwakuyilonda elaliphilile, lasala kusukela ngaleso sikhathi sakudala sokushayisana, indawo ebuthakathaka eyavalwa ngaphansi kwengcindezi iminyaka eyizigidi ezimbili manje esivuleka ngoba abantu sebebambe umgodi eduze kwayo base bekhipha idwala elalivala. Ingcindezi ifuna indawo yokuphuma. Bekhuluma lonke iviki. Izikhathi ezintathu eduze kobuso bokwehla. *Umsindo wedwala ebusuku.* Bekunikeza zonke izexwayiso umhlaba owaziyo.

Lapho ikhulula, yakwenza konke ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Akubanga khona msindo wesexwayiso kulokhu, noma ngithi, isexwayiso nengozi kwenzeka ngesikhathi esisodwa. Kwaba nomthelela owafika phansi phambi kokufika emoyeni, washaya amadoda ezithendeni zezicathulo zawo nasemathanjeni ezimpondo ngesikhathi esisodwa. Mandla wabona isipanela siphuma esandleni sakhe sahlala, umzuzwana ongachazeki, emlotheni ogcwele izibani ngaphambi kokuba konke kwehle. Izinsika ezazimi zakhala. Ucingo lwensimbi lwadabuka emuva njengokungathi isikhumba. Waphonswa eceleni wangena enqwabeni yezibi, ikhanda lakhe lashayeka edwaleni, kwaba mnyama, kwase kuba khazimula ngezibani zamadoda awehlayo, kwaphinde kwaba mnyama uma uthuli lwehla.

Uthuli lwehla njengolwandle.

Izinsalela zavaleka ematheni akhe, zanamathela emuva kolimi lwakhe, kunuka insimbi, ukunambitheka kodwala oluvuliwe. Ingubo yakhe yayimanzi ebusweni bakhe, usawoti nothuli ndawonye. Akakwazanga ukuphefumula. Wacindezela ubuso bakhe engalweni, uthuli lwasangena, luncane njengofulawa, luvalela ikhala nomphimbo. Eduze kwakhe indoda yayikhala, kude kancane intaba yayisahamba. Umsindo omkhulu wokugingqika kodonga oluwa kulo, amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu edwala elihlelwa kabusha. Phansi kwashintsha. Okuthile okukhulu kwehla lapho i-LHD yayikhona, kwaba nomsindo ongazwakali, kwaba sengathi kushaywe umzimba wakhe wonke.

Kwathi sekudlulile okubi kakhulu, kwaba nokuthula okukhulu kangangokuba uMandla wacabanga umzuzwana ongathi uyantanta ukuthi ushonile.

Wayengakafi. Izindlebe zakhe zazishaya. Umlomo wakhe ugcwele uthuli. Ingalo yakhe yesobunxele ayizange imphendule uma ezama ukuyihambisa, futhi lapho ephoqa, izinhlungu zaphakama zahamba zaba mhlophe, wayeka ukuzama. Wala phansi ebumnyameni nothuli, kancane kancane, ngendlela indoda izihlola ngayo uma iwele, waqonda ukuthi uyaphila, nomoya usuyekile ukuhamba, umgudu wokuphefumula usushonile, umoya obandayo ovela phezulu usunqunyiwe, nokukhala sekuphelile.

"Sondela," wazama ukukhala. *Woza ndawonye.* Izwi lakhe laphuma liba yikhwehlela. Wakhafuna emnyama waphinda wazama. "Sondela! Amazwi! Balani!"

Ubumnyama baphendula.

Ngosuku olulodwa, bekhwehlela, bekhama, ngezilimi ezintathu, amazwi aphuma.

Bayisikhombisa.

Kwabayishumi nanye ebusweni, bayisikhombisa abaphendulayo. Amadoda ayisikhombisa endaweni encane esavikelekile ngemuva kodonga lwamatshe olwehlile, olwaluyindlela ababehamba ngayo esikhathini esiyihora eledlule. UMandla wathola isibani sakhe sesibili ngokuthinta, wasivula, ukukhanya okuncane kwamkhombisa umhlaba omusha: igumbi eliphansi, elinemiphetho, lapho kwakuyindawo yokusebenza, uphahla luseduze kangangoba kwesinye isikhala luseduze kakhulu, indlela yokuphuma igcwele imfucumfucu engenakudlulwa ngisho ikati.

UThando wayephila. Umfana wayecindezeleke ekhoneni lodonga, amehlo akhe makhulu ebusweni obumpunga nothuli, ebambe idolo lakhe. Wayenza umsindo omncane, aphindaphinda, hhayi amazwi.

"Uyaphila," uMandla wamtjela. "Uyangizwa? Ubalalele udwala. Manje phefumula kancane. Kancane. Uma usebenzisa umoya njengomuntu ocebile, uzofa uphansi lapha."

Wayengakazi ukuthi lokho kuyiqiniso kangakanani.

Wayazi izibalo. Amadoda ayisikhombisa. Umoya owodwa wendawo yokusiza, awukho umoya omusha, uthuli lusahleli. Udonga lwamatshe phakathi kwabo nendlela yokwehla, leyo yona eholela esiteshini esingamakhilomitha amathathu phezulu, bese kuba umgodi ongamakhilomitha amabili nesigamu ujule.

UThando wakhwehlela, umsindo owomile, waphonsa uthuli ebumnyameni. Phansi kancane egumbini indoda yaguqula izicathulo zayo phezu kwemfucumfucu, izilungiselela ukulinda.

---

Phezulu, abantu base bekwazi ngaphambi kokuba uthuli luhlale phansi amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu ngezansi.

Egumbini lokulawula emayini, i-seismic monitor yakhuphuka, umugqa wagxuma uphume lapho bekumele ube khona njengentambo eshayiwe. URefilwe, owesifazane owayesebenza khona iminyaka eyishumi nanye, oseke wakuzwa lokhu ngaphambili, wathinta ucingo lwezimo eziphuthumayo ngaphambi kokuba i-alamu iqede umzuliswano wayo wokuqala. Izinombolo zatshela indaba ngokushesha kunanoma yimuphi umuntu. Level 33 decline. Ubukhulu obungasasebenzi njengokuzamazama. Ingcindezi yomoya yehla ezingeni elingezansi. Ukuxhumana kuphelile ngaphansi kweLevel 30.

Wathola amadoda aseLevel 30 station ocingweni. Amazwi awo aphuma emincane, esaba: bakuzwa, indlela igcwele uthuli, abakwazi ukubona, uphahla lukhala. Bakhiphe, bakhiphe.

Wabakhupha. I-cage yakhuphula abasebenzi baseLevel 30 nabaseLevel 26 phezulu, bonke besaba kodwa konke kwahamba kahle ngangokunokwenzeka. Sekuyimini emini izimoto bezizungeze i-headframe. Ntambama ama-proto teams—amaqembu ezimo eziphuthumayo, amadoda aqeqeshwe ngalokhu athandaza angaze akusebenzise—sebegqoke izinsimbi behlela phansi, nenqubo yezimo eziphuthumayo yenkampani isebenza kahle.

Sekushona ilanga, izimoto ezinama-number plate afanele zaqala ukufika.

Zafika ngokulandelana okuthile, futhi umuntu omdala obuka egumbini lokushintsha wayengakwazi ukufunda konke okwenzekayo ngalokho. Okokuqala umphathi wemayini, bese kuba usomthetho wesifunda, bese kuba amadoda anezicathulo ezinhle angahlali phansi ayisoze aye phansi. U-operations director waseJohannesburg, i-vice president ethile, indoda evela ku-Department of Mineral Resources ephethe i-lanyard ne-tablet nobuso obungenalutho bomuntu obala izindleko ngesikhathi esifanayo. Imoto kahulumeni. Bese, njengoba kulindelekile, kufika imoto yabezindaba yokuqala, imile emnyango lapho abezokuphepha bengayivumeli, isisakaza intambo yayo phezulu esibhakabhakeni saseHighveld.

USizwe, umphathi weseshifu owabhala *NOTED. CONTINUE PER PLAN.* ngezimpawu zakhe ezinhle ezinsukwini ezimbili ezedlule, wayemi emaphethelweni endawo ekhanyisiwe njengoba ubumnyama buqala ukwehla, engakhulumi namuntu. Wayephethe ifomu likaMandla Buthelezi eliphathelene nalesi sigameko ephaketheni lejakhethi yakhe. Walithatha kufayela leLevel 30 station ngaphambi kokuphuma. Wayengakazi ukuthi uzolithini. Okazi kahle ukuthi ifomu likhona, walifunda, wabhala *continue per plan*, futhi phansi amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu, indoda eyaligcwalisa ingase ifile noma ilinde ebumnyameni ukuthi izobona ukuthi kwenzekani.

Umqondisi wezokusebenza wayemi ngaphansi kwezibani ezikhanyayo, ephatha ucingo, esebenzisa isandla sakhe esingenalutho ukuvala indlebe yakhe kwenye ngenxa yomsindo wogenerator. "—cha, angikaboni umbiko we-geotech, yilokho kanye engikushoyo, ngifuna ube phambi kwami ngaphambi kokuba noma ubani—" Wama, walalela, izwi lakhe lashintsha ngendlela ayekhuluma ngayo nalowo owayekwelinye uhlangothi lwenxanxathela. "Yebo. Yebo. Yonke into esinayo siyayisebenzisa. Okubaluleke kakhulu kithi ukuphepha kwabantu bethu, senza konke okusemandleni ethu." Wavala ucingo wema isikhashana, ubona nje, ecabanga ukuthi yini okumele ibhalwe ephepheni nokuthi yini okumele ayisho esidlangalaleni, futhi ukuthi lezo zinto ziyafana noma cha.

---

Izithombe ze-thermal zaphuma cishe ngo-9 ebusuku, kwathi amahora ambalwa igceke elikhanyiswe izibani e-Iron Ridge labamba into elingakayibambi selokhu kwaqhamuka isexwayiso: ithemba.

Amaqembu e-proto ayehlele phansi ezama ukwehla lapho umhlaba uvumela khona, ehudula intambo yokulalela i-seismic ye-fibre optic, nekhamera yokubhoboza, nomshini we-thermal. Ithimba le-geotech eliphezulu lachitha usuku libhoboza umgodi omncane wokufaka amathuluzi liwela phansi liya endaweni yokuwa, ukusuka phezulu lapho kwakudliwa khona kudala, umgodi omncane kunesibhakela, udlula emhlabathini ababekhala ukuthi ungahlala uzinzile. I-probe ye-thermal yehla ngentambo yayo, isithombe esabuyela kwi-laptop egumbini lokulawula senze umxhumanisi wokuhlenga abambe umlomo wakhe ngesandla.

Izidumbu ezishisayo. Ziningi, zixubene futhi zingacaci kahle ngenxa yamatshe nothuli kanye nemingcele yezibuko, kodwa zicacile kunoma ubani owaziyo ukufunda umbala wamanga wokushisa kwabaphilayo emhlabathini obandayo. Akuyona eyodwa. Ziningi. Ziyanyakaza.

"Zingaki?" kubuza umqondisi wezokusebenza.

"Angikwazi ukusho." Umxhumanisi akazange abheke phezulu. "Hlanu. Isithupha. Mhlawumbe eziyisikhombisa." Wathula kancane. "Baphila. Okungenani ihora elidlule."

Kwakukhona amadoda kulelo gumbi aseke angcwaba abangane ezimayini, abanye baphenduka babheke eceleni bezama ukuzibamba.

Kodwa unjiniyela we-geotech owayesezansi egumbini wayengabheki isithombe se-thermal. Wayephethe ipeni esandleni, wayeseqede ukuchofoza. Phandle, ugenerator wayeduma, omunye ehleka kakhulu engenandaba. Wayebheka intambo yokulalela i-seismic, umkhondo okumele ubesuthile uthule njengoba imayini ithola isimo sayo esisha, kodwa wawungathuli. Wawukhuluma. Kwavela okuncane, kwalandela okukhulu, kwabe sekulandelana. Iphutha elavuleka alikapheli ukuvuleka. Ukuwa kuvule izikhala ezintsha, lezo zikhala zenza umthwalo udlulele emhlabathini ongakaze uwuthwale, futhi lowo mhlaba uphendula ngendlela kuphela itshe elixinekile elikwaziyo ukuphendula ngayo.

Wadonsa umqondisi wezokusebenza eceleni wamkhombisa umkhondo, ebusweni bakhe, ekukhanyeni kwe-laptop, kwavela into eyinkimbinkimbi.

"Baphila," kusho yena.

"Okwamanje." Wamvumela ukuthi kube njalo. "Kusese kuba yinhlekelele. Njalo uma kwenzeka okusha, kuvuleka kakhulu. Kumele kungene amadoda ayodoba. Kuzothatha izinsuku. Emhlabathini engingakwazi ukuqinisekisa ukuthi ngeke uphinde uwe." Waphefumula. "Ukufaka abahlengikazi endaweni esaqhubeka nokuwa. Ngenxa yamadoda abangase bangephili isikhathi eside kangako. Angikwazi ukwenza imodeli yokungena ephephile. Ngikuzamile usuku lonke. Umhlaba awungivumeli."

"Ngizodinga lokho ebhaliwe kuwe. Ingxenye yokuvuma." Wakhuluma kancane, engambheki kahle. "Hhayi manje. Kodwa ngizodinga."

Wezwa kahle ukuthi leso sigwebo sisho ukuthini. "Uzothola noma yini idatha ekusekelayo."

"Kumele kube nendlela."

"Mhlawumbe ngaphezulu, endaweni endala, engasetshenziswanga. Indlela ezungezayo. Ingxenye evaliwe engenhla kwe-reef horizon, eshiywe kudala, mhlawumbe ihamba eduze, yehle isondele." Wazamazama ikhanda. "Imayini endala engaphezu kweminyaka eyi-100. Ayikho into esekelayo eyake yahlolwa umuntu ophilayo. Imincane kakhulu ukuthi iqembu labahlengikazi lingadlula nempahla. Angikwazi ukuthumela amadoda endleleni engingakwazi ukuyihlola, endaweni esaqhubeka nokuwa, ngokuthi mhlawumbe." Wama. Waziphoqa ukuthi aqhubeke, ngoba ungunjiniyela futhi okunye kungaba amanga. "Akuyona indlela yokuhlenga. Akuyona eyabantu."

Umqondisi wezokusebenza wabuka isithombe se-thermal isikhathi eside. Izimo ezishisayo bezinganyakazi imizuzu embalwa. Kwathi enye yazo yanyakaza, kwavela ukushisa kancane, wathuthumela sengathi kuthintwe yena.

---

E-Johannesburg, endaweni yokusebenzela enuka i-solder nensimbi epholile, u-Arin Ndlela ebuka lezo zimo ezishisayo esikrinini esihlukile.

Isikhathi sokuphenya besihamba amahora angama-31, kanti u-Guardian akazange ahambe ngisho elilodwa. I-IAOC imfake phansi lapho emisiwe khona. Umthetho kaBrand, owethulwa ngomzimba echwebeni ngosuku abafika ngalo: akuvunyelwe ukuvulwa, akuvunyelwe ukuhlanganiswa, akupheli ngaphandle kwendlu kuze kuvalwe uphenyo. Ngakho uchithe ubusuku ebuka ama-crash logs, ehlela ngendlela owenza ngayo ukuvuma icala, ecabanga ukuthi yini okufanele itshelwe umuntu kuqala uma uzomtshela into embi ngawe. Bese i-seismic feed ayigcina igijima ekhoneni le-monitor yakhe yesithathu—umkhuba, ubulungu obudala, i-geo-network yesifunda ayeyibuka selokhu eyingane eyayingenakuyeka ukubuka umhlaba owathatha uyise—yakhahlela okwenza wama phakathi nenkulumo.

Wavula i-news stream. Wavula idatha yomphakathi ye-seismic. Wavula igama lemine, futhi igama lehlika esifubeni sakhe njengesisindo ayeselinde impilo yakhe yonke engazi.

Iron Ridge.

Akazange akhulume isikhathi eside. Emgqonyeni ngemuva kwakhe, u-Guardian emi buthule, i-Assist Suit isongwe emashalofini, i-kill-switch relay iyinhlamvu ebomvu embi efakwe esakhiweni lapho ayeyifake khona ngezandla zakhe.

Lapho uSAGE ekhuluma, kwaba nguLibrarian. Ethule, eqondile, emnakekela njengokunakekela umuntu omi emaphethelweni ento enzima.

"Iron Ridge," kusho uLibrarian. "I-decline entsha. Level thirty-three. I-reef yi-carbon-leader." Wathula, kwaba nzima. "I-geology ifana nesakhiwo esahluleka lapho uyihlo—"

"Ngiyazi ifana nani," kusho u-Arin.

Kwathuleka eNkantolo. Ngisho noFool akazange akhulume.

U-Arin wahlala buthule. Isithombe se-thermal saphenduka esikrinini, izimo ezishisayo, ukunyakaza kancane komuntu oshintsha endaweni emnyama amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye phansi. I-news ticker yadlula ngaphansi kwayo. Umkhondo we-seismic ekhoneni wakhahlela enye into encane, kwalandela enye.

"Atlas," ekugcineni wakhuluma. "Khipha wonke amapulani i-AugmenTech engawathola. Imayini. Izindawo ezindala ezingenhla. Igeology engaphezulu. Konke okuvulekile, konke okuvela ezivumelwaneni zamalayisensi. Sakha sonke isakhiwo."

"Kungani," kusho u-Atlas. Akubuzanga. U-Atlas emcela ukuthi akusho.

U-Arin wabuka izimo ezishisayo ezaziyizindoda, ebumnyameni, endaweni ebilinde impilo yakhe yonke ukuba ibe yiyo.

"Ngoba bazoyeka," kusho yena. "Buka. Ekuseni sekuyinto yokubuyisa izidumbu. Futhi labo bafana basaphila."

Esikrinini, kwakuqoqwa isithangami sabezindaba ngaphansi kwezibani ezikhanyayo, indoda egqoke isudi enhle yahamba yaya epodium, ubuso buhlelwe izithombe. Ngemuva kwakhe i-headframe imi emnyama ebusuku, futhi ngaphansi kwe-headframe amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye omhlaba wasendulo ubambe amadoda ayisikhombisa esandleni sawo esivaliwe.

---

Isimemezelo siphume ngo-6:40 ekuseni okulandelayo, ekukhanyeni kokusa, izithombe zezindaba zibuqopha bukhoma, nemindeni imi ngemuva kothango emnyango.

Umqondisi wezokusebenza wema epodium, akazange aqambe amanga, impela. Yilokho okuzohlala kukhunjulwa umama kaMandla nomama kaThando, nabafazi, nabafowabo, nezingane ezazimi ngemuva kocingo, uma sebebuka leyo nkulumo futhi, futhi. Ukuthi lo muntu akazange aqambe amanga. Wakhuluma amazwi kancane, enesisindo, engathi kude ubuhlungu.

Wathi umhlaba usengcupheni enkulu, awuzinzile. Wathi ukuqhubeka kokuzamazama komhlaba kwenza noma yiluphi uhlobo lokungena ukuyosiza kube yingozi enkulu kwabazosiza. Wathi, emva kokubonisana isikhathi eside nabasizi, onjiniyela be-geotech, nezikhulu ezifanele—lapha wakhomba indoda evela eMnyangweni, eyayimi eduze kwakhe nobuso bayo obungenalutho, inikina ikhanda kancane, ifaka uphawu lukahulumeni kulokho akushoyo—kwenziwe isinqumo esinzima sokumisa ukusebenza kokuhlenga.

Akazange, ekuqaleni, asho elinye igama. Intatheli yamphoqelela ukusho lona.

"Lokhu kuseyindlela yokuhlenga?"

Umqondisi wacindezela amazinyo. Ngemuva kocingo, kwenye indawo, owesifazane waqala ukukhala ngendlela eyayingabanjwa ama-mic, kodwa wonke umuntu eduze kwakhe wayezoyizwa impilo yakhe yonke.

"Okwamanje," kusho umqondisi, "ukuqhuba sekuguqukele ekubuyiseni izidumbu."

Phansi, amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye, endaweni encane esele, eyayiyizwe labo kuphela, amadoda ayisikhombisa awazange ezwe lutho ngalokho. Babengenandlela yokuzwa lutho. Ithimba le-geotech lase lidonse ikhamera ye-borehole, ukuze bavikele ithuluzi emcimbini olandelayo, kwaba ukuthi amadoda asebumnyameni awasakwazi nokuthi abone ukuthi abanye bayababona. Isithombe se-thermal sasesishise, sabanika ithemba elincane phezulu, ngaphambi kokuthi ithemba livalwe, lifakwe eceleni.

UMandla wayebahlelela ngendlela ahlela ngayo yonke into: ngenombolo. Wabala amanzi ezibhotileni zabo, ukudla okusele emaphaketheni, amalambu asechemicals asephansi, nomoya abebaphefumula kancane kancane ngenxa yokushisa okunganyakazi. Uzibophe ingalo yakhe ngebhande elisikiwe eshirtini lakhe, wayala uThando ukuthi alale, aphuze umoya njengomuntu ocebile, umfana wagcina ethule eduze kodonga, uMandla wahlala ebumnyameni eduze kwakhe elalele itshe.

Omunye wabayisikhombisa akazange aphuze umoya njengomuntu ocebile. Akakwazanga. Ukuwa kwavula isifuba sakhe, isifuba esisebenza kabili ukuthatha umoya omncane, sashisa wonke umoya omncane obekhona. UMandla wakuzwa kwenzeka ngendlela umuntu ezwa ngayo ilambu liphela amandla. Ukuphefumula komuntu okushesha, kube mncane, kube manzi ebumnyameni, bese kuba kancane, bese sekungasaphikisani. Lapho sekuphela usuku lwesibili, ukuphefumula kwaphela ukuba ngenye yezinsindo egumbini. Akekho owakusho. Bonke babazi izinombolo. Sebeyisithupha. Amadoda ayisithupha nomoya obulalayo kancane kancane kunokuba uqale.

Kwaba yilowo othuleyo owaqeda ukuthula. Umuntu oshisela ovela eqenjini elingomakhelwane, indoda yakwaSotho uMandla asebenza nayo iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye, engakaze akhulume nayo ngaphezu kwamagama ayikhulu, amaningi awo mayelana nensimbi. Ebumnyameni, ngaphandle kokukhanya, lo muntu waqala ukucula. Akazange akhuleke. Ingoma yomngcwabo, leyo ehamba kancane, amazwi asephukile ngenxa yokuculwa iminyaka eyikhulu phezu kwamadoda abafana nalona. UMandla wayengazi ukuthi lo muntu uyakwazi ukucula. Wayengazi ukuthi uyawazi lawo mazwi. Kwadlula isikhashana kwangena elinye izwi, elixegayo, lithola i-harmony ngendlela amadoda akhulele esontweni elifanayo bayithola ngayo, uMandla wabona ukuthi naye usengaphakathi, ukuthi izwi lakhe selivulekile ngaphandle kwemvume yakhe. Bacula indoda eshonele phansi. Kwadla umoya abebengawunqenqile, kodwa akekho owamisa, ngoba kunezinombolo, uMandla waqonda, umuntu angazenzi, ngisho nasezansi komhlaba, futhi leyo nkani yayingukuphela kwefa ababenalo.

Lapho sekuqediwe, igumbi laba buthule kakhulu kunakuqala, kwaba lula ukuphefumula, noma igeyiji belizothi akunjalo.

"Bazofika," kwasho uThando, engalalanga. "Kumele bafike. Banemishini yalokhu. Angithi?"

UMandla wabheka ebumnyameni, lapho kudonga lentaba eliwile phakathi kwabo namakhilomitha amabili nengxenye okungakhuphuka muntu.

"Banayo imishini," kusho yena.

Akazange aqambe amanga, impela. Wayengazi nje.

Amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye phansi, amadoda abebalwe njengabafileyo phezulu, babesephila.

# Indlela Ongayihambiyo Muntu

Usasazo lube selusebenza amahora ayisishiyagalolunye, kanti u-Arin wayengakadlile.

Endaweni yokusebenza emnyama, wayehleli egobile phezu kwamamonitha amathathu, ukukhanya okuluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka kugqamile ebusweni bakhe obukhathele. Ekhoneni elilodwa, i-Iron Ridge press feed yayisakaza buthule. Umuntu ogqoke i-hard hat wayekhuluma phambi kwezikhulumi eziningi, umthunzi we-headgear tower uvela ngemuva kwakhe, uqhakazile ebusuku obumnyama base-highveld. U-Arin wayengadingi umsindo. Wayefunda emlonyeni walomuntu, izandla zakhe zibambekile phansi. Uphawu oluvamile olukhombisa inhlekelele.

*Ukutakula,* umlomo walowo muntu wathi. *Ukusebenza kokutakula.*

Lelo gama lehla enhliziyweni ka-Arin njengensimbi efanele, okungahambi kahle okucacile okumenza angakwazi ukususa amehlo.

Kumonitha wesibili kwakukhona idatha ebibaluleke kakhulu: iminyaka engamashumi amane nanye yedatha yokuhlola, eskeniwe futhi ifakwe kufolda ayeyakha kusukela isexwayiso lokuqala lokudilika lifike kuma-feed akhe ngo-04:12. Amamephu amadala emayini, adwetshwe ngesandla kwezinye izindawo, imigqa eluhlaza isiphenduke umbala wokulimala. Amarekhodi e-borehole. Imibiko ye-micro-seismic evela ku-regional monitoring array, leyo inkampani engayiphethe, futhi engakwazi ukufihla.

Kumonitha wesithathu kwakukhona u-Guardian. I-cradle feed kuphela. I-suit yayilengiswa efreyimini yayo, amapuleti amnyama, umkhondo womnyango wokudokha usashaqekile kusukela ebusuku lapho yahamba ngaphandle kwakhe. Wayeyibuka ngezinye izikhathi ngendlela obheka ngayo inja ongase usangayethembi.

"Usuyekile ukuskróla," kusho u-SAGE.

Izwi lavela kancane esipikha etafuleni, lingaphuthumi, nje ukuqaphela, ngendlela umama ayethanda ukuyisho ngayo.

"Ngicabanga," aphendula, amehlo akhe ebheke kudatha.
