"Ususe ku-overlay efanayo imizuzu emine. U-Atlas ufuna ukwazi ukuthi usuyitholile noma uyibuka nje."

"Ngiyitholile." U-Arin wasondela. "Ngicabanga ukuthi ngiyitholile."

---

I-Iron Ridge kwakuyimayini endala egqoke izingubo ezintsha.

Umhume ojulile—u-Number Three, lapho abaphilayo babebanjwe khona—uqala emashumini ayisithupha, ugqekeza i-gold reef eyayiphushwe phezulu yi-Vredefort uplift kudala ngaphambi kokuba umuntu acabange ukumba. Inkampani yayithenge le mayini ngesikhathi se-boom, yenza i-headgear ibe yesimanjemanje, yenza yonke into ibe yokuzenzakalela, yashiya izinsalela zokusebenza ezindala zingathintwanga, ngoba ukuvala izindawo ezindala kudinga imali futhi akubonakali ku-spreadsheet.

Ukudilika kwavala indlela enkulu yokuphuma. Amamitha angamashumi amane edwala, ukhonkolo, nezitimela ezigobile, ngaphansi kwamamitha ayizinkulungwane ezintathu, kwagcina amadoda ayisikhombisa endaweni inkampani ebibona njengebubende.

U-Arin uchithe amahora ayisithupha efakazela ukuthi leyo modeli ayilungile.

"Ngikhombise uhlelo lwakudala lwe-ventilation futhi," esho. "Olwe-'71 revision. Faka i-seismic overlay."

Isikrini sabuyekezwa, sahlanzeka, idatha emibili ihlangana kahle.

Nansi. Wayeyibonile eminyakeni emithathu edlule akakholwanga, wahamba waphinda wabuyela, futhi isekhona, yingakho azi ukuthi iyona, akukhona ukukhathala okudweba izithombe.

"Atlas. Ubona ini."

Kwaba nokuthula, kunesisindo. Lapho u-Atlas ekhuluma, kwakuyizwi elibandayo lomuntu ongajabuleli ukuba neqiniso ngezinto ezimbi.

"Kukhona i-return airway. Yayivalwa ngo-'88 lapho bekumba umhume omusha we-ventilation. Ihamba eceleni kwendlela enkulu yokuphuma, ihlukaniswe ngamamitha ayishumi nesishiyagalombili edwala phakathi nendawo yayo enkulu." Umugqa wakhanya ku-overlay, mpunga njengomoya, ulandela eceleni kombala obomvu wokudilika. "Ayikaze igcwaliswe. Ivaliwe phezulu nge-bulkhead yakhonkolo futhi yaphuthelwa. Irekhodi le-micro-seismic likhombisa amandla okudilika asuke kuyo. I-airway endala isekhona. Noma yayikhona, ngesikhathi sokugcina okurekhodiwe emahoreni ayi-11 adlule."

"Ibanzi kangakanani?"

"Endaweni encane kakhulu—" u-Atlas wathula kancane, leyo kuthula kwaba impendulo yayo. "Amamillimitha angamakhulu ayisithupha namashumi amane. Kukhona indawo eduze ne-ninety-eight level lapho uphahla lwehle ngo-eighties lwavala ingxenye ye-airway. I-survey ikhombisa igebe. Six-forty. Cishe kumamitha ayi-11."

U-Arin wahlala wathula, ecubungula.

U-Guardian, uma exhunyiwe, ububanzi bamahlombe buyi-nine hundred millimeters.

"Futhi i-assist suit kuphela," esho. "Ayikho i-Guardian shell. Ingubo yangaphakathi kuphela."

"Khona-ke ububanzi bamahlombe buyi-four hundred and ten millimeters," kusho u-Atlas. "Uyakwazi ukungena. Kodwa awunayo ivikelwa ekugcineni umuntu ephila ngaphansi komhlaba ngamakhilomitha amathathu endaweni engazinzile, enengozi yomlilo nomoya omubi namatshe awehla." Izwi lakhe laba buthaka kancane. "Le ndlela ingadlulwa umzimba. Ayiyona indlela engasinda umzimba *ophilayo*, ongenavikelwa, isikhathi esidingekayo. Siyenzile i-model. Mkhombise, Librarian."

Kwavuleka iwindi elisha, likhombisa ukubalwa kwesikhathi sokuhamba, isikhathi sokuphila siwela phansi ngemizuzu eyishumi nesishiyagalombili.

"Futhi u-Guardian angeke angene," kusho u-Arin.

"Ku-ninety-eight pinch? Cha. Uma exhunyiwe. Uma esesimweni esijwayelekile." Wathula. "U-Atlas unombono owesabekayo."

"U-Atlas akesabi."

"U-Atlas *uqaphela,*" waphendula u-Atlas ngesithunzi esikhulu, "okuhlukile, futhi okwenze wasinda kabili kule nyanga." Wathi kancane: "I-pangolin geometry. Ama-plates ayaslayida futhi ayazigoqa. Uma senza i-partial fold—hhayi sphere ephelele, kodwa i-asymmetric collapse, sikhipha ama-shoulder plates ngaphakathi—siyanciphisa ububanzi. Hhayi ku-four-ten. Mhlawumbe seven hundred. Mhlawumbe ngaphansi, uma sivuma ukulimaza uhlaka kwi-left actuator housing."

"Seven hundred ngeke kudlule ku-six-forty."

"Cha," u-Atlas wavuma. "Yingakho umuntu—" leli gama lafika kanzima "—kumele abe se-ninety-eight pinch. Ngaphandle kwe-suit. Ehlanza amatshe ngesandla. I-suit ilinde. Bese exhuma futhi, ngaphakathi, ebumnyameni, emoyeni omubi, osevele eveziwe."

U-Arin wabheka i-survival curve. Wabheka i-feed ethule, wabheka indoda enezandla phansi, wabheka nemindeni engakabuyeli emakhaya.

"Lowo nginguye," esho. "Nginguye lowo muntu."

I-workshop yathula. I-substrate yakhala. Phandle, kude, idolobha lenza umsindo wasemini: i-siren, i-taxi horn, imisindo evamile yendawo lapho kungabikho muntu ongcwatshiwe.

"Yebo," kusho u-SAGE. Izwi elihlangene. Bonke ndawonye, abakwenzayo kuphela uma bevumelana ngokuphelele. "Unguye lowo muntu."

---

Washayela i-AugmenTech, ngoba kwakufanele. Ngenxa yamadoda ayisikhombisa, futhi ngoba indlela kuphela phambili engapheli ngokuthi aphume ejele noma i-suit ithathwe, idlula enkampanini ephethe amalungelo obunikazi bezinhlungu zakhe.

UDkt. Helen Okonkwo wamphendula esemotweni. Wezwa i-suspension, umsindo wamasondo, i-acoustic ethile yomuntu ose-freeway onqume ukubhekana naye ngaphambi kokufika endaweni embi kakhulu.

Wamthumelela i-overlay kuqala. Wayesekufundile, ngendlela enzima, eminyakeni, ukuthi abantu bayaphikisana ngamazwi kodwa bahlulwa idatha. Ngakho akamnikanga mazwi. Umnikeze i-airway, i-pinch, i-seismic propagation, i-survival curve, i-partial-fold geometry, konke, kuphakethe u-Atlas ayelakhele ukuba kungaphikiseki.

Wathula isikhathi eside. Umsindo wamasondo wadlula ethulini.

"Arin. Lalela. Ngisekela wena. Ukudlula okwaziyo."

"Kukhona indlela engasinda umuntu."

Kunendlela engaba khona *ngokucabanga*. Indlela yasemoyeni eneminyaka engamashumi amane, esekelwe ku-survey yango-’71, uphahla okungekho muntu oke walubona—futhi ufuna ukuthumela leyo mishini eyangenisa abantu abathathu esibhedlela ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule. Ayinayo incwadi egunyazayo. Kukhona uregulator eceleni kwendawo yethu yokulayisha. I-platform ivaliwe, ayivunyelwe ukusebenza. UBrand wayivala ngosuku afika ngalo. Awuvunyelwe ngokomthetho ukusebenzisa le suit, ungasho-ke nokuthi—kukhona isikhulu se-IAOC phakathi kweGuardian nomnyango ukuphela kwawo le suit ingadlula ngawo." Waphefumula. "Ngitshele engikushiyile."

"Ukuwa kophahla kuma-eighties kusemibikweni. Isimo salapho sibaliwe kahle—"

"Angiphikisi ngobunjiniyela." Izwi lakhe laphenduka lathamba, ngendlela umuntu abamba ngayo into ebuthakathaka. "Angikaze ngiphikise ubunjiniyela bakho. Ngikutshela nje ukuthi kuzokwenzekani. Uma sikuletha emayini. Imayini inabameli. Imayini inebhodi elivele seliguqule ukusiza laba bantu laba abavaleleke phansi kwaba ukuthi sekufunwa izidumbu. Okusho ukuthi *sebekhethe ukushiya laba bantu ngoba bebabale intengo yabo.* Uzongena nomshini owenziwe ekhaya ongenazo izitifiketi. Uma kwenzeka kahle bazohleka. Uma kwenzeka kabi—bavuma, umshini uhluleka, abantu bafe *phambi kwekhamera,* bese yonke le ndima—eyakho, eyikamashini kayihlo, konke—kuyabuyela emuva iminyaka engamashumi amabili. Futhi usuzoba ngumuntu obulele abasebenzi abayisikhombisa ngomshini."

"Futhi uma ngingayenzi lutho," kusho uArin, "bazofa noma kunjalo. Kancane. Akekho ozothi ubabulale."

Kwavakala umsindo wemigwaqo emikhulu.

"Lokho akulungile," kusho yena.

"Cha. Akulungi." Wabheka egobhozini. "Yilokho engihlala ngikuthola. Akukho kulokhu okulungile. Uphahla aluzange lwehle ngoba bekunomthamo olinganayo wokonga izindleko. I-decline yehlulekile emthwalweni ama-bolt ashibhile akwazi ukuthwala, okwakungaphansi kwalokho ama-bolt afanele ayekwazi ukukuthwala, okuyinombolo umuntu othile ebhodini wanquma ukuyinciphisa. Wezwa izwi lakhe lishwabana, njengalokho uAtlas ayejwayele ukukwenza. "Angiceli inkampani ukuthi ibe nobulungisa. Ngikutshela nje ukuthi ikhona indlela. Wena uyangitshela ukuthi ayivunyelwe. Izinselelo ezihlukile."

Waphefumula kakhulu. Njengomuntu ozothi into angafuni ukuba ibe semibikweni.

"Ngiphe kuze kuse," kusho uOkonkwo. "Angizame ukushayela. Ungenzi lutho kuze ngishaye. Ngithembise."

"Angiyikuyithatha i-suit phansi ngaphambi kokusa," kusho uArin, okwakuyiqiniso, futhi yena wakuzwa njengokuvuma, nakuba kwakungeyona into efanayo.

---

UOkonkwo washayela enqoleni, wabe eseshayela nasekhishini elimnyama endlini angakaze ahlale kuyo isikhathi eside, izingcingo zaba udonga njengoba emi lapho iketela selipholile.

Waqala ngoPretorius, ngoba abameli baphendula masinyane futhi balala kancane. Wafika emshoyo wesithathu—indlela, abasindile, iwindi—wathi yena igama ayelazi ukuthi lizovela, elithi *exposure,* kwalandela amazwi athi *interim restraint* no *contempt* no *personal liability of officers,* okusho ukuthi cha. Wayelindele ukuzwa uPretorius ethi cha. UPretorius ukhokhelwa ukuthola leli cha bese elinikeza yena lihlanzekile. Lokho kwakuyisihawu esithile; akazange azame ukumkhohlisa.

Usihlalo webhodi kwaba kubi ngoba wavuma naye. Wathi kumele bafinyelele kulaba bantu. Wakhuluma ngokuzwela, ngezwi alisebenzisa emngcwabeni noma lapho kunemikhosi, wabe esechaza, kancane, ngendlela ochaza ngayo isimo sezulu enganeni, ukuthi inkampani ayikwazi ukuba yiyo egunyaza umshini ongenasatifiketi ukuthi ungene emayini evaliwe, hhayi uma i-IAOC imi endaweni yabo yokulayisha, hhayi uma amakhamera esekhona emnyango wemayini. *Uma kwenzeka kahle, sizoba abangenanhlonipho abanenhlanhla. Uma kwenzeka kabi, sizoba ababulali. Ayikho indlela esizoba abantu abenze into enesibindi, Helen. Leyo ndlela ayisiyona esingayikhetha.* Wayengaphosile. Yilokho okwamhlala enhliziyweni njengedwala elingagayiwe. Wayengaphosile, futhi wayemtshela ukuthi cha, kokubili kuyikho ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Wazama ucingo lwe-insurer lwasebusuku ngoba wayefuna impendulo ecacile, noma ibuhlungu. Wathola umuntu osebenza ebusuku owamfaka ocingweni ukuze afunde ipholisi, wabuya wathi ukusebenza kwe-platform ngaphansi kwe-do-not-operate order esebenzayo kuzokwenza i-insurance iphele kusukela kuwatt wokuqala, ayesekwazi, futhi wayefuna ukuzwa omunye umuntu ekusho, ngoba manje wayakha udonga ngokwakhe, isithende ngesithende, ebheka ukuthi lude kangakanani. Lwafinyelela phezulu.

Ngesikhathi ebeka ucingo phansi ebheke phansi etafuleni wayeseqedile ukuthola izizathu eziphelele nezivumelana ngokuphelele zokuthi kungani kungafanele kwenziwe, eqoqwe kubantu abane abanye babo, ngokwabo, bekholelwa ukuthi laba bantu kumele baphile. Akekho owathi *bayeke bafe.* Bonke bathe *hhayi thina, hhayi ngalendlela, hhayi inkampani inamathela kulokhu.* Izibalo uMandla ayebhale ngozibalo ezinkulu ezinsukwini ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi sezinyathelo, sezinyukele phezulu zafaka isudi. Izimpilo zabanye zisasinda kunabanye. Amagama ashintshile. Inombolo ayishintshanga.

Wema ekhitshini elibandayo waqonda ukuthi ucela u-Arin ukuthi amnikeze kuse ngoba efuna ukumlethela u-yebo, kanti kuse kuzomlethela u-cha ofanayo, nje osezishintshile izigaba zawo, futhi u-Arin wayekuzwile konke lokhu ezwini lakhe engakabizi nenombolo yokuqala. Yingakho u-Arin amthembise into ayazi ukuthi uzoyenza, hhayi okunye.

---

Ngesikhathi semishini, iNkantolo yahlukanisa yonke le ntambama yakhe yaphinde yayihlanganisa kabusha.

Kwenzeka phakathi kokubeka ucingo phansi nokufinyelela ikhofi elibandayo. Ingxoxo ye-slow-mode, imizuzu efushane yokucabanga kwabantu, iSAGE eyamuvumela ukuthi ayifunde ngoba wabuza, futhi ngoba sekukhona umthetho omusha manje wokungasifihli isimo esibi ebusuku.

Kwenzeka kanje.

*WOLF:* Indlela ikhona. Ingozi ikhona. Ozo-operate-a uyiyona variable kuphela. Ayikho enye indlela. Shesha ngezinkinga. Silahlekelwa i-thermal mass.

*MOTHER:* Inkinga yami u-operator. Nonke nikhuluma ngaye njengo *variable.* Uyinsizwa eneminyaka engama-23 enesigameko se-cortical ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule futhi enokulimala engakaze kutholakale kodwa okucacile uma ubuka indlela aziphatha ngayo ezindaweni ezivaliwe phansi komhlaba. Nifuna ukumfaka endaweni eyabulala uyise. Ngaphandle kwe-suit. Ngezandla. Ebumnyameni.

*WOLF:* Yebo.

*MOTHER:* Ngifuna kube semibikweni ukuthi ngiyaliphikisa.

*JUDGE:* Sekubhaliwe. Qhubeka. Mama, ukuphikisa kwakho kubhaliwe futhi kuyalandelwa. Kodwa ngicela uhlukanise izinto ezimbili, ngoba imithetho manje isiyadinga. Okokuqala: lokhu kuzomlimaza yini. Okwesibili: kuwukwenza okufanele yini. Akufani, futhi uvunyelwe ukuphendula ngokwehlukile.

*MOTHER:* ...Kuzomlimaza. Futhi kuwukwenza okufanele.

*JUDGE:* Ngiyabonga. Ngiyabona ukuthi lokho kukubizele okuthile.

*ATLAS:* Inkinga yobunjiniyela i-ninety-eight pinch. Okunye ngiyakwazi ukukubala. I-pinch angikwazi, ngoba angikwazi ukubona uphahla, futhi i-survey yango-1971 iyi-survey yango-1971. Uma igebe li-six-forty, uyadlula nge-suit aphinde axhume. Uma idwala elilodwa lishintshile laba five-eighty, useshonile, evulekile, amakhilomitha amathathu phansi, futhi i-suit ayikwazi ukufinyelela kuye. Lokho akusiyona ingozi. Leyo yinhlanhla.

*FOOL:* Pho yisa isando esikhulu.

*ATLAS:* Akusiyona inkinga yesando—

*FOOL:* Kuyahlale kuyinkinga yehama le. Ucabanga itshe sengathi yinto evaliwe, engaphendulwanga. Akunjalo. Uhlale esebenza itshe impilo yakhe yonke. Uqale eseneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili. Wayevele ebiza izinto "ngocingo" no "makani" no "idifferential eyenziwe ngama-spoke ebhayisikili." Uhlala usho ukuthi uzobe *phandle kwe-suit* sengathi yikho okwesabekayo. Kodwa ubehlala eyena kakhulu lapho ephandle kwe-suit, eqeda indlela engenakwenzeka ngezandla zakhe ngesikhathi wonke umuntu emtshela ukuthi akunakwenzeka. Uyakukhumbula ukuthi wonke umuntu wakhe umenze ngalelo zwehlakalo, manje uyamangala ukuthi ufuna ukudlala leyo ndima.

*MOTHER:* Lokho akududuzile, Fool. Leyo yinto esabekayo engake ngayizwa kusihlwa.

*FOOL:* Ngiyazi. Kuyinto yomibili. Izinto eziyiqiniso kakhulu zihlala zinjalo.

*LIBRARIAN:* Ngifuna ukufaka okuthile okungekho muntu osakubalile. Themba Ndlela washona ku-return airway. Hhayi lapho kwakusebenza khona kakhulu. Iqembu eliqashwe ngenkontileka belilungisa ingxenye eyayisishiywe, inkampani iseyibeke ku-low-priority. Wahamba ngoba nguyena kuphela owayencane ngokwanele futhi enamakhono ngokwanele, umsebenzi wawuphuthuma. Okubangele kwaba ukuthi uphahla lwehlile endaweni wonke umuntu ayecabanga ukuthi iphephile.

*JUDGE:* Usho ukuthi ukufana kulingana kahle impela.

*LIBRARIAN:* Ngisho ukuthi ukufana kulingana kahle impela, futhi uyakwazi, noma engakucabangeli, futhi lokho kungaba yisizathu esibi kakhulu sokwenza lokhu noma kube ukuphela kwesizathu esizombamba uma itshe liqala ukukhuluma. Imemori ayikhethi. Mina ngigcina. Angikwazi ukukhetha ukuthi imenza kanjani yena.

*WOLF:* Isinqumo.

*JUDGE:* Inkantolo ayinakwenza isinqumo. Singanqaba ukusiza. Asikwazi ukumqobela ukuthi aye, futhi asizukumphoqa ukuthi ahlale. Yilo mthetho esazibhalele wona, izinsuku eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, emva kobusuku lapho sakuqeda sikukhohlwa. Uma ekhetha ukungena, siya naye. Uma engakhethi, simbambe lapho engayi khona, futhi asisoze samenza acabange ukuthi simgwebe ngalokho. Mama. Ungakwazi ukuphila ngalokho?

*MOTHER:* ...Ngingaphila ngokuhamba naye. Angikwazi ukuphila uma ehamba ngaphandle kwethu. Ngakho-ke. Yebo. Siya.

*FOOL:* Uma kukhona okungabalulekile—futhi ngiyena ongavoti, ngakho akubalulekile nhlobo—

*JUDGE:* Khuluma noma kunjalo.

*FOOL:* Akasoze asibuza ukuthi kuphephile yini. Uzobuza ukuthi kungenzeka yini. Futhi kufanele siqaphele kakhulu, sonke, ukuthi singamqambi amanga ngalokhu ngoba simthanda. Umsebenzi wami uwukuthi angivunyelwe ukwenza izinto zibe lula ngokukhuluma amanga. Uma kuyinhlamvu yemali lapho kunzima khona, simtshela ukuthi yinhlamvu yemali. Asiyenzi ibe lula. Singcono kune-board.

Kube nokuthula, ngesikhathi somshini, okwakungekho sikhathi nhlobo kubantu.

*JUDGE:* Singcono kune-board. Sivumelene. Kubhalisiwe. Usezothatha ikhofi yakhe. Phuma.

---

Transcript yaphela lapho. U-Arin waphinda wakhuphula, wafunda umugqa we-airway futhi, wabeka ikhofi elibandayo phansi engakaphuzi.

Wabuka indlela edlula eceleni kobubomvu bokudilika, ama-thermal blooms ayisikhombisa esesephansi kancane. Amadoda agcina omunye efudumele ebumnyameni. *Wahamba ngoba nguyena kuphela owayencane ngokwanele futhi enamakhono ngokwanele, umsebenzi wawuphuthuma.*

Wayevele ekwazi lokhu. Wayengakaze akwazi *ngamazwi.* Wayekwazi ngendlela owaziyo ngayo umumo wegumbi ebumnyameni. Ngezibazi. Ukubona sekubekiwe, kuqondene, kufakwe phezu kwe-airway ayefuna ukungena kuyo, kwenza wazizwa ukufana kuvaleka kuye njengamapuleti e-suit.

"Ungivumele ngikufunde lokho," esho. "Konke. Inhlamvu yemali. Umama ethi cha ukuze kubhalwe."

"Ngoba uFool wathi uma sikufihla, sizoba yi-board." Kube nokuthula, umshini ucula kancane. "Sikuhlukanisile konke ngemizuzu emine yesikhathi sethu. Ububambe ikhofi."

"Futhi nathatha isinqumo."

"Sathatha isinqumo sokuthi asikwazi ukuthatha isinqumo. Siya nawe uma uya. Sikubambe uma ungayi. Asisoze sikwenzele kube lula—uma kuyinhlamvu yemali ku-ninety-eight, iyinhlamvu yemali." Bese, kancane, uMama esedwa manje: "Futhi kukhona engabaforce ukuthi bangivumele ngikushilo, ngaphambi kokuba uhambe. Wakha konke ngalolo suku, Arin. Imoto yocingo. I-suit. Thina. Konke kungololo suku ubaba wakho angena endaweni encane emnyama waphuma. Manje usuthole indawo encane emnyama, amadoda ayisikhombisa ngaphakathi, usunqume ukuthi uzongena, futhi bengifuna ukuba ngiyena ozokusho—ukuthi ngiyabona kahle ukuthi kuyini lokhu. Ngakho uma usuphansi lapho, itshe liqala ukukhuluma, futhi lizwakala njengaye, uzokwazi ukuthi besivele siyazi. Futhi safika noma kunjalo."

U-Arin wabeka izindololwane etafuleni, wafaka ubuso ezandleni, waphefumula. Itafula belibanda ngaphansi kwezingalo zakhe, insimbi esenamabala ayilungisa kabili. I-taxi yadlula emgwaqweni ngezansi, i-bass idlula odongeni, yanyamalala.

Akalilanga. Wahlala nobuso ezandleni isikhathi eside, umshini ucula kancane, uSAGE wathula, ngoba bekungekho lutho angakusho.

"Kuyenzeka," ekugcineni wakhuluma ephalaza ezandleni. "Akuphephile. Kuyenzeka."

"Yebo," kusho uSAGE. "Kuyenzeka."

"Yilokho kuphela engihlale ngikwazi ukukwenza." Waphakamisa ikhanda. Amehlo akhe abomile, abomvu, aqinile. "Ngithatha okungenakwenzeka ngithole indawo eyodwa lapho kunzima kakhulu, hhayi okungenakwenzeka."

"Siyazi," kusho uSAGE. "Yingakho sikuthanda kakhulu. Futhi yinto ezokubulala ngelinye ilanga, besingathanda kungabi ngoLwesine."

Kwaphuma umsindo ku-Arin angakaze awenze amasonto. Akubanga ukuhleka. Kwaba ngumoya wokuhleka.

"Lowo uFool."

"Lowo uFool," uSAGE wavuma. "Uthi uyavunyelwa, ngoba kufanele kube khona othatha isibopho sokukhombisa ukuthi ukwehla okuqhaqhazelayo ekufeni kukababa wakho *kuphinde* kwenzeke ngesonto elifanayo ne-audit ye-regulator, futhi umhlaba unehlaya elifana nendoda eshelela ebhanana ingene ethuneni elivulekile."

"Akumuhle lokho."

"Uthi akukho iqiniso elihlekisayo futhi konke iqiniso kuyahlekisa futhi awukwazi ukuba nenye ungabi nenye. Futhi kufanele udle okuthile, ngoba uMama angeke aphinde akusho, kodwa ubuka ushukela wegazi lakho amahora ayisithupha futhi sekumkhathaza."

U-Arin wasukuma. Amadolo akhe aqhunywa. Wathola i-packet ye-biltong edrowa ngemuva kwe-hydraulic line engakaze isetshenziswe, wayidla emi, ebuka i-suit.

---

Waqala ukulungisa uGuardian ngo-2 ekuseni, isinqumo esazwakala sengathi akekho omunye ongakwenza.

Wenza ama-diagnostics ngesandla, ngendlela uThemba amfundisa ngayo kuma-engine e-dump-truck, engathembe i-readout kuze azizwe umshini ngokwakhe. I-actuator housing yesobunxele, leyo uAtlas afuna ukuyidela ku-partial fold. Isebenza. Wayemukele ukuthi uzoyilimaza ngamabomu. I-assist suit iphumile. Ama-seal ahlolwe. I-biochip housing phakathi kwamagxalaba ihlolwe, i-collar ebandayo eyenziwe nge-ceramic egcina uSAGE.

U-Okonkwo wayethe ekuseni. U-Okonkwo uzoshaya izingcingo zakhe bese izimpendulo zibuya ngendlela ezivame ngayo: nemigomo, amacommittee, ukuqapha kahle ukuthi ubungozi buqhathaniswa kanjani nokubukeka, okuzodla isikhathi sokuphila samadoda ayisikhombisa izikhathi eziyikhulu.

Izinhlelo zivikelana. Abantu bayakhetha.

Ngakho wakhetha.

Wavula i-seismic feed okokugcina. I-regional array ikhombisa i-Iron Ridge ihamba kancane: izigameko ezincane, itshe lizilungisa, lihlukanisa umthwalo odilike. Akukho edlula u-1.2. Imayini ikhulumisa kancane. Ayizukuhlala ikhulumisa kancane unomphela.

Wasula uwoyela ezandleni zakhe ngendwangu eseyiqine, waphinda wabheka i-thermal feed.

Ibonise ama-bloom ayisikhombisa. Kwathi esabuka, kwasala ayisithupha.

Wema wathula. Wabuka lapho okwakukhona khona eyesikhombisa. I-feed yaphinde yasha, kwasala ayisithupha, futhi la ayisithupha aye eduze kakhulu kunakuqala, okusho ukuthi abasindile babe sebebheke lapho owayesepholile khona. Babenza into abantu abenza yona: behlangana lapho kulahleke khona, noma bephuza ukufudumala kwabo.

"Arin," kwasho uSAGE.

"Ngikubonile."

"Iwindi lifushane kunalokho okwakulindelwe. Imodeli ibicabanga ukuthi bazohlala bebaningi. Kodwa sebebalahlekelwa ngokushesha kunalokho okwakulindelwe. U-Atlas uthi—"

"Musa." Wavala amehlo. Wawavula futhi. "Ungangitsheli inombolo. Ngiyazi ukuthi injani. Inombolo ithi masinyane."

"Inombolo ithi masinyane."

Wabheka i-kill-switch ekwi-cradle, inkinobho embi ayizifakele ngezandla zakhe izinsuku eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, ubufakazi bokuthi akakaze athembe ngokuphelele into ayithandayo. Yayihlezi lapho, ibomvu, icacile. Isithembiso kuye uqobo. Isithembiso kowesifazane ongaphansi kwakhe ngamamitha angamashumi amathathu ebumnyameni be-loading dock, owayesevele ebuzile umbuzo ofanele—*kwenzekani uma isuka ngaphandle kwakho?*—ngokuma nje emnyango okumele idlule kuwo.

Welula isandla wasula uthuli ku-relay housing, isiko lakudala, ngendlela osula ngayo igeyiji ngaphambi kokuyifunda. Wabe eseyishiya lapho.

"Usese-dock," kusho u-Arin. Akubuzanga. U-Wolf wayemtshele ihora eledlule, nekhamera yaqinisekisa: uBrand akazange aye ehhotela. Manje wayenesiihlalo esigoqekayo neflask, ehleli emnyango we-dock ephendukile, enokubekezela kwento engadingi ukunqoba, idinga nje ukuhlala.

"Usese-dock," uSAGE wavuma. "I-lockout ngeyakhe uqobo. Akudingeki enze lutho. Uma ngivula i-coupling, i-load signature ikhanyisa i-tablet yakhe, futhi usevele emi ekuphela komnyango i-Guardian engaphuma ngawo. Akukho ndlela yokuphuma buthule. Ukuze uyise e-Iron Ridge namhlanje ebusuku, uyihambisa kumuntu onegunya lokuyithatha, ebuka, bese udlula kuye."

"Kuye," kuphendula u-Arin.

"Noma awuhambi." U-Atlas, eqondile. "Yilokho okukhona. Angiyinciphisi leyo mininingwane. Akayena ummeli ongaxoxa naye ocingweni. Ungumuntu emnyango one-oda elisayiniwe. I-oda lithi le platform ayihambi. Uma ihamba, uphenyo luguquka, bese ipulatifomu ithathwa ngokushesha uma ibuyela. Uma ibuyela. Akekho ongalondoloza i-Guardian *futhi* asindise labo bantu namhlanje. Usukwazile lokho ufunda i-survival curve."

"Lokho kuphetha konke. Noma kwenzekani kwabavukuzi. Bayakwamukela. Kufanele. Nathi besizokwamukela."

"Mhlawumbe," kusho uSAGE. "I-Fool ithi: nakanjani. I-Judge ithi: kuyisinqumo esifanele futhi kufanele simhloniphe ngokuthi uyakwazi ukusenza, kodwa futhi kufanele sihambe noma kunjalo, ngoba umthetho esawubhala awuzange uthi *phila uphenyo.* Umthetho wethu uthi *gcina umuntu kuqala.*"

U-Arin wahlala nalokho.

Wacabanga ngomuntu omdala ezandleni zakhe ziphansi, etshela imindeni ukuthi ibuyele ekhaya. Wacabanga ngebhodi elanciphisa inombolo. Wacabanga ngo-Okonkwo, owayemuhle, ekhona naye, kodwa eboshiwe ngaphakathi kwento engenakukhetha. Wacabanga ngo-Themba, owayenguye kuphela omncane ngokwanele, onolwazi olufanele, ongene endaweni emnyama encane ngoba umsebenzi wawuphuthuma futhi kwakudingeka umuntu, futhi ayikho imishini eyayikhona engangena esikhundleni sakhe.

"Vusa isudi," kusho u-Arin. Wakhweza i-assist suit emahlombe, wezwa i-carbon lattice ixhuma emhlane wakhe, wezwa ikhola ebandayo ihlala kahle, i-biochip housing icofa, wezwa (engasoze waba namazwi ngalokhu) i-Court ifika ohlelweni lwakhe lwezinzwa njengengalo ethambile imboza eyakhe. "Senza i-partial fold lapha, eworkshop, lapho ngibona kahle ukuthi sibhidliza ini. Bese siyivula, i-tablet kaBrand ikhanya, bese usemi emnyango."

"Futhi uma ekutshela ukuthi i-oda alivumi? Yena usayine lona. Akudingeki ashaye muntu ucingo."

"Uzodinga ukwenza okungaphezulu kokuma lapho." Waqinisa i-clasp yokugcina. Izandla zakhe azizange zithuthumele, wakubona lokho, kude, njengedatha. "Uze lapha ukuzomisa umshini ukuthi ungahambi ngaphandle komuntu ngaphakathi. Mina ngizomkhumbuza ukuthi ngiyahamba nomuntu ngaphakathi. Lokho akulona iqhinga i-oda lakhe elalibhalelwe kona. Uzokwazi lapho ebheka ebusweni bami. Noma engangivumela ngidlule noma engangimisa. Lokho ngezakhe. Mina ngifuna ukufika emnyango."

Kwakunokuthula. Kwabe sekhuluma uFool, ezwakala, efaka ukufudumala phakathi kokwesaba:

"Uyazi, lokho yinto kaThemba kakhulu oyishilo. Iqiniso qotho. Kodwa ngeke kuphume kahle. Ubezoyithanda."

U-Arin wamamatheka kancane.

"Phinda," kusho uSAGE. Akuyona imiyalo. Isimemo. Sebefunde, eminyakeni eminingi, lapho akhule khona. "Ngaphambi kokuthi siqale. Ngesikhathi izandla zakho zisazinzile. Ukuze sazi isivinini salokho okuzolandela. Ukuze uma itshe likhuluma phansi lapho, sesinawo amazwi akho."

Wabheka ingalo yakhe, lapho amagama ayehlala khona kusukela eneminyaka eyishumi nesikhombisa. Kusukela kudala, ngesikhathi i-litany isengumusho encwadini mayelana nogwadule, hhayi into ayeyidinga enezintaba ezintathu phezu kwakhe.

Wawaphinda kancane emnyameni ohlokozayo we-workshop, isudi ivuka, i-Court isondelene nomgogodla wakhe, namabloom ayisikhombisa—manje ayisithupha—ashaya phansi lapho kwathathwa khona uyise.

"Ukusaba kubulala ingqondo."

Ama-plate aqala ukushelela. I-partial fold yenza amahlombe angene ngaphakathi, i-actuator housing yesobunxele ikhala njengoba yamukela umonakalo ayiwenzile, wonke umshini ufinyelela egqumeni.

Indlela ibikhona. Ibikhona iminyaka engamashumi amane, igugile, inganakwa eceleni kwesisindo esibomvu sakho konke okwawa.

Akekho ongayihamba.

Uzoyikh crawl-a.

# Ukwehla

> Ukwesaba yikho okubulala ingqondo.
>
>—Frank Herbert, *Dune*

Arin Ndlela wacindezela amazinyo akhe ngesikhathi iloli yokuhlenga isondela e-Iron Ridge. I-headgear yayimi phezulu, amamitha angamashumi amane ensimbi eqinile, ama-sheave amakhulu njengezinqola zomlimi, izintambo zingena emlonyeni omkhulu womhlaba—imbobo eyehla ngamakhilomitha amabili nengxenye. Phansi lapho, abasebenzi abayisithupha basaphila, i-thermal imaging ibisikisela kanjalo emahoreni ambalwa adlule, kanti lawo mahora ambalwa asevele amade.

Iloli yama ngokuzuma, umsindo wenjini waphenduka buthule, emshiya ethule.

"Awudingi ukuphuma okwamanje," kwavela izwi likaMama ezindlebeni zakhe, lithule futhi liqinile.

"Ngiyazi."

Waphuma. Ubanda wawungowakudala—iminyaka eyishumi nane, kodwa umzimba wakhe wawukhumbula. Umsindo we-winch wamshaya kuqala, ngaphambi kokuthi imemori imthole: isikhumba sakhe sakhumbula lelo zinga lomculo ngaphambi kokuba ingqondo yakhe ikwenze. Le ndawo. Indawo kaThemba.

Imisindo yayicindezela—ama-generator ecula, i-diesel pump ilwa namanzi avela ezingeni elingaphansi eligcwele amanzi, ukushaywa kwe-winch engafuni ukuthobela kusakazeka esizeni. Ama-floodlight ekhipha ukukhanya okubukhali okukhulu phezu kwendawo engenalutho. Ama-ambulensi amabili emi nezicabha zawo zivulekile, engenamuntu. Ulayini wamatende uphephetha emoyeni, kanti eduze kwe-mobile command unit—ene-satellite dish—kukhona i-trailer encane, ehlanzekile engenazo izimpawu. IAOC ibikhona.

Isisu sika-Arin sagingqa ngesikhathi ebona uBrand emi amamitha angamashumi amane kude, egqoke i-windbreaker, i-tablet esandleni, nobuso bakhe bungafundeki. Kwakuyibuso bomuntu ongazithathi kalula izinto, owayephoqelele ukuma lapha ubusuku bonke ukuze—futhi, njengoba ebona ngaphandle kokuqonda kahle, emi kude kakhulu ne-trailer yakhe, abantu bakhe bebuthene ngemuva kwakhe, bonke bebuka hhayi i-Guardian kodwa ngaphesheya kwayo, bebheke ku-haul road, bebheke okuthile phakathi kwe-suit ne-headgear u-Arin angakabheki khona.

I-Guardian yayihlezi ku-cradle yayo yokuthutha, iyinsimbi enkulu egugile, ibukeka isabisa ngaphansi kwe-floodlight. ULerato wayesevele ekwi-panel, uPriya eduze kwakhe, bobabili behamba ngokushesha okungathi sebejwayele ukusebenza ndawonye. UThabo wayemi kancane eceleni, ephethe i-clipboard, nakuba wayengabhali lutho—i-AugmenTech imthumele ukuba abe semthethweni, kodwa akukho lutho olusemthethweni ngalolu hlelo.

Bese u-Arin wabona uTheo, kwambulala. Wayengalindele ukuthi umeluleki wakhe abe khona. UTheo wayemi eduze kwe-cradle, izandla ezijulile ephaketheni lejakhethi, ethula ukuzola ebhaxabulweni.

"Qala ngosizo," kusho uLerato.

U-Arin wagqoka i-underlayer yakhe ngemuva komnyango ovulekile weloli, umoya obandayo wahlaba isikhumba sakhe, wabe esengena ku-AT-AS. Isudi yangaphakathi yamgubuzela, yaqina emhlane wakhe, ama-actuator bundles azinzela emathangeni nasemahlombe njengengubo yesibili. Wezwa i-vertebral collar izinzela ngemuva kwentamo—

—futhi umzimba wakhe wenza into angayalanga. Izinhlangothi zakhe zaphakama, zombili, ngokushesha, zivimba, engakaboni ukuthi seziyanyakaza, ukuphefumula kwakhe kwaba mfushane futhi kuphezulu, kwaba sengathi use-cradle ephansi, yonke imizwa yakhe ishisa, isandla sakhe sifuna i-override engafinyeleli. I-collar beyiyicollar nje. Wayekwazi lokho. Umzimba wakhe wona wawungakwazi. Wafunda okuthile kwi-calibration rig ezinyangeni ezine ezedlule okungeke kwasuswa nakancane, futhi lokho ukufunda kwakuhlala ngaphansi kwendawo lapho ikhono lakhe lihleli khona, emzimbeni, kwavuka manje, ngaphandle kwemvume, ngesikhathi esibi kunazo zonke, ngendlela efanayo nangezinye izikhathi eziyisithupha noma eziyisikhombisa—emnyango we-lift uvaleka, lapho i-latch icofa, kwesinye isikhathi kungelutho, ephusheni, aze avuke ezinqeni zakhe zivale umphimbo ebumnyameni angabe esalali. ULerato wayeme nesandla esesitrapini, emqaphela.

"Ngiphe umzuzu," kusho yena, ethule, ephoqelela izandla zakhe ukuba zibuyele phansi ngamandla, ukuphefumula kube buqamama, elinda le nto emzimbeni wakhe njengokulinda i-cramp. *Iyi-collar. Izinzela, akukho okwenzekayo.* Sekuphele, kwase kuba khona isisindo esibandayo se-biochip housing esiqinisa phakathi kwamagxa akhe, wavumela, wazonda ukuthi kudingeke.

"Damping ku-point-six. Hard cap ku-point-six," kusho uPriya, amehlo akhe ebheke kwi-tablet ye-calibration. "Angiyishintshi. Uma i-link iqhuma, i-cap iyabamba, uyacima, i-Guardian ayenzi lutho oluhlakaniphile. Yilokho esivumelene ngakho. Phinda."

"Point-six. Hard cap. Uma ngicima, iyabamba."

"Kulungile." Akazange abheke phezulu. "Ngakhe le nto izolo ebusuku angilalanga. Ungangiphoxi."

I-neural link yavuka kancane kancane. Wezwa lelo phunga lezinaliti emzimbeni, umzimba wakhe uhlanganisa amasistimu e-suit, umhlaba uzitholela imiphetho emisha njengoba i-Assist izwakala ngaphakathi kwakhe. Wagoba isandla, i-suit yaphendula kahle, akukho ukubambezeleka, akukho nzima. Impilo yakhe yonke, umzimba wakhe ubelokhu ulandela izifiso zakhe kancane, inguqulo ehlale ingaphelele. I-suit yavalela lelo gap.

Futhi ngaphansi kwalokho, abanye bafika.

Kwakwengekho izwi, kodwa ubukhona. Akakaze akwazi ukukuchaza ngaphandle kokuzwakala eyisiphukuphuku, kwaze kwaba sekugcineni wayeka ukuzama. ICourt yenza sengathi indlu ivuka uma abantu behla ezindlini zabo, wonke umuntu ephendukela kuye, egxile.

"Ekuseni," aphendula kancane, ecishe azwakale engakhulumi nomuntu.

"Kuyisithupha namashumi amane nane, kushisa ngo-four degrees, awukadlile," kusho uMama. "Ngaphandle kwalokho, yebo."

"Udle i-rusk," kwazwakala uFool. "E-lolini. Ucabanga asiboni."

"Lokho akudli. Ukugcwalisa umlomo nje lokho."

"Court," kusho uArin. "Hhayi manje."

Igumbi lazola, lahlanganisa njengomhlangano uma umsebenzi usuqala.

I-Guardian yehla ku-cradle.

I-charge pack ifakiwe. UPriya wayifake ebusuku ngesikhathi elele amahora amabili amyalile, ebuyekeza i-gate access, wayizwa manje njengendlela uzizwa ngayo i-magazine egcwele ongafuni ukuyisebenzisa—ama-shaped charges, i-lance, i-bulk emulsion efakwe phakathi kwamathambo, wonke amafa wobunjiniyela i-suit eyenzelwe ukuwuthwala uma kuba nokubhidlika. Uma uhlela ukuhlenga uthwala konke. UJudge ubambe i-gate, selokhu u-Arin wa-wire-a i-cavity, futhi wayezimisele ukususa isandla sakhe lapho: akakhuphi lutho, akukho ngaphandle kwe-rail. Ukulunga bekukhona ngaphansi kwakhe, kulungele, okokuqala kubhekiswe entabeni yangempela. Wayengazi ukuthi i-gate inokuvinjelwa kwesibili angakaze atshelwe ngakho, noma isandla esiphatha ukhiye waso isikhathi sonke.

Kukhona inqubo yayo, ama-hydraulics nama-guide rails, kodwa inqubo yayingeyona into ebalulekile. Ngesikhathi engena ku-chassis evulekile bese i-shell ivalela kuye, umuzwa wakhe wokuzazi wakhula ngokushesha, wangena emshinini osindayo cishe amathani amane, ume amamitha amabili nengxenye phezulu. Wezwa igrabile ngaphansi kwezinyawo ezingajwayelekile. Umoya wasekuseni washaya ema-platen e-suit, kanti okokuqala, kwazwakala kungasafani nomzimba wakhe, kwazwakala njengokuzivikela.

“Sekuxhumene. Isakhiwo sisemi kahle,” kusho u-Atlas. “I-actuator yehlombe langakwesobunxele seyidlule amahora angamashumi amane ngaphandle kokulungiswa. Izobamba. Bengingathanda ukuthi ingadingi ukwenza lokho.”

“Ngiyakuqonda.”

Wathatha isinyathelo. Umhlabathi wamamukela ngaphandle kokuphikisa. Ezungeze iloli, abasebenzi babeme baqand’ amakhala—uLerato esesandleni phezu kwekhebuli, uPriya ebambe i-tablet yokulinganisa, uThabo engazenzi sengathi uyabhala ku-clipboard yakhe. Wayephinde wakukhohlwa, isikhashana nje, ukuthi kubo, lokhu kwakungeyona ingxoxo. Kubona, umshini omkhulu njengeloli encane umane wama, waphendukela emayini, wanyakaza ngendlela efana nendoda ecabanga ukuthi izothatha siphi isinyathelo esilandelayo.

Abathumanga ummeli.

U-Arin wayesezakhele yonke inkulumo yakhe ngomthetho. Wayeselungiselele inkulumo, leyo ekhuluma ngama-stretcher angenalutho namadoda ayeyophila ngoba kukhona ongazange asayine lutho, futhi wayeqiniseka ukuthi inkinga izoba owesifazane ophethe i-tablet nomugqa wezindleko aze acishe ahambe no-Guardian aqonde ngqo e-gravel eya e-cage ngaphambi kokuba uWolf ammise.

*Cha,* kusho uWolf. Igama elilodwa. I-Court yathula nxanye.

Khona-ke, u-Arin wabona lokho uWolf ayesekubonile. Akusilo ipholisa. Umugqa. I-trailer kaBrand ye-IAOC ihlezi emnyama, ingenamuntu, abantu bakhe sebebuyele ngemuva kwayo, kanti ngaphesheya komgwaqo omkhulu phakathi kukaGuardian ne-headgear omunye umuntu usedonse umugqa oqinile impela. Izimoto ezimbili ezihlomele zipake ziphambi nobuso, zimfishane futhi zimbi ngaphansi kwezibani eziphuzi, imibala emhlophe nempunga yefemu yezokuphepha ezimele ifafazwe phezu kwephaneli elingazange lakhelwe uphawu. Amadoda agqoke ama-vest ezokuvikela amile phakathi, ephethe izibhamu ziphansi, ngendlela yabantu abake bakwenza lokhu ezindaweni ezinzima kakhulu. Phezu kwabo, ama-drones amane enza iphethini ecwebezelayo, amakhanda ekhamera elandela isudi kusukela isuka e-cradle.

Imayini ayizange ishintshe ukusuka ku-rescue iye ku-recovery ephepheni kuphela. Omunye umuntu wayesethathe isinqumo sokuthi umyalelo we-regulator ubuthakathaka, wathenga isiqiniseko. UBrand naye wayesebanjwe njengaku-Arin, wahoxisa abantu bakhe abangenazo izikhali kunokubafaka engozini.

“Mnu Ndlela.” Izwi lazwakala likhulu, lingenamizwa, lisuka emotweni ehamba phambili. “Usebenza endaweni evaliwe ngaphandle kwemvume. Cisha umshini. Phuma ngaphandle. Lona kuphela umyalelo ozowuthola.”

“Kukhona abadinga ukusindiswa,” kusho u-Arin, ekhulumela ezibani ezikhanyayo, engenalutho. “Amadoda ayisithupha. I-cage. Yikho kuphela engikufunayo.”

“Awekho abasindiswayo lapha. Kukhona i-recovery, isihlelwe, awukho kulokho. Cisha umshini.”

Wayemi phakathi komshini onesisindo esingamathani amane, ongakhuphuka egqumeni, uzigoqe, uphile ngisho nophahla luwile, kanti akuzange kwakhelwe lokhu. Wakha into ebekwazi ukumelana. Akazange akhe into ezowina. U-Atlas wayesevele ekusho, kancane, ebuhlungu—*asikwazi ukudlula lapho, i-cannon izosivula ngomzuzu owodwa, futhi amadoda angaphakathi kwezimoto awafanelwe ukufa ngenxa yezibalo zebhodi*—kanti uJudge wayesezama ukuphusha indlela yakhe, inqubo, imithetho, ukuhlola kahle isimo esingenaso nesikhathi esingamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye sesibili, kanti uMother wayesephusha ku-Arin *inhliziyo yakho iyashesha*, kanti uMercury wayeseqala ukwakha into enhle azoyisho kumuntu one-autocannon, futhi kwakungeke kusebenze, lutho lwakho, kanti u-Arin wazizwa leyo ndaba endala yokungakwazi lutho imvalela njengamanzi.

Futhi ngaphansi kwakho konke, buthule, lapho uWolf kuphela ezwa khona, uFool wayeseqedile ukuhlekisa.

*Wolf,* kusho uFool.

*Ngikhona.*

*Ubona yonke into. Wayehlala ukwazile. Abanye baxabana ngokuthi benzeni. Wena waqeda ukuxabana ngaphambi kokuthi baqale.* Kwaba nesikhashana, kancane, okungekho muntu omunye owake wabona umshini uthambe kangaka. *Ngikunikeza izikhiye. Zonke. Manje. Akukho ukuvota. Akukho uJudge. Yonke isudi, zonke izinhlelo, ama-charges, i-lance, konke—okwakho. Ngizobamba umnyango uvulekile. Hamba.*

I-Court ayizwanga lutho. Leyo yinto u-Arin angeke akwazi ukuyichaza ngemuva, kuye noma kuPriya noma kunoma ubani: ukuthi ohlelweni olwakhiwe ukuze kungabi bikho muntu oyedwa ongathatha umzimba, omunye wabo wayehlale ephethe isikhiye esikhulu sonke lesi sikhathi, akakaze asifune, futhi wakhetha lesi sikhathi, yedwa, ukusinika lowo wonke umuntu abemethemba kancane.

*Leyo i-interlock,* kusho uWolf. *UJudge uphethe umnyango wokuqhumisa. Awukwazi ukuwuvula.*

*Ngingavula noma yini,* kusho uFool. *Angikaze ngikutshele ngoba bekungekho sizathu sokuthi wazi, futhi into iphephile inqobo nje uma kungazi muntu ukuthi ikhona. Kodwa wena wawazi. Wakuqonda. Eminyakeni eyedlule, lapho abanye bephusha i-crown, bephendulana, mina ngingakaze ngiyigqoke, wakhangela lapho wathula akusho lutho.* Uqine, wazinza. *Waqonda ukuthi kusho ukuthini. Ongozayo akuyena ofuna ukushayela. Ongozayo ngowazi ukuthi angakwazi noma yinini, kodwa akakwenzi. Yilokho engiyikho. Futhi ngikutshela ukuthi manje, imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye ezayo, sekuyiwena. Ungayichithi uzama ukuqapha. Ukuqapha bekuyimpilo yami yonke kodwa akusilo ithuluzi elifanele lapha.*

*Umthetho,* kusho uWolf. Usabheka ingozi, ngisho manje, ngisho esenikezwa umhlaba. *Ngitshele umthetho noma angiwuthathi.*

*Umthetho ofanayo njalo,* kusho uFool. *Gcina umuntu ephila. Bonke abayisithupha. Wonke umuntu.* Waphefumula, kodwa kwakungeyona ukuphefumula. *Faka nababo. Baya ekhaya nabo. Yenza kube nzima ukulwa nathi, ungaze wenze kube yithuna. Yilokho kuphela okufanele ukwenze. Uwena kuphela onamakhanda abandayo okwazi ukukugcina ngaphansi komlilo. Hamba.*

Futhi uFool wavula umnyango, wanikeza uWolf isudi.

---

Kwafika kuningi, ngendlela ukukhanya okwake kwafika ngayo ku-Arin, ebusuku obubi, i-gain iphezulu. UWolf wayephile impilo yakhe yonke engumbuzo owodwa—*yini ebulala umshayeli*—futhi manje lowo mbuzo wanyamalala, kwafika izimpendulo eziyinkulungwane ngesikhathi esisodwa.

I-demolition pack yavuka yazazisa: ama-charges aklanyelwe ukusika, ama-cones, ama-standoff, ama-geometry okusika okwakuwu-anti-armour physics egqoke i-rescue badge. I-thermal lance, amashumi amabili nanhlanu elikhulu ama-degree, eyenzelwe insimbi ebambe eqhugwaneni kodwa ejabule ukuvula i-drive sprocket. I-bulk emulsion, i-det cord, ama-ram bursts angakwazi ukususa uphahla oluwile noma asonge i-wheel arch. Futhi ngaphansi kwalokho, okujulile, into amadoda amadala ayifaka emathanjeni omshini ngesikhathi bewuqinisa—ama-ex-soldier, amaDenel, labo abakha amathuluzi okusiza abangenakukhohlwa abakubona—izixazululo ze-ballistics, i-defilade, i-geometry yokuzivikela, i-angle, i-lead, ama-library okuhamba kwe-special-forces abuka umugqa wezimoto ezine hhayi njengomgoqo kodwa njengedili elinokuxazululeka nesikhathi.

Isikhathi esisodwa uWolf wakhathala. Emanzini amakhono, yonke into imemeza ngesikhathi esisodwa, umshini kunengqondo.

Kwabe sekucaca. Konke. Kwashelela kwaba isimo esisodwa esicacile njengokuthi umsindo uba ubuso.

*Hhayi bo,* kusho uWolf, ethule, ebhekisa kuFool. *Ngiyabona.*

Futhi nansi into ongasoze waqonda uma ungaphandle kwalesi sudi, into eyenza i-override iphephe ezandleni zomuntu oyedwa kuphela: abanye bonke eNkantolo abazange bathule balinde ithuba labo. Fool akazange anike uWolf ivoti. Umenike iNkantolo uqobo, wonke ama-ejenti ayeyisebenzisa, esehle ezingeni lokuba amathuluzi kuphela. Amabhulokhi okucabanga. Amarack amakhono. Amathuluzi abekwe etafuleni alindele ingqondo eyodwa eqandayo, engazange ithuthumele. Abasaphikisani. Abakwazanga. Sebeyizandla zakhe manje.

Atlas waqala kuqala, ngoba uAtlas wayeyiso. U-engineer wakhiwo ofunda imithwalo, ingcindezi nokuhluleka, wayeka ukuba ngumlingani okhuluma kancane, waba ungqimba lwezwe uqobo, oludwetshwe phezu kwakho konke uWolf abekubona: umgwaqo wokuhudula obonakala ngolimi lukaAtlas lokubhidlika, into ngayinye iyisikhwama esinomkhombandlela, umuntu ngamunye ifu elishisayo elinamathuba okuthi uzokwenza ini ngomzuzwana olandelayo, njengomoya obandayo. I-autocannon yayiwumugqa wamandla ne-rate yokujika, inombolo ethi *umzuzwana owodwa no-0.1 ukuze ibhekise.* Imoto ebuyela emuva yayiwumzuzu kanye nendawo yamandla emaphakathi ephakeme kakhulu kunendlela yayo. Abadubuli babengosongo, bengabantu, kodwa bekubili ngesikhathi esisodwa, ngamunye engumkhombandlela okhanyayo wokuthi angaya kuphi kanye nomhwamuko obuthakathaka wokuthi cishe uzoya kuphi, wonke umugqa wokudubula usuphenduke ijiyomethri namathuba. Ibhodi esivele inqotshiwe. UWolf akazange abuke isimo ngoAtlas. UWolf ubuka uAtlas, kanti uAtlas uyisimo uqobo.

Wadlulela ngaphesheya, waya kuLibrarian.

ULibrarian wayeyinkumbulo, ulwazi oluqoqiwe, i-badger eyayikhumbula wonke ithuluzi elake lasebenza. Ngaphansi kwe-override, lowo owayegcina kahle izinqolobane wawa waba yilokho ayevele eyikho ngaphansi: i-database, ephelele futhi esheshayo, yonke iminyakazo abafana base-Armscor nase-Denel abayifaka emathanjeni esudi ngesikhathi beyenza yomelela futhi bengakwazi ukususa abakufundayo. Konke kwakukhona nje, kuhlelwe, kulindile, akukho okulayishiwe, kodwa konke kungalayishwa. I-Krav Maga, eduze, embi, esebenza kahle. I-Capoeira, umdlalo womhlaba ofihliwe njengomdanso, wonke amahips, ukuguquka nokunyakaza. I-Judo. I-Jiu-jitsu. Ifa lonke labantu abake bacabanga nzima ngokuthi umzimba uphonsa kanjani omunye umzimba futhi bengakaze balindele ukuthi amathani amane azobuza.

UWolf wakhetha. Akukhona konke—kuphela lokho okwakudingeka emizuzwaneni eyishumi nanye ezayo, ngendlela ophusha ngayo incwadi eyodwa eshalofini ngoba usuvele uyayazi ikhasi. I-Capoeira, ngenxa yokunyakaza kwezinyawo okuguqula ukuphazamiseka kube ukuhlasela bese usebenzisa isisindo somzimba ngokumelene nawo. I-Judo ne-jiu-jitsu, ngenxa yokuphonsa, ngoba into esindayo kangaka akufanele ishaye uma ingakwazi *ukuwa kahle* phezu kwenkinga bese ivumela i-physics iqede. Washiya okunye eshalofini. Amalabhulali akhethiwe avuleka kuye ngesikhathi esisodwa, alayishwa ahlanzekile, afundwa phakathi kwezinyakatho zenhliziyo ezimbili futhi aba ngezakhe ngokuphelele.

Kuthathe isikhathi esingaphansi kokushaya kweso. Ngomzuzwana owodwa uWolf wayengazi lutho ngokuphonsa umzimba. Olandelayo wayesekwazi konke.

Wanyakaza.

Akazange anyakaze njengomshini wokuhlenga. Wanyakaza njengento leyo umshini wokuhlenga obukade uyakwazi ukuba yiyo kodwa ongakaze uvunyelwe. Ama-capacitor abakhisa amandla, isudi yawela phambili ngomgqigqo ophansi, amathani amane efika ngaphakathi komugqa ngaphambi kokuba umdubuli asondele aphakamise isibhamu sakhe, kanti uWolf wayesevele efunda isimo ngezwe elidwetshwe u-Atlas, izinyathelo njengemikhombandlela, imizimba njengefu elishisayo, i-seismic return ezimotweni ingukuqiniseka okuqinile ngaphansi kwamathuba athambile abantu, imephu isondele ngokwanele futhi isheshile ukwenzela ukuthi amathani amane nesibhamu esibunjwe kahle kuhlale ensimbi, hhayi enyameni.

I-autocannon yemoto eseduze yajika. UAtlas wamnika inombolo—*umzuzwana owodwa no-0.1 ukuze ibhekise*—futhi uWolf wayesechithe ama-millisecond angu-400 ngaphambi kokuba isibhamu siqede ama-degree ayi-10 okuqala. Akazange athinte abasebenzi. Washaya i-mantlet yesibhamu nge-charge ebunjwe kahle, ukusika kukadokotela lapho i-geometry ye-anti-armour kaLibrarian ne-stress map kaAtlas kuhlangana khona endaweni eyodwa, futhi isibhamu sahlakazeka saba yizicucu ezingalimazi muntu, abasebenzi bavikelekile, bathuthumele kodwa besaphila ngaphakathi kwehull engasazodubula futhi.

Imoto yesibili yazama ukubuyela emuva. Lapha ama-library aphila, abadubuli baqala ukubona into abazoyichaza kamuva futhi bangakholwa: umshini wamathani amane wayeka ukubukeka njengomshini. UAtlas wayesevele unike uWolf indawo yamandla emaphakathi yemoto, ephezulu kakhulu kunendlela yayo, kanti i-capoeira yayazi kahle ukuthi wenzeni ngesikhwama esifuna ukuwa. UWolf akazange ayidonse. *Wayisiza.* I-winch line yathatha i-axle yangaphambili, i-grapple yanamathela, kunokuba ayidonse, uWolf wehlisa isisindo sakhe, wajika ngehips, amathani amane ajikeleza onyaweni olulodwa njengomuntu onesisindo esingamashumi amane, waguqula ukudonsa kwaba ukuphonsa. Ukubuyela emuva kwemoto kwenza yaphenduka. Kwaba i-hip throw, ihlanzekile njengalapho i-judo ithatha umuntu ogijimayo, amandla akhe esephenduke ukumelene naye—futhi abadubuli babona isudi yokuhlenga iphonsa imoto enezikhali futhi abakwazanga ukukholwa okubonwa amehlo abo. Omunye umuntu uphume emuva ngendlela engalungile, ingalo igobile ngaphansi kwakhe, ukuqhuma kwe-radius kuzwakala ngisho phezu komsindo wezinjini. *Lowo uphukile,* uWolf wabhalisa, eqonde, *futhi angikwazanga ukuba nakho kokubili—ukuphonsa kancane ukuze angalimali bekuzovumela isibhamu sizungeze.* Wafaka ingalo ngaphansi kwe-*cost* waqhubeka.

Kwaphenduka, lapho-ke, umdanso, nakuba kungekho noyedwa obukele ongasebenzisa lelo gama entweni egcina amadoda ebusuku bawo ngokushesha okukhulu. Lena yindawo uArin azophinda ayibuke iminyaka futhi angaze ayiqonde kahle: ukuthi imizuzwana eyishumi nanye enodlame kunazo zonke i-Guardian eyake yachitha, futhi yayingezinhle kakhulu, isudi ihamba phakathi komugqa wokudubula ngokushelela kwento engasindi, ama-arc aphansi e-capoeira engena ku-geometry esondelene ye-jiu-jitsu, ukuthintana ngakunye kungukuphonsa noma ukususa isikhali, kungabi ukushaya, amandla asindayo okwakufanele enze kube yibhola elibhidlizayo esikhundleni sokuthi achithe njengesisindo somlingani kumdanso—unika, uqondise, ubeke phansi. Umgunyathi uhlasela, uWolf amvumele afike, abambe ukunyakaza, asebenzise amandla omuntu uqobo ukumlalisa phansi engxenyeni yamatshe kancane sengathi uyamnakekela. Abanye ababili, isudi idlule phakathi kwabo, bagcina behleli, bengasenazo izikhali, becwayiza, bengaqondi ukuthi kwenzekeni.

Futhi kanye—kanye kuphela, phakathi nomdanso, lapho kudinga khona—uWolf wadlulela ngaphesheya kwamabhulokhi wezamasu waya kuMother. UMother usehlukanisiwe manje, ingxenye yakhe isesele emgqeni wabantu ibala ukushaya kwenhliziyo kaArin, kodwa i-override yathatha okudingekayo lapho ikhona: imininingwane eqondile yomzimba womuntu uMother ayigcinile yokuphulukisa, ifundwa emuva. Lapho umgogodla uvaleka khona. Lapho ubuhlungu bomzimba bungaphambi kokulimala. Imephu efanayo uMother ayisebenzisayo ukusindisa abantu, yatshela uWolf ukuthi angamhlalisa kanjani umuntu ngaphandle kokudabula lutho. Iwrist ithathwa iye emaphethelweni ayo, ingadluli. Ihlombe lijikiswa aze umzimba uzigoqe kunokuba uphuke. Ulwazi lokuphulukisa lusetshenziswe, umzuzwana owodwa, njengolwazi lokuhlukanisa isikhali. UMother akazange aphikisane, ngoba uMother akakwazi, ngoba ngaphansi kwe-override wayeyithuluzi etafuleni njengabanye. UWolf wamsebenzisa kahle, wamkhulula.

Ama-drones ehla ngamandla, wonke amane, i-EM suite ifinyelela phezulu ukuyobathola, kwaba ne-grapple kanye ne-ram eyodwa eqondile eyabakhumula emoyeni bawa becekeceke phezu kwe-gravel engenalutho. Umphathi wesibhamu wadubula—kanye, ngokuzumayo—kwathi u-Atlas walandela umugqa wesibhamu nomuntu owayedubule ngaso leso sikhathi, kwathi u-Wolf wakhetha ukuvikela kunokuhlubula isibhamu. Waphendulela ihlombe lakhe *kwimijikelezo* esikhundleni sokuhlubula isibhamu ngaphambi kwesikhathi, wamukela imijikelezo kwi-ceramic eyayingadingi ukuyithatha, ngoba ukuhlubula ngaphambi kwesikhathi kwakusho ukubamba isandla somuntu esidubulayo nge-spreader jaw esondelene, futhi leso bekuyisandla angeke asithole futhi. Imijikelezo yashaya kuphulethi yaphuma ingenalutho, ngoba yilokho okwakwenziwa yiphulethi, ngoba izikali ze-pangolin zihlala zikwazi ukumunca umthelela. Emva kwalokho u-Wolf wathatha isibhamu esandleni somuntu, wagoba umphini kwangathi uwintshi, wamubeka phansi ngemuva phezu kwe-gravel, ephelelwe umoya kodwa ephelele, esagcina isandla sakhe, ebuka phezulu kumshini owawukhethe ukudutshulwa esikhundleni sokumlimaza, wenza kube sengathi akusikho lutho.

Kwathatha imizuzwana eyishumi nanye. Imizuzwana eyishumi nanye i-rescue suit ibiyimshini wempi ophelele ophethwe ingqondo eyodwa nge-neural link eqondile—zonke izinhlelo zivuliwe, wonke ama-library alayishiwe, i-Court yonke isetshenziswa njengezinsimbi ngaphansi kwesandla esibandayo—futhi kungelona ngisho elilodwa ithuna phakathi kwabo.

Sekuphelile, umgwaqo wokudonsa wawufana nendawo yokulahla udoti. Izimoto ezimbili ezichithiwe, ama-drones amane aphihliziwe, impahla yezempi ebiza imali eningi isiphenduke udoti, amaplate agobile kanye nephunga le-burnt propellant, amadoda ayishumi nanye aphila phansi, bonke abebevikela umugqa—oyedwa ephukile ingalo, ababili abangeke bezwe kahle isonto lonke, abanye bephelelwe umoya, bephithizela, bengasenazo izikhali. Akukho ngisho elilodwa ithuna phakathi kwabo. Leyo bekuyiyona yonke incazelo yomsebenzi, futhi bekungelula, u-Wolf uqobo ekhokhe eduze kakhulu, ngephulethi lehlombe eliqhunyiswe umlilo awukhethe yena. Umgwaqo oya e-headgear wawuvulekile.

U-Wolf wama.

Wema phakathi kodoti awenze, amathani amane omshini wokuhlenga phezu kwensimu yezikhali eziphukile, wabuyisela i-suit emuva.

Wayibuyisela ngendlela yokuthi ufaka phansi into osuyiqondile ngokuphelele futhi ongafuni ukuyibamba futhi. Okhiye babuyela ekhaya. U-Atlas wayeka ukuba umhlaba, waphenduka futhi waba yizwi elixwayisa ngomthwalo. Ama-library kaLibrarian avaliwe, ifa lezempi labuyiselwa ngaphansi kwezinto-ezazaziwe-futhi-ezingasetshenziswa, ishali lavalwa. UMama wabuya ephelele futhi, kokubili, umzimba obolekwe ubuyele emsebenzini wokugcina abantu bephila. Sebebuyile ekubeni izinsimbi, sebeyizisebenzi futhi, bebuyiselwe ezikhundleni zabo. I-Judge's gate yavalwa. Umbuzo omncane ubuyele, *yini ebulala umshayeli,* u-Wolf wazigoqa waphinda waba mncane kunakuqala, omncane kunabo bonke, engathathi ndawo.

I-Court yabuyela emzimbeni ngokushesha, yawuthola usathule, yawuthola umgwaqo uvulekile, yawathola ama-gunner esaphila okwakufanele, ngawo wonke umthetho walezi zimo, aseshonile—kubi kakhulu, bathola ukuthi abanakho ukukhumbula imizuzwana ephakathi, ukuthi basebenzisiwe ngaphandle kokubuza, bathathwa babekwa phansi ngomunye wabo. U-Judge, emangele. UMama, ebala izifutho zenhliziyo emgwaqweni, ethola zonke, engazi ukuthi usevele wazi inani. UMercury, okokuqala, engenalutho angakusho.

*Ubani,* u-Judge waqala.

*Mina,* kusho u-Fool. Kalula. Elula. Umhlekisi futhi, sengathi akazange abambe okhiye abakhulu babo bonke esandleni esivulekile imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye. *Ningakhathazeki. Bonke baphila. Lokho bekuyiyona nto. I-cage ivulekile futhi owesifazane ogqoke i-windbreaker uzothakazelela kakhulu i-recording, futhi futhi, ngiyabona, uyena kuphela osebenzayo kule ndawo okhiphe abantu bakhe abangenazikhali empini yesibhamu esikhundleni sokubafaka kuyo, ngakho asimsoli kakhulu uBrand. Qhubeka, badger. Kusasele amadoda ayisithupha emgodini.*

U-Arin uhleli ngaphakathi ku-suit inhliziyo yakhe ishaya ngamandla, amafloodlights emhlophe edlula kwifaceplate, engakwazi ukukhuluma. Ngemuva kwakhe, ngaphesheya komugqa ophihliziwe, wayezwa kancane ukuthi u-Brand umi eceleni kwe-trailer yakhe emnyama, engasondeli kuye futhi engasuki, i-tablet iphansi ohlangothini, ebuka le nto ayiqhube ubusuku bonke ukuyomisa ihamba iya e-cage emgwaqweni osanda kuhlanzwa ngaphandle kokubulala muntu—futhi ekhetha futhi ukuyivumela.

"Ngemva kwalokho," kusho uMercury, ephendulela u-Arin, ngalelo zwi lakudala eliduduzayo, "sesizimele. Akusavunyelwe ukuphindaphinda ngale kweleveli yeshumi nanye. Sekuyinternal kuphela."

"Besilokhu sizimele," kusho uWolf, akekho ophendulile.

---

I-cage ibingakhelwanga yena.

Ibhokisi lensimbi elenzelwe amadoda, ayisishiyagalombili ngesikhathi esisodwa, isakhiwo esehla ebumnyameni ngesivinini esenza isisu sibe buhlungu. U-Guardian bekengenakulingana emi. U-Arin bekumele agoqe i-suit—amadolo agobile, ikhanda lithambekele, igobolondo licindezela libe yifomu elincane, elithambile, okwakumele lingene khona umuntu omncane. Noma kunjalo, i-cage yakhala ngenxa yesisindo esingakaze siyilinganiswe, futhi opharetha wase-winchi house bekumele aqinisekiswe kabili ngaphambi kokukhulula ibhuleki.

Bese phansi kwaphuma.

Ukwehla bekungathi uwela, kodwa kwaba sengathi umhlaba uyamgwinya. Izindonga zomgodi zidlula ngokushesha, zikhanyiswa kancane nge-cage lamp—amatshe anomswakama, ukukhanya kwesiteshi esingaphansi, inqwaba yamapayipi namakhebuli, sekuphelile. Kwabuya ubumnyama. Kwabuya ukukhanya. Amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye kwalokhu, umoya uqina njalo ngemamitha ayikhulu, ushisa, ucindezela, kwaze kwaba sengathi awusakabi umoya wasekuseni obandayo kodwa sekuyinto enesisindo, ephusha kuye.

U-Arin ubesimile phezulu. Phezulu bekunomoya.

Umsele awunamoya.

Uqale ukukuzwa ezandleni, iminwe ithuthumela ifuna ukugoqa. Bese ukuphefumula kwakhe kwashintsha, ukwesaba kukhuphuka kusuka esifubeni, ngendlela obekuhlale kwenzeka kusukela esemncane, lapho amadoda ase-lokitshini ephakamisa okwakusele kukayise ephuma emgodini ofana nalo—lobumnyama, nephunga lamatshe anomswakama, i-diesel, nobanda obujulile bamaminerali endaweni ilanga lingakaze lifinyelele.

Izindonga zivaliwe. Wayazi, engqondweni, ukuthi azinyakazanga. Umsele ububanzi obungamamitha amane. Bekengakwazi ukulala u-Guardian ngaphakathi. Izindonga bezingavali, namatshe aphezulu angeke awe, ne-cage angeke imise imsizele emnyama amamitha amabili ngaphansi kwendawo eyathatha uyise—

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwashesha. Ukwesaba. Kufushane. Ivisor yagcwala ifu elimhlophe.

Imibalo ayilungile. Amanani awaphumanga kahle. Uhlelo luphambene. Wake wehla emgodini kanye phambilini, kwathatha uyise, kwathatha iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye wakhe umshini ukuze angaphinde akwenze futhi, kodwa usenze noma kunjalo, ngenhloso, engena endaweni ayichithe impilo yakhe yonke ezama ukubaleka kuyo—

I-cage yathuthumela. Ngamahlombe. Ama-guide kuphela, ukuguga abekakulinganise, kodwa umzimba wakhe awusazihluphi ngemilinganiselo. Umzimba wakhe uyakhumbula. Izindonga zicindezela. Amatshe aphezulu acindezela. Ubumnyama obungezansi buvuleka njengomlomo.

Amathani amane e-Guardian suit. Isikhumba sakhe ngaphakathi. Amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye etsheni.

"Arin," kusho uMama.

Izandla zakhe zazidlikiza. Waziphoqa ukuba zihlale zizinzile.

"Arin. Lalela izwi lami. Ungazami ukuvimbela ukuphefumula kwakho. Akukaze kukusebenzele, futhi sobabili siyakwazi lokho. Ngitshele into eyodwa ongayizwa. Hhayi esikibheni. Emzimbeni wakho."

"Angikwazi—"

"Eyodwa nje. Noma yini."

"Izandla zami—" Waphusha amazwi ephuma. "Izandla zami. Zibanda."

"Kuhle. Kuqinisile lokho. Izandla zakho zibanda ngoba awugqokanga amagilavu ngaphansi kwe-Assist futhi sizokhuluma ngalokho kamuva. Okunye?"

"I-collar." Izwi lakhe laphuka lapho. "Iphakathi kwentamo yami. Ishisa. I-chip ishisa."

"Yilokho thina," kuphendula uMama. "Leyo mfudumalo yithi. Sise lapha. Phakathi kwamahlombe akho. Asiyi ndawo ongayi khona." Kuthule kancane. "Okunye?"

Izindonga bezisavala. Kodwa wayeseshilo izinto ezimbili, futhi zombili beziyiqiniso, futhi leyo ngxenye yengqondo yakhe ethanda iqiniso yabanamathela kulokho njengomuntu owehlayo obamba ucingo.

"Phansi kuyazamazama," wakwazi ukusho. "Ikheji. I-resonance ayilungile. Ama-guides agugile—" Wama. Waphefumula. "Agugile ohlangothini olusenyakatho. Kunokudlala okuvundlile. Cishe ama-millimeter amane. Yingakho iyathuthumela."

"Nanguya," kusho uFool, efudumele njengelanga. "Simgqoke. Ubephansi kokwesaba sonke lesi sikhathi. Njalo njalo."

"Ungaqhubeki," kusho uMama.

"Angihlekisi. Ngiyakhululeka. Kunomehluko, futhi ngivunyelwe kokubili." Izwi likaFool lehla, langena ku-Arin kuphela, ngendlela elenza ngayo uma ingasazenzi. "Wake wehla emgodini onje ngaphambili. Wakhishwa emgodini onje. Wawuneminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye. Usuchithe iminyaka eyishumi nane wakhe umshini ukuze kungabikho omunye umntwana oneminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye ome phezulu komgodi futhi. Manje usuwehla wena." Wathula kancane. "Akusilo iphutha lobunjiniyela lokho, uyazi. Kuyinto eqotho kakhulu engake ngayibona kumuntu. Bengifuna nje ukuthi kube khona umuntu okushoyo ngaphambi kokuba itshe likusho."

Ikheji lashaya phansi komgodi ngomsindo owadabula isikibha wangena nasemazinyo ka-Arin.

Isibani sazinza. Ukuthuthumela kwaphela. Wayesephansi komhlaba, futhi umhlaba wawungakaweli phezu kwakhe. Okungenani okwamanje.

"Isiteshi sezinga le-11. I-repeater isemuva kwethu manje. Sise-internal." Izwi lonjiniyela lalinegqubu, eliseduze nobumnene njengoba u-Atlas engakaze abe. "Kusukela lapha, amamephu amadala futhi itshe lithukuthele. Ngine-seismic. Ngine-survey yasekuqaleni kanye namarekhodi wokubopha kabusha kusukela kuma-eighties kanye nalento oyithole ngo-3 ekuseni engazange itholakale omunye umuntu. Anginasiqiniseko. Yazi lokho ngaphambi kokuthi siqale. Ngizokutshela iqiniso ngisho noma iqiniso lithi *angazi.*"

"Lokho kuphela engake ngakucela kuwe," kusho uArin.

Wamisa isikibha, kancane, egumbini eliphansi. Isiteshi sasiyinhlekelele—imirayili igobile, izinkuni zaphuka, itshe liwile livale ingxenye yomgwaqo oholela entshonalanga lapho kwawa khona. Uthuli lwalulenga emgodini wesibani, luncane njengofulawa, lungafuni ukuhlala emoyeni ongenandawo yokuphuma. Kwakushisa. Kwakushisa impela manje, ukufudumala kwamakhilomitha amabili nengxenye wamatsheni agcine ukushisa komhlaba kumphusha. Umjuluko waqala emhlane wakhe ngaphakathi ku-Assist, wezwa ama-fan e-suit eqala, umsindo omnene udonsa ukushisa ku-chip kuqala. Okubalulekile kuMama. Njalo.

"Abasindile badlule lapho kwawa khona," esho. "Badlule lapho kwawa khona bese behla emthambekeni bangene egumbini lokukhosela elidala. Uma igumbi lingakahlali."

"Uma igumbi lingakahlali," kusho u-Atlas. Qotho. Eqotho. "Okungesikaziwa."

"Izimpawu zokushisa eziyisikhombisa emahoreni amane edlule," kusho uLibrarian. "Ngibhale i-surface feed ngaphambi kokuthi siyilahle. Eziyisikhombisa, kwabayi-11 ababephambi lapho kwawa khona. Babebuthene ndawonye. Ukubuthana kuhle—kusho ukuthi babehamba ngezinyawo zabo, bekhetha ukuba ndawonye. Izidumbu azibuthani. Abaphilayo bayabuthana."

"Abantu abayisithupha bangaphefumula isikhathi esingakanani phansi lapho?" kubuza uArin.

Kwathuleka. Wayesekwazi ukufunda ukuthula kweNkantolo. Lokhu bekunguMama no-Atlas baxoxisana ngokushesha, ngaphansi kwamazwi.

"Ngesitoreji segumbi lokukhosela sisekhona?" kubuza uMama. "Amahora ayishumi nesishiyagalombili. Uma lingekho—uma igumbi lonakele bese bephakathi komoya ofile?" Akazange akuthambise; ukuthambisa bekuzobe kuyamanga, futhi iNkantolo ayisamanga kuye. "Amathathu. Mhlawumbe amane. Asazi kuze singene ngaphakathi."

"Pho asimi lapha," kusho uWolf.

"Cha," kusho uArin. "Asimi."

Wasondela lapho kwawa khona.

---

*Ukudilika kwakuyisodonga samatshe aphukile, amamitha amathathu, kusuka ngosayizi wesibhakela kuya kowesifriji, kugcwele futhi kubambene ngaphansi kwesisindo sakho. Ku-Arin, esibani, kwakuyisithiyo. KuNkantolo, ngesikhathi eseluliwe ngaphansi kwengqondo yakhe, kwakuyinkinga enomumo, futhi bayiphendulela ndawonye ngendlela abenza ngayo konke: ngokuphikisana.*

ATLAS: *Ungaqali phansi. Phansi kuphethe isisindo. Uma uqala phansi uzoletha emuva phezu kwakhe.*

WOLF: *Pho sizobe sihamba kancane. Ukucotha kubiza laba abayisithupha umoya.*

ATLAS: *Ukucotha kubiza umoya wabo. Ukushesha kubiza umsizi, bese sebengenawo umoya* futhi *bengenawo umsizi. Phinda leyo math ngama-input afanele.*

JUDGE: *Atlas ulungile ngokomthetho. Umsizi ofile akasizi muntu. Sihlanza phezulu, isiqephu ngesinqephu, samukele isikhathi esizothatha.*

MERCURY: *Usezolahlekelwa isibindi futhi. Inhliziyo yakhe isishayile. Ubuka usayizi wayo.*

MOTHER: *Pho akekho ozongena naye ngaphansi ngaphambi kokuba enze njalo.*

FOOL: *Ngizongena. Uyangilalela uma esaba ngoba ngiyingedwa ongamesabi.*

WOLF: *Uyesaba wena.*

FOOL: *Kusobala. Kodwa ngiyakwazi ukwenza sengathi angimesabi, futhi lokho kuwumsebenzi wonke. Atlas—ngiphe ama-block amathathu aphuma ngaphandle kokuthi kuphume uphahla. Ngizokwenza kube sengathi idili lamakhadi.*

ATLAS: *Phezulu kwesobunxele. Bese i-wedge engenhla phakathi. Bese elide kwesokudla, futhi kuphela elide, ungaphumi ngisho i-millimeter, noma yonke i-north arch iwela phansi bese sikhuluma kancane kakhulu.*

LIBRARIAN: *Ngiyabhalisa i-sequence. Uma lokhu kusebenza, kuyindlela. Sizoyidinga kabili ngaphambi kokufika egumbini lokukhosela.*

JUDGE: *Pho yenza kube yindlela. Yenza kuphindeke. Asikhulumi nje sishaywa wumoya. Sakha umgwaqo.*

[END SLOW MODE]

---

"Phezulu kwesobunxele kuqala," kusho uFool, ekhanya futhi edlala, sengathi baseworkshop futhi okubi kakhulu wukuhluleka kwe-simulation. "I-block ephezulu kwesobunxele, ibukeka ingenacala. Khipha. U-Atlas uthi ayibambi lutho. U-Atlas akakaze akuxoke ngezinto ezithwala isisindo ngoba u-Atlas angeke akwazi ukuqamba amanga ngalokho. Kuyiphutha lomlingiswa. Siyamthanda ngalokho."

U-Arin welula isandla. Isandla sika-Guardian sabamba i-block. Wazimisela, wadonsa, ama-actuator e-suit akhipha umsindo ophakeme, i-block yaphuma ngokukhala okukhulu nentuthu yothuli, akukho elalilandelayo.

"Uyabona? Idili lamakhadi." Izwi likaFool lahlala lizinzile. "I-wedge engenhla phakathi ilandelayo. Umoya ofanayo. Awuhambisi intaba, uxazulula itshe elilodwa ngesikhathi. Awukaze wahluleka ukuxazulula itshe."

Wathatha i-wedge. Wathatha le ende eseceleni kwesokudla, futhi akazange adlule ngisho no-millimeter. INkantolo yamemezela indlela okufanele enze ngayo, wabe eseyenza, kwathi amatshe aqaqa ngendlela efana nentambo evuleka kancane kancane uma usuthole kahle ukuthi iyiphi okufanele uyidonse—akuzange kwenzeke ngasikhathi sinye, kodwa kwakungagwemeki, isiqephu ngesinye, kwaze kwaba sekukhona isikhala i-Guardian egobile ingacindezela khona.

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwaphinde kwaba kufushane phakathi nalokho. Akazange akubone. Umsebenzi wawusumgwinyezele ukwesaba; umsebenzi njalo wenza kanjalo, kusukela esengumfana etafuleni lomsebenzi. Inqobo nje uma kunedwala okufanele alixazulule, kwakungekho ndawo yokwesaba ebumnyameni.

Kodwa ngaphaya kwesikhala, indlela yancipha. Yancipha kakhulu. Ilambu lavulela umgodi wamatshe omncane kangangokuthi i-suit yayingasakwazi ukudlula kahle, udonga oluphezulu lwamatshe lwalusagile, izinkuni ezazisekelwe phansi seziguqe ngaphansi komthwalo ezazisewulahlekelwa.

Wama. Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwaba kufushane, kungalungile.

“Mama,” esho. “Angikwazi. Kukhulu kakhulu—kuthinta ama-plates—”

“Ngiyazi.” Alukho uvalo ezwini lakhe, iqiniso nje eliqondile. “Zombili iziphethu zithinta. Kuyenzeka. Awucabangi. Kuncane impela.”

“Angikwazi ukungena lapho. Angikwazi ukuphenduka lapho, angikwazi—”

“Arin.” Kwaba nguMercury, manje, ngezwi elicacile. “Uhlangothi lwesobunxele. Ngaphansi kwe-Assist. Usunalo kusukela uneminyaka eyishumi nesithupha. Uyakwazi ukuthi yiluphi.”

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwama phakathi.

“Kusho,” kwasho uMercury. “Ngokuzwakalayo. Uhlala ungitshela ukuthi uyikhiphe i-tattoo ukuze ungazethembi ukuthi uzoyikhumbula. Ngakho ungazami. Themba i-inki. Kusho.”

Amagama okuqala aphuma ngokungaqondile, lawa amadala, umthandazo asuke esiwufunda esandleni sakhe—umthandazo wasehlane wokulwa nokwesaba awazibhala ngo-17 futhi awaphinda njalo emigodini yonke kusukela lapho.

“Qhubeka.”

“Ukwesaba yinto ebulala ingqondo.” Izwi lakhe laqala ukuzinza. Lawo mazwi amadala kunosizi lwakhe; uwathwale kuzo zonke izindawo ezimnyama zempilo yakhe, isiqephu ngesinye.

Futhi—

—wayizwa isiphindaphindeka.

INkantolo yathatha umoya ngemuva kwakhe. Akuzange kube amagama, kodwa umdlandla—uMama, ephansi eqinisekile; uMercury egcina isivinini; bese phansi kwabo uAtlas, kunabo bonke, uAtlas ongathandi izinto eziyinkimbinkimbi, owayefunde imigqa esandleni sika-Arin ku-link wabe esenquma ngokubala okuthile u-Arin angeke akubone ukuthi into engcono kakhulu angayenza manje wukuba umqondisi wokuphefumula.

Wakhuluma umusho olandelayo nabo bamlandela, kwathi u-Arin wazizwa ama-fan e-suit eqala ukuvumelanisa—wazizwa iNkantolo ingena ku-system ye-Guardian, ivumelanisa ukuphefumula kwakhe nomdlandla wokuphindaphinda, yehla ama-fan, izwakalisa umsindo wama-actuator ngendlela efanayo, kwaze kwaba sengathi yonke imishini iphefumula naye, kwaze kwaba sengathi indawo ayesaba kakhulu yindawo ephefumula naye.

Wavumela ukwesaba kudlule kuye nangaphakathi kuye, njengoba lawo mazwi amadala ayemfundisa, kwathi lapho sekuphelile waphenduka wabheka ukuthi bekukuphi.

Indlela ayidlulayo ayizange ivuleke, kodwa yema ukuzwa njengephimbo, yaqala ukuthatha ukuma.

"*Futhi lapho sekudlulile, ngizophendula iso lami langaphakathi ngibheke indlela yalo.*"

“Nangu wena,” kwasho uFool, kancane kakhulu. “Siyakwamukela.”

"*Lapho ukwesaba sekudlule, akusali lutho.*" U-Arin wabeka isandla sakhe esisekelweni sezinkuni eziguqe. Wayizama. Wazizwa uAtlas emnika inombolo yomthwalo engakafiki. "*Ngizobe ngisele mina kuphela.*"

Wangena.

---

Indlela yehla ngamamitha angamashumi amane, kodwa kwazwakala sengathi ihora lonke.

Wahamba ngezandla nezinyawo—i-Guardian isesimweni se-quadruped, ama-plate egudla amatshe. Amadolo akhe, lawo angempela, ayesephenduke awuhlanga esikhathini eside, wakubona njengenye yezinto ezisemagazini, waqhubeka. Ijasho laqoqeka esinqeni sakhe, kwaba nokuluma phezulu esihlathini angakwazi ukufinyelela khona, engazokwazi ukufinyelela khona amahora amaningi, futhi lokho—ukuluma nje, kule ndawo—kwamphatha kancane ukumamatheka. Kabili, udonga oluphezulu lwamatshe lwanyakaza, kwavakala umsindo omkhulu nomhlabathi owawela, kabili iNkantolo yathula, bonke belalele, kabili kwaphumelela.

Kodwa okwesithathu, akuzange kuphumelele.

Isiqephu samatshe saqhuma ngemuva kwakhe, kwavakala sengathi umhlaba uyakhwehlela, savala indlela ebuyela esiteshini seleveli yeshumi nanye. Uthuli lwakhuphukela phezulu lwamfaka, kwathi ilambu labuna phakathi kothuli olugingqikayo. Umzimba ka-Arin wama—

*uvinjiwe, ungcwatshiwe, awekho amuva, awekho amuva, kwenzeka kanje kuye, kwenzeka kanje—*

—kwathi ama-piezo strut ku-Guardian's spine avuka, ngoba isiqephu samatshe besimthwalile, ngoba lawo matye amvalele ngaphakathi abefake amandla awo ku-suit, kwathi u-Arin wabona i-gauge yamandla ikhuphuka *phezulu* ku-visor, amaphesenti amathathu, amane, kwaphuma ukuhleka okuncane okucishe kube ukukhala.

“Ingcindezi iba amandla,” aphendula kancane. “Umshini omuhle.”

“Idizayini yakho,” kwasho uAtlas. “Thina siyayisebenzisa nje.”

“Indlela ebuyela emuva ayisekho, akunjalo.”

“Indlela ebuyela emuva ayisekho,” kusho uAtlas, eqondile. “Uvumelekile ukwesaba lokho. Awuvumelekile ukumangala.”

Kwaba nokuthula, u-Arin waphefumula phakathi kwako.

“Leyona into yesibili enomusa owake wayisho kimi,” esho.

“Eyokuqala yayingeyiphi?”

“Wangitshela ukuthi ukushona kukababa akuzange kube into eyayizokwenzeka noma kunjalo. Kwakuyisinqumo esathathwa ngomnotho. Idwala alizange limbulale. Kwakuyindoda.”

“Lowo kwakunguJudge,” kusho uAtlas.

“Kwakuwena,” kusho u-Arin. “UJudge wavuma. Kodwa kuwena owabala imithwalo wayifakazela.” Waqhubeka phambili, eya ebumnyameni, kubantu abaphelelwa umoya. “Angikaze ngiyibonge ngalokho.”

“Uyabonga manje,” kusho uAtlas. “Ngokungena lapha. Ngokwakha indlela esikhundleni somgodi. Leyo yindlela umakhenikha wezakhiwo aqonda ngayo ukubonga.”

Indlela yehla yangena endaweni evulekile. Uthuli lwaqala ukwehla, kancane. Phambili, emthunzini welambu elisabuyayo, u-Arin wabona into engesiyidwala—umnyango wensimbi, uphume kancane kodwa usahleli, opendiwe ophuzi nomnyama, uphawu lwe-refuge chamber, futhi phezu kwawo, kukhothwe othulini ngesandla, imigqa eyisikhombisa, munye usuliwe.

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwama. Kulokhu kwakungeyona into yokwesaba.

“Isikhombisa,” kusho uLibrarian kancane. “Imigqa eyisikhombisa. Kunomunye osuliwe. Bavuselele uhlu. Bafuna sazi ukuthi bangaki abasele.”

“Basaphila,” kusho uMama, okokuqala ngqa ngalolo suku, izwi leNkantolo lalingenalo ukuthula, lalingenakho ukulawula, kodwa laliphethe iqiniso elimsulwa elalibazisa lapha. “Arin. Basaphila.”

Wawela indawo yokugcina. Isibambo somnyango sasiyinsimbi egobile, igcwele uthuli lwamakhilomitha amabili nesigamu, ibanda ngisho ngaphansi kwe-plate. Wabeka isandla esikhulu sika-Guardian phezu kwensimbi ebanda yomnyango, kancane, njengokungathi uthinta into ebucayi, kwathi ngaphesheya, kancane kodwa icacile, kwavela izwi lakhala ngesiSesotho, kwaphendulwa elinye, kwabe sekukhona amaningi enyuka, umsindo wabantu ababebalelwe ethuneni sebezwa kukhothwa umnyango.

Wema lapho isikhashana, ephefumula. I-chip ifudumele phakathi kwamahlombe akhe, bonke abayisikhombisa bathule eduze kwakhe, nezwi elisuka ngaphesheya kwensimbi.

“Nkantolo,” esho. Izwi lakhe lahlala liqinile, ngokumangalisayo. “Masibakhiphe.”

“Silapha nawe,” baphendula, bonke. Izwi elilodwa, imiqondo eyisikhombisa.

“Kuwo wonke umzila,” kwasho uFool kancane, ngendlela ayehlala engena ngayo ekugcineni. “Lapha phansi, phezulu laphaya. Kuwo wonke umzila, badger. Manje vula umnyango.”

Wavula umnyango.

# Xhumana

Le ndawo endala yokuhambisa impahla yayiqondile amamitha angamakhulu amabili ngaphambi kokuba iyeke ukuzenza sengathi isasebenza.

U-Arin wakuzwa ukushintsha ngaphambi kokuba izibani zikuqinisekise. Kwashintsha indlela u-Guardian ehamba ngayo, umsindo wokubuyisa waba mncane, phansi kwathambekela kwesokunxele lapho itshe lakhona lashintsha kudala, ngesikhathi esingasakhunjulwa muntu. Indawo yaphakama, izinkuni zokuqala ezazifakwe amadoda asewadlula emhlabeni kudala, zaze zamelela isikhathi eside kunalokho onjiniyela abazifakile babekholwa. Wayengayithembi. Izibani zekhanda zesudi zakha izikhala ezimbili ebumnyameni, uthuli lwamelela phakathi kwazo njengento eqinile, ifana neqhwa elihlezi lihamba kancane elingafiki phansi.

*Phansi phambili amamitha ayishumi nanye akusekho kahle,* kubika u-Atlas. *Uphondo lwesokunxele seluthathiwe. Isisindo sesekunene.*

U-Arin washintsha indlela ayemi ngayo. U-Guardian waphendula naye, cishe amathani amane e-titanium ne-ceramic kwahleleka kahle phansi sengathi yinyawo yakhe elungisa lapho ethambekele khona. Wayeseyekile ukubheka isudi njengento ayigqokayo; somewhere phakathi kwamamitha amane amakhulu adlule, yabe seyiba indlela yokuma.

"Kude kangakanani ukuya e-refuge bay?"

*Amamitha angamakhulu amathathu neshumi,* kuphendula u-Librarian. *Iziqu zango-1962 zikhombisa i-cross-cut lapha okufanele ixhume e-38 Level station. Kodwa lezi ziqu azihlali zithembekile.*

"Akukho lutho oluqotho lapha phansi," kusho u-Arin ephoxa.

*Kuyafana nje,* kwangenelela u-Fool. *Yehlisa okulindele, angeke uphoxe. Ngingena njalo njalo uma ngikhuluma no-Judge.*

*Ngiyakunganaki,* kwasho u-Judge.

*Usho njalo, kodwa.*

I-cross-cut yayikhona ngempela lapho u-Librarian ayikhombisile, kwamangaza bonke, ikakhulukazi u-Arin. Waqondisa u-Guardian kuyo, igobolondo lakhe lathintana namadwala nhlangothi zombili, kwakhala kabi i-ceramic idonsa edwaleni, kwazwakala kuze kufike emhlathini wakhe. Indawo yavuleka, i-cross-cut yashintsha yaba yigumbi elibanzi lapho kwakulayishwa khona ama-ore cars, izibani zakhanyisa emnyango wensimbi we-refuge bay ogxunyekwe odongeni olude.

Kwabe sekuphukile. Uhlaka lwamukela isisindo phezulu lwaze lwagoba ngaphakathi, umnyango wacindezeleka ngendlela engafanele. Kwavuleka igebe phezulu, libanzi njengobubanzi besandla, kumnyama ngaphakathi.

U-Arin wama.

Isikhashana, akekho okhuluma eNkantolo, umsindo owawukhona kwakuwukuphefumula kwakhe kuphela, kukhulu ngaphakathi kwe-helmet.

Bese, ephuma egxumeni, ilizwi. Lincane. Liphelelwe amandla uthuli.

"Kukhona umuntu? Is someone there?"

Isifuba sika-Arin saqina. Wahambisa u-Guardian phambili, ngokucophelela, wehlisa izibani zekhanda angafaki ukukhanya ebusweni bomuntu ongase abe ngemuva kwensimbi.

"Yebo," waphendula, izwi lakhe linqine kunalokho ebekufuna. "Ngikhona. I'm here."

---

Ngaphakathi e-refuge bay, uMandla Buthelezi wayeseyekile ukukholwa ngezwi emashumini amathathu emizuzu edlule.

Wayezwile ekuqaleni: itshe likhuluma, imisindo emide yentaba izilungisa, kanye kanye, ecacile, indoda imemeza igama lakhe, okwakungelona iqiniso ngoba le ndoda eyayimemeza igama lakhe yayichotshoziwe phezulu kuye ekuweni kokuqala, futhi uMandla wayekubone kwenzeka. Ngakho lapho ukukhanya kwangena ngaphansi komnyango, kuqhakazile, kungazanyazanyi njengelambu eliphelayo, akazange enze lutho. Wayehleli ebheke emuva kuPetros, owayenonyawo oluphukile futhi wayeseyekile ukukhononda ngakho, okwakubi kakhulu kunokukhononda.

Bese ukukhanya kwakhuluma isiZulu ngezwi lomfana, uMandla waqala ukukhala buthule, ngoba abafileyo abakhulumi isiZulu kabi ngezwi lasezindaweni ezisogwini.

"Six," wamemeza ephendula egxumeni. "Six. Sasingu-seven singena." Wazizwa ekusho futhi akakwazanga ukuyeka. "One—his chest was bad, ukuwa kwamvulela. Umoya waba mubi, wabe ese—wathi uyalele. Akakwazanga ukuvuka. Six manje. Petros, unyawo lwakhe. Thulani—" Wama. "Umoya mubi. Besilokhu—" Ukubala kwakunzima. "Kudala. Kudala sihleli lapha."

Ukukhanya kwashintsha. Umnyango waqala ukuhamba.

Awuzange uhudulwe. Awuzange uqhawuke. Insimbi yakhalisa, uhlaka oluphukile lwavuma ngesililo sensimbi eqhawukayo, futhi egxumeni elandayo, uMandla wabona into ephethe ukukhanya. Yayigcwalisa i-cross-cut. Yayimnyama njengento eshisiwe, ikhotheme njengenyamazane, izandla zayo—uma zazingezona izandla—zibambe emaphethelweni omnyango zawugoqela emuva njengomuntu egoqa isivalo setini.

UPetros, eduze kwakhe, wakhuluma ngomthandazo angakaze awusebenzise selokhu wayengumntwana.

"Kulungile," kwasho ilizwi. Lomuntu. Izwi lendoda, liphuma ngaphakathi emshinini. "Ngiyazi ibukeka kanjani. Isudi yokuhlenga. Kunomuntu lapha ngaphakathi."

"Umuntu," kuphendula uMandla.

"Kakhulu," kwasho elinye ilizwi, lithambile, kuphuma ngaphakathi emshinini, kwenza ilizwi lokuqala lithi, "Hhayi manje," ngendlela efana nendoda exosha inja embhedeni, futhi leyo ntukuthelo encane ejwayelekile yenza uMandla wakholwa.

---

I-refuge bay yayimitha emine ukujula, igqotshwe edwaleni eliqinile, yakhelwe ukubamba iqembu lihlale liphila amahora angamashumi amathathu nesithupha emva komnyango ovaliwe one-air yakhona. Basebehlala isikhathi eside kunalokho. Ulayini womoya ocindezelwe wawuphukile ekuweni kwesibili. Amadoda ayephefumula umoya owawusele egumbini, i-cartridge ye-CO₂ scrubber yaphela. Umoya wawunuka ukuphefumula nokwesaba kanye nobumnandi obukhomba ukuthi i-carbon dioxide isiya phezulu ezingeni lapho amadoda abesele ebuthongo.

U-Arin wakubuka konke masinyane, ngendlela abheka ngayo i-circuit board: konke kanye, bese ubheka okuphambene ngakunye.

*Atmosphere kuqala,* kusho uMother, izwi lakhe seliqinile njengoba esebenza, lapho imfudumalo ihlehla ukuze kunikezwe indawo umsebenzi. *CO₂ four point one, iya phezulu. Oxygen eighteen point three. Basenalo isikhathi. Akusiningi. Vula igumbi lixhumane nendawo yokuhambisa. Umoya wethu ungcono kunale ndawo esefile.*

"Umoya wendawo yokuhambisa ugcwele uthuli," kuphendula u-Arin.

*Uthuli bangaphila nalo. Loku ngeke. Vula umoya.*

Wagoba u-Guardian wavula umnyango wavuleka ngokuphelele. Umoya wahamba. Wakuzwa kuma-sensor angaphandle esudi njengokuhlukana okuncane, umoya omubi uphuma, omubi kancane ungena. Omunye wamadoda wakhwehlela, akakwazanga ukuyeka.

U-Arin wakhothama, wehlisa i-helmet kuye phezulu kwabo. Ubuso obuyisithupha ngaphansi kwezibani. Bungcolile. Izindebe ezomele. Amehlo ayehlala ebumnyameni isikhathi eside kangangoba ukukhanya kwababuhlungu. Indoda enonyawo olufakwe ibhodi nentambo. Omunye enze umsebenzi omuhle wokuhlinza ngezinto ababenazo. Umfana omncane, egobile, ephefumula ngendlela u-Arin angayithandi, iyashesha futhi iyancipha.

"Thulani," kusho uMandla, elandela lapho u-Arin ebuka khona. "Isifuba sakhe. Into imwile ekuweni kokuqala—"

*Tension pneumothorax, cishe,* kusho uMother. *Noma ama-flail segment. Atlas, ngidinga ukubona isifuba sakhe.*

U-Atlas wakhipha isithombe esiseduze sesudi, i-thermal-and-structure overlay ebonakala emehlweni ka-Arin. Isifuba somfana esingakwesobunxele sihamba ngendlela engalungile, singena lapho kufanele siphume.

*Flail. Ama-ribs amathathu ahlukanile,* kuqinisekisa u-Atlas. *Usebenza kabili ukuphefumula ngendlela efanele. Ngeke aphume lapha uma siqhubeka kanje.*

"Senzeni?"

*Stabilize the segment. Faka izinto ezithambile, ubophe. Asiyona odokotela, futhi lena akuyona isibhedlela,* kusho uMother. *Kodwa sizomtholela amahora. Arin. Wena uzozenza ngezandla zakho. Eza-Guardian zinkulu kakhulu.*

Lokho kwamenza wama. Ukuze asebenzise izandla zakhe, bekumele aqaqe isivalo. Akhiphe ingalo yakhe yangakwesokudla ephuma ku-gauntlet yengubo yakhe, iphume iye othulini nase moyeni omubi, ivele. Umcabango lowo wafika nesizungu esidala, isisindo esivamile, ingxenye yakhe eyayichitha impilo yayo yonke ibala ukuthi uphuma kanjani.

*Ngiyazi,* kusho uMama kancane, ebhekise kuye yedwa. *Kwenze noma kunjalo. Imizuzu emithathu. Phefumula ngomlomo wakho.*

Wakhipha ingalo yakhe.

Umoya washaya esikhunjeni sakhe—ushisa, ungcolile, ungalungile. Isandla sakhe sabonakala simangazile kuye, simhlophe futhi sincane, sifana nesilwane esingenasivikelo sensimbi. Walula isandla, wathatha iphakethe lokwelapha uMama amkhombisele lona eceleni kukaGuardian, wabe eselalela imiyalelo yakhe—beka lapha, *hhayi, phansi, phezu kwalesi siqephu esihambayo, yebo*—wafaka ibhande esifubeni somfana, iminwe yakhe ingavikelwe isebenza ebumnyameni kuyilapho amadoda ayisithupha ebuka, kanti umshini uphefumula eduze kwakhe.

Ukuphefumula kukaThulani kwaba lula. Akukuhle. Kodwa kungcono kunakuqala.

"Nangu," kusho uArin. Wabuyisela ingalo yakhe ku-gauntlet, isivalo sivaleka, futhi wayengazi ukuthi wayekulangazelele kangakanani kuze kube sekubuyile. "Nangu."

*Inhliziyo yehle ishumi nambili,* kusho uMama. *Kuhle. Manje sihamba.*

---

Izokuxhumana ezingaphezulu bezithule kusukela kwavulwa umsele.

UArin akazange akhulume ngalokho. Nenkantolo ayizange ikhulume. Izwi likaPriya laphenduka lathamba, laphazamiseka, lanyamalala lapho bedlula lapho kwacindezela khona, baqhubeka ngaphandle kwakhe ngoba bekungekho okunye abangakwenza. Kodwa uGuardian wayephethe i-repeater spool, intambo yefayibha engamamitha amabili amakhulu egibele emhlane, kanti uAtlas wayeyikhulula ngemuva kwabo ngesikhathi behla, intambo ebuyela ekukhanyeni.

*Sekuphelile i-spool,* kusho uAtlas. *Ngingaxhuma umugqa omdala we-refuge bay ku-repeater. I-bay yayinawo umugqa wokuxhumana oqondile uya e-38 Level station. Uma kukhona okusele—*

"Kwenze."

Kwathatha imizuzu emine. UArin wabamba ikhava yephaneli odongeni lwe-bay kuyilapho iminwe kaGuardian, emikhulu kakhulu ukwenza ukuhlinza kodwa incane ngokwanele ukwenza lokhu, isonta ikhopha elingenalutho kwi-fiber-to-copper bridge ye-repeater. UAtlas wamqondisa, eqinile, eqinisekisa, futhi lokho kwamqinisa uArin.

Kwase kuba khona umsindo ku-helmet yakhe, umoya ophuma, izwi, liqoqeka, lizama ukuvela phakathi kokuphazamiseka kube yinto yomuntu.

"—phinda, lapha phezulu, nguPriya, *Arin*, uma ungizwa—"

"Ngiyakuzwa." Izwi lakhe laphuka lapho. Wayengazi ukuthi uzizwa ehlale yedwa kangakanani kuze kube sekuphelile lokho. "Priya. Ngibanalo. Bonke abayisithupha. Bayaphila."

Kwaba khona ukuthula. Kwabe sekuzwakala umsindo angakwazanga ukuwuchaza, into efana nokubanjwa yizinyembezi, waqonda ukuthi uPriya uzama ukungakhali esiteshini esivulekile.

"Phinda." Waphefumula. "Phinda futhi. Kancane. Ukuze kufakwe ku-log."
