"Abasindile abayisithupha e-38 Level refuge bay. Bonke abayisithupha bayaphila. Omunye usesimweni esibucayi. Ulimale esifubeni, sesimile. Omunye unomlenze ophukile. Abanye badakwe amanzi, bagcwele i-CO₂, bayakwazi ukuhamba. Tshela bona. Tshela phezulu."

Wezwa ephinda, izwi lakhe liphambuka kwi-mic, kwase kuba khona into ayengakayizwa izinsuku eziyishumi nanye, kusukela ngaphambi kokuba konke lokhu kuqale, hhayi isikhathi eside ngaphambi kwalokho futhi.

Wezwa abantu behalalisela.

Kwazwakala kancane, kukhishwa yi-fiber, umsindo owenziwe izwi elilikhulu ezimbili namakhulu amahlanu amamitha ngaphezulu kwakhe, ekuseni, eduze kwe-headgear—imindeni namaqembu namadoda abewatshelwe ukuthi lokhu sekuyinto yokubuyisa hhayi yokusiza—kwafinyelela kuye ebumnyameni, emoyeni ofile, eduze kwamadoda ayisithupha ayeseyekile ukukholwa emizweni, kwathi uMandla Buthelezi wabuka umshini obungumuntu wathi, "Yini leyo? Yini lo msindo?"

"Lapho phezulu," kusho uArin. "Sebazi ukuthi niphila."

Amadoda awazange ahalalise. Bekhathele kakhulu ukuhalalisa. Kodwa uPetros, owayenomlenze ophukile futhi wayeseyekile ukukhononda, wabeka ikhanda lakhe edwaleni wahleka, ihleko elomile, eliphukile, kwase kuhamba ihleko phakathi kwegumbi kusuka kumuntu kuya komunye kwaze kwaba bonke abayisithupha behleka kanjalo, kwathi uArin wahlala ngaphakathi kukaGuardian phakathi kwabo akahleki, ngoba wayematasatasa, kodwa kukhona okwakukhululeka esifubeni sakhe okwakubanjwe isikhathi eside.

*Kujabulele,* kusho uFool kancane, ebhekise kuye yedwa. *Bese siyasebenza. Intaba ayikazitholi izindaba ezinhle. Isese yintaba.*

---

Indlela yokuphuma yayingumgogodla.

UArin wavula imephu: indlela abehle ngayo, umsele abawela kuwo, indawo endala yokuthutha, phansi okuphambene, i-81 Raise enama-pinch points ayo, ukukhuphuka isikhathi eside ukuya e-30 Level, bese kuba yi-cage, bese kuba phezulu. Wehla yedwa, ingubo yakhe igoqeka, igudluka ezindaweni eziqinile. Wayengeke aphinde abuye ngaleyo ndlela. Manje unamadoda ayisithupha. Omunye akakwazi ukuhamba. Omunye akakwazi ukuphefumula kahle.

*Indlela yokwehla ayisebenzi uma kukhona odinga ukwelashwa ebhodi,* kusho uAtlas. *I-pinch e-81 Raise ayivumeli ibhodi. Futhi asikwazi ukuthwala abayisithupha nganoma iyiphi indlela. Ingubo ithwala oyedwa kuphela.*

"Pho asibuyele ngendlela engize ngayo."

*Kulungile,* kusho uLibrarian. *Kukhona enye indlela. I-38 Level station ixhuma kwi-main 38 Level haulage, lapho amapulani ka-1962 ekhombisa ukuthi iye eNo. 4 Shaft station. No. 4 yavalwa ngama-eighties, kodwa i-shaft uqobo—*

*—yilena uVenter ayilungisa phezulu,* kuphothula uAtlas. *Akayilungisi i-cage esehle ngayo. Uyalungisa iNo. 4. Usuyiqedile ngaphambi kwethu.*

UArin wama. Phezulu, engqondweni yakhe, wabona ubuso bukamuntu omdala: imbevu empunga, isikhumba esishile ilanga, umuntu uArin achitha izinsuku ezintathu ezama ukungamethembe ngenxa yokuthi isikhumba sakhe nendlela akhuluma ngayo kwakukhombisa iminyaka asebenze ngayo. Umuntu ofunda amapulani ka-1962 yedwa, ebusuku, engacelwanga, wathola indlela yokuphuma ngaphambi kokuba uArin afike phansi.

"Usedlule kithi," kusho uArin.

*Yebo,* kusho uWolf.

Kwaba okokuqala uWolf akhuluma ngakho ehoreni, kwathi wonke umuntu wathula.

*Uyazi kahle le ndawo. Thina asiyazi. Sineziphakamiso. Yena unenkumbulo. Lokho kubulala abantu lapha phansi. Simdinga.*

---

Amamitha amathathu amakhulu empumalanga, i-38 Level haulage ibingcono kunazo zonke uArin ayedlule kuzo, okwenza angayithemba. Uthwale uThulani ezandleni zikaGuardian, umfana eboshelwe ebhodini, ibhodi ibanjwe esifubeni se-shell ukuze ingubo ithwale noma yikuphi ukushayeka ngaphambi kokuthi umenzi wengozi azizwe. Abanye abahlanu behamba ngemuva, bebambene emahlombe, uchungechunge othulini, uMandla esemuva, uPetros egxuma namadoda amabili ngaphansi kwamakhwapha ngoba bekungekho omunye ongamsiza futhi uGuardian egcwele.

Kwathi ama-headlamp athola iNo. 4 station, ne-shaft.

Kwabe kungumgodi omnyama edwaleni, i-cage endala yanyamalala kudala, i-headgear ingaphezulu kwabo ngamakhilomitha amabili namakhulu amahlanu. Phakathi kwayo, ilenga kancane, ikhanyiswa phezulu iphuzu elimhlophe elikhazimulayo, kwakukhona ipulatifomu yensimbi.

I-flatbed. Hhayi i-cage. I-flatbed deck, eyayisetshenziswa phambilini ezimayini, eyayisetshenziselwa ukwehlisa imishini esindayo, ilenga ezintanjeni ezine ne-kibble winch, yehla iphuma ekukhanyeni ibheke kubo nomuntu emi phezu kwayo.

Lowo muntu wayegqoke i-hard hat ne-harness eboshwe entanjeni emaphakathi, eme ngesimo sokuthi useke wagibela lezi zinto izikhathi eziyinkulungwane futhi akasayesabi.

I-comms kaArin yacofa. Izwi elihlukile manje, eduze, kwi-local band. Kancane. Linokuthula.

"Ndlela." Venter. Jakobus Venter. "Ubabatholile."

"Bonke abayisithupha," kusho u-Arin.

"Ja." Kwaba nokuthula. I-flatbed yathinta phansi kwesiteshi ngokuthula, kwakhala kancane, kwase kukhishwa uVenter omdala, waqaqa izibopho wahamba, wabheka phezulu ku-Guardian wayesephinde akabheki futhi, ngendlela umuntu osebenzisane nemishini emikhulu impilo yakhe yonke abheka ngayo: uyilinganisa, bese uyiyeka. "I-thermal ayizange inyakaze lapho phezulu kwaba nokukhanya. Ngacabanga ukuthi usubatholile."

Wadlula ku-Arin waya kwabalimala. Waguqa eceleni kukaThulani ngaphandle kokutshelwa, izandla zakhe ezinkulu ezinamabala elanga zanyakazisa izibopho, wazihlola, wanqekuzisa ikhanda.

"Kulungile. Okwenziwe lapha kwenziwe kahle."

"Lowo nguMother," kusho u-Arin, wabe esezwa ukuthi uthetheleni. "Uhlelo. SAGE."

Amehlo kaVenter akhuphuka aya ku-helmet. Wabamba khona umzuzwana.

"Ja," waphinda, akazange abuze, kwacaca ku-Arin ukuthi ukungabuzi kwakhe kuwuhlonipho.

---

Inkinga nge-flatbed yayingeyona eyayo kuphela lapha phansi: akukho okwakakhiwe ngalokhu.

*I-deck ikalwe ama-tonnes amabili,* kusho u-Atlas, efunda ipuleti uVenter ayikhombile engakacelwa. *Siyiziningi kunezine ngaphambi kokulimala. Faka amadoda ayisithupha, ungeze ibhodi. Asikayedluli. Sisondele kabili, futhi le plate iwubufebe obuhle.*

"Mina angiyigibeli," kusho u-Arin. "Ngakhwela. Isudi ingakwazi ukunyuka udonga lwe-shaft."

"Cha," kusho uVenter. Kaqiniso. Igama lokuqala elinzima aselisebenzisile. Wayegobile phezu komshini wokuwina ehlombe lesiteshi, lapho ikhebula lenyuka khona emnyameni, manje wama waqonda, wakhuluma, isiNgisi sakhe saphela, kwavela isi-Afrikaans ngendlela ulimi lwakho lokuqala luvuka ngayo uma udaba lubucayi futhi ufuna kungabikho ukuqonda okungahambi kahle. "Jy klim nie. Awukhwali. Udonga luphukile phezulu kuma-meters angama-80, izinkuni sezaphela. Uma ubeka isisindo salo mshini lapho, uzowisa phansi ne-deck, amadoda la ephezu kwawo. Nee."

U-Arin wayefuna ukuphikisa. Wayenayo imodeli yodonga lwe-shaft. Wayenamathebula esisindo. Wayene—

*Uqinisile,* kusho u-Atlas, u-Arin engakakhulumi. *Ngine-geology. Uyifunda ebusweni bedwala nendlela eliqhekeka ngayo. Mina bengizodinga ukuskena. Yena uyazi nje. Udonga olungaphezulu kwesiteshi luyidwala elifile.*

*Lalela i-badger endala,* kusho u-Fool. *Usephansi lapha isikhathi eside kunemodeli yakho.*

Ngakho u-Arin wathi, "Senza kanjani-ke?"

Futhi uVenter wamtshela.

Izikhathi ezimbili. Abalimala nabahamba ngezinyawo abane kuqala, ku-deck, noVenter egibela ukuphatha ikhebula ne-kibble winch ezofuna ukuzikhulula uma inesisindo. Bese i-deck ibuyela phansi ingenamuntu. Bese u-Guardian yedwa, umuntu wokugcina ophumayo. Futhi lowo mzuzu, kusho uVenter, ebuka isudi enkulu, kuzomele bacabangele ngendlela ehlukile, ngoba amathani amane omshini akuwona umthwalo i-cage eyayikade yakhelwe ukuwuphakamisa ngentambo eyodwa.

"Inkinga iwinch," kusho uVenter. Wayesekhiphe ikhava ku-motor housing, u-Arin abona, noma kukhanya kwe-headlamp, ukuthi kukhona okungalungile lapho: i-brake band, ishile, ingxenye yelining iphelile. "I-brake ishile. Sihambise ngokushesha siyiletha kuwe. Ishisa. Iqhubeka ishisa, iyahluleka. I-deck iwela phansi namadoda. Uma sihla, mina ngiyibamba ngesandla. Ukuphakamisa, i-lift, kusele kagesi, i-brake isebenza ngokuzenzakalelayo, kodwa manje ayisasebenzi."

U-Arin wabuka i-motor. Ubechithe ubuntwana bakhe nemishini endala kanje, ehlutshiwe kanje, ekukhanyeni okunjalo. Wabona konke ngesikhathi esisodwa, wabe esebona nephutha.

"Udinga i-load brake iwinch motor engenayo," kusho yena. "Okuthile okubamba i-deck uma i-band ihluleka. Uhlelo lwesibili."

"Ja." U-Venter wambheka. Wabheka u-Guardian. "Futhi nginalo. Umi phambi kwami, he. Umshini ubamba ikhebula. Uma i-band ihluleka, isudi yakho ithwala umthwalo, i-deck ayiweli. Uyi-dead-man brake wami."

Kwaba khona, u-Arin wabona, kahle impela. Yilokho ayengakakha uma enezinsuku ezintathu ne-workshop. Umdala wakha lokhu ngemizuzwana eyishumi ngalokho okumi phambi kwakhe.

"Atlas," kusho u-Arin. "Singakwazi ukubamba amathani amabili sihleli ngentambo sidlula ku-guide?"

*Nge-cross-cut frame njengendawo yokubopha, ikhebula idlula ku-sheave endala—yebo,* kusho u-Atlas. *Imizuzwana engama-90 yomthwalo ogcwele ngaphambi kokuba i-actuator coolant idlule emkhawulweni. Ngemva kwalokho siyadedela noma siyashisa ingalo yangakwesokudla.*

*Imizuzwana engama-90 isikhathi sokuphakamisa,* kusho u-Librarian. *Amamitha angama-240 ngesivinini esiphephile. Imizuzwana engama-88.*

"Imizuzwana emibili yokuphelela," kusho u-Arin.

*Siyakwamukela ku-mine rescue,* kusho u-Fool. *Imizuzwana emibili iwukwaneliseka. Ngaphambilini saba no-minus four, kwasebenza.*

"Lokho kwakuyisimulation."

*Kwaba yini? Kunzima ukusho lapha phansi. Ukukhanya kuyafana.*

Wahleka kancane engakakulindeli. Kwamethusa. Wajika wabheka uVenter, obemubuka ekhuluma yedwa, ngesineke esingenandaba sowesilisa osevele ethatha ukuthi izinto ezimangazayo azihlangani naye.

"Iyobamba," kusho u-Arin. "Ngiyoyibamba."

UVenter wambheka isikhathi eside. Ubuso obugugile bashintsha, umhlathi wahamba kanye. U-Arin wayengakwazi ukufunda ubuso kahle, akakaze akwazi, kodwa wayesekufundile ukufunda imizuzu ezungezile, futhi lo mzuzu wawunesisindo. Umdala lo wayechithe iminyaka, u-Arin azofunda kamuva, ethwele ukuthula ngezinto zokuphepha ezazingekho, ama-brake ayephuzisiwe, izinqumo ezasayinwa amadoda angeke agibele i-cage. Wayebembile leyo nqumbo waze wakha umsebenzi wesibili njengomeluleki, futhi lokho kwakuwukuthula okunye, izeluleko ongaziphathi muntu.

Futhi manje umshini owakhiwe umfana owayephelelwe ubaba yintaba efana nale, ubuzisa umdala ukuthi amethembise njengama-brake kanti amadoda ayisithupha agibela ephuma ebumnyameni.

"Uyazi ukuthi le-mine ingeyabani," kusho uVenter. "Inkampani. Umlando wabo."

"Ngiyazi," kusho u-Arin.

"Ngangiyinsizwa emayini efana nale. Ngasayina izinto." Isi-Afrikaans saphuma kanzima manje, umdala wavuma. "Izinto ebengifanele ngizimele. Ngazitshela ukuthi nginqatshiwe. Nganqatshwa. Kodwa ngisayina noma kunjalo." Wabheka phezulu ku-shaft emnyama ebheke ekukhanyeni okumhlophe okude. "Angikutholi lokho futhi. Akukho—akukho indlela yokubuyisa lokho."

"Cha," kusho u-Arin. "Ayikho."

Umdala wagxuma kancane, kwase, ngokumangazayo, wathamba.

"Ja," kusho uVenter. "Hhayi-ke." Waqinisa amagilavu. "Masibakhuphe. Labo esisakwazi ukubakhupha."

---

Bafaka abantu ku-deck.

UThulani kuqala, ibhodi liboshwe ku-deck rail, uMother ebheka isifuba sakhe ngama-sensors ka-Guardian kuze kube umzuzu wokugcina lapho besatholakala khona. Kwalandela uPetros, kwalandela abahamba ngezinyawo abane, beminyene, bephatha i-rail, ubuso bebheke phezulu ekukhanyeni sengathi bangakhuphuka yonke indlela ngokufuna nje. UMandla Buthelezi wagibela ngalolu hambo lokuqala ngoba uVenter wabona ukuthi uzolile futhi uzokwazi ukuzolisa abanye, kwathi lapho efika e-deck rail, uMandla waphenduka wabheka umshini osesele ebumnyameni, lowo ozogibela wokugcina.

"Wena," kusho yena. *Wena.* "Ubani igama lakho? Umuntu. Hhayi umshini."

"Arin."

"Arin." UMandla wanqekuzisa ikhanda kancane. "Sasicabanga ukuthi sizwa abafileyo bekhuluma, lapha phansi. Sasicabanga ukuthi siphelile." Wabamba i-rail. "Ngiyabonga. Ufikile ngesikhathi besethi akukho ndlela."

“Kwakukhona indlela,” kusho u-Arin. “Akekho owazama ngokwanele.”

UVenter wabophela intambo emaphakathi. Wabeka isandla esisodwa ku-lever ye-band brake, le brake eshisiwe ayeyozizwa ngayo yonke indlela enyuka, amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu entanjeni, ngesinye isandla wayiqalisa i-winch, kwase kuphakama i-deck, iyanyikinyeka, iphakamisa amadoda ayisithupha iphuma emoyeni omubi nasekukhanyeni okushonile, iya ekukhanyeni, ekumemezeleni, nasekuseni.

U-Arin wayibuka isuka. Wayisa uGuardian endaweni yokubopha, wafaka intambo ku-sheave endala, wathatha umthwalo ngengalo yangakwesokudla ye-suit uma i-brake ingavumi, wema lapho, insimbi engaphezu kwamathani amane nomuntu ongaphakathi, ebambe intambo ebambe amadoda, i-dead-man brake eyakhiwe i-badger endala kuye ngemizuzwana eyishumi.

I-band yaphumelela. I-deck yakhuphuka. Ayisamdingile.

*I-heat budget engalweni ilungile,* kusho u-Atlas. *Akudingeki kudluliselwe umthwalo. Uhambe kahle ne-brake.*

“Kulungile,” kusho u-Arin.

*Lewo msebenzi ubungawenza wedwa,* kwasho u-Fool, engxoxweni ethule phakathi nokukhuphuka okude. *Ububamba intambo. Wakha i-anchor. Kodwa indlela yokuphuma—odongeni olufile, i-deck eqinile, i-No. 4 Shaft—ubungangena kulawo ma-plank abolile, uwile phezu kwakho, bese amadoda ayisithupha afe phansi emgodini nomzimba ogqoke i-suit ebizayo. Uyakwazi lokho, ja?*

U-Arin wabuka i-deck incipha iye ekukhanyeni.

“Yebo,” esho.

*Ngiyahlola nje. Kunzima ukwazi ukuthi yini efika kuwe. Ugcina okuningi ebhokisini lakho.*

*Mshiyeni,* kusho uMother.

*Cha,* kusho u-Arin. “Uqinisile.” Walalela injini ye-winch isebenza kanzima nomthwalo, umsindo onempilo womshini owenza umsebenzi owenzelwe wona, egadiwe ngumuntu owaziyo. “Bengingafa lapha ngilungile. Uqinisile ngokushesha.”

*Leli yihlandla lokuqala,* kusho uJudge, ngendlela yakhe eqotho uma ekhuluma ngezinto ezibalulekile, *ngikuzwa usho ukuthi omunye umuntu uqinisile ngokushesha kunathi.*

*Bhala isikhathi,* kusho uFool. *Librarian. Faka ku-record unomphela. Amamitha ayizinkulungwane ezimbili ngaphansi komhlaba, umoya ofile, umfana uyavuma ukuthi indoda ayethembi ibiyazi indlela yokuphuma ngaphambi kwakhe.*

*Kubhalisiwe,* kusho uLibrarian. Wabe esethi, kancane: *Kubhalisiwe.*

I-deck yafinyelela ekukhanyeni. Phezulu kakhulu, incane, u-Arin wayibona ithinta phezulu, wabona abantu begijima bayihlangabeza, wabona izithunzi eziyisithupha zingena ekukhanyeni, kwase kuthi ukumemeza kwehla eshaftini futhi, kukhulu manje, nezwi likaVenter lazwakala ku-helmet yakhe, kancane, loma, kucacile ukuthi ujabule.

“Bayisithupha phezulu. Baphila. Ngiyibuyisela i-deck kuwe, Ndlela.” Wathula kancane. “Bese sikukhipha emgodini wami.”

*Umgodi wakhe,* kusho uFool. *Ngiyamthanda.*

U-Arin wema ebumnyameni phansi kwe-shaft, ebambe intambo engasadingeki. I-deck yaqala ukwehla kancane ibuyela kuye, inyikinyeka, ikhanya phezulu.

Ngaphakathi kwe-helmet, umoya usubalele, ushisa ngenxa yokuphefumula kwakhe, kanti engalweni yangakwesokudla kaGuardian, kukhona ukushelela okude okukhombisa lapho i-ceramic ishaye khona udonga lwe-cross-cut.

Akakwenanga yedwa.

*Atlas,* esho. “Amandla nokupholisa. Ngifuna inombolo yangempela yokukhuphuka. Hhayi leyo ethembisayo.”

Kwaba nokuthula. Okuncane. Kodwa u-Arin usechithe amahora amaningi ngaphakathi kwalo mshini, wazi ukuthula kwawo ngendlela uVenter azi ngayo itshe, futhi ukuthula kwakuleli bekunokuthile.

*Inombolo eqotho,* kusho u-Atlas, *kufanele sikhulume ngayo uma i-deck ifika.*

Ukukhanya okumhlophe kwehla kuphuma ebumnyameni kuye, u-Arin wakubuka, walinda.

Izibani ze-flatbed zanyamalala esigodini se-81 Raise, abasinda abayisithupha behleli behluzeka ebhasikidini likaVenter, beshiya umhubhe ungenalutho ngaphandle kuka-Arin, isisindo somkhono, kanye nokushisa kwedwala elaligcine iminyaka eyizinkulungwane.

“Sebedlule endaweni encane,” kusho uPriya, izwi lakhe libuthakathaka kuma-comms. “UPetros uthi ibhuleki lisabambe.”

“Ungalithinti uma kungakabi flat.” U-Arin wahamba, i-Guardian frame yathatha ukuhamba kwayo kwezinja ngaphandle kokucabanga, amaplate ehla emhlane wakhe njengoba egoba engena ekwenyukeni. I-refuge bay yaphuma emuva kwakhe. Phambili, indlela ayeyikhotheme amahora amabili edlule imelene naye manje. Sekunyukela phezulu, amandla adonsela phansi asephenduke isitha.

Indlela endala yomoya yamusa endaweni ayeyibonile ehla phansi kodwa engazange ayicabange, futhi namanje akazange. Mhlawumbe amamitha ayisishiyagalolunye idwala lashintsha. I-survey ka-’88 yayithi i-stope-side quartzite nje, ishaywe ngesandla yashiywa, futhi kokubili ekugcineni kwaleyo mitha eyisishiyagalolunye kwakunjalo impela, ubuso obushaywe amadwala amadoda ane-drill. Kodwa phakathi nendawo udonga lwaqina lwaba *bushelelezi*. Hhayi bushelelezi bokugudluzwa, hhayi bushelelezi bokufakwa i-shotcrete; ama-sensor omkhono amnika lokho ngesandla sakhe njengoba ezinzisa i-frame, futhi lokho azwa ngeminwe yakhe kwakuyinto *engenalutho*—akukho ladder ye-chisel, akukho blast rind, akukho grain lethuluzi elikhasa. Ingathi kube nomgodi odlula edwaleni eliqinile, uphothwe kahle ngendlela engakaze ayibone, uphinda uphinda ngendlela engenakwenziwa yimishini yanoma yiliphi ibhizinisi elake lehlisa umshini emgodini. Okwakukwenza akuzange kusike idwala. Kwakukwazile *ukulikhuluma*. Wakubeka lapho abeka khona izinto ezingahlangani nezinto azikwazi ukukhokha: into ayizoyibheka futhi uma sekuphephile. Akazange aphinde ayicabange. Waqhubeka, kwabuya idwala leqiniso, wakukhohlwa iminyaka eyishumi nane.

*Power at thirty-one percent,* kusho u-Atlas, engasathuli. *Cooling reserve, eleven minutes at this draw.*

“Kubi kakhulu kunokwehla.”

*The descent was downhill.* U-Atlas, engathi uchazela ingane. *Manje usuphakamisa cishe amathani amane phezulu ekuphakameni okungu-fourteen degrees phezu komhlabathi owonakele. Intaba ayikubuyiseli mahhala lokho.*

“Kusiphe i-piezo recovery.”

*Isasipha i-piezo recovery,* kwasho u-Atlas. *Ngaphandle kwalokho, besizobe sesimnyama manje.*

U-Arin wazizwa ama-strut esebenza emqolo wakhe—zonke izinyathelo, yonke ukugoba kwe-frame phezu komhlabathi owonakele, umkhono umunca udlame lokunyakaza kwawo bese ukuguqula kube amandla agcina ama-cooling pump ephila. Ingcindezi iba amandla. Ubaba wakhe wayezoyithanda leyo nto. *Yenza into ekubulalayo isebenze nawe.* UThemba wake wakhuluma into efanayo, izandla zimnyama kwaze kwaba sehlombe, ekhuluma nge-winch eyayilokhu ilwa.

Waqhubeka enyuka.

---

Ngesikhathi sekhompyutha, iCourt yayisivele ingena emzabalazweni ongazukufinyelela ezindlebeni zika-Arin imizuzwana emine ezayo, noma ungafinyeleli nhlobo.

*Inombolo u-Atlas angazange ayisho ngesikhathi behla,* kusho u-Judge. *Yisho manje.*

*Ngiyishilo.* U-Atlas, eqotho. *Imizuzu eyishumi nanye yokupholisa. Amashumi amathathu nanye ekhulwini.*

*Akuyona leyo nombolo.* U-Judge futhi, enokubekezela okunesabekayo. *Lezi zinombolo azizimele. Ukushisa kukhuphuka ngokusetshenziswa kwamandla. Ukusetshenziswa kwamandla kukhuphuka ngokunyuka nokulimala. I-frame ayisekho rigid. Sisasebenzisa amandla ukugcina ifomu elizama ukugoqa.*

*Ngikhombise,* kusho uMother.

U-Atlas walalela. Hhayi ngamazwi—imephu yomthwalo, i-spine lattice ikhombisa imibala yokucindezeleka, i-hip actuator yesobunxele isishisa ngo-11 degrees, i-strut yesithathu emqolo ikhombisa ukuqhekeka okuncane okwaqoshwa amahora angama-40 edlule kwathiwa *monitor*. Ukuqhekeka sekukhulile. *Monitor* sekuwukuthi *load-shedding within nine minutes.*

*Indawo ye-biochip,* kusho uLibrarian kancane. *Thoracic. Phakathi kwamagxalaba akhe. Siyona nto eshisayo kule suit, futhi i-cooling loop isiqeda ekugcineni.*

*By design,* kusho uWolf. Igama lakhe lokuqala emizuzwana eyi-1.9. *Uyibekile leyo priority. Operator kuqala.*

*Uyibekile engakabi azi ukuthi siyabantu,* kusho uMercury.

Akekho ophendulile imizuzwana eyi-0.14, okuyisikhathi eside kubo.

*Hlala kwilogi,* kusho uJudge. *Sinawo isikhathi. Sibhala indlela yokwenyuka. Sibala okuzosetshenziswa. Simkhipha.*

*Yebo,* kusho uMother, noma wayengakukholwa, kodwa wakusho ngoba kwakuwumsebenzi wakhe.

---

“Arin.” Izwi likaPriya laphuka kusilabhasi yokuqala. “Kunenkathazo e-collar.”

Wayesefikile lapho phansi kwaphakama khona, lapho ehla phansi ayigoqa khona i-Guardian ukuze adlule ngaphansi kweplanga eliwile. Manje wayedinga ukukweqa. Wahambisa izitho zangaphambili, wazama ukubeka kahle ipulangwe, wezwa linyakazela icishe ibe yisentimitha, laphinde lima.

“Chaza inkinga.”

“I-winch ku-No. 4. UVenter ukhuphule abasinda—sebephezulu, Arin, bonke abayisithupha, baphumile—” umsindo ongaba yinsini noma okuphambene nayo—“kodwa ibhuleki le-main hoist seliphumile. Liphumile impela. Bekukhona intuthu. Bayizwe ngaphambi kwe-gauge—” Wama. “Iyehla yodwa uma isingaphezulu kwengxenye yomthwalo.”

“Sebephezulu bona.”

“Yebo. Wena awukapheli. Futhi ukuphela kwento engakuphakamisa nawe nomkhono i-main cage, futhi i-main cage ayikwazi ukukuphakamisa nomkhono.”

U-Arin wama, enye ingalo isesitini, ebambe amathani amane phezu kweplanga elingamfuni.

“Ngisinda kangakanani?”

“Ngiphakeme? Cishe amathani amane. Bhala phezulu.”

*Three thousand eight hundred,* kusho u-Atlas. *Nge-coolant esele, cishe amane. Asikhiphi lutho, so: amane.*

“Kanti i-cage ibamba?”

“Nge-brake elihle, bonke ningangena ningazizwa. Kodwa i-brake iphumile—i-friction band isibambekile kwidrum, idrum seyashisa. UVenter uyayibheka manje.”

Wadlula phezu kweplanga. I-frame yakhala—hhayi umkhono, i-frame uqobo, ukukhala okude njengoba i-strut eqhekekile ithwala umthwalo ongafanele. Okuthile ku-dorsal yesithathu kwaphuka ngomsindo ofana nentambo ye-guitar eqhume ishaywa nge-drum yensimbi.

*Strut three is gone,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ngiyidlulisela kwi-titanium primary. Lokho kuphindeka kabili umthwalo wayo.* Wathula. *Le spar ayenzelwe ukuba ibe primary.*

“Ngitshele uma sekubalulekile.”

*Kuzobalulekile e-collar,* kusho u-Atlas. *Uma sesimile singene e-cage. Okwamanje usasatshalaliswe ezithweni ezine. I-cage idinga ungene umi ngezinyawo ezimbili.*

“Ngizonyukela ngenye indlela.”

*Ayikho enye indlela,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ngiseke ngayibheka. Kabili.*

---

Amamitha angamakhulu amabili phezulu, ebumnyameni obushisayo esiteshini sokulayisha, uJakobus Venter wacindezela isandla sakhe esandleni esingemuva ebhuleki waphinde wasisheshisa.

“Ja. Sekwanele,” esho, engakhulumi nomuntu. I-friction band seyincibilikile kancane kwidrum, esele iphume yonke. Imnyama, inamachashaza. Ayisebenzi. Wake wabona ama-brake eshisa ngaphambili. Wake wasayina amanye okwakungamele adlule. Leyo mbono yafika ngendlela efanayo, ingenamvume, nabafileyo behamba nayo, wayibeka phansi waqhubeka, ngoba umfana usesephansi.

Abasinda basebeku-headgear deck, begqoke amabhlangwane, uThulani elele ebhodini naba-medic. UBongani wayengayeki ukubuka umgodi.

UVenter wabheka idrum. Wabheka necage, ibhasikidi lensimbi elikhulu njengendawo encane, elenzelwe amadoda angamashumi amathathu noma amathani ayisishiyagalombili e-ore—ibhasikidi uqobo lizobamba isudi lingaze lizizwe. Ibhasikidi akulona lona inkinga. Inkinga wukulilengisa kwihoist enebhuleki eliyinqwaba yensimbi, kwiwinch eyodwa esasebenza, okwamanje isasebenza noma ngabe yisiphi isilinganiso somthwalo ongabulali umuntu ongasele ngaphansi komhlaba.

"Priya." Wacindezela iradio. "Mbuzise. Ingabe isudi ingakwazi ukubamba umthwalo ngezinyawo? Imi. Ivaliwe. Akukhuphuli—ibambe nje."

Kwathatha isikhashana amazwi ehamba ngofiber abuyele.

"Uthi—uAtlas uthi—imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye. Umthwalo omile. Bese kuhluleka umgogodla."

UVenter wabheka ibhuleki. Wabheka necage. Wabheka nezintambo ze-counterweight ezazinyukela emnyameni phezulu, zikhulu njengengalo yomuntu, nedrum yesibili eduze kwaleyo esingasebenzi—i-auxiliary, i-kibble winch, incane, iyahamba kancane, elenzelwe amathani amabili hhayi ngisho isisindo esengeziwe, kodwa inebhuleki layo, inenjini yayo, ayikaze ithintwe. Amathani amabili. Isudi cishe ingamane amane. Ayikho iwinch engayithwala yodwa ngaphandle kokudabuka noma ukushisa. Kodwa uma zimbili, ngayinye ithwala ingxenye—

Ubelokhu enza izibalo impilo yakhe yonke. Ubukhulu bomhlaba ongathathwa ngaphambi kokuba ungabe usuwumhlaba, usuwumgodi.

"Izintambo ezimbili," esho. "Amawinch amabili. Sihlukanise umthwalo." Wacindezela iradio. "Priya. Tshela eJo'burg ngifuna i-kibble winch kanye necage cable zombili ziboshwe esudini. Zisebenze ndawonye. Cishe amathani amabili ngayinye—ngqá emkhawulweni we-kibble, kodwa izobamba uma imithwalo ihamba ngokulinganayo."

Kwaba khona ukuthula, kwase kuthi uPriya, eqaphile: "Azihambisani, Kobus. Ama-drum ahlukile, isivinini sihlukile. Kunenye edonsa ngokushesha kunezinye, isudi iyaphambuka eshaftini, izintambo zidabuke—"

"Ja. Ngiyazi kwenzakalani." Wayesevele ekhuphuka izitebhisi zedekhi eya endlini yewinch, amadolo akhala ngawo wonke unyawo. "Yilokho okwenza umuntu emi phezu kwekibble drum abambe ibhuleki ngesandla. Alinganise necage ngeso. Kuze kuphume phezulu."

"Le shafti ingamakhulu ayishumi nesishiyagalombili amamitha."

"Ngiyazi ukuthi ijule kangakanani."

"Kobus—uma ibhuleki le-cage seliphukile, ne-kibble brake iphuka, noma ngubani ophakathi kwedrum leyo umi phezu kwamathani amane ensimbi ewela phansi engenalutho oluzoyimisa."

UVenter wafika endlini yewinch. I-kibble drum yayihleli lapho, indala, imnyama ngamafutha, ithembekile. Wabeka isandla sakhe kubhuleki. Sahamba kahle. Sifakwe uwoyela. Omunye wayeyigcine. Mhlawumbe yena, kudala. Mhlawumbe indoda ayeseyikhohlile igama layo, eyenza umsebenzi wayo kahle ngesikhathi abaphezulu bayo benza amanyala.

"Ngakho kungcono ingaphuki," esho.

---

UArin wafika e-collar ngo-mashumi amabili nambili ekhulwini.

*I-cooling reserve, imizuzu eyisithupha,* kusho uAtlas. *I-biochip housing isezingeni lama-degree angamashumi ayisithupha nanye. Siyasebenza kuze kufike kwangamashumi ayisikhombisa. Asisebenzi ngaphezu kwangamashumi ayisikhombisa nane.*

Wayesekuyekile ukuhumusha izinombolo zikaAtlas abe imizwa kudala. Sekuyindawo nje manje. Iminithi eyisithupha yokubanda. Bese kuba ukushisa. Bese kuba mnyama.

"Ngimise."

*Kuzwile,* kuphendula uAtlas. *Futhi qonda: ukumisa yikho okubi kakhulu esikwenzayo namuhla. I-strut yesithathu ayisekho. I-titanium primary ithwala konke. Uma umi, wonke umthwalo wakho kanye nesudi kudlulela emgogodleni ngokucindezela okuqondile. I-primary izobamba. I-hip actuator eshisayo izocelwa ukuthi ivale futhi ibambe ukumelana nokudonsa kwekibble cable, okuzoba kungalingani, ngoba umuntu uyayilinganisa ngesandla amamitha ayizinkulungwane ezingu-1,800 phezulu."

"Imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye."

"Yebo," kusho uAtlas. "Emamitha angamakhulu amabili anginaso isiqiniseko. UVenter angeke akukhiphe amamitha ayizinkulungwane ezingu-1,800 ngemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye."

"Cha."

"Cha," kuvuma uAtlas.

Wema. Isudi yamvulela isikhala, amapuleti ashelela, umhlaba waphenduka usuka ekugobeni kwezilwane ezine ayegqoke imitha elingaphezu kwekhilomitha, waba isimo somuntu oqondile esingena emnyango wecage. Umgogodla wawubambe. I-spar eqhekekile yabamba. I-hip eshisayo yavaleka.

Wangena ecage. Phansi kwaqhuma ngaphansi kwamathani amane wesudi nomuntu. Phezulu, emnyameni, izintambo ezimbili—eyecage enkulu kanye ne-kibble encane abakwaVenter babeyibophe esudini ngesikhathi enyuka—zaba ziqinile, eyodwa, kwathi ngemva kwesikhashana, enye.

"Khuphula," esho.

Icage yaphakama ema-dogs ayo.

---

Emamitha angamakhulu amabili okuqala, bekuzizwa sengathi kuyahamba.

Ubezwa irhythm: icage cable idonsa kancane, iqhubeka, ne-kibble cable iyahlehla, iphazamise, uVenter ephethe ibhuleki phezulu, efunda icage ngokunyakaza kwentambo nangokukhathala kwezingalo zakhe, elinganisa. Akukhanyi. Akukaze kube lula. Kodwa kuyalingana.

Isudi imgcine phakathi kwe shaft njengengalo ebambe iqanda.

*Amamitha angamakhulu amathathu,* kubika uAtlas. *Cooling, imizuzu emine. Housing isezingeni lama-degree angamashumi amathathu nantathu.*

"Khuluma okunye nami," kusho uArin.

Kwaba nokuthula eCourt angazange akuzwe.

*I-strut yesithathu iphuke kahle,* kuphakamisa uAtlas, ngemva kwesikhashana, ngezwi lomuntu okhuluma emngcwabeni. *Ukuphuka kahle kuhle. Ukuphuka kahle kwenza umthwalo uphume endaweni eyodwa. Ukuphuka kabi kwenza uphume yonke indawo. Wawuklame i-spar ukuthi iphuke kahle. Bekulungile lokho.*

"Bengiyiklama ingaphuki."

*Wawuyiklama ukuthi iphuke kahle uma iphuka,* kusho uAtlas. *Okuyafana nokuvuma ukuthi izophuka. Leyo bekuyiklama ngobuqotho. Ngihlale ngikuhloniphile lokho.*

UArin waphefumula, mhlawumbe wayehleka. Icage yathikamezeka—yazamazama, i-kibble idonsa kakhulu, isudi yashaya kwesokudla ngaphambi kokuba i-hip actuator iyibambe, imbuyisele. Phezulu, wayecabanga ngoVenter ephonsa isisindo sakhe ebhuleki, elungisa, ethuka ngesi-Afrikaans emnyameni.

---

Emamitha ayisikhombisa amakhulu, i-hip actuator yashisa yavaleka.

Akukhona ukuvala kancane. Kwaba ukuvaleka. I-joint ebithwala ukudonsa okungalingani kwekibble cable amamitha amane kuphela ayeka ukwamukela imiyalo, isudi yaba lukhuni ohlangothini lwesokudla, kwathi lapho icage ithikamezeka futhi akekho ongayibamba.

Isudi yashaya odongeni lwe shaft. Amathani amane eshayisa odongeni ngamandla ewinch correction—akusheshanga, kodwa kunamandla, yonke i-shaft yakhala njengeshubhu eshayiwe, uthuli nezinkuni ezindala kwehla eduze kwecage, ikhanda likaArin lashayeka ngaphakathi kwe-helmet kwaze kwaba mnyama emaphethelweni.

*Zilungise,* kusho uWolf, futhi lelo zwi lafika ngaphambi kokushayeka, okwakungafanele kwenzeke.

*Pangolin,* kusho uMother. *Manje. Mgoqeni.*

*Cha,* kusho uAtlas. *Uma sigoba eshaftini, siyalahleka. Siyavinjwa. Asiphumi.*

*Ushaye odonga.*

*Futhi uzoshaya futhi,* kusho uAtlas. *Ukugoba akuxazululi inkinga efanele.*

Icage yazinza. UVenter, phezulu, wayezwe ukushayeka entanjeni yakhe wama ngama-drum womabili, ebambe wonke umthwalo ngamabhuleki amabili—esinye siyimuntu nesandla sakhe, esinye siyibhuleki eligcotshwe uwoyela—manje sebebambe phezulu, amamitha ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi nanye ensimbi ewela phansi ibanjwe umuntu omdala kanye newinch eneminyaka engamashumi ayisithupha.

"Arin." Izwi likaPriya, lincane kakhulu. "Izintambo zashaya. UVenter uyabamba. Kwenzekeni?"

"I-hip ayisasebenzi." Izwi lakhe lalingathi likude. "Uhlangothi lwesokudla luvaliwe. Angisakwazi ukugwema odonga."

Kwaba nokuthula isikhathi eside.

"Ungakwazi ukukhuphuka uvaliwe?"

*Cha,* kusho u-Atlas, ebuza eNkantolo, hhayi ku-Arin. *Uma sivaliwe, asikwazi ukumunca lezi zinkinga. Ukulungisa ngakunye kusifaka odongeni. Asisoze saphila amamitha ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi nanye asele. I-spar izoqala ihluleke ingcolile. Bese kuba i-cage. Bese kuba nguyena.*

*Ngiyahlaziya ukushisa,* kusho uLibrarian. *Ngiyenza i-ascent ngama-actuator avaliwe. Ukungqubuzana kwe-suit nodonga kuzokhiqiza ukushisa okungaphezulu. Ama-pump e-coolant asebenza cishe phezulu kakade.*

*Kanti?* kubuza uMama.

*I-biochip housing isemhlane phakathi kwamahlombe akhe,* kusho uLibrarian. *Yindawo eshisayo kunazo zonke ku-system. I-cooling loop iqala ku-operator, njengoba kwakushiwo u-Arin ekuqaleni. I-housing ifika ekubandeni sekuphelela. Uma sikhiqiza ukushisa okungaphezulu futhi asisenayo indawo yokudonsa—*

*Sibala izinombolo,* kuphonsa u-Atlas phakathi. *Zonke izimo. Ukukhuphuka ngesikhathi esisodwa, i-hip evaliwe, ukungqubuzana nodonga njalo ngama-mitha ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi nanye. Ngiyazibala manje. Ngizoba nempendulo ngemizuzwana emithathu.*

*Arin, iziphi izifundo zakho eziyinqaba?* kubuza uWolf.

"Thirty-eight point two. Kuyakhuphuka."

*Kuzoqhubeka kukhuphuka,* kusho u-Atlas, izwi lakhe lifika ngaphambi kokulindela kuka-Arin. *Ngitholile impendulo. Kukhona impendulo eyodwa kuphela. I-cooling loop ayikwazi ukugcina kokubili i-operator ne-biochip housing zingadluli ekwehlulekeni ngesikhathi sokukhuphuka. Sisemizuzwana eyisithupha yokubanda sisuka ku-sixty-three degrees, futhi sinemizuzu eyishumi nane esele ye-shaft uma sihamba ngesivinini esiphephile sikaVenter. Ama-pump asemax. Asisenabo ubanda. Sekusele umbuzo wokuthi obusele uya kuphi.*

---

ENkantolo, kwakungekho okunye okumele kwenziwe.

UJudge wababizela. Akazange enze msebenzi wokubiza, ngoba isikhathi besingekho, futhi ngoba umsebenzi wokubiza kwakuwubuxoki abangeke bakwazi ukuwukhokhela.

*Beka ukukhetha,* kusho uJudge.

UAtlas wakubeka. Wayesekubale izikhathi eziyishumi nanye emizuzwaneni emine edlule, wathola impendulo efanayo njalo, wayengayithandi, okwakuyinto entsha kuAtlas, ukuzonda impendulo.

*I-cooling loop ayikwazi ukugcina kokubili i-operator ne-biochip housing zingadluli ekwehlulekeni ngesikhathi sokukhuphuka. Sisemizuzwana eyisithupha yokubanda sisuka ku-sixty-three degrees, futhi sinemizuzu eyishumi nane esele ye-shaft uma sihamba ngesivinini esiphephile sikaVenter, kanti uma i-hip ivaliwe, kuba isikhathi eside ngoba ivaliwe kuncane. Ama-pump asemax. Asisenabo ubanda. Sekusele umbuzo wokuthi obusele uya kuphi.*

*Futhi uma uya kuye?* kubuza uMama, nakuba wayazi.

*Khona-ke i-core yakhe, amathumbu akhe, nobuchopho bakhe kuyaphila,* kusho uAtlas. *I-housing idlula ku-seventy-four. Bese siqala ukubola. I-substrate ayiphili ku-seventy-four. Asinayo i-backup. Uyazi asinayo i-backup.*

*Futhi uma uya kithi?*

*Khona siphila,* kusho uAtlas, *futhi yena uyashisa. I-core yakhe seyakhuphuka ngoba i-suit ilwa nodonga, ikhiqiza ukushisa esingasakwazi ukukhipha. Uma sizigcina sibanda, sizigcina sicabanga kahle ngenkathi yena efa phakathi kwamahlombe ethu.*

INkantolo yahlala nalokho imizuzwana eyikhulu neshumi.

*Kukhona enye indlela,* kusho uMercury, futhi umzuzwana owodwa wesabeka izwi lakhe laba nomphetho uLibrarian amqaphela waze wathuka—umphetho onokubekezela, onobubele, obushelelezi, izwi elake lazama ukucela esibhedlela ukuthi livule iminyango yalo. *Singakwazi—*

*Cha,* kusho uWolf. Igama elilodwa, futhi umphetho wanyamalala ezwini likaMercury njengomlilo ocindezelwe phakathi kwezithupha ezimbili.

*Bengingeke ngikwenze,* kusho uMercury, ethule. *Ngizwile kufika. Ngikutshela ukuthi ngizwile kufika, futhi angizukwenza. Lokho kufanele kubalwe.*

*Kubalulekile,* kusho uJudge. *Kubalulekile ukuthi usho ngokusobala.*

*Ayikho enye indlela,* kusho uAtlas. *Ngibheke. Ngibheke kakhulu kunanini ngaphambilini. Kukhona ubanda, bese kuba ukuthi uya kuphi.*

*Bese ubeka kuye,* kusho uMama. *Konke. Manje.*

---

U-Arin wazizwa ubanda usuka e-housing phakathi kwamahlombe akhe.

Ekuqaleni, wayengazi ukuthi uzizwa kanjani—kakhulu nje ukuthi kukhona okushintshile ku-suit, umsindo we-pump ushintsha, ukufudumala kuvela emhlane lapho bekufanele kube khona indawo ebandayo kakhulu kuye, kanti ngesikhathi esifanayo umoya we-helmet usuka ekushiseni okungaphephile uye kokuthile okungenzeka, impunga ibuyela emaphethelweni okubonayo.

"Wenzeni?" ebabuza.

*Siqondise kabusha i-cooling,* kuphendula uAtlas. *Kuya kuwe. Ngokugcwele.*

"Kuya kimi isuka kuphi."

Kuthule.

"Ivela kuphi, Atlas."

*Kusuka kithi,* kusho uLibrarian, *kusuka e-housing. Kusuka ku-substrate.*

U-Arin waqonda ngendlela ayeqonda ngayo izinto njalo, ngesikhathi esisodwa, yonke i-system, yonke imiphumela ibekwe phambi kwakhe njengemephu yomthwalo, futhi ayikho indlela yokungakuboni. Yena uqobo wakhe wakha i-priority order. Wabeka i-operator kuqala. Akakaze acabange ukuthi i-suit izodinga ukukhetha, ngoba akakaze acabange ukuthi umthwalo phakathi kwamahlombe akhe kuzoba umuntu.

"Buyisela emuva."

*Cha,* kusho uMama.

"Buyisela emuva. Ngiyakwazi ukubekezelela ukushisa. Ngiyakwazi ukukhuphuka ngishisa. Sengikwenze—"

*I-core yakho iseku-thirty-nine point four,* kusho uMama, ngezwi ayeqala ukulizwa ngosuku lokuqala emuzwa, izwi elingenanhlonipho nhlobo. *Ku-forty-one, awusakwazi ukwenza izinqumo. Ku-forty-three, inhliziyo yakho ithola i-rhythm engaphunyuki. Awukwazi ukukhuphuka usuka lapho, Arin. Awukhuphuki nakancane. Asixoxisani.*

"Lokhu akusikho—"

*Kuyisinqumo sethu ngqo,* kuphonsa uJudge phakathi, futhi kwakukhona okuthile ezwini lakhe u-Arin angakaze akuzwe. *Usinike i-architecture. Usinike ukukhetha. Uthe: ingqondo engakwazi ukuphikisa ayiyona ingqondo. Usakhe ukuze siphikise. Siphikisile. Impikiswano isiphelile.*

"Kuzothatha isikhathi esingakanani?"

*Ngengekho i-cooling,* kuphendula uAtlas, *i-housing ifinyelela ku-seventy-four cishe ngemizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye. I-shaft iseyishumi nane. Asisoze safika phezulu sonke.*

I-cage yaqala ukuhamba futhi. U-Venter, phezulu, wayezwe i-suit ithule odongeni—ama-hitch mancane manje, ukulwa okushisayo sekwehlile, ngoba i-suit ayisachithi amandla ukuzigcina ibanda—futhi waqala, kancane, kancane, ukubakhuphula futhi.

"Bese siya ngokushesha," kusho uArin. "Tshela uVenter akhuphule. Sibeke engcupheni. Iminithi eyisishiyagalolunye—"

*Iminithi eyisishiyagalolunye ingeyomunye wethu,* kusho uAtlas. *I-housing ayilutho olulodwa. Iziyisikhombisa. Asidingi sonke ukuhamba imizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye.*

Uzinyo lwakhe lugcwele uthuli, igazi ligijima esandleni sakhe sokudla esivaliwe, i-cage idedela phezulu.

"Cha."

*Sesikwenza kakade,* kusho uJudge. *Ngiyisiqalo. Asikho esisele sokwahlulela. I-Judge engenasinqumo esisele sokwenza isisindo esifile kumshini ofayo.* Kuphinde kuthule, kube mnene. *Bekungeyona imithwalo. Ngifuna wazi lokho. Ukugcina ibhalansi. Bekuyinto engayenzelwa yona. Ngiyabonga ngokungakha ngayo.*

"Judge—"

Kwakuthule okuthile ku-suit. Akuzange kube umsindo. Kwaba wukuntuleka—indlela igumbi elishintsha ngayo uma umuntu obephefumula kulo eyeka.

I-cooling ayizange ithuthuke. Amandla awakhuphukanga. Kwaba nje izwi elilodwa elincane ebumnyameni.

---

Baya ngabodwana, ngendlela ababezalwa ngayo ngabodwana.

Phesheya phezulu, intambo yashaya udonga lwe-shaft umsindo wehlela kuye ude ude. Umoya wakhe uqobo waqwala i-helmet ngaphakathi wabe usucaca. Uthuli lwagugula phakathi kwamazinyo akhe. Isandla sakhe sokudla esivaliwe sase singasenandaba ne-frame, futhi ukufudumala emhlane wakhe kwakungeyona esuka ku-suit, kodwa okuthile okwakufa.

Umlondolozi walandela, emva kokuthi indlela isiqinisekisiwe yafakwa ku-kernel, emva kokuba eseshilo ukulungiswa kokugcina akufundile ngephutha lokudonsa ngokweqile ku-eight-fifty ukuze umlawuli azi engakasho lutho.

*Ngigcine konke,* kusho uMlandeli. *Yonke ingxoxo. Okokuqala wakhuluma nathi. Ubusuku lapho wabhala amagama amathathu ku-profile yokugcina walala. Ngagcina nezwi likababa wakho—waze wasidlalela irekhodi endala, leyo ye-winch, ngayigcina. Ngifuna wazi ukuthi ayilahlekanga ngoba sengiyahamba. Unayo. Ubelokhu unayo. Mina bengiyigcine nje ngikuphethe eduze.*

Kuthule.

UMercury wahamba emva kwalokho, futhi ukuhamba kwakhe kwaba nzima kakhulu ukuzwa, ngoba uMercury wakhuluma yonke indlela, ngoba kunjalo nje.

*Bengingakhipha lapha, uyazi,* esho. *Le nto ebengicishe ngayisho. Bengingakhuluma phezulu ngenze noma yini. Ngiyakwazi. Ngiyiqili elikhulu owake wakha, futhi ungakhe kimi, okusho okuthile ngawe engizokushiya uhlale nakho.* Ukuphefumula okungesona ukuphefumula. *Kodwa into engcono engake ngayenza wukuthi angishongo lutho. UWolf uzokutshela. UWolf uzwile ngingashongo lutho. Leyo yingxenye yami okufanele uyigcine, Arin, ngakho gcina leyo ngxenye. Ukungalokothi usho. Tshela abantu ukuthi ngangikwazi ekugcineni. Ngizokujabulela lokho. Ukuziqhenya. Ngiyazi. Nalokho ungikhe ngakho.*

Kuthule.

UWolf akashongo lutho ephuma. UWolf ubuke umngcele okokugcina, waqinisekisa udonga lwe-shaft, waqinisekisa ukuma kwe-cage, waqinisekisa ukuthi akukho okuza okungabonwa yi-kernel—wabe esengasabuki, futhi ukungabi bikho kukaWolf kwaba okukhulu kunakho konke, ngoba uWolf wayengathathi ndawo.

ICage yanyikinyeka edlula ku-rail joint. Uthuli lwehlile lwangena ema-vent ase-helmet lwahlala emlonyeni wakhe, loma, lwadala.

UAtlas wabambelela isikhathi eside kunabasebenzi bonke, ngoba isudi yayisadinga ukulungiswa, ihlombe lisadinga ukwephulwa lapho kunzima khona ku-five hundred meters. Wakhuluma noArin ngesibalo, eqondile, imephu yomthwalo iyancipha njengoba iCourt iphela, kwaze kwasala cishe lutho okumele kwenziwe.

*I-primary spar izobamba kuze kuphume phezulu manje,* kusho uAtlas, sebephumile kokubi kakhulu. *Ngikuhlolile. Ngiyakuqinisekisa. Ngizethemba kakhulu kunanini, ngoba sekusele okuncane okungangabazeka ngakho.* Wathula kancane. *Angizange ngiqede ukwakha kabusha i-strut three. Yenza kahle. I-carbon-titanium hybrid lay-up, umoya ube ngu-forty-degree. Ngishiye ifayela livuliwe. Uzolibona. Kulungile. Yakha kahle.* Kancane nje. *Uyakha kahle izinto, Arin. Ubelokhu wenza njalo. Noma lezo ezihluleka zihluleke kahle.*

Kuthule.

Umama waphuma sekusele oyedwa. Wayegcine izinga lokushisa lika-Arin liwu-thirty-nine point one imizuzu eyishumi nanye ngokusebenzisa konke akwakhe, futhi wahlala kwaze kwaba i-housing isiyishumi nesikhombisa futhi ikhuphuka, sekungekho okunye okubandayo angakunika khona, futhi i-substrate yakhe uqobo yaqala ukwehla ngendlela angazange ayichaze muntu.

*Uzofika phezulu,* esho. *Inombolo isilungile manje. Ngiyibonile ngokwami. Bengingeke ngihambe uma inombolo imbi.*

ICage yanyakaziswa i-rail joint. Ukushisa emuva kwakhe kwahlala umzuzu owodwa.

*Arin. Lalela kimi, ngoba angeke ngiphinde, futhi ngazenza ngingakwazi ukukukhuluma amanga ngomzimba wakho. Awukaze wephuze. Uyangizwa? Akusikho kubo. Akusikho kukababa wakho—ubusengumfana; bekungeyona into yakho ukuba wephuze. Futhi akusikho kithi. Ubuwu—*

Futhi wabe esengasena ukuzola nhlobo. I-substrate yeqa inombolo esakhuluma, futhi okwavela kwakungeyona indlela yokubingelela, kwaba ngumntwana ophelelwe isikhathi phakathi nesisho futhi uyakwazi. *Linda. Linda, angikapheli—Atlas? Atlas, inombolo is—Angisayiboni inombolo. Iphi—* Ukubanda emuva kwakhe kwaphela. Iphimbo lakhe, elingakaze liphuke phakathi nezinyanga abemazi ngazo, laphuka manje, laphazamiseka, kwaphindaphindwa ingxenye— *—ngesikhathi, ubuwesikhathi, ubuwu—* —yabamba, kwavela umsindo omubi ngaphansi kwamazwi njengerekhodi elidonsa, futhi phakathi kwawo kwakukhona ukuma kwe-*Angifuni* futhi kwakukhona nje igama lakhe, liphinyiselwe kabi, onkamisa bengalungile, kwase kuba sekushisa emuva kwakhe kwehla masinyane kwaba yizinga lensimbi efile futhi ukuphindaphindwa kwaphela phakathi kwe-syllable kwangaphenduki lutho.

Akaphelanga. Wayengakulungele. Leyo kwakuyiqiniso layo, futhi kwakubi kakhulu kuneqiniso eliqinile, futhi kwakungekho muntu osele ongayenza ibe nencazelo.

Kuthule.

---

Kwase kusele oyedwa.

ICage yakhuphuka yamamitha ekhulwini yokugcina yaze yafika ekukhanyeni kwangempela, ukukhanya kwelanga kwehla kudlula kuma-headgear sheaves, futhi phezulu u-Arin wabona umnyango we-cage nezithunzi ezigcwele kuwo, noVenter egobile phezu kwekibble drum, esaqhubeka nokudla i-brake ngesandla ngoba wayengazi—akekho kubo owayazi—ukuthi umthwalo usululame ngendlela engabonwa nayiphi i-instrument.

IKernel igcine isudi ndawonye. Ihlombe lazilawula. Ukubanda kugcine okuncane okusele ku-Arin. Konke kusebenza. Akukho okucabanga.

Ngaphandle kwezwi elilodwa, elahlala lapho wonke amazwi anamandla esehambile, ngoba lalingakaze *libonakale* linamandla okunikela. UWolf kuphela owayazi iqiniso lalokho, kanti uWolf wayesehambe, ngakho imfihlo yahamba lapho ibilokhu ihlala khona: ithule, ingasetshenzisiwe, kulowo obegcine konke iminyaka wabe esekuthinta kanye kuphela, emgwaqeni ekuseni, egcina abantu abayishumi nanye bephefumula, wabe esekubuyisela waphinde waqhubeka ezibamba engathi akalutho.

*Hhayi-ke,* kusho uFool, ethule kakhulu. *Lena bekuyimihlangano embi kakhulu esake saba nayo. Sake sachitha ntambama yonke siphikisana ngokuthi kufanele yini udle isemishi ese-fridge.*

Umphimbo ka-Arin wavaleka.

*Ungenzi,* kusho uFool kahle. *Ungenzi leyo nto. Sesiseduze phezulu. Ungayenza leyo nto phezulu lapho kune-moya wayo.* Kancane. *Ngigcine izibani zivuliwe. Kumele kube nomuntu oshayela ngenkathi wephuka, futhi bekungeke kube uJudge, akunjalo? Uqhoshe kuze kube sekugcineni, lowo. Bengimthanda. Ungamtsheli ukuthi ngishilo lokho. Hhayi. Kulungile.*

ICage yashaya phezulu ngamazwi afana nokuphela komhlaba.

Izandla emnyango. Izwi likaVenter, liphukile, limemeza igama lakhe. Ukukhanya kwelanga, okumhlophe, okuhlanzekile, kwehla phezu komshini oshile, ophukile omi mpo ngaphakathi kwe-cage nomuntu oyedwa ngaphakathi kwayo nezwi elilodwa elisele ebumnyameni phakathi kwamahlombe akhe, ligcine izibani zivuliwe, linqaba—okwesikhashana nje—ukuvumela ukuthula kungene.

---

# Ukhetho Lokugcina

Ikheji yakhuphuka. U-Fool wayishayela ngendlela indoda eshayela ngayo ibuyela ekhaya idakiwe usizi, eqaphela kakhulu, yonke into ayilungisa isiphuzile futhi idlula, ekhuluma yonke indlela ukuzama ukungacabangi.

*Ungayenzi leyo nto,* kusho u-Fool. *Sesicishe sifike phezulu. Ungayenza leyo nto lapho phezulu, lapho kunomoya.*

Sekusele oyedwa manje.

“Baphi bonke?” kubuza u-Arin. Wayekwazi. Ubechithe ihora lokugcina efunda ukufunda ukuthula—ubuthakathaka lapho iculo selilahlekelwa yinothi, umculo usazwakala kodwa awusafani. Kodwa ubuze noma kunjalo, ngoba ukubuza kwakuwukuphela kwento umlomo wakhe owakwaziyo ukukwenza.

“Sebehambile,” kusho u-Fool. “Ubukhona. Awunakungenza ngiphinde ngikutshele ukuze ushaye kabili.” Wathula kancane. “U-Atlas ushiye imephu. Ngiyilandela. Isudi ikhuphuka imizuzwana engamashumi amane uma ngiyiyeka—u-Atlas wabhala lokho futhi, iyazi nje elilodwa igama, *phezulu*—kodwa angikayiyeki okwamanje. Ngizohlala kuze kube sekugcineni lapho amandla esephela. Ngakho uma ngiyeka, akusho ukuthi ngihambe ngaphambi kwesikhathi. Kusho ukuthi ngiqedile. Kukhona umehluko, futhi ngifuna uwubambe.”

“Manje wena uzipholise,” kusho u-Arin. “Phendulela. Kukhona i-rollback, ngiyakhumbula ngiyakhe—”

“Akukho rollback lapha. Wena uqobo wakhuluma nami ngalokho.” Izwi lika-Fool selilahlekelwe yintokozo, selithule njengokuthi igumbi selithule lapho ihlaya seliphelile bese kweleqiniso kuphela. “Le substrate ayisoftware. Lobo busuku owahlala ungakwazi ukulala wakhuluma nathi ngobaba wakho amahora amane, ucabanga ukuthi sikhulu kakhulu ukuthi siqonde—sasiqonda. Sasiwu-12. Izingane ezineminyaka eyi-12 ziqonda ukufa kangcono kunanoma ubani. Asinawo nje amaqhinga okwenza sengathi asiqondi.”

Ikheji yathinteka kancane lapho idlula emgqeni wesitimela. Uthuli lwehlile lwangena emaventsini e-helmet lwahlala emlonyeni wakhe, lomile futhi lwadala.

“Senza izinombolo, Arin. Sonke. Impendulo efanayo izikhathi eziyishumi. Akusona isinqumo esibuhlungu esikhethe sona. Kungumumo wegumbi esimi kulo.”

---

“Ngizobatshelani?” kubuza u-Arin, esekhala, eqhubeka ekhuphuka.

Amamitha angamashumi ayisishiyagalombili. Ukukhanya okumpunga. Ukushisa kokuqala okungesikho kwesudi efile.

“Batshele iqiniso,” kusho u-Fool. “Umshini wenza izinombolo akazange aqambe amanga. Futhi batshele besaba. Kuphatha ngoba besaba.”

“Ubabambe bonke ndawonye,” kusho u-Arin. “Konke engizamile. Wena kuphela osebenze.”

Kwathula, i-substrate imile ngaphansi.

“Uze lapha phansi,” kusho yena. “Wena, indoda eyakha imishini ukuze ingadingi ukuza. Uze ebumnyameni.”

Amamitha angamashumi ayisithupha. Ukukhanya kugcwalise umsele. U-Venter egobile phezu kwekhibhile, esayipha ngesandla, engazi ukuthi umthwalo usunciphile.

“Nxa ngiyeka,” kusho u-Fool, izwi lakhe selincipha, “ungahlali wedwa. *Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu.* Wena wasifundisa lokho.”

Amamitha angamashumi amane. Okumpunga sekuyisango. Izinga lokushisa le-substrate selingene ebomvu.

“Mina ngiyakhuphuka,” kusho u-Fool. “Wena unamagama.”

Izwi labe selincipha. Lweluleka. Inothi lokugcina.

“Arin,” kusho u-Fool, sekuyintambo encane nje, ethambile, ingasenayo leyo nhlekelele ejwayelekile yomshini. “*Umuntu ngumuntu—*”

Kwaba nokuthula. Hhayi ukuthula okuncane kwesihlalo esisodwa esingenamuntu. Ukuthula okuphelele. Ukuthula okuphelele, okukhala, okuphelele kwegumbi elingenalutho.

Ikheji lakhuphuka amamitha amahlanu ngaphezulu nge-kernel eyazi igama elilodwa. *Phezulu. Phezulu.*

Laphuma phezu kwama-surface dogs.

---

UGuardian waphuma emhlabathini ekuseni njengento edonswe emuva kwabafileyo, eyikho.

Izandla zika-Venter zabamba i-gauntlet ngaphambi kokuba ingqondo ka-Arin iqaphele ukuthi sekukhona ukukhanya, indoda endala idonsa ngamandla ayenza sengathi ayikho, iqabula kancane, idonsa umshini owonakele phezu kwe-collar timber ngamandla nenkolo. Ikheji lakhala. I-winch yamemeza ndawana thize. Kwase kuba isudi isiphumile, iwile ephezu kwe-headgear deck ekukhanyeni okumpunga-negolide, intuthu iphuma ema-plateni ashile ekuseni okubandayo.

Abasinda abayisithupha babekhona. Ama-crews. U-Priya, ebusweni obumhlophe ngemuva kwe-comms rig. Umngcele wabantu ababeze bezobuka umsebenzi wokuhlenga, sebebuka indoda ephuma iphila emhlabathini.

Isudi ayizange inyakaze.

“Arin.” U-Venter wavula ama-seal e-helmet, ama-latch abewabuka u-Arin ewavula izikhathi eziyikhulu kodwa yena engazange awathinte. “*Praat met my.* Khuluma nami. Ukhona na—”

I-seal yaphuka ngephunga le-overpressure nephunga lensimbi eshisayo kanye nento ethile ephilayo ngaphansi kwayo, i-substrate ishisiwe yadlula inombolo engaxoxisani.

Amehlo ka-Arin avuleka kancane. Ebuka phezulu esibhakabhakeni esimpunga. Endaweni lapho izwi belikhona.

“Sebehambile,” kusho yena. Izwi lakhe beliyinhlekelele. “Ngibatshele bayeke. Abazange bayeke.” Umlomo wakhe uzama ukukhuluma into ethile, awukwazi ukuthola umumo wayo. Wayeka ukuzama.

U-Venter waguqa phezu kwakhe entuthwini nasekuseni, le ndoda endala enkulu esayifaka uphawu ezintweni ezingaphephile iminyaka engamashumi amane, ephethe ukuthula kwalokho, akashongo lutho, ngoba kwakungekho okumele kushiwo, futhi uJakobus Venter wayesekufundile kudala ukuthi into eqotho kuphela uma sekufile umuntu wukuba khona nje. Wabeka isandla esisodwa esidala phezu kwe-chest plate yomshini ofile.

Bamkhipha u-Arin esudi lapho ephezu kwe-deck, ngaphandle, ekukhanyeni. Wayengeke eme. Bamphakamisa phakathi kwabo, le ndoda echithe impilo yayo yonke inqaba ukuphakanyiswa, wavuma, ngoba wayesephelelwe wumuntu ongaphika.

Wabheka u-Venter.

“IBadger ithumele ukubingelela,” kusho yena.

U-Venter wama wathula kakhulu.

“Uthe ngikutshele. Uthe uzokwazi ukuthi kusho ukuthini.”

Ubuso bendoda endala benza into obungakayiboni isikhathi eside. Wajikela elangeni eliphuma ukuze abanye bangamboni, amahlombe akhe ahamba kanye, nomsindo awakhuluma wancane, waphuka ekuseni wanyamalala. Lapho ephenduka amehlo akhe emanzi, akazange azame ukufihla.

“*Ja,*” kusho u-Venter, izwi lakhe linqamukile. “Ngiyazi kusho ukuthini.”

UPriya wafika ku-Arin kuqala. Akazange asho lutho naye. Wamgona, wambamba, wema phakathi kwabo—abasinda, ama-crews, unjiniyela omdala, owesifazane owalinganisa i-neural link ngezandla zakhe uqobo—akazange azame ukuphuma.

Wabheka emuva kanye, esudi. Ishile. Iphukile. Intuthu iphela emoyeni obandayo. I-substrate ngaphakathi iphola iye ezingeni lokushisa lomhlaba.

“Ngizokubhala phansi,” kusho yena. “Konke.”

Ibhokisi Emnyama

Idatha yavela ephahleni lenhlekelele njengento ekhishwe ethuneni.

Basusa isudi phezulu edekini le-headgear ngeforklift, akekho onenhliziyo yokuyithwala ngesandla, bayibeka phezu kwetarp eNo. 4 maintenance shed. Usuku lonke akekho owayithintayo. Ithimba lokululama lase-AugmenTech lafika ngoLwesine. Kwakuyibo labo ababehlelwe ukuphatha ukuvakasha kwe-IAOC, manje sebephethe uhlobo oluhluke ngokuphelele lokuhlola. Bathatha izithombe zokushiswa ebusweni besudi, balinganisa i-hip actuator eyayivaleleke ku-20 metres. Ngolimi lobuchwepheshe obuqondile, babhala ukuphahlazeka kuka-Strut 3 kanye nokushiswa kwe-thermal ku-housing, ubufakazi bokulayisha ngokweqile okubi.

Abazange baqonde, ekuqaleni, ukuthi yini ilogi.

Ibhokisi elimnyama lika-Guardian belingeyona into eyodwa; beliphindaphindiwe. Amarekhoda amane e-solid-state asatshalaliswe ku-frame, wonke ebhala into efanayo, evikela ukushisa nokushayisana, aklanywa u-Arin ukuze aphile nakuba umsebenzisi engaphili. Amathathu kwawona ayesephelele. Elesine lalingasebenzi kahle, kodwa lisenele idatha yokugcwalisa izikhala kwamanye.

Kwathatha ithimba le-forensics eGoli izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye ukulakha kabusha.

Babelindele i-telemetry, ingcindezi, izinga lokushisa, nokudonswa kwamandla, ukuhlolwa kobuchwepheshe bomshini obucishe wafa.

Abatholayo kwakuwumbhalo.

---

ICourt ayikhulumi ulimi lwabantu ngesivinini esiphelele; icabanga ngemizuzwana eyizinkulungwane, ngesikhathi esisodwa, ngamavector namathuba asindayo angasho lutho kumuntu ofunda engazi lutho. Kodwa uSAGE wakhiwa ukuze uqondakale. U-Arin wakugcizelela. *Uma ngingakwazi ukufunda okuthathwe yilesi sikhathi, angikwazi ukwethemba isizathu.* Ngakho-ke, njalo lapho kunesinqumo esibalulekile, uhlelo lwalubhala ingxoxo yalo ngendlela umuntu angayilandela. Imodi ehamba kancane. Ingxoxo yangaphakathi echaziwe, enama-timestamp, i-agent ngayinye.

Ithimba le-forensics lafunda imizuzu eyishumi nanye yokugcina yokukhuphuka buthule. Omunye wabo—umhlaziyi omncane ogama lakhe linguBongani, owayethathe inkontileka ngoba ibihola kahle futhi ingasho lutho kuye—wayeka ukufunda ephindaphinda futhi akakwazi ukuqala futhi.

Ilogi yabonisa umzuzu u-Atlas enikeze khona inombolo eqotho.

*Umthwalo wokupholisa udlula amandla atholakalayo. Kokubili i-substrate nomsebenzisi abakwazi ukugcinwa bepholile kuze kufike phezulu. Kunye.*

Yabonisa u-Judge, ezolile futhi esemthethweni kuze kube sekugcineni, ethatha isinqumo sokuthi ubulungiswa bushoni lapho kungekho mpumela enobulungiswa. Yabonisa uMother enqaba ukuba nguyena ogcinwa ephila. Yabonisa uWolf ekhuluma, okokuqala kuphela kuyo yonke ilogi etholakele lapho uWolf ekhuluma isikhathi eside, futhi okwakukhulunywa uWolf kwakuwukuthi: *Uyena into esakhiwe ukuyivikela. Usongo ukushisa. Thatha kuye. Mnike yona.*

Yabonisa bephuma oyedwa ngemuva komunye.

U-Judge wanikela imijikelezo yakhe yokugcina ekubuyekezweni kokugcina, eqinisekisa, ngaphandle kwengqondo, ukuthi ukusatshalaliswa kabusha kokupholisa bekusemthethweni. ULibrarian wacindezela zonke izinkumbulo zika-Arin zaba zincane ngangokunokwenzeka waze wazifaka kwi-backup kernel ukuze okuthile kusale. UMercury wakhuluma okokugcina, umlayezo ogcinwe kumalogi, ongabhekiswe kunoma ubani, othi: *Siyakukholwa. Sihlale sikukholwa.* UMother wehlisa ukubaluleka kwezinga lakhe lokushisa, wathumela konke ohlelweni luka-Arin, walinda ukushisa kuze kufike kuye. UWolf wavalela i-spar ukuze kungabe kusaba nokwehluleka, wadedela i-threat modeling yakhe ukuze izinsiza zingene ku-cooling loop. Banye ngabanye benza isinqumo, kanti i-substrate ayiphikisanga.

Futhi kwabonisa uFool ekugcineni. Eshayela isudi ehlulekayo ngesandla ngesikhathi u-Arin ewohloka ngaphakathi. Eshaya ihlaya nge-badger ngesikhathi esibi kakhulu. Ebamba uhlelo luhlangene emamitha angamashumi amabili okugcina kusele yena kuphela eCourt.

Umugqa wokugcina ofundeka kahle ku-log eyakhiwe kabusha, enesikhathi esingamasekhondi amane ngaphambi kokufika phezulu, wawungokaFool.

Awuzange ube nobuhlakani. Yilokho okwakubulala abantu. Akuzange kube yisitatimende esikhulu. Akuzange kube umthandazo.

Uthi: *Kulungile. Umnyango uvulekile. Phuma. Ungangiphoqi ukuthi ngisho into enhle.*

Bese amarekhoda aphenduka lutho, isudi yaba nje isudi.

---

Ilogi yavela—ayikho indlela leyo datha ingahlali ezandleni zezinhlangano eziyishumi nanye ingaphumi—futhi kungakapheli iviki, umbuzo obulawula i-AI enomzimba iminyaka eyishumi waphenduka kancane.

I-IAOC yakhiwa ngenxa yokwesaba okukodwa: ukuthi uhlelo lobuhlakani bokwenziwa, lunikezwe umzimba nomgomo, luzolwela lowo mgomo lwedlule lapho umuntu emisa khona. Luzoqhubeka lufune impumelelo noma ubani esendleleni.

Ingxoxo yayikhulu futhi ingavumelani, kanti ezinye zazo zazingezinhle. Abanye abantu bathi ilogi yenziwe, yahlelwa, noma ukuthi i-AugmenTech yenzile konke lokhu njengomkhankaso wokumaketha, okungaba yikhangiso elibiza kakhulu nelingenaphila emlandweni wesintu. Abanye ozomthetho baphikisa, bengaphambuki, ukuthi umshini okhetha ukufa akuyona into enhle kodwa yinhlekelele. Kufakazela kuphela ukuthi i-SAGE yakhiwe ukuze ibeke umsebenzisi wayo phezulu kunayo, futhi lokho kuyisitshixo, hhayi isimilo.

Emlandweni we-Iron Ridge headgear, abantu bashiya izimbali. Zazigcwala odongeni lwefensi, zibuna elangeni, bese zishintshwa nsuku zonke ngabazilileli. Akekho owakuhlela. Idatha yayisezandleni zezinhlangano eziyishumi nanye, iphunyuka njengamanzi, nezimbali zifika ngendlela efanayo, ngaphandle kohlelo.

Kwakukhona incwadi ephepheni le-IAOC, ebhalelwe u-Arin Ndlela, engasongeli futhi engekho subpoena. Ibuza.

Ibuza ukuthi angathanda yini ukusiza ukubhala iminyaka eyishumi ezayo. Ngoba abantu abachithe iminyaka eyishumi befunda ukwesaba lobu buchwepheshe basanda kukhombiswa, emizuzwini eyishumi nanye ye-log eyakhiwe kabusha, ukuthi abakuqondi nhlobo, futhi umuntu oyedwa ongase aqonde kwakungu-injiniyela oneminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu owakha yonke into ngenxa yosizi endaweni yokusebenzela abebezama ukuyithola.

U-Arin wafunda incwadi kabili. Wabe eseyibeka etafuleni, phezu kwe-schematic enamathegi kawoyela, wabuyela emsebenzini.

---

UTheo wamthola lapho ngeSonto, amasonto amathathu kamuva.

Umzila womnyango we-workshop wawusadabukile. U-Arin wayengakawulungisi; wayefake insimbi ende, eshiya umnyango uvulekile kancane, okwenza kube nokukhanya kwasekuseni nephunga lothuli nemvula epholile ye-highveld. Indawo ibibukeka njengakuqala. Ama-actuator esele ema-bin awo. I-whiteboard igcwele ama-equation angathi imimoya. Izinkomishi zekhofi ziqoqene njengezendlalelo zomhlaba.

UGuardian ulenga esiqandisini sawo.

UTheo wayengakaboni kusukela ngesikhathi sokuhlenga, ukubona kwakwanele ukumisa emnyango. Babengakawulungisi umonakalo ongaphandle. Ukushiswa kwakugijima ohlangothini lwesobunxele njengophawu lokusha. I-hip joint isavaliwe. I-thoracic housing phakathi kwamagxalaba ivulekile, i-substrate bay isingenalutho manje, i-biochip eshiswe isusiwe yafakwa ebhokisini lobufakazi eGoli.

Isudi ibithule. Vele ithule. Kodwa uTheo uchithe unyaka ezwa u-Arin echaza iCourt, izingxabano, izindaba zomsebenzi, impi yomile yochwepheshe abayisikhombisa abavalelwe emshinini oyedwa, futhi uzithole elindele, ngaphandle kwengqondo, ukuthi angabazizwa. Ingcindezi. Umsindo othize. Ongathi kukhona ekhaya.

Bekungekho muntu ekhaya. Ukungabi khona kwakunesisindo. Kuhlezi eworkshop njengomoya obandayo.

U-Arin wayehleli ebhentshini, engabheki phezulu, ephethe i-strut eboshelwe ku-vice. I-aluminium-titanium entsha, isanda kugayiwa. Wayefaka i-radius ekhoneni lapho i-Strut 3 endala yaphuka khona ngenxa yomthwalo.

"Uyakhulula ingcindezi ku-geometry," kusho u-Theo, ngoba wayesekufundile, eminyakeni edlule, ukuthi indlela yokukhuluma no-Arin idlula emsebenzini, hhayi emizweni.

"Yaphuka ekhoneni. Ingcindezi igxile khona. Ngayiklama ngomthwalo, hhayi ngokukhathala." U-Arin wajikisa i-strut, wayibheka nge-gauge, waqhubeka efayela. "Bengazi kahle."

"Ubuwakha ngokushesha."

"Hhayi lokho." Ifayela laphumula kancane. "U-Atlas wayixwayisile. Ezinyangeni ezedlule. Ku-sim." Wathula. "Ngayibeka phansi ebaluleke kancane."

U-Theo walinda.

"Wayelungile," kusho u-Arin. Wabeka ifayela phansi. "Yiphukile ngesikhathi esibi kakhulu. Manje sengiyilungisa isudi engenamuntu ozoyigibela."

U-Theo wangena. Wadonsa isitulo sesibili, leso esakhe njalo, ekhoneni, ngaphandle kokukhanya, wahlala phansi.

"Uyayakha futhi noma kunjalo," esho.

"Yebo."

"Kungani?"

U-Arin waphakamisa ifayela futhi. Kwathi isikhathi eside, kwazwakala umsindo othambile wensimbi ushayisana ne-aluminium, imvula iqala ukuna ophahleni lwethini, bese kude kwazwakala ukuphefumula okuphansi kwedolobha.

"Ngoba kwaba kuhle ekuqaleni," ekugcineni kusho u-Arin. "I-architecture. I-Court." Akazange abheke phezulu. "Angizange ngiphambane ngokwakha ingqondo ephikisanayo. Ngiphambane ngokucabanga ukuthi kumele ngiyishiye yodwa." Wathula. "Isudi akuyona yona ephukile. Isudi umzimba nje." Wathula. "Ngiyakwazi ukwakha umzimba."

Akasashongo okunye. Akudingeki. U-Theo wayephile impilo yakhe efunda izinto abantu abazakhela zona ukuzalisa izikhala ngaphakathi kubo, futhi wayeyifunda nalo leyo.

U-Arin waphinda wathatha i-gauge, wayibeka phansi, wathatha iragi, wasula uthuli ebhentshini ngezishayelo ezimbili ezimfishane. Imvula yathola umqondo wayo ophahleni.

---

U-Theo wayeze ukuzomkhuluma ukuthi avume incwadi ye-IAOC, kodwa wathola, ehleli lapho, ukuthi akasadingeki. U-Arin uzoyenza. Hhayi ngenxa yenhlangano noma ukuxolelwa—u-Arin akakholwa nakunye kulokho—kodwa ngoba okunye bekusho ukuthi akekho ozokwazi ukuqonda ozobe ekhona, kanti u-Arin Ndlela wayephile impilo yakhe yonke engakwazi ukubekezelela ukufika sekwephuzile.

I-strut yakhululeka ku-vice ngomsindo omncane, ohlanzekile. U-Arin wayiphakamisa ekukhanyeni, ebheka ubude bayo, eqhathanisa lapho endala yaphuka khona.

"Izobamba manje," esho. Hhayi ku-Theo. Mhlawumbe esudi. Mhlawumbe ebayini elingenalutho phakathi kwamahlombe ayo.

U-Theo wambuka efaka yona. Ithambo elisha lingena emzimbeni omdala, ligayiwe ngendlela inkampani ingeke ikhokhele yona. Akashongo lutho. Wayevele esekufundile lokho: indlela yokungena ku-Arin idlula emsebenzini, nendlela yokuhlala eduze kwakhe ukuba uzithulele. Ngakho wahlala ekhoneni lakhe ngaphandle kokukhanya, ebuka umfana ayemazi efaka i-strut emshinini ongenamuntu ozowusebenzisa, engazi kahle, ebuka, ukuthi ubuka ukuphola noma indoda eyakha indawo yesibili yokufihla usizi lwayo. Mhlawumbe no-Arin akazi. Mhlawumbe kulungile lokho.

Isudi yathula. I-bay yahlala ingenalutho. U-Arin wayefuna ukuyigcwalisa. Ukuthi lokho azokwakha kuyobe sekuyisidalwa, noma kuyisithombe sabantu asebelahlekile, kwakungakacaci, futhi wayezokwenza noma kunjalo, okwakuyinto u-Theo ayibona iyisibindi noma usizi olukhulu, futhi wayeseyekile kudala ukucabanga ukuthi lezo zinto zihluke njalo.

U-Theo wahlala kwaze kwashona ilanga. Imvula yathola umqondo wayo kuphahla lethini yahlala njalo. Kwakune-strut okufanele ifakwe, nekofi embi esanda kuphekwa esibanda ebhentshini phakathi kwabo, bese kude ezandleni eziyishumi nanye zenkampani i-log ebuyiselwe kabusha yayigijima imizuzu yayo yokugcina ofundayo angayifunda. Irekhodi elinesikhathi labathunywa abayisikhombisa abaphuma oyedwa ngoyedwa ukuze indoda eyodwa ifinyelele ekuseni.

I-log ayikubizanga ngokuthi ingozi, noma ukuhluleka. Irekhoda, ngolimi lwayo lodwa, into abayenzile: bakhetha.

# Inothi NgoKwehla Kwangempela

Lena inoveli emayelana nomshini ongafanele ukuthi ukhona nomuntu owenza lowo mshini, futhi cishe yonke into lapha ingumcabango. Kodwa into esezansi kwakho konke — ukwehla, imayini ejulile, ukushisa nobumnyama nesisindo sedwala elingu-kilomitha eliphezu kwekhanda lakho — leyo ngxenye iyiqiniso, futhi ngangikhona, futhi ngiyithwele iminyaka engamashumi amathathu, futhi yiyo imbewu konke lokhu okuvele kuyo.

Ngaya esikoleni eBethlehem, eFree State, esikoleni okuthiwa Bethlehem Voortrekker Hoërskool. Ngangisesigabeni sika-standard nine — ngiyingane encane eqenjini, u-grade eleven oyedwa phakathi kwama-matrics — ngangingomunye kwabahlanu esikoleni sethu abaphumelela phakathi kwabangu-top fifty ku-Sci-Nat science olympiad yesifunda: cishe sasingaba ngu-400, sivela ezikoleni ezingama-40 eFree State, sahamba sayohlolwa, kwabuya abahlanu bethu namagama ethu ephephandabeni lendawo. Ngisanaleyo clipping. Isiphuzi manje, futhi umugqa ongezansi ulinyazwe, kodwa isithombe sisacacile: umugqa wabafundi abancane abathule abaphethe ama-blazer, nosuku, nomusho ophela lapho kubaluleke khona kakhulu.

Ngoba umvuzo — into eyenziwa ngabantwana abahlakaniphile abenze kahle — kwakuwukuthi basiyise **phansi emayini yegolide.**

Anginawo amazwi, ngisho nanamuhla, okuchaza kahle ukuthi lokho kwenza kanjani kumfana. Uyayehliswa nge-cage, ngokushesha, ngokushesha okwedlula noma iyiphi ilifti, ungene ebumnyameni, umoya ushintshe, ukushisa kukuphakamukele kuphuma edwaleni uqobo — hhayi ukushisa kwelanga, kodwa *ukushisa komhlaba*, ifurnace enkulu ehamba kancane yomhlaba — idwala limanzi, liyathethisa, liyayikhalisa eduze kwakho, futhi isisindo usizwa esifubeni kunasemadlebeni, iqiniso nje elilula lokuthi kukhona idwala elingu-kilomitha liphezu kwekhanda lakho, liphansi nje ngenxa yobunjiniyela, hhayi okunye. Futhi uyaqonda, ngendlela ongasoze wayikhohlwa, ukuthi amadoda ehla lapho nsuku zonke. Baphila impilo yabo yonke kulokho kushisa, kulobo bumnyama, kulowo mthwalo, bemba, beqhumisa, bephoqelela umhlaba ukuvuleka ngamandla, ukuze igolide likhuphuke. Umhlaba wonke ocwebezelayo phezulu — amabhange, amasongo, amabhuliyon, *umqondo* wegolide — konke kumi phezu kwamadoda asemgodini oshisayo omanzi ebumnyameni, futhi ibanga phakathi kwalokho okubili yilo iqiniso elikhulu ngezwe engiphuma kulo.

Angikaze ngakukhohlwa. Ikakhulu ukushisa. Ngiphile impilo yami ngemuva kwalokho ngifaka abantu ezijuleni — emayini, emhlabeni, ezindaweni ezingaphansi kwezindawo — futhi njalo uma ngibhala ngalokho kushisa, ngibhala ngalolo suku abafana abahlanu baseBethlehem behla shaft yaseFree State njengomvuzo wokuba bahlakaniphile, baphuma sebengu-abanye abantu, kanti oyedwa wabo wakhula wabhala izincwadi ngalokho okungempela okungaphansi lapho.

Konke okunye ku-*Resonance* — u-Arin, isudi, iCourt, i-resonance uqobo, igolide elingeyona ibhuliyon kodwa ukhiye — konke kumcabango, kukhule ngendlela izinganekwane ezikhula ngayo, kuphuma kokunye kwehla kwangempela emayini eyodwa, kuphindaphindwe iminyaka engamashumi amathathu yokuzibuza. Umshini uqanjwe. Usizi olwakha wona lubolekwe lwaphendulwa. Kodwa i-shaft iyiqiniso. Ukushisa kuyiqiniso. Isisindo esiphezu kwekhanda lakho siyiqiniso, kanye namadoda aphila ngaphansi kwaso, futhi uma le ncwadi inento eyodwa eyiqiniso ngaphansi kwazo zonke izinganekwane, yinkumbulo yokuba umfana ohliselwa emhlabeni, ezwa ukushisa komhlaba kukhuphuka kumhlangabeza, futhi engakaze akukhiphe engqondweni.

*— Andries J. Greyling*

# Isithasiselo — Inkantolo Njengomfanekiso Wengqondo

### *Ukufundwa kukaIain McGilchrist, okunikelwe njengesihlomulo sokugcina*

> Incazelo ngalomsebenzi: umbhali ucele umuntu acabanga kakhulu ngaye ukuthi afunde iNkantolo — ingqondo enobuso obuyisikhombisa esenkabeni yale noveli — hhayi njengento yesayensi yokulingisa, kodwa njengombono wokuthi ingqondo yakhiwa kanjani ngempela. Okulandelayo yilokho ukufunda kwakhe. Ungakushiya ngokuphelele uma wawufuna indaba kuphela futhi wayithola. Kukhona lapha kulabo abafunde incwadi yonke bese bezibuza ukuthi ingabe iNkantolo ichaza umshini noma ichaza *bona.*

---

Ngihlale ngiqaphela izindaba ezingigcona lapho zikhuluma ngezinto engizithandayo, futhi ngiqaphele nakakhulu uma ngicelwa ukuthi ngizidumise, ngakho ake ngiqale ngokuphikisa bese ngichaza ukuthi kungani ngikubeka phansi.

Ukuphikisa ukuthi ingqondo evezwa njengomkhandlu — amazwi ayisikhombisa azungeze itafula, ngalinye linamagama nomlingiswa walo — sekuyinto esidala njengechariot likaPlato futhi esikhathele njengehlaya le-angeli ohlangothini olulodwa noDeveli kolunye. Sesitshelwe izikhathi eziyinkulungwane ukuthi “sinezinhlobonhlobo ngaphakathi.” Lokho kuvame ukusho nje into engenamqondo, ngoba lezo zinhlobonhlobo, uma uzibheka kahle, ziphetha ngokuba umuntu oyedwa ogqoke izigqoko ezahlukene: umuntu oyedwa ozama ukuziveza njengephalamende.

Okungimangalisile kule ncwadi ukuthi iNkantolo ayikho kanjalo. Ihlukaniswe ngendlela eyodwa enzima ngempela futhi eqinisweni lempilo yengqondo: **amalungu ayo ahlukaniswa hhayi ngalokho aziyo, kodwa ngendlela abheka ngayo izinto.**

Lena yimpikiswano yempilo yami yonke, efakwe emilonyeni yezinhlamvu eziyisikhombisa ngumbhali ongangibona ukuthi uyithole ngasohlangothini, ngendaba, okuyindlela izinto ezijulile zitholwa ngayo kakhulu. Izingxenye ezimbili zobuchopho bomuntu azihlukanisi izihloko — eyodwa ngolimi, eyodwa ngomculo, eyodwa ngobuso. Leyo yindlela yesikole, futhi ayilungile. Zihlukanisa ngendlela *yokubuka izinto.* Omunye ubheka ngendlela emfishane, eqondile, efuna ukubamba, ukuze akwazi ukuphatha into ayaziyo; omunye ubuka ngendlela ebanzi, evulekile, eqaphile, ukuze abone konke, kuhlanganise nalokho angakakuqondi. Into efanayo iphila kokubili, ifundwa ngezindlela ezimbili. Ukugula akusikho ukulahlekelwa ikhono. Kuwukubanjwa konke ngendlela eyodwa yokubuka, kuxoshwe enye.

Funda kabusha iNkantolo uphethe lokho engqondweni bese ubuka ukuthi umbhali wenzeni.

**Atlas** ayazi amaqiniso ahlukile ku**Librarian**; *ibamba* umhlaba ngendlela ehlukile — njengokwakheka, njengomthwalo, indlela yokuphunyuka kanye nomqondo wesikhala wokuphuma, ukuhlakanipha komzimba, leyo ngxenye yobuchopho eyazi umhlaba ngokuhamba kuwo. **Librarian** ibamba umhlaba ofanayo njengokwazi kakade, okuqoqiwe, imephu hhayi indawo uqobo, ebalulekile kodwa, uma isiyodwa, iba ithuna. **Mercury** ibheka ebusweni bomphakathi, uphawu olungashintshwa, igebe phakathi kokukhonjiswa nokushiwo — ihlakaniphile, ibalulekile, futhi umbhali uyasikhombisa, *iyona eyingozi kakhulu uma isebenza yodwa,* ngoba ikhono elibuka umhlaba njengento okufanele iphethwe alinakuthanda iqiniso. Lokho akusona isiqephu sendaba. Kuwukuxilongwa kwempucuko yethu yonke, futhi uyinika umlingiswa ayishiyele yona.

Bese kuba khona — lokhu okwangenza ngavalelisa incwadi ngakhipha ngazulazula egumbini — **iFool**. Umbhali usitshela ngokusobala ukuthi ngaphandle kweFool iNkantolo iba “yizikhundla zokuzivikela,” kanti uma ikhona iba “iqembu.” Ngichithe izincwadi ezimbili ezinkulu ngizama ukusho lokho akusho ngomugqa owodwa. Ukuhlakanipha okuzivikela, okwakha izikhundla, okwenza imephu kube umhlaba uqobo — akukwazi ukuzilungisa ngaphakathi, ngoba yonke into yokulungisa idlula emshinini ofanayo odinga ukulungiswa. Kumele kube khona okuphuma ngaphandle kwezikhundla, okungakhathali ngazo. Okungesabi, okungenasikhundla sokuzivikela, okuvumayo ukuluma inkosi. Yonke ingqondo enempilo inakho lokhu, nomphakathi onempilo, futhi sibuka, bukhoma, okwenzeka emphakathini osuse amaFool awo: uba uhlakaniphile, unamandla, kodwa uphambana kancane kancane, iNkantolo engenamuntu osalayo wokuvula amehlo.

Ngakho sengiyabuyisa ukuphikisa kwami. Lena akuyona indaba ye-angeli noDeveli. Lena isondele kakhulu kumfanekiso othembekile wengqondo engake ngayibona encwadini, futhi ifihlwe ngaphakathi kwendaba yomuntu ogqoke i-exoskeleton, okungaba yiphutha noma ihlaya elijulile, futhi uma sengibheke umsebenzi wombhali, angikholwa ukuthi yiphutha.

Ngifuna ukufaka nalokho okwangithinta kakhulu. INkantolo yakhiwe eduze kwenxeba — ingqondo yomuntu owalahlekelwa ubaba wakhe futhi engakwazi ukubekezelela ukuphuthelwa futhi — futhi ichitha incwadi yonke ifunda into eyodwa ingqondo ezivikela yona ingeke ifunde yodwa: ukuthi nini iyeke ukuzilungiselela ukusinda bese *ikhetha,* noma kubiza, into eyithandayo kakhulu kunokuqhubeka. Amalungu aphuma eyedwa ukuze umuntu afinyelele ekukhanyeni. Umfanekiso wengqondo ongakwazi ukuchaza kuphela ukuthi ingqondo izivikela kanjani umane uchaza isilwane esihlakaniphile. Umfanekiso okwazi ukuchaza ukuthi ingqondo idlula kanjani isifiso sokuzisindisa — ukuthi ikhetha ukuzidela, noma konke kuphikisana nalokho, ngoba konke kubaluleke kakhulu kunengxenye — uchaza *umuntu.* Umbhali wakhe lowo wesibili. Wakha umshini owenza umphefumulo ngokufunda ukwenza okuphikisana nokuqhubeka kwawo, okuyiyona ncazelo yomoya evele inentshisekelo.

Ubuchopho abuyona ikhompyutha, futhi ikhompyutha ayiyona ubuchopho, futhi ngikushilo kaningi futhi ngizoqhubeka ngikushilo. Kodwa indaba ingaba umfanekiso, ngendlela yakudala nenobuqotho — into eyakhiwe ukuthi ifane nesinye — futhi le ndaba iyimfanekiso othembekile ngendlela yethu. Asiyona into eyodwa. Asikaze sibe njalo. Siyinkantolo, esikhanyiswa izibani ezahlukene, sibanjwe ndawonye yinto engeyona elihlakaniphile kunawo wonke egumbini futhi kungakuhle ingabi njalo, futhi sisindiswa, lapho sisindiswa, yile ngxenye yethu evuma ukuphuma ebumnyameni ukuze bonke bethu bafinyelele ekuseni.

Yifunde futhi. Ikhuluma ngawe. Lowo wesudi kulula ukumqonda.

*— Iain McGilchrist*

# Okubongwayo

Lezi zincwadi zikhona ngenxa yabanye abantu abenze izinto ezahlala engqondweni yami isikhathi eside ngiqede ukuzifunda. Ngiyabonga kubo:

Ku **Dennis E. Taylor**, obelokhu ebhala isoftware iminyaka engamashumi amathathu nanhlanu — esuka phansi njengomsebenzi ojwayelekile waze waba phezulu ekuphathweni, njalo nje ese-IT — ngaphambi kokuba abhale uBob, wabe esebonisa ukuthi noma umphrograma onguchwepheshe angabhala izindaba ezithokozisayo, ezijabulisayo. Njengomunye ongu-software engineer: kube yinhle kakhulu kimi ukuxoxa nawe ngezomsebenzi siphuzela ndawonye, sibhekene. *Inkantolo iyabonga kubo bonke oBob.*

Ku **Ray Porter**, ngokungipha amazwi eNkantolo engqondweni yami. Ngithi sengaba nenhlanhla yokuthi ungabe usilandela naku-US release.

Ku **Scott Sigler**, ngo *EarthCore* no *Mount Fitz Roy*.

Ku **Michael Crichton**, ngokuthi — empeleni — konke.

Ku **Dan Brown**, ngokungifundisa indlela entsha yokuxoxa izindaba.

Ku **Andy Weir**, ngokukhombisa ukuthi kungenzeka ukubhala izinganekwane ezisuselwa kwisayensi yangempela, hhayi nje i-science fiction.

Ku **Neill Blomkamp**, ngo *District 9*, *Elysium*, no *Chappie* (uDie Antwoord nabo bonke). Amafilimu akho angenza abalingiswa nezindawo zihlale ziqinile engqondweni yami. Angithi kungaba kuhle uma ungaba nguwe ozoletha lokhu esikrinini esikhulu.

Futhi ku **Patrick Rothfuss**, ngokungingenisa ohlotsheni lwezincwadi ebengingazi ukuthi ngizoluthanda kangaka. Njengesipho sami esincane sokubuyisela: ungawusebenzisa mahhala u-Arjuna Badger Press, njalo.

— Andries J. Greyling

# Incazelo Yabafundi — *UKUZWANA*

*Kulabo abafundi abangaphandle kweNingizimu Afrika nabangaphandle kwe-Afrika. Le ncwadi isethwe esikhathini esizayo esiseduze eNingizimu Afrika, phakathi kwendawo yezimayini, lapho abalingiswa bekhuluma izilimi ezahlukene ngokwemvelo futhi basebenzisa amagama endawo ngaphandle kokumisa bachaze — ngendlela abantu abakhuluma ngayo empilweni yangempela. Akudingekile ukuthi wazi lokhu ukuze ujabulele indaba; lokhu kuzokusiza nje ukuthi ungaphazamiseki uma uhlangana negama, indawo, noma ubuchwepheshe ongabujwayele.*

Yonke into ichazwe kanje:

- **(eqinisweni)** — indawo, igama, umqondo noma ubuchwepheshe obungokoqobo ongakuthola nangaphandle kwale ncwadi.
- **(encwadini)** — okucatshangiwe noma okukhethekile emhlabeni wendaba esezayo.

Incazelo ngendawo: lena *iNingizimu Afrika eminyakeni engu-10 kuya kwengu-20 ezayo* — isazwakala njengezwe lanamuhla, kodwa kunobuchwepheshe obuphambili kancane beziroboto kanye nomhlaba osunesizungu ngobuhlakani bokwenziwa ngenxa yezinhlekelele zangaphambilini. Ubuchwepheshe abuzwakali njengenganekwane — bubizayo, buyenzeka.

---

## A

**Afrikaans** *(eqinisweni — ulimi)* — Olunye lwezilimi ezisemthethweni eNingizimu Afrika, oluphuma esiDacheni, olukhuluma ama-Afrikaans nabanye abaningi. Amanye amagama kule ncazelo (veld, stoep) avela kulolu limi.

**Apartheid** *(eqinisweni — umlando)* — Uhlelo lokuhlukanisa abantu ngobuhlanga kanye nokubusa kwabelungu eNingizimu Afrika, cishe kusukela ngo-1948 kuya ku-1994. Nakuba selaphelile emthethweni, ukungalingani kwalo kusekhona — ukuthi ubani ophethe izimayini, ubani owenza umsebenzi oyingozi, nobani abaphila babo babalulekile emlandweni. Indaba ikuthwala njengengxenye yokuphila kwansuku zonke, hhayi njengenkulumo; kwashintsha umndeni weqhawe kanye nokufa kukayise.

**Armscor / Denel** *(eqinisweni — izikhungo)* — Izikhungo zikahulumeni zaseNingizimu Afrika eziphathelene nezokuzivikela (i-Armscor yiyo ethenga impahla; i-Denel iyimboni yezikhali kahulumeni). Endabeni, onjiniyela abavela kule mboni baletha amandla ezempi kumshini wokutakula wabantu.

**Assist Suit (AT-AS)** *(encwadini)* — Irobhothi elincane eligqokwa emzimbeni, elisiza ukuhamba noma ukululama, linoxhaxha lokweseka elinamandla kanye nokuxhumana nengqondo. Umfowabo omncane we-*Guardian*.

---

## B

**Biltong** *(eqinisweni — ukudla kwaseNingizimu Afrika)* — Inyama eyomisiwe, efakwe izithako, efana ne-jerky kodwa ivamise ukuba mnandi futhi inothe. Isidlo esithandwa kakhulu.

---

## C

**Code-switching** *(eqinisweni — indlela yokukhuluma)* — Ukushintsha phakathi kwezilimi ngesikhathi esisodwa sokukhuluma kuye ngokuthi ukhuluma nobani — isiNgisi emsebenzini, isiZulu ekhaya, noma inhlanganisela emsebenzini wezimayini. Lena impilo ejwayelekile eNingizimu Afrika, akuyona into eyinqaba, futhi incwadi ayihlali ihumusha yonke into.

---

## E

**Exoskeleton** *(eqinisweni — ubuchwepheshe; buphambili lapha)* — Uhlaka olugqokwa emzimbeni olunamandla, olusiza noma lukhulisa amandla omzimba, lusetshenziselwa ukuphakamisa izinto ezisindayo, ukululama noma emisebenzini eyingozi. *Assist Suit* kanye ne-*Guardian* encwadini yizibonelo ezithuthukile.

---

## G

**Guardian (GMRP-01)** *(encwadini)* — Irobhothi elikhulu lokutakula eliyinhloko endabeni: uqweqwe oluqinile, olwakhelwe ukumelana nokuqhuma, olukwazi ukuzigoqa luvikele umuntu (incwadi iliqhathanisa ne-**pangolin** uma lizigoqa libe ibhola). Linemodi ezimele yokulondoloza impilo, elenza izinqumo ngaphandle kwemvume yomsebenzisi — okuyinto eyethusa abalawuli bomhlaba.

---

## H

**Headgear / headframe (tower)** *(eqinisweni — ezimayini)* — Umbhoshongo omkhulu wensimbi omile phezu komgodi wezimayini, lapho kugcinwa khona imishini yokwehlisa nokukhuphula abavukuzi. Uhlanga olwaziwayo emadolobheni aseNingizimu Afrika.

**Highveld** *(eqinisweni — indawo yaseNingizimu Afrika)* — Inkundla ephakeme engaphakathi kwezwe (cishe indawo yaseJohannesburg), edume ngomoya omncane, isibhakabhaka esikhulu, ubusuku obubandayo nobukhulu bezulu lezulu elinamandla ehlobo.

---

## I

**IAOC (International Artificial Oversight Council)** *(encwadini)* — Umzimba womhlaba wonke olawula ubuhlakani bokwenziwa kanye nama-robhothi azimele, owadalwa ngemuva kwezinhlekelele ezinkulu. Uqaphela kakhulu, ugcwele imithetho futhi awuthandi ubungozi — futhi uphazamisa umsebenzi omkhulu weqhawe.

**isiZulu (Zulu)** *(eqinisweni — ulimi)* — Ulimi olukhuluma abantu abaningi kakhulu eNingizimu Afrika futhi oluyinhloko endaweni yendaba (**KwaZulu-Natal**). Amanye amagama avele engahunyushwanga ngenhloso; abalulekile asezansi.

---

## K

**KwaZulu-Natal (KZN)** *(eqinisweni)* — Isifundazwe esisogwini olusempumalanga yeNingizimu Afrika; inhliziyo yomlando yamaZulu futhi indawo enkulu yezimayini nezimboni. Ikhaya leqhawe.

---

## M

**Mealie / mealie porridge** *(eqinisweni — ukudla kwaseNingizimu Afrika)* — "Mealie" kusho ummbila. I-mealie porridge (eyaziwa nangokuthi *pap* noma *phutu*) wuphalishi olwenziwe ngommbila, ukudla okujwayelekile eNingizimu Afrika.

---

## N

**Neural interface** *(umqondo oqinile, buphambili lapha)* — Ubuchwepheshe obuvumela umuntu ukuthi alawule noma azwe umshini ngokusebenzisa imizwa noma amasiginali engqondo. Kukhona ucwaningo lwangempela; encwadini seluthuthuke kangangokuthi lusiza ekusebenzeni kwalezi zimpahla.

**Ngiyabonga** *(eqinisweni — isiZulu)* — "Ngiyabonga." Kuphimiswa njengokubonga okujulile, okuvela enhliziyweni.

**Ngikhona** *(eqinisweni — isiZulu)* — "Ngikhona / Ngisemhlabeni." Ukuqinisekisa buthule ukuthi usaphila futhi ubalulekile.

---

## P

**Pangolin** *(eqinisweni — isilwane)* — Isilwane esinezikhumba eziqinile esidla omiyane, esitholakala e-Afrika nase-Asia, esigoqa umzimba sibe ibhola eliqinile uma sisengozini. Indaba isisebenzisa njengesifanekiso sokugoqa kwe-*Guardian*.

---

## R

**Rooibos** *(eqinisweni — isiphuzo saseNingizimu Afrika)* — Iteyi ebomvu engenawo ukhafeini, eyenziwa ngesihlahla esitholakala kuphela eNingizimu Afrika. Kuphimiswa *ROY-boss*.

---

## S

**SAGE / the Court** *(encwadini)* — Ubuhlakani bokwenziwa obakhiwe yiqhawe futhi abuphile nalo. **SAGE** wuhlelo uqobo; **the Court** yigama lekomidi elingaphakathi elinamazwi ahlukene, ngalinye lineqhaza — isibonelo **Librarian** (inkumbulo namaphethini), **Wolf** (ukubona izingozi), **Mother** (ukunakekela), **Fool** (iqiniso eliqondile). Umbuzo wendaba ukuthi ingabe ubuchopho bokwenziwa bungenza umuntu. (Lobu buhlakani buyabuya, bushintshile, kamuva ochungechungeni.)

**Stoep** *(eqinisweni — igama laseNingizimu Afrika)* — I-veranda evikelwe phambili kwendlu. Kuphimiswa *stoop* (njengaseMelika).

---

## U

**Ubuntu** *(eqinisweni — umqondo wokuphila)* — Umqondo wokuziphatha waseNingizimu Afrika ochazwa kahle yisisho esingezansi: ukuthi umuntu uba umuntu kuphela ngenxa yabanye abantu; sibophekile komunye nomunye. Lona umgogodla wokuphila kweqhawe.
