**Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu** *(eqinisweni — isisho sesiZulu)* — *"Umuntu ungumuntu ngabantu."* Isisho esibaluleke kakhulu encwadini — inhliziyo ye-**ubuntu**. Kuphimiswa cishe *oo-MOON-too ngoo-MOON-too nga-BAN-too*.

---

## V

**Veld** *(eqinisweni — igama laseNingizimu Afrika)* — Izwe elivulekile, elingalinywanga; ihlathi noma utshani obude. Kuphimiswa *felt*. ("Highveld" yindawo ephakeme.)

**Isakhiwo sokushaywa kweVredefort** *(siyiqiniso)* — Lona umkhondo osuphelile wokushayeka kwenkwali enkulu kunazo zonke futhi endala emhlabeni (cishe iminyaka eyizigidi eziyizinkulungwane ezimbili ubudala), eseNingizimu Afrika esifundazweni saseFree State. Ingcindezi enkulu yalelo shayeka lasendulo yenza kwaba khona izimayini zegolide nezimbiwa ezijulile kule ndawo — futhi, encwadini, limela uphawu: ingcindezi iletha ingcebo, ukufa, kanye neqhawe uqobo.

---

## W

**Witwatersrand (i-"Rand")** *(siyiqiniso)* — Leli idwala eliqhamuka negolide elizungeze iJohannesburg, eNingizimu Afrika — indawo enegolide eliningi kakhulu emhlabeni kanye nezimayini ezijulile kakhulu (zifika cishe ku-3 kuya ku-4 km phansi), lapho amatshe ashisayo futhi ingcindezi inkulu kakhulu. Leli gama lisho ukuthi "umqansa wamanzi amhlophe" ngesi-Afrikaans. Umhlaba wezimayini kule ncwadi usendaweni le.

---

**Yebo** *(siyiqiniso — isiZulu)* — "Yebo." Kuphinyiselwa *YEH-bo*. Impendulo elula, eqinisa inhliziyo.

---

*Okokugcina: incwadi ish deliberately ishiyela ezinye izigaba ngesiZulu, ithemba ukuthi umzuzu uqobo uzoletha incazelo — njengoba nje ulimi lusebenza nsuku zonke empilweni yaseNingizimu Afrika. Lezi zinguqulo ngenhlonipho nje, hhayi ukulungisa.*

# Ibhadi Yesihlahla

![Ibhadi yesihlahla, *Mellivora capensis*, lithathwe ebusuku.](/Users/ajgreyling/code/africangold/design/creatures/honey-badger.jpg)

*Ibhadi yesihlahla (*Mellivora capensis*) yisilwane esimele i-Arjuna Badger Press. Konke okungezansi kuyiqiniso. Okuningi kwakho kuzwakala sengathi kuyamanga.*

Ibhadi yesihlahla selake safakwa ohlwini lwe-*Guinness World Records* njengesilwane esinesibindi esikhulu kunazo zonke emhlabeni. Asizange sifake isicelo salokho futhi asizange sikhathale ngisho nokuba khona emcimbini.

Sidla izinyoka ezinobuthi — ama-puff adder, ama-cobra, zonke nje. Uma silunywe kabi size singenwe ubuthongo, kube sengathi siwile, sibonakala sihlala phansi, silale, silinde ubuthi buphele, sivuke ngemva kwehora noma amabili, **siqedele inyoka esasilokhu siyidla.**

Isikhumba saso sibanzi, sithambile, futhi cishe akunakwenzeka ukusiluma. Uma ibhadi yesihlahla libanjwe ngemuva entanyeni, lingaziphendulela *ngaphakathi esikhumbeni salo* lilume noma yini elibambile. Imijovo yezinyosi, imikhonto yephungula, amazinyo obhejane — alikhathali lutho olungaqeda ezinye izilwane.

Liyahlasela ezinyovini lithathe uju nama-grub, limukele izinduku eziningi zezinyozi njengento evamile nje yokwenza umsebenzi. Yingakho nalo igama lalo livela lapho.

Lingazimela ngisho phambi kwezimpisi, amabhubesi, noma amangqina. Akusikho ukuthi lilindele ukunqoba. Kungenxa yokuthi enye indlela — *ukukhathala* — ayikaze ifike engqondweni yalo.

Elidume kakhulu kubo, **uStoffel**, lihlala e-Moholoholo rehabilitation centre eNingizimu Afrika, futhi wayebheka noma iyiphi indawo abemvalela kuyo njengokuhlambalaza kwakhe. Wayebeka amatshe enze izitebhisi zokukhuphuka odongeni. Wayegingqa udaka enze izinyathelo. Wayesebenzisa ama-rake asala epenshini njengemililwane. Wafunda nokuvula amasango. UStoffel, empeleni, ungumvikeli wabo bonke onjiniyela abangakaze bamukele ukuthi into ethile ayinakwenziwa.

Ibhadi yesihlahla alikhathali.

# Iphengwini Elikhulu

*Uma i-honey badger ibonisa ukuthi ukungesabi kubukeka kanjani, iphungwini lona libonisa ukuthi ukungesabi kwenziwa **ngani**. Konke okungezansi nakho kuyiqiniso.*

Iphengwini ukuphela kwesilwane esincelisayo emhlabeni esimbozwe izikali. Lezo zikali zenziwe ngekeratin — okufanayo okwakha izinzipho zakho — futhi yilokho okwenza kube usizi lwakhona, esizokhuluma ngakho.

Iphengwini elikhulu (*Smutsia gigantea*) yilo elikhulu kunawo wonke phakathi kwezinhlobo eziyisishiyagalombili, lingafinyelela cishe ku-1.5m ukusuka ekhaleni kuya umsila. Alinawo amazinyo. Lidla ngolimi oluqala eduze nomgogodla walo, olude kangangokuba lungedlula nomzimba walo wonke — lude ngokwanele ukuze luqoqe izinkulungwane zezimpukane nofezela ebusuku obubodwa.

Ukuvikelwa kwalo ukuphela kwalo, wukugoqa libe yibhola. Igama lithi lisuka esiMalay *pengguling* — "ongagoqayo." Uma seligoqiwe, izikali zalo zivalana zibe yisivikelo esiqinile kangangokuba ngisho nempisi ivame ukusishiya isilahle, ekugcineni, ihambe. Alilumi. Aligijimi. Limane lilinde, ligoqiwe, kuze ingozi idikibale.

Lobu buthule bulo yilona elenza kube isilwane esithathwa kakhulu emhlabeni wonke — izigidi zithathwa ngenxa yezikali zaso nenyama yaso. Zonke izinhlobo eziyisishiyagalombili sezivikeliwe; ezinye sezisengozini enkulu yokushabalala. Leli bhola elilisindisa empisini, yilo elenza kube lula ukuthi umuntu alimuke nje kalula.

Isilwane esithobekile, esinezikhali, esiphuma ebusuku, esidla izimpukane kuphela futhi esingafuni lutho emhlabeni ngaphandle kokuthula. Kufanele kube yikho ukungesabi okuvikela izidalwa ezifana nalo.
